### [38] [FlowEval: Reference-based Evaluation of Generated User Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04165)
*Jason Wu,Priyan Vaithilingam,Eldon Schoop,Jeffrey Nichols,Titus Barik*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了FlowEval框架，通过对比真实网站与生成UI的导航轨迹相似度来评估UI生成质量，在保证可扩展性的同时与人类专家判断高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UI开发中LLM和编码代理的评估存在两难：人类专家评估准确但缓慢昂贵，自动评估可扩展但准确性低且不透明，缺乏可靠的方法来评估生成UI的视觉和交互设计能力。

Method: 提出FlowEval框架，这是一种基于参考的评估方法。通过从真实网站收集导航轨迹作为参考，在生成的UI类比上执行相同的交互流程，使用动态时间规整（DTW）等基于参考的相似度度量来比较两个轨迹的相似性，从而判断生成UI是否支持现实的交互流程。

Result: 在小型专家UI评估者研究中，基于参考的度量指标与人类判断呈现强相关性，证明了该方法能够有效评估UI生成系统的质量。

Conclusion: FlowEval成功填补了UI生成评估领域的空白，提供了一种既可扩展又值得信赖的评估方案，为UI生成系统的自动化质量评估开辟了新途径。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) and coding agents are often applied to user interface (UI) development, developers find it difficult to reliably assess their proficiency in visual and interaction design. Existing evaluations either rely on human experts, who can accurately assess usability by testing critical flows but are slow and costly, or on automated judges, which are scalable but less accurate and opaque. We present FlowEval, a reference-based framework that measures whether a generated UI supports realistic interaction flows by comparing navigation traces from real websites to traces from generated analogs using reference-based similarity metrics (e.g., dynamic time warping). In a small-scale study with expert UI evaluators, we show that reference-based metrics strongly correlate with human judgments, suggesting that they can provide scalable yet trustworthy evaluation for UI generation systems.

</details>


### [39] [ARMATA: Auto-Regressive Multi-Agent Task Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04225)
*Yazan Youssef,Aboelmagd Noureldin,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集中式端到端自回归框架，通过多阶段解码机制联合求解多智能体系统的区域分配与路径规划问题，相比工业求解器在秒级时间内实现高达20%的解质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将多智能体协调问题中的区域分配与路径规划两个阶段解耦，忽略了阶段间的相互依赖关系；或依赖缺乏全局上下文的分布式启发式方法，导致难以找到全局最优解。

Method: 提出一种集中式、完全端到端的自回归框架，核心是多阶段解码机制。该机制在单次自回归过程中统一生成高层分配决策和底层路径序列，同时维护集中式全局状态，使模型能够隐式地平衡工作负载分配与路径效率。

Result: 在广泛实验中，该方法显著优于多种基线方法。与Google OR-Tools、IBM CPLEX和LKH-3等工业求解器相比，解质量提升高达20%，且计算时间从数小时缩减至数秒。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过集中式端到端学习联合求解分配与路由问题的有效性，为复杂层次化组合优化问题提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Coordinating multi-agent systems over spatially distributed areas requires solving a complex hierarchical problem: first distributing areas among agents (allocation) and subsequently determining the optimal visitation order (routing). Existing methods typically decouple these stages ignoring inter-stage dependencies or rely on decentralized heuristics that lack global context. In this work, we propose a centralized, fully end-to-end auto-regressive framework that jointly generates allocation decisions and routing sequences. The core contribution of our approach is a multi-stage decoding mechanism that unifies high-level allocation and low-level routing in a single autoregressive pass while maintaining a centralized global state. This enables the model to implicitly balance workload distribution with routing efficiency, avoiding local optima common in decentralized methods. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms diverse baselines, achieving up to a 20\% improvement in solution quality over industrial solvers such as Google OR-Tools, IBM CPLEX, and LKH-3, while reducing computation time from hours to seconds.

</details>


### [40] [Governed Collaborative Memory as Artificial Selection in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04264)
*Diego F. Cuadros,Abdoul-Aziz Maiga,Helen Meskhidze,Andre Curtis-Trudel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了“治理式协作记忆”框架，解决持久化LLM多智能体系统中哪些候选记忆应转化为共享制度状态的设计问题，并提出了分层架构与设计议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着持久化记忆使LLM智能体从无状态转变为跨会话、跨版本的状态承载组件，仅依靠检索准确率或访问控制已无法回答核心设计问题：哪些候选记忆应当成为共享的制度状态？记忆治理成为一个亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 将记忆治理问题框架化为“治理式协作记忆”，视其为选择机制，区分了四种治理方式（无治理持久化、宪法式/混合选择、自动度量选择、人工批准的人工选择）。提出分层架构，分离智能体本地记忆、共享制度记忆、档案记忆与项目延续记忆，并通过来源追溯与版本谱系使选择可审查。使用一个运行中的LLM多智能体生态系统的文档化追踪案例进行验证。

Result: 通过案例分析展示了未管理的虚假记忆持久化、被批准的制度记忆、拒绝与修订、保持身份的扩展以及治理即学习等现象，验证了该框架的有效性。提出了记忆评估的六个维度：来源忠实度、选择可追溯性、认识质量、修正路径和角色保持。

Conclusion: 持久化LLM多智能体系统的设计议程应超越传统的召回率与性能评估，将来源忠实度、选择可追溯性、认识质量、修正路径和角色保持纳入记忆评估体系，以实现有效的记忆治理与制度状态管理。

Abstract: Persistent memory is turning language-model-based agents from stateless participants in isolated interactions into state-bearing components of LLM-based multi-agent systems. As memory becomes durable, reloadable, and behavior-shaping across agents, sessions, or versions, a design question arises that is not captured by retrieval accuracy or access control alone: which candidate memories should become shared institutional state? This Viewpoint frames that problem as governed collaborative memory. We argue that memory governance functions as a selection regime, determining which memory variants persist, which remain private, and which are rejected, abstained from, or superseded. We distinguish ungoverned persistence, constitutional or hybrid selection, automatic metric-based selection, and human-ratified artificial selection, emphasizing that these regimes are not a ranking but a design choice over target properties. We then describe a layered architecture that separates agent-local memory, shared institutional memory, archive memory, and project-continuity memory, with provenance and version lineage making selection inspectable. Documented traces from one running LLM-based multi-agent ecosystem illustrate unmanaged false-memory persistence, ratified institutional memory, rejection and revision, identity-preserving expansion, and governance-as-learning. The contribution is a design agenda: persistent LLM-based multi-agent systems should evaluate memory not only for recall and performance, but also for provenance fidelity, selection traceability, epistemic quality, correction pathways, and role preservation.

</details>


### [41] [DAO-enabled decentralized physical AI: A new paradigm for human-machine collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04522)
*Mark C. Ballandies,Florian Spychiger,Uwe Serdült,Claudio J. Tessone*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了DAO赋能的去中心化物理AI（DePAI），一种用于协调人类与自主机器、运营和治理物理-数字系统的民主化架构，旨在通过链上激励和无许可参与实现可扩展、有韧性的自组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物理基础设施、人工智能与数字系统的深度融合，缺乏一种能够有效协调人类与自主机器、兼顾透明规则与人类自主性的治理架构。现有系统在安全性、中心化风险及激励失效等方面存在挑战，亟需一种将去中心化自治组织（DAO）、数字民主与物理基础设施网络相结合的创新框架。

Method: 首先，综合区块链、DAO与加密经济学的基础理论；其次，将DAO设计与数字民主中的审议和投票研究相结合，实现两者互相促进；然后，将DAO治理的去中心化物理基础设施网络（DePIN）置于垂直集成技术栈中，连接能源、感知、连接、存储/计算、模型与机器人；最后，构建将机器执行与人类监督耦合的工作流，实现技术-社会-经济系统的自组织，并分析风险与提出价值敏感设计。

Result: 成功构建了DePAI架构，实现了物理基础设施、AI与社区所有权的整合；建立了透明规则、链上激励和无许可参与的治理模式；分析了包括安全、中心化、激励失效、法律风险及内在动机挤出等风险，并提出了通过价值敏感设计和持续适应性治理来应对。

Conclusion: DePAI为可扩展、有韧性的自组织提供了一条可行路径，在整合物理基础设施、AI与社区所有权的同时，通过透明规则、链上激励和无许可参与，旨在保护人类自主性，推动技术-社会-经济系统的民主化治理。

Abstract: We propose DAO-enabled decentralized physical AI (DePAI), a democratic architecture for coordinating humans and autonomous machines in the operation and governance of physical-digital systems. We (1) synthesize foundations in blockchains, decentralized autonomous organizations (DAOs), and cryptoeconomics; (2) connect DAO design with digital-democracy research on deliberation and voting, showing how each can advance the other; (3) position DAO-governed decentralized physical infrastructure networks (DePIN) within a vertically integrated stack that links energy and sensing to connectivity, storage/compute, models, and robots; (4) show how these elements specify workflows that couple machine execution with human oversight, enabling enhanced self-organization of techno-socio-economic systems, which we call DePAI; and (5) analyze risks, including security, centralization, incentive failure, legal exposure, and the crowding-out of intrinsic motivation, and argue for value-sensitive design and continuously adaptive governance. DePAI offers a path to scalable, resilient self-organization that integrates physical infrastructure, AI, and community ownership under transparent rules, on-chain incentives, and permissionless participation, aiming to preserve human autonomy.

</details>

*Wei Hu,Quanyi Liang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散时间异构多智能体系统的自治同步问题，通过将其转化为稳定模态的渐近解耦问题，提出了基于智能体初始动态矩阵平均值的同步充分条件，降低了现有条件的保守性并实现了同构与异构系统的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离散时间异构多智能体系统的自治同步问题。现有方法通常要求智能体动态矩阵之间的差异较小，条件较为保守，且难以统一处理同构和异构系统。因此需要寻找更宽松、更具一般性的同步条件。

Method: 将同步问题转化为一类离散时间线性时变系统中稳定模态的渐近解耦问题，并为其提供了一个充分条件。基于该条件，利用智能体初始动态矩阵的平均值来建立同步判据，而不需要对矩阵间差异的大小进行限制。

Result: 建立了新的同步充分条件，该条件仅依赖于智能体初始动态矩阵的平均值，无需假设矩阵间差异很小。数值仿真结果验证了所提理论方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法显著降低了现有同步条件的保守性，成功实现了同构与异构多智能体系统同步分析的统一框架，为异构系统的协调控制提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates the autonomous synchronization problem for discrete-time heterogeneous multiagent systems.
  The synchronization problem is transformed into the asymptotic decoupling problem of stable modes in a class of discrete-time linear time-varying systems,
  for which we provide a sufficient condition.
  Leveraging this condition, synchronization conditions are established.
  The synchronization conditions are based on the average of the agents' initial dynamic matrices,
  without requiring the differences among these matrices to be small.
  This approach reduces the conservativeness of existing conditions and achieves a unification of both homogeneous and heterogeneous systems.
  Numerical simulation results are provided to support the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [43] [SWE-WebDevBench: Evaluating Coding Agent Application Platforms as Virtual Software Agencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04637)
*Siddhant Saxena,Nilesh Trivedi,Vinayaka Jyothi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SWE-WebDev Bench，一个针对"vibe coding"AI应用构建平台的68指标评估框架，涵盖25个主要指标和43个诊断指标。对6个平台的评估揭示了四个主要缺陷：规格说明瓶颈、前后端解耦、生产就绪性陡坡以及安全基础设施失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着"vibe coding"平台的兴起，用户可以用自然语言描述应用，AI代理自主生成全栈软件。现有评估仅关注代码级基准，缺乏将这些平台视为虚拟软件开发机构进行系统评估的方法，需要评估其理解业务需求、架构决策、编写生产代码、处理迭代修改和维护业务就绪性的能力。

Method: 提出SWE-WebDev Bench评估框架，包含68个指标（25个主要指标+43个诊断指标），分为七组，沿三个维度组织：交互模式（应用创建请求ACR vs. 应用修改请求AMR）、代理角度（产品经理PM、工程、运维）和复杂度层级（T4多角色SaaS、T5 AI原生）。对六个平台、三个领域、18个评估单元进行了系统性评估。

Result: 评估揭示了当前AI应用构建平台的四个普遍缺陷：（1）规格说明瓶颈——将丰富业务需求压缩为过度简化的技术方案；（2）普遍的前后端解耦——视觉精美的UI掩盖了缺失或损坏的后端基础设施；（3）陡峭的生产就绪性悬崖——无平台工程质量得分超过60%，生成后人工投入差异显著；（4）广泛的安全与基础设施故障——无平台安全得分超过65%（目标90%），并发处理能力低至6%。

Conclusion: 这些发现描述了当前样本的特征，需要更大规模复制以确立普遍性。作者发布SWE-WebDev Bench作为社区基准，以促进复制研究并帮助平台构建者识别和解决这些差距。代码和基准资源已在GitHub和官网公开。

Abstract: The emergence of "vibe coding" platforms, where users describe applications in natural language and AI agents autonomously generate full-stack software, has created a need for rigorous evaluation beyond code-level benchmarks. In order to assess them as virtual software development agencies on understanding business requirements, making architectural decisions, writing production code, handling iterative modifications, and maintaining business readiness, we introduce SWE-WebDev Bench, a 68-metric evaluation framework spanning 25 primary and 43 diagnostic metrics across seven groups, organized along three dimensions: Interaction Mode (App Creation Request (ACR) vs. App Modification Request (AMR)), Agency Angle (Product Manager (PM), Engineering, Ops), and Complexity Tier (T4 multi-role SaaS, T5 AI-native).
  Our evaluation (six platforms, three domains, 18 evaluation cells) reveals four recurring shortcomings in the current generation of AI app builders: (1) A specification bottleneck, where platforms compress rich business requirements into oversimplified technical plans, (2) A pervasive frontend-backend decoupling, where visually polished UIs mask absent or broken backend infrastructure, (3) A steep production-readiness cliff, where no platform scores above 60% on engineering quality and post-generation human effort varies substantially across platforms and (4) Widespread security and infrastructure failures, with no platform exceeding 65% Security Score against a 90% target and concurrency handling as low as 6%. These observations are descriptive of our sample and require larger-scale replication to establish generality. We release SWE-WebDev Bench as a community benchmark to enable such replication and help platform builders identify and address these gaps.
  Code and benchmark resources are available at: https://github.com/snowmountainAi/webdevbench and https://webdevbench.com/.

</details>


### [44] [Hierarachical Multiagent Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Group Tax Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04741)
*Honglei Guo,Yuhan Zhao,Yexin Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于双层训练框架的多智能体强化学习方法，用于解决多政府竞争下的税收策略优化问题，有效避免了博弈崩溃并显著提升了政策的可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的经济决策模型大多关注单一政府-家庭群体，忽视了现实中多政府竞争存在的策略互动（如税收竞争），无法捕捉跨政府间的博弈关系。

Method: 将税收问题建模为分层多群体博弈：群体内部为政府-家庭的领导-跟随博弈，群体间为政府间的竞争博弈。提出了结合课程学习和闭环序贯更新策略的双层训练框架，以稳定多智能体强化学习的训练过程。

Result: 在基于经典经济模型的税收博弈仿真环境中进行实验。与未使用所提更新机制的基线相比，有效博弈时长延长60.92%，政府间GDP差距减少44.12%，成功避免了博弈过早崩溃，学习到了稳定且惠及所有参与群体的税收政策。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效解决复杂的分层多群体经济博弈问题，学习出可持续且稳健的税收政策，为多政府竞争环境下的经济决策建模提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has increasingly been used to study economic decision-making, such as taxation, public spending, and labour supply. However, most existing RL-based economic models focus on a single government--household group, thereby overlooking the strategic interactions that arise when multiple governments compete while managing their own populations. In practice, many economic systems (e.g., taxation) exhibit a multi-group structure, where each government must optimize its fiscal policy in response not only to household behaviour within its jurisdiction, but also to the policies of other competing governments. To capture this structure, we formulate taxation as a hierarchical multi-group game. Within each group, the interaction between the government and households is modelled as a leader--follower game; across groups, governments are modelled as players in a competitive game. This results in a hybrid hierarchical game that is difficult to solve using standard multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithms. We therefore propose a bi-level training framework built on multi-agent reinforcement learning, together with \textit{ Curriculum Learning} and a \textit{ Closed-Loop Sequential Update} strategy, to stabilize training and promote convergence. We instantiate this framework in a taxation game simulation environment grounded in classical economic models. The environment supports the evaluation of different taxation algorithms and provides multiple economic indicators for assessing policy performance. Experiments show that our approach can learn stable tax policies that benefit all participating groups. Compared with a two-group baseline without the proposed update mechanisms, our method avoids premature game collapse, extends the effective game duration by 60.92\%, produces more sustainable and robust tax policies, and reduces GDP disparities among governments by 44.12\%.

</details>

*Jinghui Xu,Boyi Shangguan,Mengke Zhu,Hao Liu,Junhuan Jiang,Guangjun He,Pengming Feng,Shichao Jin,Bin Liang,Yongzhe Chang,Junbo Tan,Tiantian Zhang,Xueqian Wang*

TL;DR: 本文提出了轻量级多模态元规划器（LMMP）框架，通过引入元任务库注入遥感专家知识，结合双感知机制和两阶段训练流程，显著提升了自主地球观测智能体的工具调用准确率和任务成功率，具有良好的即插即用通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的自主地球观测智能体架构将规划与执行集成在单一模型中，在动态地球观测场景下难以应对组合复杂性问题，且容易出现推理错误，亟需一种能够有效处理多步任务并确保物理可行性的新框架。

Method: 提出轻量级多模态元规划器（LMMP）框架：1）引入双感知机制，将多模态图像特征与高层任务语义相结合；2）构建元任务库，将遥感专家知识直接注入工作流，标准化领域逻辑并确保规划物理可行；3）采用两阶段训练流程，通过专家蒸馏监督微调初始化元规划器，并基于执行反馈进行直接偏好优化精调。

Result: 在EarthBench和ThinkGeo衍生的数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，LMMP显著提升了工具调用准确率和任务成功率，并展现出强大的"即插即用"通用性，能够持续提升不同执行器骨干在未见过的地球观测任务中的性能。

Conclusion: LMMP框架有效解决了自主地球观测智能体在动态场景下的规划与执行挑战，通过融合专家知识与多模态特征，实现了更高的任务执行效率和更强的泛化能力，为未来自主地球观测系统的发展提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous Earth Observation (EO) agents are transitioning from passive perception to complex, multi-step task execution. However, current architectures that integrate planning and execution within a single model often struggle with combinatorial complexity and reasoning errors in dynamic EO scenarios. To resolve these challenges, we propose the Lightweight Multimodal Meta-Planner (LMMP) framework. LMMP incorporates a dual-awareness mechanism that grounds strategic plans in both multimodal image features and high-level task semantics. Crucially, we introduce a Meta Task Library to inject remote sensing expert knowledge directly into the workflow, which standardizes domain logic and ensures plans are physically feasible. We further implement a two-stage training pipeline, initializing the Meta-Planner via expert-distilled Supervised Fine-Tuning and refining it through Direct Preference Optimization based on execution feedback. Extensive experiments on a dataset derived from EarthBench and ThinkGeo demonstrate that LMMP significantly improves tool-calling accuracy and task success rates. Moreover, the framework exhibits strong ``plug-and-play'' versatility, consistently enhancing the performance of diverse executor backbones across previously unseen EO missions.

</details>


### [46] [Tree-based Credit Assignment for Multi-Agent Memory System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04811)
*Marina Mao,Alexandr Liu,Pengbo Li,Siheng Li,Bo Zhou,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了TreeMem方法，通过树结构和蒙特卡洛平均将粗粒度的最终奖励转化为针对每个智能体的细粒度优化信号，解决了多智能体记忆系统中信用分配困难的问题，无需任务特定的标注即可有效提升长程任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的多智能体记忆系统存在信用分配困境：若对全部智能体使用统一的最终任务奖励（如QA准确率），信号过于粗粒度和模糊；若为不同子任务的智能体设计特定奖励，则需要昂贵的标注成本且定义困难。因此，迫切需要一种既能提供细粒度优化信号又无需额外标注的方法。

Method: 提出Tree-based Credit Assignment for Multi-Agent Memory Systems (TreeMem)。该方法将多智能体流水线（构建器-摘要器-检索器）扩展为树结构，每个智能体的输出作为节点展开为多个后续分支。通过蒙特卡洛方法对其后续分支进行平均，估算每个智能体对最终奖励的贡献，从而将粗粒度的最终奖励转化为针对特定智能体的优化信号，并以此同时更新所有智能体的策略。

Result: 在长程基准测试上的实验结果表明，TreeMem相比强基线显著提升了记忆系统的性能，验证了树形信用分配机制在多智能体记忆系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: TreeMem成功解决了多智能体记忆系统中的信用分配问题，实现了无需任务特定标注的细粒度优化，促进了异构智能体的有效专业化，为提升LLM长程任务能力提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Memory systems are widely adopted to enhance LLMs for long-horizon tasks, and are commonly organized as multi-agent pipelines with memory building, summarizing, and retrieval agents. To empower this system, existing RL-based methods either apply final downstream task rewards (e.g., QA accuracy) for all agents uniformly, which are coarse and ambiguous, or design task-specific rewards for agents on different subtasks, which require costly annotations (e.g., key evidence) and are difficult to define reliably. To address these limitations, we propose Tree-based Credit Assignment for Multi-Agent Memory Systems (TreeMem), which derives agent-specific credit from the final reward without task-specific annotations. Specifically, TreeMem extends the multi-agent pipeline (builder--summarizer--retrieval) into a tree structure, where each agent's outputs are expanded into multiple subsequent branches. The contribution of each agent is estimated via Monte Carlo averaging over its subsequent branches, capturing how intermediate agent actions may influence the final reward. This converts the coarse final reward into agent-specific optimization signals. These signals are then used to update all agent policies simultaneously, helping heterogeneous agents specialize effectively. Experiments on long-horizon benchmarks show that TreeMem improves memory system performance over strong baselines, validating the effectiveness of tree-structured credit assignment for the multi-agent memory system.

</details>


### [47] [Evolving Idea Graphs with Learnable Edits-and-Commits for Multi-Agent Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04922)
*Jiangwen Dong,Bo Li,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了EIG（Evolving Idea Graphs），一种基于图的multi-agent科学创意生成框架，通过显式图状态表示和学习的双头控制器显著提升了研究创意的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动multi-agent系统通过临时文本（如草稿或聊天记录）协调代理，难以定位生成创意中的弱点并理解代理如何改进这些创意。

Method: 提出了Evolving Idea Graphs (EIG)框架，将部分形成的研究提案表示为演化的创意图：节点捕获科学主张，边编码关系（如支持和冲突），使未解决的弱点在整个创意演化过程中保持可识别。设计了一个学习的双头控制器：一个头选择图编辑操作供代理执行，另一个头决定何时将图提交为最终提案合成。

Result: 在AI Idea Bench 2025和LiveIdeaBench上，EIG在自动基准分数和盲审专家评分方面均优于所有对比系统。消融实验表明显式图状态提供了主要性能增益，学习的编辑-提交控制增加了持续改进。

Conclusion: 基于图的显式状态表示比纯文本协调更有效，能够生成具有更高新颖性、可行性和清晰度的高性能研究创意，为科学发现加速提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: LLM-empowered multi-agent systems offer new potential to accelerate scientific discovery by generating novel research ideas. However, existing methods typically coordinate agents through temporary texts, such as drafts or chat logs; it is difficult to pinpoint the weaknesses in the generated ideas and how the agents refine them. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Evolving Idea Graphs} (EIG), a graph-based multi-agent scientific ideation framework that can generate high-performance research ideas across various benchmark-native metrics, such as novelty, feasibility, and clarity. Instead of coordinating solely through texts, EIG represents a partially formed proposal as an evolving idea graph, where nodes capture scientific claims and edges encode relations (e.g., support and conflict), enabling unresolved weaknesses to remain identifiable throughout the idea evolving process. Specifically, a learned two-head controller operates over the evolving graph to guide the ideation: one head selects graph edits for agents to execute, while the other decides when the graph is ready for commit as final proposal synthesis. On AI Idea Bench 2025 and LiveIdeaBench, EIG outperforms all compared systems on both automatic benchmark scores and blind expert ratings. Ablations further show that explicit graph state provides the main performance gains, and learned edit-and-commit control adds consistent improvements.

</details>

### [48] [OpenCLAW-Nexus: A Self-Reinforcing Trust Framework for Byzantine-Resilient Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04091)
*Wenyang Jia,Qiankang Xu,Ziwei Yan,Chunhua Kang,Yang Yang,Jinglu He,Kai Lei*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenCLAW-Nexus框架，通过统一的折扣Beta信誉模型解决了去中心化联邦学习中的信任缺口问题，在无需可信根数据集的情况下实现了抗拜占庭和Sybil攻击的节点选择、聚合与共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习虽然消除了中心聚合器，但引入了严重的"信任缺口"：在没有可信协调者的情况下，系统容易受到拜占庭攻击和女巫攻击。现有解决方案将节点选择、聚合和共识视为独立模块，且通常依赖在真正去中心化环境中不可用的可信根数据集。

Method: 提出OpenCLAW-Nexus自增强信任框架，使用单一原语——折扣Beta信誉模型，统一了基于信誉的节点选择、信誉加权聚合（Rep-FedAvg）和信誉感知的BFT共识。其中Rep-FedAvg消除了对可信根数据集的依赖，并在非独立同分布数据和噪声评估下形式化证明了诚实节点与拜占庭节点之间的信誉分离特性。

Result: 在跨三个云服务商、九个区域、包含1000个节点的全球测试平台上：Rep-FedAvg在非独立同分布CIFAR-10数据集上，面对20%拜占庭节点和记录级差分隐私时达到72.6%准确率，与集中式FLTrust仅相差0.5个百分点。在300节点女巫攻击下，信誉加权共识保持84.2%的验证正确率，显著优于PoW（62.8%）和PoS（47.6%）。

Conclusion: OpenCLAW-Nexus成功弥合了去中心化联邦学习的信任缺口，通过统一的信誉机制实现了无需可信根数据集的鲁棒性，在真实规模部署中展现出优异的抗攻击性能，为去中心化联邦学习提供了可信赖的解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) eliminates the central aggregator but introduces a severe 'trust gap': without a trusted coordinator, the system becomes vulnerable to Byzantine and Sybil attacks, while existing solutions treat node selection, aggregation, and consensus as isolated modules, often relying on a trusted root dataset unavailable in truly decentralized settings.We propose OpenCLAW-Nexus, a self-reinforcing trust framework that bridges this gap through a single primitive, a discounted Beta-reputation model, that unifies reputation-based node selection, reputation-weighted aggregation Rep-FedAvg, and reputation-aware BFT consensus. Rep-FedAvg eliminates the trusted root dataset requirement; we formally prove reputation separation between honest and Byzantine nodes under non-IID data with noisy evaluations.On a 1,000-node global testbed spanning three cloud providers and nine regions, Rep-FedAvg achieves 72.6% accuracy on non-IID CIFAR-10 with 20% Byzantine nodes and record-level differential privacy, within 0.5,pp of centralized FLTrust.Under a 300-node Sybil attack, reputation-weighted consensus maintains 84.2% validation correctness versus 62.8% (PoW) and 47.6% (PoS).

</details>


### [49] [Resilient AI Supercomputer Networking using MRC and SRv6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04333)
*Joao Araujo,Alex Chow,Mark Handley,Ryder Lewis,Christoph Paasch,Jitendra Padhye,Michael Papamichael,Greg Steinbrecher,Amin Tootoonchian,Lihua Yuan,S. Anantharamu,Abhishek Dosi,Mohit Garg,Mahdieh Ghazi,Torsten Hoefler,Deepal Jayasinghe,Jithin Jose,Abdul Kabbani,Guohan Lu,Yang Wang,K. Doddapaneni,Murali Garimella,Vipin Jain,Yanfang Le,H. Nagulapalli,S. Narayanan,Rong Pan,Rathina Sabesan,Raghava Sivaramu,Rip Sohan,Eric Davis,Dragos Dumitrescu,Mohan Kalkunte,Bhaswar Mitra,Guglielmo Morandin,Adrian Popa,Costin Raiciu,Eric Spada,John Spillane,Niranjan Vaidya,Aviv Barnea,Idan Burstein,Elazar Cohen,Yamin Friedman,Noam Katz,Masoud Moshref,Yuval Shpigelman,Shahaf Shuler,Shy Shyman,Sayantan Sur*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对超大规模同步预训练任务中的尾部延迟问题，提出了一种包含新传输协议MRC、多平面Clos拓扑和静态源路由(SRv6)的三管齐下解决方案，已在OpenAI和Microsoft的超大规模训练集群中成功应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超大规模集群上运行同步预训练任务时，尾部延迟（Tail latency）成为主导性能瓶颈，严重影响训练效率。为了解决这一问题，需要从传输协议、网络拓扑和路由机制三个方面进行创新。

Method: 提出三管齐下的方法：（1）设计基于RDMA的新型传输协议MRC，通过在多路径上分散传输并主动负载均衡来消除流碰撞问题；（2）采用多平面Clos拓扑结构，结合高交换机基数和冗余优势，使超过10万GPU的训练集群能够构建为两层拓扑；（3）使用SRv6静态源路由，赋予MRC自主绕过故障的能力。

Result: MRC和静态SRv6路由已在OpenAI和Microsoft的最大训练集群中部署运行，用于训练最新的前沿模型。实验证明MRC能够使AI训练任务从容应对许多原本会中断训练的网络故障，显著提高了系统的容错能力。

Conclusion: 通过结合多路径传输协议、优化拓扑结构和智能路由机制，可以有效解决超大规模AI训练中的尾部延迟问题，提升训练任务的网络容错性和整体性能，为构建更大规模的训练集群提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tail latency dominates the performance of synchronous pretraining jobs when running at very large scales. We describe a three-pronged approach: (1) a new RDMA-based transport protocol, MRC, sprays across many paths and actively load-balances between them, eliminating the issue of flow collisions (2) the use of multi-plane Clos topologies to get the benefits of high switch radix and redundancy, allowing training clusters well over 100K GPUs to be built as two-tier topologies while increasing physical redundancy, and (3) the use of static source-routing using SRv6 to allow MRC the freedom to bypass failures by itself. We describe our experiences running MRC and static SRv6 routing in production in OpenAI and Microsoft's largest training clusters, where it has been used to train the latest frontier models. We demonstrate how MRC allows AI training jobs to ride out many network failures that previously would have interrupted training.

</details>


### [50] [Worst-Case Discovery and Runtime Protection for RL-Based Network Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04373)
*Hongyu Hè,Minhao Jin,Maria Apostolaki*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了ReGuard框架，通过双层后悔最大化问题发现RL网络控制器的最坏情况，并编译成轻量级逻辑规则进行推理时保护，在无需重训练的情况下显著缩小性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）控制器在网络任务（如拥塞控制和自适应码率流媒体）中实现了强大的平均性能，但在仍可获得良好性能的网络条件下，其性能可能会严重下降。由于枚举方法不可行且形式验证方法对顺序闭环的RL控制器不实用，识别这些条件并量化性能差距成为挑战。

Method: ReGuard框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 发现模块：将最坏情况发现问题形式化为双层后悔最大化问题，产生最坏情况下性能差距的认证下界；(2) 保护模块：将发现的轨迹作为反事实进行分析，编译成轻量级逻辑规则，仅在检测到风险状态时进行干预，否则保持控制器行为不变。

Result: 在Pensieve、Sage和Park三个RL网络控制器上的评估表明：(1) ReGuard发现了控制器性能比可达到性能差43-64%的场景；(2) 发现的差距比最强基线大57%到6倍；(3) 通过轻量级基于规则的保护，将性能差距缩小79-85%，同时保持标称性能；(4) 保护效果扩展到发现的场景之外，改善了更广泛网络条件下的性能。

Conclusion: ReGuard成功实现了RL网络控制器的最坏情况发现和推理时保护，无需重训练即可显著提升控制器在极端条件下的鲁棒性，为RL系统的安全部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: RL-based controllers achieve strong average-case performance in networking tasks such as congestion control and adaptive bitrate streaming. Yet their performance can degrade severely under network conditions where strong performance is still achievable. Identifying such conditions and quantifying the resulting performance gap is intractable by enumeration, while the sequential and closed-loop nature of RL controllers makes formal verification methods impractical.
  We present ReGuard, a framework that discovers worst-case scenarios for a given RL controller and protects it against them at inference time without retraining. Discovery is formulated as a bilevel regret-maximization problem, which yields a certified lower bound on the worst-case performance gap. The discovered trajectories are then analyzed as counterfactuals and compiled into lightweight logic rules that intervene only when a risky state is detected, leaving the controller's behavior unchanged otherwise.
  We evaluate ReGuard across three RL-based network controllers: Pensieve, Sage, and Park. ReGuard discovers scenarios in which the controller's performance is 43$-$64% worse than what is achievable. ReGuard not only discovers gaps 57% to 6$\times$ larger than those found by the strongest baselines but also shrinks them by 79$-$85% via lightweight rule-based protection while preserving nominal performance. ReGuard's protection extends beyond the scenarios it discovers, improving performance across a wider range of network conditions.

</details>


### [51] [Joint Optimization of Trajectory Control, Resource Allocation, and Task Offloading for Multi-UAV-Assisted IoV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04436)
*Maoxin Ji,Qiong Wu,Pingyi Fan,Cui Zhang,Nan Cheng,Wen Chen,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了密集城市环境下的多无人机辅助车联网任务卸载系统，提出了一种结合二阶锥规划、深度强化学习与大语言模型的分层优化框架，有效降低了系统延迟与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集城市场景中，车联网任务卸载面临系统延迟与能耗高、资源分配存在长尾失衡以及多约束耦合导致优化困难等挑战，传统多智能体强化学习方法难以有效应对。

Method: 首先，将非凸优化问题解耦为分层执行框架；其次，提出基于二阶锥规划（SOCP）的序贯分布式优化算法优化无人机3D飞行轨迹；随后，构建融合深度强化学习（DRL）与大语言模型（LLM）的混合资源调度范式，DRL负责初始资源分配，LLM作为语义宏调度器修正长尾分配失衡，并引入奖励解耦机制隔离LLM干预以确保策略收敛；最后，在交替优化循环中利用线性规划（LP）精确确定任务卸载比例。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在任务成功率和系统效率方面显著优于传统多智能体强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过分层解耦与DRL-LLM协同调度，能够有效解决复杂约束下的多无人机辅助车联网任务卸载问题，提升系统整体性能。

Abstract: This paper investigates a multi-Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) joint base station-assisted Internet of Vehicles (IoV) task offloading system in dense urban environments. To minimize system delay and energy consumption under strict coupling constraints, the complex non-convex optimization problem is decoupled into a hierarchical execution framework. First, a sequential distributed optimization algorithm based on Second-Order Cone Programming (SOCP) is proposed to optimize the 3D flight trajectory of each UAV, ensuring adaptive network coverage. Second, a novel hybrid resource scheduling paradigm synergizing Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and Large Language Models (LLMs) is developed. Within this framework, the DRL agent dictates the initial resource allocation, while the LLM acts as a semantic macro-scheduler to rectify long-tail allocation imbalances for failed and surplus tasks. Crucially, a reward decoupling mechanism is introduced to isolate DRL training from external LLM interventions, thereby ensuring policy convergence. Finally, the task offloading ratios are precisely determined via Linear Programming (LP) within an alternating optimization loop. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms traditional multi-agent reinforcement learning baselines in terms of task success rate and system efficiency.

</details>


### [52] [Queue-Aware and Resilient Routing in LEO Satellite Networks Using Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04448)
*Mudassar Liaq,Mahyar Tajeri,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于LEO卫星网络路由的队列感知多智能体深度强化学习(MA-DRL)框架，相比Dijkstra算法在保持竞争性延迟的同时显著降低了开销(约为Dijkstra的50%)，并展现出更好的可扩展性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座作为全球互联网覆盖的新兴解决方案备受关注，但其路由面临高度动态拓扑、时变流量条件和链路故障易感性等根本性挑战。传统路由算法通常假设静态链路度量，未能考虑队列积压或实时系统变化，在这些环境中效果较差。

Method: 提出队列感知多智能体深度强化学习(MA-DRL)框架用于LEO卫星网络路由。将每颗卫星建模为独立智能体负责本地路由决策，实现分布式和可扩展的解决方案。框架制定了延迟感知优化问题，结合背景流量、每颗卫星的队列动态以及弹性评分来提高鲁棒性。

Result: 与SARSA和Dijkstra算法进行对比评估。Dijkstra在理想条件下实现最低端到端延迟，但随着网络规模扩大其计算和信令开销成为显著瓶颈。所提方法开销显著更低(在5秒重计算间隔下约为Dijkstra的50%)，随网络规模高效扩展，并在增加流量负载下有效管理队列积压和弹性。

Conclusion: 该方法在LEO卫星网络中展现出增强的鲁棒性和可扩展性，同时保持竞争性的延迟和弹性评分，为动态LEO网络环境提供了有效的分布式路由解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid growth in data demand and stringent latency requirements of modern applications has driven significant interest in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations as an emerging solution for global Internet coverage. However, routing in LEO networks remains a fundamental challenge due to highly dynamic topologies, time-varying traffic conditions, and its susceptibility to link failures. Conventional routing algorithms typically assume static link metrics and fail to account for queue backlogs or real-time system variations, making them less effective in such environments. We propose a queue-aware multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MA-DRL) framework for routing in LEO satellite networks. Each satellite is modeled as an independent agent responsible for making local routing decisions, enabling a distributed and scalable solution. The proposed framework formulates a latency-aware optimization problem that incorporates background traffic, queue dynamics at each satellite, and a resilience score to improve robustness. We evaluate the proposed approach against the state-action-reward-state-action (SARSA) and Dijkstra algorithms. While Dijkstra achieves the lowest end-to-end latency under ideal conditions, its computational and signaling overhead becomes a significant bottleneck as the network scales. In contrast, our proposed approach incurs significantly lower overhead (approximately 50% of Dijkstra at a 5 s recalculation interval), scales efficiently with network size, and effectively manages queue backlogs and resilience under increasing traffic load, demonstrating enhanced robustness and scalability in LEO satellite networks while maintaining competitive latency and resilience scores.

</details>


### [53] [SADE: Symptom-Aware Diagnostic Escalation for LLM-Based Network Troubleshooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04530)
*Kuan-Hao Tseng,Niruth Bogahawatta,Yasod Ginige,Kosta Dekic,Arunan Sivanathan,Suranga Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了SADE框架，通过编码经典的Cisco分层故障排查方法论，在NIKA基准测试上将根因定位F1分数提升了37个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型代理在网络故障排查中表现不佳，主要原因是缺乏人类网络工程师使用的结构化、逐层排查方法，而是依赖自由形式的推理，导致证据获取与假设验证混淆。

Method: 提出SADE（症状感知诊断升级）框架，将经典Cisco故障排查方法论编码为显式策略。核心包括：(1)阶段门控诊断工作流，分离证据获取与假设验证过程；(2)故障家族技能路由库；(3)高收益诊断辅助工具。

Result: 在NIKA基准测试的523个未见场景事件集上，SADE相比ReAct+GPT-5基线提升根因F1分数37个百分点。对照实验表明，其中22个百分点的提升仅来自诊断策略本身，而非模型升级的副作用。

Conclusion: 将专家方法论显式编码为诊断策略能够显著提升LLM代理在网络故障排查中的性能，证明了结构化工作流分离证据获取与假设验证的重要性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to network troubleshooting, but root-cause localization on public benchmarks remains well below practical deployment thresholds. We argue this is because existing agents do not encode the disciplined, layer-by-layer methodology that human network engineers use, and instead rely on free-form deliberation that conflates evidence acquisition with hypothesis commitment. We present SADE (Symptom-Aware Diagnostic Escalation), an agent that encodes the classical Cisco troubleshooting methodology as an explicit policy. SADE pairs a phase-gated diagnostic workflow, which separates evidence acquisition from hypothesis commitment, with a routed library of fault-family skills and high-yield diagnostic helpers. On a held-out 523 incident set of the public NIKA benchmark covering eleven unseen scenarios, SADE improves root-cause F1 by 37 percentage points over a ReAct + GPT-5 baseline; a model-controlled comparison against the same Claude Sonnet backend without the SADE policy attributes 22 of those points to the diagnostic policy alone, showing that the gain is not a side-effect of the model upgrade.

</details>


### [54] [A Separation Between Optimal Demand-Oblivious and Demand-Aware Network Throughput](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04699)
*Matthias Bentert,Chen Avin,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文证明需求感知网络拓扑在最坏情况下可优于需求无关拓扑（吞吐量至少为5/8 vs 约1/2），解决了领域内开放问题；同时揭示了计算复杂度的分界：弱吞吐量计算是NP难，但直接路由的弱吞吐量存在多项式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对需求无关吞吐量的理解已较成熟，但对需求感知吞吐量（即针对通信需求优化网络拓扑以提升吞吐量）的理论界限和计算复杂度知之甚少。随着可重构光技术在数据中心的应用普及，理解需求感知网络的潜力与局限成为迫切需求。

Method: 系统研究四种需求感知吞吐量变体：经典吞吐量与本文新定义的弱吞吐量；每种定义下区分直接路由（单跳）与一般路由（多跳）两种模型。在理论层面推导吞吐量上界与下界，在计算复杂度层面分析各变体的求解难度。

Result: 主要分离结果：需求感知拓扑在最坏情况下的经典吞吐量渐近至少达5/8，而需求无关拓扑吞吐量为n/(2n-1)（约1/2），证明了需求感知拓扑的优越性，解决了文献中的开放问题。计算复杂度方面：需求感知弱吞吐量计算为NP难，但需求感知（弱）直接吞吐量可在多项式时间内求解。

Conclusion: 从理论和计算角度为需求感知网络设计提供了基础性指导：需求感知拓扑在最坏情况下仍具优势，且不同问题变体的计算复杂度存在本质差异，为后续算法设计与实际部署提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The performance of distributed applications often critically depends on the interconnecting network or more specifically on its throughput: how fast data can be carried across a network. Over the last years, great progress has been made in understanding demand-oblivious throughput: how fast a given demand matrix describing pairwise communication requirements can be served on a given network. However, surprisingly little is known today about the achievable demand-aware throughput: the throughput on a network topology which can be optimized toward the demand. Such demand-aware networks have recently gained popularity in datacenters and are enabled by emerging reconfigurable optical technologies.
  In this paper, we are interested in both the achievable demand-aware throughput bounds as well as in the computational complexity of finding a throughput-optimizing network topology. We take a systematic approach and investigate four variants of demand-aware throughput: we analyze, and derive bounds for, two definitions of throughput, the classic throughput usually considered in the literature, and a new generalized definition which we call weak throughput; for each of them, we consider two routing models, a direct one, where demand can only be served on a single hop, and a general one, where multi-hop routing is allowed.
  Our main result is a separation result which solves an open problem in the literature about the classic throughput definition, showing that demand-aware topologies can outperform demand-oblivious topologies even in the worst case: the demand-aware throughput asymptotically approaches at least 5/8, while it is known that the demand-oblivious throughput is n/(2n-1), which is roughly 1/2. In terms of computational complexity, we show that computing the demand-aware weak throughput is NP-hard, but computing the demand-aware (weak) direct throughput is polynomial-time solvable.

</details>


### [55] [Traffic Chunk Sizing vs. Optical Switching Speed in Future All-Optical Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04829)
*Sleman Mouammar,Thomas Röthig,Soheil Hosseini,Ítalo Brasileiro,Admela Jukan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了全光卫星网络中流量块大小与光交换架构性能之间的关系，发现流量块大小对星载光交换架构的性能要求有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在严格的体积、重量和功耗（SWaP）约束下实现透明全光交换卫星传输的高效资源利用，需要研究不同交换范式（分组、突发或电路）下地面站流量汇聚与路径计算算法对交换架构设计的影响，以及满足特定延迟性能要求的交换架构性能。

Method: 分析了基于最大可调度流量块大小的全光卫星网络性能，评估了未来全光星座中光交换架构的性能；考虑了多种光交换技术（包括MEMS和集成光子学解决方案），从交换速度、功耗和插入损耗等维度进行了对比分析；通过仿真评估流量块大小对星载光交换架构性能的影响。

Result: 仿真结果表明，流量块大小对星载光交换架构所需的性能具有关键影响；不同光交换技术（MEMS和集成光子学）在交换速度、功耗和插入损耗方面各有特点；流量块大小是决定交换架构性能要求的重要因素。

Conclusion: 流量块大小的合理设计对全光卫星网络中光交换架构的性能优化至关重要，在设计星载光交换系统时需要充分考虑流量汇聚策略与交换技术特性的匹配，以满足延迟性能和资源利用效率的要求。

Abstract: To enable efficient resource utilization under stringent Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints through transparent and all-optical switched satellites transmission, various switching paradigms can be considered, including packet, burst, or circuit. To this end, the traffic assembly and algorithmic design for path computations at the ground stations play a key role in determining the switching fabric design. Generally, traffic can be buffered and assembled in chunks at the ground stations and forwarded over the pre-computed optical path in space, similar to terrestrial optical burst switching or fast circuit switching. Regardless of the chosen paradigm, the switching fabric must satisfy specific latency performance requirements. This paper studies the performance of all-optical satellite networks based on the maximum traffic chunk sizes that can be scheduled and the performance of optical switching fabrics in the future over all-optical constellations. We consider various optical switching technologies, including MEMS- and integrated photonic-based solutions, in the context of switching speed, power consumption, and insertion loss. Simulation results indicate that traffic chunk size critically impacts the performance required by optical switching fabrics onboard a satellite.

</details>


### [56] [Look Once, Beam Twice: Camera-Primed Real-Time Double-Directional mmWave Beam Management for Vehicular Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05071)
*Avhishek Biswas,Apala Pramanik,Eylem Ekici,Mehmet C. Vuran*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出VIBE，一种基于视觉辅助的混合模型闭环学习架构，用于毫米波V2X网络的实时双向波束管理，有效降低了链路建立延迟与中断率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波V2X网络面临严重的路径损耗和移动性导致的波束失准问题，需要快速的双向波束对齐。现有方法训练开销大且难以泛化到未知场景，难以满足实时可靠通信需求。

Method: 提出VIBE架构，融合机器学习、模型推理与闭环射频反馈。利用摄像头观测缩小波束搜索空间，避免穷举训练；引入轻量级波束细化与偏移跟踪机制，根据动态需求自适应调整波束。

Result: 在室内外测试平台、公开数据集及实时车辆实验中验证，VIBE相比5G NR分层波束成型具有更低的中断率；在公开数据集上优于现有端到端ML模型，中断率低至1.1-1.4%。

Conclusion: 混合模型驱动的闭环学习架构比纯端到端训练的ML模型更适合现实世界的毫米波车载通信场景，具备强泛化能力与实时性。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) frequencies promise multi-gigabit connectivity for vehicle-to-everything (V2X) networks, but face challenges in terms of severe path loss and mobility-related beam misalignment. Reliable V2X connectivity requires fast, double-directional beam alignment. However, existing methods suffer from high training overhead and limited generalization to unseen scenarios. This paper presents VIsion-based BEamforming(VIBE), a hybrid model-based, closed-loop, learning architecture for real-time double-directional mmWave beam management primed by camera sensing. VIBE fuses machine learning, model-based reasoning, and closed-loop RF feedback to balance beam-pair establishment latency with link quality. VIBE bypasses exhaustive training overhead and accelerates link establishment by leveraging camera observations to reduce the beam-search space. Lightweight beam refinement and offset tracking mechanisms adaptively refine beams in response to dynamic application requirements. VIBE is implemented and evaluated across online indoor/outdoor testbeds, public datasets, and real-time vehicular experiments, demonstrating strong generalization capabilities, making it suitable for real-time V2X communication. Comparisons with 5G NR hierarchical beamforming show that VIBE consistently maintains lower outage rates. Furthermore, VIBE outperforms state-of-the-art end-to-end ML models for beam selection when evaluated on public datasets and achieves outage rates as low as 1.1-1.4 %. The results show that a hybrid model-based, closed-loop learning architecture is better suited for real-world mmWave vehicular connectivity than end-to-end trained ML models. For reproducibility, we publish our code to https://github.com/UNL-CPN-Lab/Look-Once-Beam-Twice.

</details>


### [57] [SILC: Lookahead Caching for Short-form Video Delivery Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05188)
*Maleeha Masood,Shreya Kannan,Om Chabra,Deepak Vasisht,Indranil Gupta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SILC，一种利用短视频推荐系统的未来请求可见性和流行度分布特征的预感知缓存系统，相比现有缓存策略可降低CDN回源带宽成本11.1%至111%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台（如TikTok、Instagram Reels和YouTube Shorts）流量巨大且持续增长。现有研究忽视了短视频系统两个独特特性带来的优化机会：1）推送式推荐系统使用户被动接收算法推荐的视频序列，这为系统设计提供了对未来请求的可见性；2）视频流行度服从高度倾斜的帕累托分布，导致服务中的视频在地理和时间上存在重叠。

Method: 构建了SILC（lookahead-aware caching system），一种预感知缓存系统，核心思想是利用推荐系统提供的未来请求信息进行缓存决策。该方法利用推送式系统的特性获取即将到来的请求序列信息，结合视频流行度的时空重叠特征优化缓存策略，以同时减少CDN缓存未命中率并降低CDN与源站之间的回源带宽。评估数据来源于真实用户：包括现场用户研究和涉及全球100名TikTok用户的数据捐赠项目，模拟了10,000个并发用户的流量场景。

Result: 相比10种最先进的启发式和基于学习的缓存淘汰策略，SILC将CDN的回源成本降低了11.1%至111%。

Conclusion: 短视频平台的推送式推荐机制和内容流行度分布特征可以被有效利用来优化CDN缓存性能。SILC系统证明了预感知缓存策略在短视频分发场景中的显著优势，为短视频平台的CDN优化提供了新的设计思路和实用方案。

Abstract: Short video platforms like TikTok, Instagram Reels, and YouTube Shorts have gained immense popularity in the last few years and are responsible for a large and growing fraction of Internet traffic. We identify two unique opportunities for improving short video delivery using their existing interactions with content delivery networks (CDNs). First, short videos use a push-based recommendation system, where the user is presented a sequence of videos recommended by the algorithm rather than user explicitly picking content to watch (e.g., in YouTube). Such push-based short video systems offer a unique opportunity for system design by providing visibility into upcoming requests. Second, the popularity of these videos follows a highly skewed Pareto distribution, leading to geographical and temporal overlap amongst videos being served. We leverage these opportunities to build SILC - a lookahead-aware caching system, aimed at (i) reducing CDN cache miss rates, as well as (ii) reducing midgress bandwidth between the CDN and the origin server. Our evaluation of SILC uses traces that we collect from real users, through (i) an in-person user study, and (ii) a data donation program involving 100 TikTok users across the world. Using a combination of these traces, we simulate traffic from 10,000 simultaneous users. Our evaluation shows that, compared to 10 state-of-the-art heuristic and learning-based cache eviction policies, SILC reduces a CDN's midgress costs by 11.1% to 111%.

</details>

### [58] [A Physics-Aware Framework for Short-Term GPU Power Forecasting of AI Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04074)
*Mohammad AlShaikh Saleh,Sanjay Chawla,Sertac Bayhan,Haitham Abu-Rub,Ali Ghrayeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于物理信息的DLinear时间序列模型（PI-DLinear），用于AI数据中心短期（5-80分钟）电力需求预测。该模型结合了多节点集总热阻-容网络和牛顿冷却定律，通过新推导的含时常微分方程建模GPU计算、内存利用率和温度之间的关系。实验结果表明，相比当前最先进的Transformer和非Transformer模型，PI-DLinear在MSE、MAE和RMSE指标上分别提升了0.782%-39.08%、0.993%-51.82%和0.370%-22.28%，且预测结果符合物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心由于需要支持异构计算任务（如大语言模型的推理与训练），其电力需求呈现快速波动特性。显著的功率差异可能导致电网不稳定，因此需要准确的短期电力预测方法来保障电网稳定运行。

Method: 提出PI-DLinear模型，该方法将物理知识融入DLinear时间序列预测模型中。物理部分基于多节点集总热阻-容网络，遵循牛顿冷却定律，通过新推导的含时常微分方程（ODE）分别建模并关联GPU计算、内存利用率、温度与功耗之间的关系。模型在真实AI数据中心数据集上进行训练和评估。

Result: PI-DLinear模型在预测精度上优于当前最先进的Transformer和非Transformer模型。平均而言，MSE提升0.782%-39.08%，MAE提升0.993%-51.82%，RMSE提升0.370%-22.28%。更重要的是，在功率节流和负载瞬态事件下，模型的预测曲线始终遵循底层物理规律。

Conclusion: PI-DLinear模型成功实现了AI数据中心短期电力需求的准确预测，证明了将物理信息融入深度学习模型的有效性。该方法不仅提高了预测精度，还确保了预测结果符合物理约束，为AI数据中心的电力管理提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: AI data centers experience rapid fluctuations in power demand due to the heterogeneity of computational tasks that they have to support. For example, the power profile of inference and training of large language models (LLMs) is quite distinct and big divergences can result in the instability of the underlying electricity grid. In this paper we propose, to the best of our knowledge, the first physics-informed DLinear time-series model that can accurately forecast power utilization of an AI data center 5-80 minutes (short-term forecasting) into the future. The physics, based on a multi-node lumped thermal resistance-capacitance (RC) network consistent with Newton's law of cooling, is captured using newly derived time-dependent ordinary differential equations (ODE) that separately models and interlinks power consumption with the GPU compute and memory utilization and temperature. The resulting model, that we refer to as PI-DLinear, trained and evaluated on a real AI data center dataset and is not only more accurate than the state-of-the-art (SOTA) models tested, but the forecast profile respects the underlying physics under power throttling and load transient events. Relative to the SOTA transformer-based and non-transformer-based models, improvements in forecasting accuracy (averaged across all look-back and prediction windows) range from 0.782%-39.08% for MSE, 0.993%-51.82% for MAE, and 0.370%-22.28% for RMSE.

</details>


### [59] [Endogenous Regime Switching Driven by Scalar-Irreducible Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04054)
*Sheng Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“标量不可约动力学”概念，证明了其能通过快慢变量反馈自然产生内生性的体制切换，为构建自主智能系统提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习框架难以实现内生性的体制切换（即行为模式自主转换），通常依赖外部设定。实现这种内生切换对于产生自主智能至关重要，因此需要探索能够自主生成体制转换的新机制。

Method: 提出了动力学系统的分类框架，区分了“标量可约动力学”（可表示为标量目标驱动的梯度流）和“标量不可约动力学”（无法简化为此形式）。通过构建一个极简动力学模型，展示快速动态变量与慢速结构适应之间的反馈机制如何运作。

Result: 研究结果表明，标量不可约动力学能够自然地产生持续的内生体制转换，无需外部调度。与现有机器学习系统主要属于标量可约类别不同，这类动力学提供了内部生成体制切换的机制。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个新的动力学范式用于体制探索，为构建自适应行为由内部组织而非外部规定的自主学习系统提供了潜在路径，推动了从外部驱动向内部自主调节的范式转变。

Abstract: Achieving endogenous regime switching is crucial for the emergence of autonomous intelligence, yet remains a central challenge for existing machine learning frameworks, where such transitions are typically externally imposed. In this work, we introduce a classification that distinguishes scalar-reducible dynamics, which can be expressed as gradient flows driven by a scalar objective, from scalar-irreducible dynamics that cannot be reduced to such a form. While most existing machine learning systems operate within the scalar-reducible class, we demonstrate that scalar-irreducible dynamics naturally enable internally generated regime switching through feedback between fast dynamical variables and slow structural adaptation. Using a minimal dynamical model, we illustrate how this mechanism produces sustained endogenous regime transitions without external scheduling. Our results suggest a new dynamical paradigm for regime exploration and provide a potential route toward autonomous learning systems whose adaptive behavior is organized internally rather than externally prescribed.

</details>


### [60] [FASQ: Flexible Accelerated Subspace Quantization for Calibration-Free LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04084)
*Ye Qiao,Yian Wang,Zhiheng Chen,Hyoukjun Kwon,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FASQ框架，通过乘积量化实现了大语言模型的无校准压缩，在连续设计空间内提供灵活的压缩率，并设计了自定义CUDA内核，首次实现了在消费级GPU上压缩模型推理速度超越FP16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型压缩方法存在明显局限性：传统标量量化仅支持固定位宽（如8/4/3位），压缩点离散有限，且通常需要校准数据；难以在消费级GPU上实现灵活、高效的大模型部署。

Method: 提出FASQ（灵活加速子空间量化）框架：采用乘积量化（Product Quantization）处理LLM权重矩阵，通过调节子向量大小和码本基数两个参数，构建连续设计空间；设计了两种自定义CUDA内核：解码阶段的LUT-free直接计算GEMV，以及预填充阶段的输出平稳双缓冲LUT GEMM，均采用split-K并行技术。

Result: 在Meta-Llama-3-8B上，FASQ在模型大小37-42%时达到67.1-67.7平均准确率，超越4位GPTQ和AWQ；在RTX 3090上，有效4位时达45.2 tok/s（内存减少2.56倍），有效3位时达51.8 tok/s（内存减少2.80倍），均超越FP16张量核心性能（43.9 tok/s）；吞吐量达AWQ的1.6-1.8倍、GPTQ的2.5倍、RTN的4.3-5倍。

Conclusion: FASQ是唯一能实现解码速度超越FP16的压缩方法，为大语言模型在单卡消费级GPU上的部署提供了无校准压缩、连续的大小-质量权衡以及实时推理能力，填补了固定位宽量化方案无法达到的压缩空白。

Abstract: Compressing large language models (LLMs) for deployment on commodity GPUs remains challenging: conventional scalar quantization is limited to fixed bit-widths (e.g., 8/4/3-bit), offers only a few discrete compression points, and typically requires calibration data. We present FASQ (Flexible Accelerated Subspace Quantization), a calibration-free framework that applies product quantization to LLM weight matrices. By tuning two parameters, sub-vector size and codebook cardinality, FASQ exposes a continuous design space spanning 27-49% of the original FP16 model size, filling compression gaps that fixed-bit schemes cannot reach. On Meta-Llama-3-8B, FASQ surpasses 4-bit GPTQ and AWQ in accuracy (67.1-67.7 avg.) at 37-42% model size, with consistent results on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3.5-9B-Base. To make product quantization practical at inference time, we design custom CUDA kernels: a LUT-free direct-compute GEMV for decode and an output-stationary double-buffered LUT GEMM for prefill, both with split-K parallelism. On an RTX~3090, FASQ achieves 45.2 tok/s decode at effective 4-bit (2.56x memory reduction) and 51.8 tok/s at effective 3-bit (2.80x), both surpassing FP16 tensor-core performance (43.9 tok/s) and delivering 1.6 to 1.8x the throughput of AWQ, 2.5 to 2.5x of GPTQ, and 4.3 to 5x of RTN. FASQ is the only compressed method that accelerates decode beyond FP16, offering calibration-free compression, continuous size-quality trade-offs, and real-time inference on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [61] [A Self-Attentive Meta-Optimizer with Group-Adaptive Learning Rates and Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04055)
*JiangBo Zhao,ZhaoXin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaAdamW优化器，通过自注意力机制动态调节各参数组的学习率和权重衰减，在五类任务上相比AdamW最高可提升11.08%性能或减少17.11%训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应优化器（如AdamW）对所有参数组使用统一超参数，忽视了不同层和模块间异质的优化动态特性，限制了优化效率和性能。

Method: 提出MetaAdamW优化器：1) 使用轻量级Transformer编码器处理各参数组的统计特征（梯度范数、动量范数、相关性）；2) 通过自注意力机制动态生成每组的学习率和权重衰减调节因子；3) 引入结合梯度对齐、损失下降和泛化差距的元学习目标训练注意力模块；4) 提出注入任务优先级的同方差不确定性加权方法，实现领域知识指导的自动损失平衡。

Result: 在时间序列预测（ETT）、语言建模（WikiText-2）、机器翻译（Multi30k）、图像分类（CIFAR-10）和情感分析（IMDB）五类任务上，MetaAdamW在验证损失、准确率或困惑度指标上均一致优于AdamW基线；根据任务不同，可减少最高17.11%训练时间或提升最高11.08%性能，仅引入中等开销；在某些情况下还能缓解因过早停止导致的收敛不足问题。

Conclusion: MetaAdamW通过自适应调节各组超参数有效解决了传统优化器忽视参数异质性的问题，消融实验验证了各组件（特征版本、分组策略、优先级注入的不确定性加权）的有效性，为优化器设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers like AdamW apply uniform hyperparameters across all parameter groups, ignoring heterogeneous optimization dynamics across layers and modules. We address this limitation by proposing MetaAdamW - a new optimizer that integrates a self-attention mechanism to dynamically modulate per-group learning rates and weight decay. The modulation factors are produced by a lightweight Transformer encoder that operates on statistical features (gradient norms, momentum norms, correlations) extracted from each parameter group. To train the attention module, we introduce a meta-learning objective that combines gradient alignment, loss decrease, and generalization gap. A key novel contribution is the extension of homoscedastic uncertainty weighting (HUW) with task-specific priorities that directly scale the regularization terms - enabling domain knowledge to guide automatic loss balancing. Extensive experiments on five diverse tasks-time series forecasting (ETT), language modeling (WikiText-2), machine translation (Multi30k), image classification (CIFAR-10), and sentiment analysis (IMDB) - demonstrate that MetaAdamW consistently outperforms the standard AdamW baseline in terms of validation loss, accuracy, or perplexity. Depending on the task, MetaAdamW either reduces overall training time (by up to 17.11%) or improves performance (by up to 11.08%) while introducing only moderate overhead; in some cases, it can also mitigate issues of insufficient convergence caused by premature early stopping. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each component, including feature versions, grouping strategies, and the proposed priority-injected uncertainty weighting.

</details>


### [62] [HERCULES: Hardware-Efficient, Robust, Continual Learning Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04103)
*Matteo Gambella,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述提出了HERCULES框架，将神经架构搜索（NAS）的发展从单一的硬件效率导向扩展为效率、鲁棒性和持续学习三重目标，通过新的分类体系分析了现有方法，并指出了该领域未来的研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI从静态基准测试转向实际部署，传统的仅关注硬件效率的NAS方法已无法满足现实需求。现代NAS方法正演变为解决三重目标：效率（资源受限环境下的可行性）、鲁棒性（环境变化下的可靠性）和持续学习（避免灾难性遗忘的适应能力）。现有研究往往孤立地看待这些目标，缺乏统一的视角。

Method: 提出了一种基于效率、鲁棒性和持续学习三重目标的NAS方法分类体系，将现有方法映射到HERCULES（Hardware-Efficient, Robust, and ContinUal LEarning Search）框架中。定义了HERCULES的十二项任务（the twelve labours），解决多目标NAS中搜索空间探索与巨大计算成本之间的平衡挑战。

Result: 通过统一的分类视角发现，效率、鲁棒性和持续学习这三个研究轴心虽然常被孤立研究，但实际上是相互增强的。成功构建了HERCULES框架，系统性地梳理了当前NAS方法的研究现状。

Conclusion: 本综述识别了现有研究中的关键空白，为实现真正可部署的终身学习AI系统，提出了集成算法、架构和软硬件协同设计的综合研究路线图。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a powerful framework for automatically discovering neural architectures that balance accuracy and efficiency. However, as AI transitions from static benchmarks to real-world deployment, the traditional focus on hardware-aware efficiency is no longer sufficient. We observe that modern NAS methods, especially those that target edge AI, are evolving to address a triple objective: Efficiency, Robustness, and Continual Learning. While efficiency ensures feasibility in resource-constrained environments, robustness guarantees reliability under environmental variabilities, and continual learning enables adaptation to sequential tasks without catastrophic forgetting. We propose a taxonomy of NAS approaches through this triple lens, distinguishing between methods targeting resource optimization, environmental resilience, and architectural plasticity. This unified perspective reveals that these axes, though often studied in isolation, are mutually reinforcing. Building on this taxonomy, we map the current landscape of these NAS methods into a new framework called Hardware-Efficient, Robust, and ContinUal LEarning Search (HERCULES). We define the desiderata, the twelve labours of HERCULES, addressing the non-trivial challenge of balancing an adequate search-space exploration with the immense computational costs of a multi-objective NAS, accounting for these crucial objectives of current AI systems. By identifying critical gaps in existing research, this survey outlines a roadmap toward integrated algorithmic, architectural, and hardware-software co-design for truly deployable, lifelong-learning AI systems.

</details>


### [63] [Transformation Categorization Based on Group Decomposition Theory Using Parameter Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04056)
*Takayuki Komatsu,Yoshiyuki Ohmura,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数分裂方法，通过同态约束将单个变换的参数分解为组件，从而在无监督学习中实现对变换的代数分解，避免了以往方法中的辅助假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管无监督表示学习在提取有意义的特征方面取得了进展，但缺乏一个原则性的框架来定义什么是"好"的表示。经典的解纠缠方法假设各因素相互独立，在因素耦合时失效。先前基于伽罗瓦理论的方法虽然可以处理交换和非交换情况，但依赖于不必要的辅助假设（如运动和等距限制），且消融实验未分离理论与辅助效应。

Method: 提出参数分裂方法：将单个变换的参数分裂为组件，施加同态约束将全变换映射到一个组件，并将正规子群识别为该组件固定为单位元时的变换集合。该方法直接基于群分解理论，摒弃了先前方法中的辅助假设，具有更广泛的适用性。

Result: 在涉及旋转、平移和缩放的图像对上进行了评估，消融实验表明基于群分解的约束驱动了恰当的分类。该方法成功实现了无监督变换分类，且无需依赖运动或等距等辅助假设。

Conclusion: 通过参数分裂和同态约束，本文建立了一个更纯粹的代数框架用于无监督表示学习中的变换分解，为理解表示学习中的群结构提供了理论基础，同时验证了群分解约束在无监督分类中的有效性。

Abstract: Representation learning seeks meaningful sensory representations without supervision and can model aspects of human development. Although many neural networks empirically learn useful features, a principled account of what makes a representation "good" remains elusive. We study unsupervised categorization of transformations between pairs of inputs under algebraic constraints. Classical disentanglement favors mutually independent factors and fails when factors are coupled. Our prior Galois-theoretic approach decomposes a group via normal subgroups by learning a product of two transformations with one factor constrained to a normal subgroup, covering both commutative and non-commutative cases. That method, however, relied on auxiliary assumptions (e.g., motion and isometry restrictions) not required by decomposition theory, and ablations did not separate theory-based from auxiliary effects. We propose parameter division for a single transformation: we split its parameter into components, impose homomorphism constraints mapping the full transformation to one component, and identify the normal subgroup as the set of transformations when that component is fixed to the identity. This formulation drops the previous auxiliary assumptions and applies more broadly. We evaluate on image pairs involving rotation, translation, and scale; ablations show that group-decomposition constraints drive appropriate categorization.

</details>


### [64] [Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation for LLM-Driven Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04057)
*Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对神经架构搜索中存在的“功能纠缠”问题，本文提出了SPARK方法，通过显式选择功能因子进行条件化编辑，显著提升了架构搜索的样本效率和OOD准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索（NAS）面临评估成本高和难以融合既有架构知识的挑战。尽管大语言模型（LLM）能将先验知识转化为代码编辑，但在实践中，局部修改常因“功能纠缠”现象引发非预期的全局性能变化，导致LLM的知识难以被有效利用。

Method: 提出结构化渐进式知识激活（SPARK）框架。该方法通过显式选择待修改的功能因子，并以该因子为条件引导编辑过程，实现了因子条件化编辑，从而减少了纠缠带来的副作用，生成更具针对性和可靠性的架构修改。

Result: 在CLRS-DFS数据集上，SPARK实现了28.1倍的架构演进样本效率提升，并在分布外（OOD）准确率上取得了22.9%的相对提升。

Conclusion: SPARK成功解决了LLM辅助NAS中的功能纠缠问题，有效激活了相关先验知识，实现了高效且高性能的神经架构搜索。

Abstract: This paper focuses on a key challenge in Neural Architecture Search (NAS): integrating established architectural knowledge while exploring new designs under expensive evaluations. Large language models (LLMs) are a promising assistant for NAS because they can translate rich architectural and coding priors into executable code edits. However, in practice, seemingly local revisions often propagate into non-local behavioral and performance shifts because a single edit can inadvertently couple multiple interacting functional factors, a phenomenon we refer to as functional entanglement. To make LLM knowledge usable under such entanglement, we propose Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation (SPARK), which activates relevant priors by explicitly selecting the functional factor to modify and conditioning the edit on that factor. This factor-conditioned editing reduces entangled side effects and yields more targeted, reliable architecture modifications. On CLRS-DFS, SPARK achieves a 28.1x sample-efficient architecture evolution speedup and yields a 22.9 percent relative improvement in OOD accuracy.

</details>


### [65] [AxMoE: Characterizing the Impact of Approximate Multipliers on Mixture-of-Experts DNN Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04754)
*Omkar B Shende,Marcello Traiola,Gayathri Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究近似乘法对混合专家(MoE)架构的影响，提出AxMoE框架，发现Dense基线在CNN中抗干扰性最强，而Hard MoE在ViT-Small上配合激进近似策略时表现更优，为近似计算与MoE的协同设计开辟了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端的深度神经网络推理需要同时提升准确性、计算效率和能耗表现。近似计算和混合专家(MoE)架构各自被视为高效推理的独立路径：前者通过低功耗近似乘法器替代精确运算，后者通过专用专家子网络的条件计算来优化。然而，两者之间的相互作用从未被探索过，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出AxMoE框架，系统评估近似乘法对MoE DNN架构的影响。实验设置包括：三种MoE变体（Hard MoE、Soft MoE、Cluster MoE）对比Dense基线；三种CNN架构（ResNet-20、VGG11_bn、VGG19_bn）在CIFAR-100数据集上；ViT-Small在Tiny ImageNet-200数据集上；使用EvoApproxLib库中的8个8位有符号乘法器（含一个精确基线）。评估分为无重训练和近似感知重训练两个阶段。

Result: 无重训练时：Dense基线在所有CNN架构中表现出最强的抗干扰性，而在ViT-Small上，所有拓扑结构无论采用何种路由策略，其退化速率相当。近似感知重训练后：恢复程度因架构、拓扑和乘法器而异——ResNet-20在整个乘法器范围内实现完全恢复；VGG架构在温和近似下可恢复，但在激进近似下除VGG11_bn上的Cluster MoE外均不可逆失效；在ViT-Small上，Hard MoE在激进近似且推理成本相等的情况下优于Dense基线。

Conclusion: 本研究首次揭示了近似计算与MoE架构间的复杂交互关系，证明了近似乘法对MoE网络的影响因架构和路由策略而异。关键发现包括ResNet-20的强恢复能力和Hard MoE在特定条件下的优势。这些成果为未来近似MoE硬件-软件协同设计策略奠定了基础，具有重要的实践指导意义。

Abstract: Deep neural network (DNN) inference at the edge demands simultaneous improvements in accuracy, computational efficiency, and energy consumption. Approximate computing and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have each been studied as independent routes towards efficient inference, the former by replacing exact arithmetic with low-power approximate multipliers, the latter by routing inputs through specialized expert sub-networks to enable conditional computation. However, their interaction remains entirely unexplored. This paper presents AxMoE, the first study of the impact of approximate multiplication on MoE DNN architectures. We evaluate three MoE variants: Hard MoE, Soft MoE, and Cluster MoE against dense baselines across three CNN architectures (ResNet-20, VGG11_bn, VGG19_bn) on CIFAR-100 and a Vision Transformer (ViT-Small) on Tiny ImageNet-200 dataset, using eight 8-bit signed multipliers (including one exact baseline) from the EvoApproxLib library. Results show that, without retraining, the Dense baseline is the most resilient topology across all CNN architectures, whereas on ViT-Small, all topologies degrade at comparable rates regardless of routing strategy. After approximate-aware retraining, recovery varies substantially across architectures, topologies, and multipliers. ResNet-20 achieves full recovery across the entire multiplier range, whereas VGG architectures recover at moderate multipliers but fail irreversibly at aggressive ones for all topologies except Cluster MoE on VGG11_bn; on ViT-Small, Hard MoE outperforms Dense under aggressive approximation at equal normalized inference cost. These results pave the way for future approximate MoE hardware-software co-design strategies.

</details>


### [66] [YOTOnet: Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Fault Diagnosis via Domain-Conditioned Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04528)
*Zesen Wang,Zihao Wu,Yue Hu,Yang Gao,Fuzhen Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出YOTOnet，一种基于基础模型原理的跨域机械故障诊断架构，实现了“仅训练一次”的鲁棒部署，在五个公共轴承数据集上验证了优越性能和明确的规模效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械设备是现代工业生产的关键支柱，但领域偏移严重限制了基于深度学习的故障诊断模型在不同设备和工况下的泛化能力。受基础模型在零样本泛化方面成功的启发，旨在解决跨域故障诊断难题。

Method: 提出YOTOnet架构，包含三个核心组件：(1)物理感知不变特征提取器，利用多尺度扩张卷积和基于FFT的时频融合提取域无关表示；(2)域条件稀疏专家(DC-MoE)，通过学习的门控机制自适应路由输入到专门处理器，无需外部元数据；(3)带有辅助监督的双头分类系统。

Result: 在五个公共轴承数据集(CWRU、MFPT、XJTU、OTTAWA、HUST)上通过30个跨数据集协议进行广泛验证，证明了YOTOnet相比其他先进方法的优越性。观察到明确的规模效应：平均测试F1从0.5339(1个训练数据集)提升至0.705(4个数据集)，从3到4个数据集时有明显增益。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了实证证据，表明基础模型原理可以实现工业故障诊断的鲁棒“训练一次”部署，为跨域故障诊断提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mechanical equipment forms the critical backbone of modern industrial production, yet domain shift severely limits the generalization of deep learning based fault diagnosis models across different equipment and operating conditions.Inspired by the success of foundation models in achieving zero-shotgeneralization, we propose YOTOnet (You Only Train Once), a novel architecture specifically designed for cross-domain fault diagnosis in mechanical equipment.YOTOnet comprises three core components: (1) a physics-aware Invariant Feature Distiller that extracts domain-agnostic representations using multi-scale dilated convolutions and FFT-based time-frequency fusion,(2) Domain-Conditioned Sparse Experts (DC-MoE) that adaptively route inputs to specialized processors via learned gating without external meta-data, and (3) a dual-head classification system with auxiliary supervision.Extensive validation on five public bearing datasets (CWRU, MFPT, XJTU,OTTAWA, HUST) through 30 cross-dataset protocols demonstrates the superiority of YOTOnet compared with other state-of-the-art methods. Critically, we observe a clear scaling effect-average test F1 improves from 0.5339(1 training dataset) to 0.705 (4 datasets), with a clear gain when moving from 3 to 4 datasets. These findings provide empirical evidence that foundation model principles can enable robust, train-once deployment for industrial fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [67] [MP-ISMoE: Mixed-Precision Interactive Side Mixture-of-Experts for Efficient Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04058)
*Yutong Zhang,Zimeng Wu,Shangcai Liao,Shujiang Wu,Jiaxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MP-ISMoE框架，通过高斯噪声扰动迭代量化方案和交互式侧面混合专家机制，在保持内存效率的同时显著提升了内存高效迁移学习方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效迁移学习虽然减少了可训练参数，但反向传播仍带来巨大内存开销；现有的内存高效迁移学习方法通过轻量级侧面网络绕过骨干梯度计算，但严格的内存约束严重限制了侧面网络的学习能力，导致性能显著下降。

Method: 提出混合精度交互式侧面混合专家框架(MP-ISMoE)：1) 设计高斯噪声扰动迭代量化(GNP-IQ)方案，将权重量化为低位数同时有效降低量化误差；2) 利用量化节省的内存，采用交互式侧面混合专家(ISMoE)扩展侧面网络，该机制通过与冻结骨干的显著特征交互来选择最优专家，从而抑制知识遗忘并提升性能。

Result: 在多种视觉-语言和纯语言任务上的大量实验表明，MP-ISMoE相比最先进的METL方法显著提升了准确率，同时保持了相当的参数和内存效率。

Conclusion: MP-ISMoE成功解决了内存高效迁移学习中内存约束与学习容量之间的矛盾，通过混合精度量化和交互式专家机制实现了性能与效率的有效平衡。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient transfer learning (PETL) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm for adapting pre-trained foundation models to downstream tasks, significantly reducing trainable parameters yet suffering from substantial memory overhead caused by gradient backpropagation during fine-tuning. While memory-efficient transfer learning (METL) circumvents this challenge by bypassing backbone gradient computation via lightweight small side networks, its stringent memory constraint severely limits learning capacity of side networks, thereby significantly compromising performance. To address these limitations, we propose a novel Mixed-Precision Interactive Side Mixture-of-Experts framework (MP-ISMoE). Specifically, we first propose a Gaussian Noise Perturbed Iterative Quantization (GNP-IQ) scheme to quantize weights into lower-bits while effectively decreasing quantization errors. By leveraging memory conserved from GNP-IQ, we subsequently employ Interactive Side Mixture-of-Experts (ISMoE) to scaling up side networks without sacrificing overall memory efficiency. Different from conventional mixture-of-experts, ISMoE learns to select optimal experts by interacting with salient features from frozen backbones, thus suppressing knowledge forgetting and boosting performance. Extensive experiments across diverse vision-language and language-only tasks demonstrate that MP-ISMoE remarkably promotes accuracy compared to state-of-the-art METL approaches, while maintaining comparable parameter and memory efficiency.

</details>


### [68] [Graph-SND: Sparse Aggregation for Behavioral Diversity in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05020)
*Shawn Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Graph-SND，一种利用图结构替代完全图的稀疏化方法，将系统神经多样性(SND)的计算复杂度从二次方降低到线性级别，同时保持度量语义不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统神经多样性(SND)通过计算所有智能体对的平均距离来衡量多智能体强化学习中的行为异质性，其计算成本随团队规模呈二次方增长（O(n²)），成为大规模应用中的性能瓶颈。

Method: 作者提出Graph-SND，将完全图上的平均替换为任意图G边上的加权平均。该方法支持三种模式：(1)完全图精确恢复SND；(2)固定稀疏图以O(|E|)成本实现局部化度量；(3)随机边采样提供无偏估计。理论分析包括扩展图的转发索引失真界、低秩距离结构下的谱细化，以及随机d-正则图的无条件概率界。

Result: 在VMAS实验中，Bernoulli-0.1 Graph-SND在n=100时将每次度量时间减少约10倍，同时准确追踪完整SND；在n=500时实现预期的加速比。随机d-正则扩展图在Θ(n log n)条边下达到SND比值[0.9987, 1.0013]。在DiCo多样性控制任务中，该方法在保持设定点跟踪性能的同时将度量成本降低约9.5倍。

Conclusion: Graph-SND成功消除了SND聚合瓶颈，提供了一种保持度量语义的即插即用稀疏替代方案，支持被动测量和闭环多样性控制，可扩展至超越完全图SND的大规模场景。

Abstract: System Neural Diversity (SND) measures behavioral heterogeneity in multi-agent reinforcement learning by averaging pairwise distances over all $\binom{n}{2}$ agent pairs, making each call quadratic in team size. We introduce Graph-SND, which replaces this complete-graph average with a weighted average over the edges of an arbitrary graph $G$. Three regimes follow: $G=K_n$ recovers SND exactly; a fixed sparse $G$ defines a localized diversity measure at $O(|E|)$ cost; and random edge samples yield an unbiased Horvitz-Thompson estimator and a normalized sample mean with $O(1/\sqrt{m})$ concentration in the sampled edge count $m$. For fixed sparse graphs we prove forwarding-index distortion bounds for expanders and a spectral refinement under low-rank distance structure; for random $d$-regular graphs we prove an unconditional probabilistic $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(D_{\max}/\sqrt{n})$ bound. On VMAS we verify recovery, unbiasedness, concentration, and wall-clock scaling, with a PettingZoo TVD panel checking non-Gaussian transfer. In a 500-iteration $n=100$ PPO run, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND tracks full SND while reducing per-call metric time by about $10\times$, and frozen-policy GPU timing up to $n=500$ follows the predicted $\binom{n}{2}/|E|$ speedup. Random $d$-regular expanders empirically achieve $\mathrm{SND}_{G}^{\mathrm{u}}/\mathrm{SND} \in [0.9987, 1.0013]$ at $Θ(n \log n)$ edges. In DiCo diversity control at $n=50$, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND preserves set-point tracking with paired reward differences indistinguishable from zero across nine matched cells while cutting per-call metric cost by ${\sim}9.5\times$. Together, these results show that the SND aggregation bottleneck can be removed without changing the metric's semantics, yielding a drop-in sparse alternative that scales beyond complete-graph SND and supports both passive measurement and closed-loop diversity control.

</details>


### [69] [Continual Distillation of Teachers from Different Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04059)
*Nicolas Michel,Maorong Wang,Jiangpeng He,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了持续蒸馏（CD）新范式，让学生模型从不保留历史访问权限的教师模型流中顺序学习。针对未见知识迁移（UKT）和未见知识遗忘（UKF）的权衡问题，提出了自外部数据蒸馏（SE2D）方法，通过在外部数据上保存logits来稳定学习，有效减少了知识遗忘并提升了跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型规模不断增长，部分模型存储需求已超过许多大规模数据集。现有知识蒸馏方法通常假设教师模型固定且可访问，但在实际场景中，教师模型可能以流的形式顺序出现且无法保留历史访问权限。这引发了两个关键挑战：教师训练数据不可用，以及教师模型具有不同的专业领域知识。

Method: 提出持续蒸馏（CD）框架，引入未见知识迁移（UKT）利用外部无标签数据获取教师训练数据中不存在但教师已知的知识；识别出顺序蒸馏会导致未见知识遗忘（UKF）问题。提出自外部数据蒸馏（SE2D）方法，通过在外部数据上保存logits来稳定来自异构教师模型的学习过程，以更好地平衡UKT与UKF之间的权衡。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，SE2D方法有效减少了未见知识遗忘（UKF），同时改善了跨域泛化性能。外部无标签数据的引入成功实现了未见知识迁移，使学生能够获取教师训练数据领域之外的知识。

Conclusion: 持续蒸馏为处理大规模模型的顺序知识迁移提供了有效解决方案。SE2D方法通过在外部数据上保存logits的策略，成功平衡了知识迁移与遗忘之间的矛盾，为异构教师模型的顺序蒸馏提供了稳定的学习机制，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Deep learning models continue to scale, with some requiring more storage than many large-scale datasets. Thus, we introduce a new paradigm: Continual Distillation (CD), where a student learns sequentially from a stream of teacher models without retaining access to earlier teachers. CD faces two challenges: teacher training data is unavailable, and teachers have varying expertise. We show that external unlabeled data enables Unseen Knowledge Transfer (UKT), allowing the student to acquire information from domains not present in the training data, while known to the teacher. We also show that sequential distillation causes Unseen Knowledge Forgetting (UKF) when transferred knowledge is lost after training on later teachers. To better trade off between UKT and UKF, we propose Self External Data Distillation (SE2D), a method that preserves logits on external data to stabilize learning across heterogeneous teachers. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SE2D reduces UKF and improves cross-domain generalization. The code and implementation for this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/Nicolas1203/continual_distillation.

</details>


### [70] [Lookahead Drifting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04060)
*Guoqiang Zhang,Kenta Niwa,W. Bastiaan Kleijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出前瞻漂移模型（lookahead drifting model），通过顺序计算并融合多个漂移项以捕获高阶梯度信息，在图像生成任务上优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的漂移模型（drifting model）在分布映射与图像生成上已取得SOTA性能，但其每次训练迭代仅计算单一漂移项，未充分利用高阶梯度信息。为提升优化方向的信息丰富度与生成质量，需要一种能够捕获更高阶梯度信息的改进方法。

Method: 提出前瞻漂移模型：在每次训练迭代中顺序计算一组漂移项，每项的计算利用先前计算的漂移项、正样本与模型输出；后续阶段获得的漂移项可捕获朝向正样本的高阶梯度信息；最终以加权求和的漂移项方向推动模型输出进行优化。

Result: 在toy示例和CIFAR10数据集上的实验表明，新方法在图像生成性能上优于基准漂移模型。

Conclusion: 通过引入多步前瞻漂移项捕获高阶梯度信息，前瞻漂移模型在分布映射与图像生成任务中取得了更优性能，为后续生成模型优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recently, a new paradigm named \emph{drifting model} has been proposed for mapping distributions, which achieves the SOTA image generation performance over ImageNet via one-step neural functional evaluation (NFE). The basic idea is to compute a drifting term at each training iteration and then push the output of the model towards the direction of the drifting term. In this paper, we propose a \emph{lookahead drifting model}. At each training iteration, we compute a set of drifting terms sequentially. Each drifting term is calculated by making use of previously computed ones as well as the positive samples and the output of the model. %One key step is to properly scale the drifting terms so that their magnitudes are in a comparable range. In principle, the drifting terms obtained at a later stage capture higher order gradient information towards the positive samples. At each training iteration, the model is optimized by pushing its output towards the direction of the (weighted) summation of the drifting terms. Experimental results on toy examples and CIFAR10 demonstrate the better performance of the new method than the baseline.

</details>


### [71] [Single-Position Intervention Fails: Distributed Output Templates Drive In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04061)
*Bryan Cheng,Jasper Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现线性探测准确率无法预测任务表示的因果重要性，揭示了上下文学习（ICL）任务身份的分布式编码机制：任务信息以输出格式模板的形式分布在演示词元上，在约30%网络深度处存在普适的干预窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型如何从少样本演示中编码任务身份是机械可解释性的核心开放问题。先前工作使用线性探测定位任务表示，报告了特定层的高分类准确率，但探测准确率与因果重要性之间的关系尚不明确，需要深入研究ICL任务身份的真实编码机制。

Method: 1. 对比线性探测准确率与因果干预效果，发现二者完全解离
2. 系统比较单位置干预与多位置同时干预的因果效应
3. 在四个模型（LLaMA、Qwen、Gemma）和三个架构家族上进行因果追踪实验
4. 通过内部表示兼容性测试排除表面相似性解释
5. 采用因果追踪分析查询位置和演示位置的不同作用

Result: 1. 探测准确率与因果重要性完全解离：单位置干预在所有28层均实现0%任务转移，尽管探测准确率达100%
2. 多位置干预在Layer 8实现高达96%的任务转移（95% CI: [87%, 99%]）
3. 发现普适干预窗口位于约30%网络深度
4. 查询位置严格必要（53-100%破坏），而单独演示位置均非必要（0%破坏）
5. 转移依赖于内部表示兼容性（r=0.31）而非表面相似性（r=-0.05）

Conclusion: 本研究提出了"分布式模板假说"：ICL任务身份以输出格式模板的形式编码，分布在演示词元上。这一发现从根本上重塑了我们对上下文学习机制的理解，揭示了任务编码的分布式本质，首次精确定位了ICL任务身份的因果位置，并为理解大语言模型的表示机制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding how large language models encode task identity from few-shot demonstrations is a central open problem in mechanistic interpretability. Prior work uses linear probing to localize task representations, reporting high classification accuracy at specific layers. We reveal a striking dissociation: probing accuracy completely fails to predict causal importance. Single-position activation intervention achieves 0% task transfer across all 28 layers of Llama-3.2-3B-despite 100% probing accuracy at those same positions. This null result is itself a key finding, demonstrating that task encoding is fundamentally distributed. Multi-position intervention-replacing activations at all demonstration output tokens simultaneously-achieves up to 96% transfer (N=50, 95% CI: [87%, 99%]) at layer 8, pinpointing for the first time the causal locus of ICL task identity. We establish the generality of these findings across four models spanning three architecture families (LLaMA, Qwen, Gemma), discovering a universal intervention window at ~30% network depth. Causal tracing uncovers an asymmetric architecture: the query position is strictly necessary (53-100% disruption) while no individual demonstration position is necessary (0% disruption)-resolving a key ambiguity in prior accounts. Crucially, transfer depends on internal representation compatibility, not surface similarity (r=-0.05 vs r=0.31), ruling out trivial explanations. These results establish the distributed template hypothesis: ICL task identity is encoded as output format templates distributed across demonstration tokens, fundamentally reshaping our understanding of how in-context learning operates.

</details>


### [72] [EdgeRazor: A Lightweight Framework for Large Language Models via Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04062)
*Shu-Hao Zhang,Le-Tong Huang,Xiang-Sheng Deng,Xin-Yi Zou,Chen Wu,Nan Li,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EdgeRazor框架，实现了大语言模型的混合精度与极低比特量化，在1.88-bit精度下超越所有3-bit竞争方案，优于领先2-bit PTQ方法11.3个点，训练预算比领先QAT方法低4-10倍，显著降低了存储需求并加速了解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型量化方法存在明显局限性：训练后量化(PTQ)在4-bit以下性能严重下降；量化感知训练(QAT)需要大量计算资源；量化感知蒸馏方法需要手动选择蒸馏特征且严重依赖教师模型特定数据。这些限制阻碍了LLMs在资源受限设备上的高效部署，亟需一种轻量级、高性能的极低比特量化方案。

Method: EdgeRazor框架包含三个核心模块：(1)混合精度量化感知蒸馏，实现精度的细粒度控制；(2)自适应特征蒸馏，从16-bit教师模型推导出n-bit学生模型；(3)熵感知KL散度，应用于人工标注和蒸馏数据集，其前向-反向平衡仅由教师模型的输出分布决定。

Result: 在基础模型、指令微调模型和多模态LLMs上的实验表明：1.88-bit EdgeRazor超越所有3-bit竞争方案，比领先2-bit PTQ方法性能提升11.3个点，训练预算比领先QAT方法低4-10倍。1.58-bit Qwen3-0.6B将存储从1.41GB减少到0.28GB，解码速度相比16-bit基线加速15.1倍，在所有比特宽度下均实现更高压缩率。

Conclusion: EdgeRazor成功实现了大语言模型的高效极低比特量化，在保持优异性能的同时大幅降低了存储需求和计算开销，为大语言模型在资源受限边缘设备上的部署提供了有效的解决方案，证明了混合精度量化与自适应蒸馏方法的有效性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an increasing interest in deploying LLMs on resource-constrained devices, among which quantization has emerged as a promising lightweight technique that converts full-precision model weights and activations into lower-bit formats. Existing weight quantization approaches can be roughly divided into three categories: Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) that calibrates quantized parameters on a small dataset without retraining but suffers from severe performance degradation below 4-bit, Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) that searches low-bit parameters using surrogate gradients but demands substantial computational resources, and Quantization-Aware Distillation that integrates QAT with knowledge transfer from a full-precision teacher but manually selects features to distill and relies heavily on teacher-specific data.
