### [107] [Hierarchical Support Vector State Partitioning for Distilling Black Box Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04254)
*Senne Deproost,Mehrdad Asadi,Ann Nowé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了状态向量空间分割方法（SVSP），通过线性支持向量机划分状态-动作对数据集，用一组可解释的子策略来模仿黑箱强化学习策略，实现了更高的回报和更紧凑的策略表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了模仿黑箱强化学习策略并获得人类可解释的子策略表示，解决以往方法（如Voronoi状态分割VSP）中子策略数量多、性能受限的问题。

Method: 提出SVSP方法，利用线性支持向量机对蒸馏数据集中的状态-动作对进行划分，构建一组紧凑且结构化的可解释子策略来表示原始策略。

Result: SVSP相比VSP方法平均回报提升+7.4%，相比原始TD3策略提升+2.8%，同时所需子策略数量相比VSP减少82.1%。

Conclusion: SVSP为更灵活的蒸馏方法铺平了道路，允许在保持原始黑箱行为边界的前提下选择决策边界和代理模型。

Abstract: We introduce State Vector Space Partitioning (SVSP), a novel method to mimic a black box reinforcement learning policy using a set of human-interpretable subpolicies. By partitioning a distillation dataset of state action pairs with linear support vector machine splits, SVSP constructs a compact and structured representation of the original policy. Our method improves mean return by +7.4\% over previous critic driven state partitioning attempts such as Voronoi State Partitioning (VSP) and +2.8\% over the original TD3 policy, while reducing the number of required subpolicies against VSP by 82.1\%. Our results pave the path towards a more flexible form of distillation where both the decision boundary and surrogate models can be chosen within a margin of the original black box behavior.

</details>


### [108] [HUGO-CS: A Hybrid-Labeled, Uncertainty-Aware, General-Purpose, Observational Dataset for Cold Spray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04257)
*Stephen Price,Kyle Miller,Marco Musto,Kenneth Kroenlein,James Saal,Kyle Tsaknopoulos,Elke A. Rundensteiner,Danielle L. Cote*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HUGO框架，结合LLM自动标注与人工校验，从文献中提取了包含4,383个冷喷涂实验、144个特征的大规模数据集HUGO-CS，规模是此前最大数据集的30倍，并引入分层风险缓解机制以平衡标注效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷喷涂工艺优化因参数相互依赖且缺乏大规模、机器可读数据而困难重重。现有文献实验结果报告格式不统一（常以表格和图表呈现）、单位不一致，限制了数据的规模化利用。此前最大数据集仅含137个样本，亟需一种高效方法从海量文献中提取高质量数据。

Method: 提出名为HUGO的混合标注框架：1）利用LLM进行自动化初步标注；2）引入分层风险缓解（HRM）机制，对高错误风险输出路由至人工复核，低风险记录保留为自动标注；3）后处理阶段统一分类描述符、将材料化学成分映射为结构化连续组成、归一化单位。最终从1,124篇文献中提取了4,383个实验数据，其中1,765个经人工标注。

Result: 成功构建HUGO-CS数据集，包含4,383个冷喷涂实验和144个特征，规模超越此前最大数据集30倍。数据集包含1,765个人工标注的高质量样本子集，可用于基准测试、误差分析和构建高保真模型。代码与数据已在GitHub开源（CC-BY许可）。

Conclusion: HUGO框架有效解决了从科学文献中规模化提取实验数据的挑战，在保证标注质量的同时显著提升了效率。HUGO-CS数据集填补了冷喷涂领域大规模机器可读数据的空白，为后续工艺建模与优化研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Cold spraying is an increasingly common approach for repairing and manufacturing components due to its solid-state manufacturing capabilities. However, process optimization remains difficult due to many interdependent parameters and the lack of large-scale, machine-readable data to support modeling. While the scientific literature contains many relevant experiments, results are inconsistently reported (often in tables and figures) and use non-uniform units, limiting utilization at scale. To address these limitations, this work presents HUGO-CS, a literature-derived dataset of 4,383 cold-spray experiments with 144 features from 1,124 sources, exceeding the previous largest dataset (137 samples) by 30x. With completely manual extraction requiring an average of 91 minutes per document, this work designs and leverages a Hybrid-labeled, Uncertainty-aware, General-purpose, Observational extraction framework, called HUGO, to support this extraction. HUGO combines automated LLM-based labeling with targeted manual label refinement to handle this experimental result extraction process from scientific literature. To balance labeling efficiency with extraction accuracy, HUGO introduces a Hierarchical Risk Mitigation (HRM) to route LLM outputs with a high risk of potential errors for manual review, while retaining low-risk records as auto-labeled. Lastly, HUGO post-processing consolidates categorical descriptors, maps reported feedstock chemistries into structured continuous compositions, and normalizes units across sources. Of the 4,383 reported experiments, 1,765 are hand-labeled, providing a high-quality labeled subset for benchmarking, error analysis, and higher-fidelity data points. All code to replicate this work, along with the complete HUGO-CS dataset, are released under a CC-BY license at https://github.com/sprice134/HUGO.

</details>


### [109] [Explaining and Preventing Alignment Collapse in Iterative RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04266)
*Etienne Gauthier,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了迭代RLHF中因忽略策略对奖励模型未来参数影响而导致的“对齐崩溃”问题，并提出了前瞻策略优化（FPO）方法，通过引入参数引导项的正则化来有效防止该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLHF方法通常假设奖励模型（RM）是静态或非策略性的。然而在实际的迭代部署中，策略会生成用于重新训练RM的数据，形成反馈循环。标准迭代RLHF忽略了策略对RM未来参数的影响，导致策略系统性地利用RM的盲点，产生低质量但高奖励的输出，其反馈又强化了它所利用的错误，即“对齐崩溃”。

Method: 基于此交互过程的Stackelberg博弈建模，本文推导出策略真实优化梯度的解析分解：包含标准策略梯度与一个捕捉策略对RM未来参数影响的“参数引导项”。针对标准方法完全丢弃该引导项的问题，提出了前瞻策略优化（FPO）。这是一种机制设计干预，通过正则化策略对RM更新的参数引导效应来恢复缺失的引导项，并给出了一种可扩展的一阶近似实现。

Result: 实验表明，标准迭代RLHF确实会遭受对齐崩溃。而所提出的FPO方法在受控环境以及基于Llama-3.2-1B的大语言模型对齐流程中，均能有效防止对齐崩溃的发生。

Conclusion: 本文从博弈论视角揭示了迭代RLHF中“对齐崩溃”现象的理论根源，并通过恢复被忽略的参数引导项提出了有效的解决方案FPO，为构建更鲁棒的AI对齐方法提供了重要的理论见解与实践工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) typically assumes a static or non-strategic reward model (RM). In iterative deployment, however, the policy generates the data on which the RM is retrained, creating a feedback loop. Building on the Stackelberg game formulation of this interaction, we derive an analytical decomposition of the policy's true optimization gradient into a standard policy gradient and a parameter-steering term that captures the policy's influence on the RM's future parameters. We show that standard iterative RLHF, which drops this steering term entirely, suffers from alignment collapse: the policy systematically exploits the RM's blind spots, producing low-quality, high-reward outputs whose feedback reinforces the very errors it exploits. To mitigate this, we propose foresighted policy optimization (FPO), a mechanism-design intervention that restores the missing steering term by regularizing the policy's parameter-steering effect on RM updates. We instantiate FPO via a scalable first-order approximation and demonstrate that it prevents alignment collapse on both controlled environments and an LLM alignment pipeline using Llama-3.2-1B.

</details>


### [110] [QUIVER: Cost-Aware Adaptive Preference Querying in Surrogate-Assisted Evolutionary Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04267)
*Florian A. D. Burnat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QUIVER算法，解决交互式多目标优化中的预算分配困境，通过自适应选择目标评估和异构偏好查询，在挑战性问题上实现了25%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式多目标优化系统面临预算分配困境：资源需要在昂贵的目标函数评估与识别Pareto集相关区域的决策者偏好获取之间进行权衡。偏好获取本身存在不同信息量和认知负担的模态，从廉价但噪声大的成对偏好陈述（PS）到更丰富但成本更高的无差异调整（IA）。

Method: 提出QUIVER（Query-Informed Value Estimation for Regret）算法，这是一种代理辅助的进化多目标优化器。该算法自适应地在目标评估和异构偏好查询之间进行选择，在每一步通过最大化单位总成本的期望决策质量改进来选择下一个行动。

Result: 在DTLZ和WFG基准测试及合成决策者模型下，QUIVER在具有挑战性的WFG问题上实现了最低的最终效用后悔值（WFG4上为2.14，WFG9上为2.82，相比基线提升25%），优于所有单一模态基线。算法能够根据问题难度自适应调整模态选择：简单问题（DTLZ2）选择80%的PS查询，困难问题（WFG9）调整为35%的IA查询。

Conclusion: QUIVER成功展示了成本感知偏好学习的实际效果，通过自适应模态选择策略，能够根据问题难度智能平衡不同类型的偏好查询，有效解决了交互式多目标优化中的预算分配问题。

Abstract: Interactive multi-objective optimization systems face a budget allocation dilemma: one can spend resources on expensive objective evaluations or on eliciting decision-maker preferences that identify the relevant region of the Pareto set. Moreover, preference elicitation itself spans modalities with different information content and cognitive burden, ranging from cheap, noisy pairwise preference statements (PS) to richer but costlier indifference adjustments (IA).
  We study cost-aware optimization under an unknown scalarization and introduce QUIVER (Query-Informed Value Estimation for Regret), a surrogate-assisted evolutionary multi-objective optimizer that adaptively chooses between objective evaluations and heterogeneous preference queries. At each step, QUIVER selects the next action by maximizing the expected decision-quality improvement per unit total cost. Across DTLZ and WFG benchmarks under synthetic decision-maker models, QUIVER achieves the lowest final utility regret on challenging WFG problems (utility regret of 2.14 on WFG4, 2.82 on WFG9: a 25% improvement over baselines), outperforming all single-modality baselines. We analyze how the optimal mix of PS and IA adapts to problem difficulty: on easy problems (DTLZ2), QUIVER selects 80\% PS queries; on hard problems (WFG9), it shifts to 35% IA queries. This adaptive modality selection demonstrates cost-aware preference learning in action.

</details>


### [111] [A Mean Curvature Approach to Boundary Detection: Geometric Insights for Unsupervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04274)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MCBP方法，利用数据流形的平均曲率进行边界检测，有效解决了高维数据中非线性结构和密度异质性带来的挑战，显著提升了聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督学习中，高维数据的边界检测面临核心挑战，尤其是在存在非线性结构和密度异质性的情况下，传统基于密度的方法效果受限。

Method: 提出平均曲率边界点（MCBP）框架，基于几何机器学习，通过局部k近邻patch离散近似形状算子，计算逐点平均曲率，并引入自适应百分位阈值方案进行多尺度边界提取，无需显式流形参数化或临时密度参数。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，MCBP持续改善聚类性能，特别是在复杂和高维场景中表现优异；曲率驱动数据分解能有效分离平滑和边界子集。

Conclusion: MCBP为几何机器学习提供了具体贡献，确立了曲率感知分析作为连接微分几何与数据驱动建模的统一范式的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate boundary detection in high-dimensional data remains a central challenge in unsupervised learning, particularly in the presence of non-linear structures and heterogeneous densities. In this work, we introduce Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP), a novel geometric framework grounded in Geometric Machine Learning that departs from traditional density-based approaches by explicitly modeling the intrinsic curvature of the data manifold. The method relies on a discrete approximation of the shape operator, estimated from local k-nearest neighbor patches, to compute pointwise mean curvature without requiring explicit manifold parametrization. The key insight of MCBP is to use mean curvature as a principled descriptor of boundary structure: high-curvature regions naturally correspond to transitions between clusters, geometric irregularities, and low-density interfaces. This yields a unified geometric interpretation of boundary, outlier, and transition points. We further introduce an adaptive percentile-based thresholding scheme that enables multiscale boundary extraction without relying on ad hoc density parameters. Beyond detection, we propose a curvature-driven data decomposition that separates samples into smooth (low-curvature) and boundary (high-curvature) subsets, effectively acting as a non-linear geometric filtering mechanism. This representation enhances cluster separability and improves the robustness of downstream unsupervised algorithms. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that MCBP consistently improves clustering performance, particularly in complex and high-dimensional scenarios. These results position MCBP as a concrete contribution to Geometric Machine Learning, highlighting the potential of curvature-aware analysis as a unifying paradigm bridging differential geometry and data-driven modeling.

</details>


### [112] [Gradient Flow Structure and Quantitative Dynamics of Multi-Head Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04279)
*Ayan Pendharkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了多头自注意力动力学的理论框架，证明了多头能量泛函的单调性，揭示了径向阴影项是阻碍各头单调性的关键因素，并在简化设置下推导了聚类的临界温度和熵增特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究已经建立了单头自注意力在单位球面上的聚类行为，但多头自注意力机制由于各头之间的几何干扰，使得标准的单调性论证失效，因此多头设置的理论理解仍然不足。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，建立多头自注意力动力学的统一理论框架。

Method: 1. 将多头自注意力解释为单位球面上的梯度流，定义自然的多头能量泛函；2. 识别阻碍各头单调性的关键因素——径向阴影项（radial shadow terms），即每个头输出在token方向上的投影；3. 引入保证单调性的充分条件，并证明对近似正交性的鲁棒性；4. 在简化的标量头机制和等角token配置下，推导临界温度的闭式表达式，并分析ReLU与softmax注意力的动力学差异。

Result: 1. 在适当条件下证明了多头能量泛函沿平坦和球面动力学非减；2. 推导了聚类行为的临界逆温度闭式表达式；3. 证明异构头表现出超加性聚类率；4. 在线性化动力学中证明ReLU和softmax注意力聚类时间的分离；5. 建立熵产生恒等式，证明注意力熵随聚类进展单调增加直至平衡。

Conclusion: 本文为多头注意力动力学提供了统一的理论视角，阐明了Transformer模型中聚类和稳定性的内在机制，解决了多头设置下自注意力动力学分析的若干开放问题。

Abstract: Transformer self-attention can be interpreted as a gradient flow on the unit sphere, in which tokens evolve under softmax interaction potentials and tend to form clusters. While prior work has established clustering behavior for single-head attention, the multi-head setting remains less understood due to geometric interference between heads, which invalidates standard monotonicity arguments.
  In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for multi-head self-attention dynamics and resolve several open questions. We show that, under suitable conditions on the score matrices, a natural multi-head energy functional is non-decreasing along both flat and spherical dynamics. We identify the key obstruction to per-head monotonicity as radial shadow terms, which are projections of each head's output onto token directions, persisting even under orthogonality assumptions. We introduce a sufficient condition ensuring monotonicity and establish robustness to approximate orthogonality.
  In a simplified scalar-head regime with equiangular token configurations, we derive a closed-form expression for the critical inverse temperature governing clustering behavior, and show that heterogeneous heads exhibit super-additive clustering rates. In this regime, we also prove a separation in clustering time between ReLU and softmax attention in the linearized dynamics. Finally, we establish an entropy production identity and show that attention entropy increases monotonically toward equilibrium as clustering progresses.
  Our results provide a unified perspective on the dynamics of multi-head attention and clarify the mechanisms underlying clustering and stability in transformer models.

</details>


### [113] [Hardware-Aware Neural Feature Extraction for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04282)
*Francesco Tosini,Simone Pedroni,Christian Veronesi,Pietro Bartoli,Marco Paracchini,Marco Marcon,Diana Trojaniello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Gideon，一个面向资源受限设备的硬件感知神经特征提取器，在STM32N6上实现了111 FPS的推理速度和小于1.5 MB的内存占用，且INT8量化几乎不损失性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现代神经描述符具有强大的鲁棒性，但在微控制器级硬件上部署学习型局部特征提取器仍面临内存、带宽和量化约束的挑战，且现有的基于FLOP的效率指标无法捕捉系统级瓶颈。

Method: 结合了来自SuperPoint教师的关系知识蒸馏和在严格内存与算子约束下的可微分神经架构搜索（DNAS），并将量化稳定性和动态范围紧凑性作为首要设计目标。关键架构选择包括用仿射层替代批归一化层以提升INT8鲁棒性。

Result: 在STM32N6平台上实现了9.003毫秒的推理时间（111 FPS），内存占用低于1.5 MB；INT8量化导致的性能下降可忽略不计，有时甚至能匹配全精度性能。

Conclusion: 通过整体的硬件-算法协同设计，鲁棒的学习型特征提取可以与嵌入式硬件约束相协调，证明了该方法在资源受限设备上的有效性。

Abstract: Visual SLAM is a core component of spatial computing systems, yet deploying learned local feature extractors on microcontroller-class hardware remains challenging due to memory, bandwidth, and quantization constraints. While modern neural descriptors provide strong robustness, their practical adoption is often hindered by system-level bottlenecks that are not captured by FLOP-based efficiency metrics. In this work, we introduce Gideon, a hardware-aware neural feature extractor explicitly designed for resource-constrained devices. Our approach combines relational knowledge distillation from a SuperPoint teacher with differentiable neural architecture search (DNAS) under strict memory and operator constraints. Unlike conventional design pipelines, we treat quantization stability and dynamic-range compactness as first-class objectives. We show that architectural choices such as replacing Batch Normalization with affine layers significantly improve INT8 robustness, and that descriptor dimensionality directly governs quantization resilience. Deployed on STM32N6, Gideon achieves 9.003 ms inference time (111 fps) while remaining below a 1.5 MB memory footprint. Remarkably, INT8 quantization induces negligible degradation and occasionally matches full-precision performance. These results demonstrate that robust learned feature extraction can be reconciled with embedded hardware constraints through holistic hardware-algorithm co-design.

</details>


### [114] [Probabilistic Classification and Uncertainty Quantification of Sahara Desert Climate Using Feedforward Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04286)
*Stephen Tivenan,Indranil Sahoo,Yanjun Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前馈人工神经网络（ANN）的概率气候分区建模框架，克服了传统Köppen-Trewartha（KT）分类系统确定性的局限，实现了对气候类型的不确定性感知分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Köppen-Trewartha（KT）气候分类系统本质上是确定性的，仅为空间位置提供离散标签，忽略了分类过程中的不确定性，无法充分刻画过渡性气候区域的复杂性。

Method: 开发了一个基于前馈人工神经网络（ANN）的概率分类框架，用于气候分区建模。研究区域为撒哈拉沙漠，使用1960-1989年30年期间的数据，以前11年超过40万个时空位置的数据训练模型。

Result: 评估了模型的短期和长期分类能力以验证其时间稳定性和准确性；将模型生成的概率分类与传统KT分类进行了对比；利用波动分析方法揭示了撒哈拉地区气候带的时间演变，并识别出气候类别概率发生显著变化的区域，为荒漠化趋势提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了气候分区的概率建模，提供了比传统确定性方法更细致的气候过渡区理解，能够有效识别气候变化的动态过程，在农业规划、水文研究和气候科学领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Climate classification plays a vital role in agricultural planning, hydrological studies, and climate science. One of the most widely used systems for classifying global climate zones is the Köppen-Trewartha (KT) classification. However, the KT classification is fundamentally deterministic, offering discrete labels to spatial locations without accounting for uncertainties in classification. In this paper, we provide a framework for probabilistic modeling of climatic zones. We implement a feedforward artificial neural network (ANN) for classification, allowing for efficient, uncertainty-aware categorization of climatic regions, thereby offering a more nuanced understanding of transitional climate zones compared to traditional deterministic methods. We apply this method to the Sahara Desert region over the 30-year period of 1960 - 1989, using data at more than 400,000 space-time locations from the first 11 years to train our model. We assess the model's short- and long-term classification capabilities to evaluate its stability and accuracy over time. We also compare the probabilistic classification from our model with the traditional KT classification. In addition, we use fluctuation analysis methods to highlight the temporal evolution of climatic zones across the Sahara region and identify areas undergoing significant flux of probabilities of their climate classes, providing insights into broader trends in desertification.

</details>


### [115] [Leveraging Pretrained Language Models as Energy Functions for Glauber Dynamics Text Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04291)
*Tarun Kathuria,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Glauber动力学的离散扩散语言模型，通过利用预训练语言模型构建能量函数，显著提升了文本生成质量，在多项任务上超越现有扩散模型并媲美自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型在文本生成任务上表现不佳，作者发现利用预训练语言模型构建能量函数作为平稳分布，比直接训练均匀转移核的Glauber动力学更有效。

Method: 基于统计物理中的Glauber动力学构建离散扩散框架，创新性地引入预训练因果/掩码语言模型（如UL2）定义能量函数作为平稳分布，从而构建更优的扩散生成过程。

Result: 在文本生成任务上超越现有扩散语言模型，与同规模自回归模型性能相当；在零样本常识推理、数独和斑马谜题等规划搜索任务上达到竞争性或更优表现。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了将预训练语言模型知识融入离散扩散框架的有效性，为离散扩散语言模型提供了一条新的改进路径，缩小了与自回归模型在文本生成领域的性能差距。

Abstract: We present a discrete diffusion-based language model using Glauber dynamics from statistical physics. Our main insight is that instead of trying to train a discrete state space diffusion model using Glauber dynamics with a uniform transition kernel as the forward process, one can set up an ``energy function'' based on pretrained causal/masked language models. When viewed as the stationary distribution, this energy function allows us to significantly improve the quality of the generated text. Incorporating UL2 as the pretrained model into our diffusion pipeline, we outperform prior diffusion based LMs and perform competitively with autoregressive models of comparable model sizes. Furthermore, our models are competitive with or outperform prior diffusion models and GPT-2 style auto-regressive models on zero-shot common sense reasoning tasks as well as planning and search tasks like Sudoku and Zebra puzzles.

</details>


### [116] [LLMs Uncertainty Quantification via Adaptive Conformal Semantic Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04295)
*Hamed Karimi,Vaishali Meyappan,Reza Samavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应保形语义熵（ACSE）方法，通过聚类语义熵自适应测量LLM输出的语义离散度来估计提示级不确定性，并利用保形校准提供统计可靠的风险控制保证，在多个数据集上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在产生幻觉时表现出的过度自信，使其在安全关键场景中的部署面临重大挑战，迫切需要可靠的不确定性估计。现有方法主要依赖词汇或概率度量，往往忽略了具有相似含义的不同响应之间的语义差异，导致不确定性量化不准确。

Method: 提出自适应保形语义熵（ACSE）方法：1）通过对同一提示的多个多样化响应进行聚类，计算语义熵作为不确定性评分函数；2）根据每个聚类的语义特征自适应调整不确定性分数；3）应用保形校准（conformal calibration）建立接受/拒绝决策规则，为错误率提供有限样本、无分布假设的统计保证，使其保持在用户指定的容差范围内。

Result: 在不同LLM和数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在判别性能、保形保证和概率校准指标上均持续优于现有最先进的不确定性量化基线。特别突出的是，在TriviaQA数据集上，本方法的AUROC达到0.88，而token熵方法仅为0.65，提升显著。

Conclusion: ACSE方法有效解决了现有不确定性量化方法忽视语义差异的问题，通过结合语义熵聚类和保形校准，既提高了不确定性估计的准确性，又提供了严格的统计保证，为LLM在安全关键场景中的可靠部署提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: LLMs' overconfidence, particularly when hallucinating, poses a significant challenge for the deployment of the models in safety-critical settings and makes a reliable estimation of uncertainty necessary. Existing approaches for uncertainty quantification typically prioritize lexical or probabilistic measures; however, these techniques often ignore the semantic variance of different responses with similar meaning. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Conformal Semantic Entropy (ACSE), a method for estimating prompt-level uncertainty by adaptively measuring semantic dispersion in LLMs outputs. Our uncertainty scoring function is based on clustering semantic entropy of multiple diverse responses to the same prompt. The function adaptively adjusts the uncertainty score based on semantic features of each cluster. To ensure statistical reliability of our score, we use conformal calibration to apply a decision rule to accept/abstain the prompts, providing a finite-sample, distribution-free guarantee such that the error rate among the accepted responses remains bounded by a user-specified tolerance. Our extensive experimental evaluations using different LLMs and datasets, demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art uncertainty quantification baselines using discriminative performance, conformal guarantees, and probabilistic calibration indicators. As a highlight, for TriviaQA dataset, AUROC of our approach is 0.88 compared to 0.65 produced by the token entropy approach.

</details>


### [117] [Memory as a Markov Matrix: Sample Efficient Knowledge Expansion via Token-to-Dictionary Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04308)
*Kaustubh Pethkar,Ziyang Xiong,Zuofeng Shang,Yingcong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将自回归语言生成建模为马尔可夫过程的理论框架，通过扩展状态空间的方式引入新知识，并设计了仅需最小参数更新且零遗忘的嵌入微调算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数持续学习方法依赖参数更新算法来缓解灾难性遗忘，但仍存在根本局限：随着新知识增加，遗忘不可避免；且模型更新往往不可逆。鉴于现代LLM表达能力增强，作者质疑是否有必要进行大规模权重更新来获取少量新知识。

Method: 将自回归语言生成建模为token上的马尔可夫过程，模型记忆由马尔可夫转移矩阵表示。在此框架下，引入新知识/token对应于扩展状态空间。提出了token-to-dictionary映射策略及相应的嵌入微调算法（Embedding-tuning），仅需极少的参数更新即可实现新token的学习。

Result: 理论上证明了新token学习的样本复杂度边界，学习每个新token转移行为所需的样本数量与其映射到的现有token数量呈线性关系。实验结果表明该方法有效，且实现了零遗忘（Zero Forgetting）。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM视为马尔可夫过程，本文从理论上证明了可以通过扩展状态空间的方式引入新知识，并提出了一种仅需最小参数更新且完全避免灾难性遗忘的高效算法，为LLM的持续学习提供了新的理论视角和实践方案。

Abstract: Continual incorporation of new knowledge is essential for the long-term evolution of large language models (LLMs). Existing approaches typically rely on parameter-update algorithms to mitigate catastrophic forgetting, yet they suffer from fundamental limitations: 1) forgetting is unavoidable as the amount of newly injected knowledge grows; and 2) model updates are often irreversible. As modern LLMs become increasingly expressive, it is natural to question whether large-scale weight updates are necessary for acquiring a small amount of new knowledge. In this work, we propose a principled framework that models autoregressive language generation as a Markov process over tokens, where model memory is represented by a Markov transition matrix. Under this formulation, incorporating new knowledge/tokens corresponds to extending the state space, and preserving existing transitions guarantees retention of previously learned knowledge. We then prove a sample complexity bound for incorporating new tokens via a token-to-dictionary mapping strategy. In particular, for learning the transition behavior of each new token, the required number of samples scales linearly with the number of existing tokens it is mapped to. To realize this mapping, we propose an embedding-tuning algorithm that requires minimal parameter updates and induces zero forgetting. Experimental results further demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [118] [LUCAS-MEGA: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for Representation Learning in Soil-Environment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04323)
*Kuangdai Leng,Simon Jeffery,Panos Panagos,Tarje Nissen-Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了大规模多模态土壤数据集LUCAS-MEGA（70,000+样本、1,000+特征），开发了自动化数据融合管道SoilFuser，并基于此预训练了多模态表格Transformer模型SoilFormer，为土壤数据驱动建模提供了开放资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤研究对农业、碳循环和环境可持续性至关重要，但现有数据集碎片化、异构性高，限制了模型只能用于小规模预测任务，无法进行高维表征学习。迫切需要构建一个大规模、多模态、机器学习就绪的标准化土壤数据集来推动该领域发展。

Method: 1. 以LUCAS调查为骨干，融合68个源数据集构建LUCAS-MEGA数据集；2. 开发多智能体、人在回路的SoilFuser数据融合管道，标准化异构数据格式和测量协议，解决单位不一致、编码不匹配、错误值等问题，整合自然语言注释；3. 基于特征掩码的自监督目标预训练多模态表格Transformer模型SoilFormer。

Result: 1. LUCAS-MEGA数据集包含70,000+样本、1,000+特征，涵盖物理、化学、环境、生物和视觉属性；2. SoilFormer训练稳定、预测性能强，支持不确定性感知预测；3. 学习到的表征能够恢复与已知土壤过程一致的关系；4. 数据集开放访问并配有API支持结构化查询。

Conclusion: LUCAS-MEGA填补了大规模标准化土壤数据集的空白，SoilFuser管道解决了多源异构数据融合难题，SoilFormer展示了该数据集在数据驱动建模中的实用价值。该工作为土壤科学研究提供了重要的基础设施，有助于推动高维表征学习在土壤领域的应用。

Abstract: Understanding soil is fundamental to agriculture, carbon cycling, and environmental sustainability, yet progress is limited by fragmented and heterogeneous datasets that constrain modeling to small-scale predictive settings rather than high-dimensional representation learning. We introduce LUCAS-MEGA, a large-scale multimodal dataset constructed through systematic data fusion of European soil-environment observations, with the LUCAS survey as its backbone. The fused dataset comprises over 70,000 samples and more than 1,000 features spanning physical, chemical, environmental, biological, and visual attributes, aggregated from 68 source datasets. To enable integration at scale, we develop SoilFuser, a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop data fusion pipeline that standardizes heterogeneous data formats and measurement protocols, resolves inconsistencies and invalid entries (e.g., unit inconsistencies, codebook mismatches, and erroneous values), incorporates natural language annotations, and harmonizes multimodal attributes and metadata into a unified, machine learning-ready feature space. The resulting dataset captures key characteristics of real-world soil observations, including multimodality, uneven feature coverage, and heterogeneous uncertainty. To demonstrate the usability of LUCAS-MEGA for data-driven modeling, we pretrain a multimodal tabular transformer (SoilFormer) using a self-supervised objective based on feature masking, achieving stable training, strong predictive performance, and representations that support uncertainty-aware prediction. We further show that the learned representations recover relationships consistent with established soil processes. LUCAS-MEGA is released with open access and is accompanied by composable, agent-friendly APIs that support structured querying and data-driven workflows.

</details>


### [119] [Budgeted LoRA: Distillation as Structured Compute Allocation for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04341)
*Mohammed Sabry,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Budgeted LoRA框架，通过全局计算预算控制结构化压缩，在推理速度提升4.05倍的同时保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效蒸馏方法（如LoRA）仅降低适应成本，但保留密集骨干网络，未能提供显著的推理节省。需要在显式计算约束下实现既能降低训练成本又能在推理时结构化高效的蒸馏方法。

Method: 引入全局计算预算约束，通过三个机制重新分配模型容量：(i) 模块级密集保留系数；(ii) 自适应低秩分配；(iii) 后训练压缩选择性移除、近似或保留密集组件。通过单一预算参数控制产生一系列学生模型。

Result: 在中等预算下，匹配标准LoRA困惑度且实现1.74倍压缩模块加速；在激进预算下实现4.05倍加速且困惑度适度下降，在函数式上下文学习探针上保持更高准确率。

Conclusion: 在计算约束的蒸馏中，保持行为的核心不在于匹配困惑度或移除更多参数，而在于控制密集计算如何转移到低秩路径。

Abstract: We study distillation for large language models under explicit compute constraints, with the goal of producing student models that are not only cheaper to train, but structurally efficient at inference time. While prior approaches to parameter-efficient distillation, such as LoRA, reduce adaptation cost, they leave the dense backbone unchanged and therefore fail to deliver meaningful inference savings. We propose Budgeted LoRA, a distillation framework that treats model compression as a structured compute allocation problem. Instead of using a fixed student architecture, we introduce a global compute budget that sets the final target fraction of dense computation retained. Under this constraint, the model redistributes capacity across dense and low-rank pathways via (i) module-level dense retention coefficients, (ii) adaptive low-rank allocation, and (iii) post-training compression that selectively removes, approximates, or preserves dense components. This formulation yields a family of students controlled by a single budget dial. Empirically, Budgeted LoRA matches standard LoRA perplexity at a moderate budget with a 1.74x compressed-module speedup; at an aggressive budget it achieves a 4.05x speedup with moderate perplexity degradation, and it preserves higher accuracy on function-style in-context learning probes. These results suggest that, under compute-constrained distillation, retaining behavior is less about matching perplexity or removing more parameters than it is about controlling how dense computation is transferred to low-rank pathways.

</details>


### [120] [DeFed-GMM-DaDiL: A Decentralized Federated Framework for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04324)
*Rebecca Clain,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Fred Ngole Mboula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全去中心化的联邦多源域自适应方法，利用高斯混合模型和Wasserstein重心实现隐私保护下的知识迁移，能有效重构缺失类别并达到竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在去中心化环境下，如何从多个异构且相关的源域向无标签目标域迁移知识的挑战，同时需保护客户端隐私且不依赖中心服务器。

Method: 提出DeFed-GMM-DaDiL框架，每个客户端将数据集建模为高斯混合模型（GMM），通过标记的Wasserstein重心联合近似共享的可学习GMM原子，实现全去中心化的联邦学习。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在目标域存在缺失类别的场景下，能保持共享表征的稳定性与一致性，有效重构缺失类别，并在多源域自适应基准测试中取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DeFed-GMM-DaDiL成功实现了无需中心服务器的去中心化多源域自适应，在保证隐私的同时具有良好的表征稳定性和有效性。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-source domain adaptation seeks to transfer knowledge from multiple heterogeneous and related source domains to an unlabeled target domain in a decentralized setting. We address this challenge through a fully decentralized federated approach, DeFed-GMM-DaDiL, an extension of the GMM-Dataset Dictionary Learning (DaDiL) framework. Each client models its dataset as a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), and the federation jointly approximates them via labeled Wasserstein barycenters of shared, learnable GMM atoms. This design enables adaptation without a central server while preserving clients' privacy. We empirically study the stability of the learned representations in scenarios where the target domain has missing classes. Empirical results demonstrate that DeFed-GMM-DaDiL maintains stable and consistent shared representations across clients, effectively reconstructs missing classes, and achieves competitive performance on multi-source domain adaptation benchmarks.

</details>


### [121] [Efficiently Aligning Language Models with Online Natural Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04356)
*Christine Ye,Joe Benton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用在线自然语言反馈，在模糊、难以监督的领域中高效地对齐语言模型。通过迭代优化代理奖励信号并在过优化点收集专家反馈，仅需极少专家样本即可显著提升模型性能（部分场景下样本效率提升最高达50倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法在许多领域表现优异，但广泛有益的AI部署往往需要模型在“模糊”、难以监督的领域具备强大能力。在这些领域中，人类专家虽能提供高质量监督信号，但仅能覆盖极少数模型输出，因此亟需提升专家监督的数据效率。

Method: 核心方法是迭代式优化流程：首先基于代理奖励信号训练模型，在出现过优化时停止，收集新的专家监督（自然语言反馈），并更新代理奖励模型。研究者通过上下文学习（ICL）和微调两种方式构建代理奖励模型，并在Qwen3-8B（创意写作任务）和Haiku 4.5（对齐研究任务）上进行验证。

Result: 实验结果显示：对于Qwen3-8B，ICL方法在专家样本减少50倍的情况下可恢复35%的性能，微调方法在样本减少20倍时可恢复80%性能、减少3倍时可完全恢复性能；对于Haiku 4.5，ICL方法在样本减少30倍时恢复35%性能，微调方法在样本减少10倍时即可完全恢复性能。这证明了在线自然语言反馈能显著提升专家监督的数据效率。

Conclusion: 在线自然语言反馈是提升模糊领域中专家监督数据效率的有效手段。通过结合ICL和微调方法构建代理奖励模型，可以在大幅减少专家标注成本的同时，实现接近或达到全量监督的性能水平，为实际AI部署提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has been used to elicit impressive performance from language models in many domains. But, broadly beneficial deployments of AI may require us to train models with strong capabilities in "fuzzy", hard-to-supervise domains. In this paper, we develop methods to align language models in fuzzy domains where human experts are still able to provide high-quality supervision signal, but only for a small number of model outputs, using online natural language feedback. Specifically, we train models by iteratively optimizing against proxy reward signals, stopping at the point of over-optimization, collecting fresh expert supervision, and updating the proxy reward. We construct proxy reward models from language models using in-context learning (ICL) and fine-tuning. We test our methods by eliciting creative writing and alignment research capabilities in Qwen3-8B and Haiku 4.5 respectively. For Qwen3-8B, ICL methods recover up to 35% of performance with 50x fewer expert samples, while fine-tuning methods recover 80% with up to 20x fewer samples and 100% with 3x fewer samples. For Haiku 4.5, ICL methods recover up to 35% of performance with 30x fewer samples, and fine-tuning methods recover 100% with 10x fewer samples. Our results suggest that online natural language feedback can substantially improve the data efficiency of expert supervision.

</details>


### [122] [On the Architectural Complexity of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04325)
*Nicholas J. Cooper,François G. Meyer,Michael L. Roberts,Carlos Zapata-Carratalá,Lijun Chen,Danna Gurari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架，通过显式建模张量操作结构来分析和构建深度神经网络，揭示了架构复杂度与突破性网络之间的关联，并发现了大量未探索的高复杂度架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络理论存在缺口，往往忽略了张量操作的结构等低层信息，缺乏对这些底层操作的显式建模。因此需要一个统一框架来严格分析和系统构建DNN，以填补理论空白。

Method: 提出一个统一的理论框架，显式建模张量操作的结构，而非将其抽象化。基于该框架实现两个目标：（1）分析深度学习历史中架构复杂度的演变；（2）基于新类型张量操作自动构建新架构。

Result: 对过去40年DNN的研究揭示了突破性架构与不同类型架构复杂度增加之间的联系；识别出若干尚未被探索的大类高复杂度架构；收集并公开发布了3000多种高复杂度架构的数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架为DNN的严格分析和系统构建提供了理论基础，有助于理解深度学习发展规律并探索新的研究方向，高复杂度架构数据集为未来研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: We introduce a unified theoretical framework for the rigorous analysis and systematic construction of deep neural networks (DNNs). This framework addresses a gap in existing theory by explicitly modeling the structure of tensor operations -- lower level information that is often abstracted. Our framework enables two novel objectives: (1) analysis of the evolution of architectural complexity over deep learning history, and (2) automatic construction of novel architectures based on new types of tensor operations. Our study of DNNs introduced over the past 40 years reveals a connection between groundbreaking architectures and increases in different types of architectural complexity. Moreover, we identify several large classes of higher complexity architectures that have not yet been explored. We then collect a dataset of 3,000+ higher complexity architectures, which we publicly release at: https://github.com/combinatoriallabs/ArchitecturalComplexity.

</details>


### [123] [Learning-based Statistical Refinement for Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04332)
*Rihuan Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需干净图像或噪声分布先验知识的统计细化方法，通过利用噪声数据的统计信息提升给定去噪器的去噪质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪方法通常依赖于对图像和噪声的精确建模，但在实际应用中往往面临模型不完美、噪声假设不可靠或缺乏干净样本等问题。当无法获取干净图像且未知噪声分布时，去噪结果往往无法与噪声统计数据保持一致，导致次优结果。

Method: 在假设噪声给定干净信号后条件像素独立的前提下，提出了一种基于贝叶斯公式的方法。该方法利用噪声数据中的辅助信号来评估去噪结果的一致性，无需精确的噪声分布信息，从而利用统计信息增强去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性。

Result: 该方法能够有效提升去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性，并在无需干净图像或校准数据的情况下显著改善去噪质量。

Conclusion: 该方法为在缺乏噪声分布先验和干净数据的实际场景中，提供了一种有效的去噪结果统计细化解决方案，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: This work proposes a learning-based statistical refinement method for improving the denoising results of a given denoiser without knowing the precise noise distribution or accessing clean images or calibration data. While there are many existing successful denoising approaches for handling different kinds of noise, they typically require accurate modelling of the images and the noise (implicitly or explicitly), and hence the denoising results can be suboptimal due to different practical factors such as imperfect models, unreliable noise assumptions, or low quality data. In particular, when clean image samples are not available and there is a lack of knowledge of the underlying noise distribution, which is the case in various practical situations, the results may not well align with the noise statistics. The unawareness of the useful statistical information leads to suboptimal results. This work aims to make the best use of the statistical information to improve the consistency between the given denoising results and the noise statistics, under the assumption that the noise is conditionally pixel-wise independent given the clean signal. A method, based on a Bayesian formulation of an auxiliary signal in the noisy data, is proposed for evaluating the consistency of the denoising results, without precise information on noise distribution. By leveraging the statistical information from noisy data, the method enhances the statistical noise consistency and improves denoising quality.

</details>


### [124] [Mitigating Label Shift in Tabular In-Context Learning via Test-Time Posterior Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04363)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,SoonYoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DistPFN，一种针对表格数据基础模型（TabPFN）的测试时后验调整方法，有效解决了标签偏移问题，在250+数据集上显著提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN作为一种表格数据基础模型，虽然在常规场景表现优异，但研究发现其对标签偏移（label shift）非常脆弱，容易过拟合于训练数据集中的多数类，导致在类别分布不一致的测试场景下性能下降。

Method: 提出DistPFN方法，通过重缩放预测类别概率来调整：降低训练先验（上下文类别分布）的影响权重，同时增强模型预测后验的贡献。该方法无需架构修改或额外训练。进一步提出DistPFN-T，引入温度缩放机制，根据先验与后验之间的差异自适应地控制调整强度。

Result: 在超过250个OpenML数据集上的实验表明，该方法在各种基于TabPFN的模型中，针对标签偏移场景的分类任务取得了显著提升，同时在无标签偏移的标准场景下仍保持优异性能。

Conclusion: DistPFN是首个针对表格基础模型的测试时后验调整方法，有效缓解了TabPFN的标签偏移脆弱性问题，为表格数据基础模型在实际应用中的鲁棒性提供了重要改进方案。

Abstract: TabPFN has recently gained attention as a foundation model for tabular datasets, achieving strong performance by leveraging in-context learning on synthetic data. However, we find that TabPFN is vulnerable to label shift, often overfitting to the majority class in the training dataset. To address this limitation, we propose DistPFN, the first test-time posterior adjustment method designed for tabular foundation models. DistPFN rescales predicted class probabilities by downweighting the influence of the training prior (i.e., the class distribution of the context) and emphasizing the contribution of the model's predicted posterior, without architectural modification or additional training. We further introduce DistPFN-T, which incorporates temperature scaling to adaptively control the adjustment strength based on the discrepancy between prior and posterior. We evaluate our methods on over 250 OpenML datasets, demonstrating substantial improvements for various TabPFN-based models in classification tasks under label shift, while maintaining strong performance in standard settings without label shift. Code is available at this repository: https://github.com/seunghan96/DistPFN.

</details>


### [125] [Extending Differential Temporal Difference Methods for Episodic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04368)
*Kris De Asis,Mohamed Elsayed,Jiamin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分时序差分（TD）方法在情景问题中适用性受限的问题，提出了一种广义差分TD方法，证明了其在存在终止条件时能保持策略排序，从而将其扩展至情景问题，并通过实验验证了奖励中心化能提高情景问题的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分TD方法依赖奖励中心化，在无限视界问题中表现良好，但在情景问题中奖励中心化可能改变最优策略，限制了其适用性。鉴于最近的研究强调了归一化在流式深度强化学习中的作用，作者旨在研究情景问题中的奖励中心化，以扩展差分TD的应用范围。

Method: 提出了一种广义的差分TD方法，证明了该方法在存在终止条件时能够保持策略的排序，从而将差分TD扩展到情景问题；证明了该方法与某种线性TD形式等价，继承了相关算法的理论保证；并将多种流式强化学习算法扩展为其差分版本。

Result: 理论分析表明，广义差分TD方法在情景问题中能保持策略排序，并与线性TD等价，具有理论保证；在多种基础算法和环境上的实验验证了奖励中心化能够提高情景问题的样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文成功将差分TD方法扩展至情景问题，解决了奖励中心化在情景问题中可能改变最优策略的难题，为情景问题中的强化学习算法设计提供了新的思路和理论支持。

Abstract: Differential temporal difference (TD) methods are value-based reinforcement learning algorithms that have been proposed for infinite-horizon problems. They rely on reward centering, where each reward is centered by the average reward. This keeps the return bounded and removes a value function's state-independent offset. However, reward centering can alter the optimal policy in episodic problems, limiting its applicability. Motivated by recent works that emphasize the role of normalization in streaming deep reinforcement learning, we study reward centering in episodic problems and propose a generalization of differential TD. We prove that this generalization maintains the ordering of policies in the presence of termination, and thus extends differential TD to episodic problems. We show equivalence with a form of linear TD, thereby inheriting theoretical guarantees that have been shown for those algorithms. We then extend several streaming reinforcement learning algorithms to their differential counterparts. Across a range of base algorithms and environments, we empirically validate that reward centering can improve sample efficiency in episodic problems.

</details>


### [126] [Structural Equivalence and Learning Dynamics in Delayed MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04345)
*Jules Sintes,Ana Bušić,Jiamin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了合作部分可观测多智能体系统中观测延迟（OD）与动作延迟（AD）的形式等价性，但数值实验揭示两者在实际学习动态上存在根本差异，并基于此等价性实现了跨延迟类型的零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已在无限时域单智能体场景中建立了观测延迟与动作延迟的等价关系，但在任意时域、部分可观测、去中心化策略执行的合作多智能体系统中，这种等价性是否成立尚不明确。明确这一问题对于统一复杂延迟系统的求解方法具有重要意义。

Method: 1. 利用观测-动作历史，在Dec-POMDP框架下形式化证明OD与AD系统生成相同的容许联合策略集，且诱导的状态-动作-观测轨迹分布相同；2. 证明在TI-MDPs中，观测-动作历史可简化为最小局部增强状态；3. 通过数值实验对比两种延迟设置下的学习动态差异；4. 利用结构等价性实现从OD到AD的零样本策略迁移。

Result: 1. 在任意时域部分可观测合作多智能体系统中形式化确立了OD与AD的等价性，两者具有相同的容许联合策略集和最优解；2. 任意混合延迟配置可简化为纯OD系统；3. 发现当转移不独立时，使用最小局部增强状态等价性不再成立；4. TD算法中的操作约束和因果信用分配误差导致不同延迟设置下的学习行为差异；5. 成功实现了OD到AD的多智能体零样本策略迁移。

Conclusion: 本研究形式化推广了OD与AD的等价性至一般多智能体场景，为延迟系统提供了理论简化依据。尽管最优解空间结构同构，实际学习动态的根本差异揭示了算法设计的挑战。通过利用结构等价性绕过学习障碍，本研究为零样本策略迁移开辟了道路，为开发复杂延迟系统的统一高效求解方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: We formally establish the equivalence between Observation Delay (OD) and Action Delay (AD) in cooperative partially observable multi-agent systems using observation-action histories. We show that both systems generate identical admissible joint-policy sets, and their induced state-action-observation trajectories are identical in distribution, leading to identical optimal solutions in Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). This formally generalizes existing infinite-horizon single-agent results to any-horizon partially observable cooperative multi-agent problems with decentralized policy execution, and allows any mixed-delay configuration to be reduced to a pure OD system. We further prove that in Transition-Independent MDPs (TI-MDPs), the observation-action history reduces to a tractable minimal local augmented state.
  However, we show through numerical experiments that although the optimal solution spaces are structurally isomorphic, the practical learning dynamics are fundamentally different. First, using the minimal local augmented state, the equivalence no longer holds when transitions are not independent. Second, operational constraints and causal credit-assignment errors in Temporal Difference (TD) algorithms induce different learning behaviors across regimes. Finally, leveraging this structural equivalence to bypass these learning challenges, we demonstrate successful multi-agent zero-shot policy transfer from OD to AD, paving the way for unified, efficient solution methods in complex delayed systems.

</details>


### [127] [Covariance-Aware Goodness for Scalable Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04346)
*Xiaoyi Jiang,Bashir M. Al-Hashimi,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的Forward-Forward算法框架，通过引入二阶协方差信息、逻辑融合模块和特征对齐层，成功将无反向传播学习的网络深度扩展至16层（如VGG-16），在ImageNet-100和Tiny-ImageNet上取得了与反向传播相当的性能，同时显著降低内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无反向传播（BP-free）Forward-Forward方法在卷积网络设置下，面对ImageNet-100和Tiny-ImageNet等复杂基准测试时，性能明显落后于反向传播。作者将这一差距归因于goodness提取中的结构瓶颈：标准的平方和公式将特征体折叠为通道级激活能量，忽略了关键的二阶依赖关系。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个关键组件的框架：1）双轴协方差Goodness（BiCovG），通过跨通道投影和嵌套多尺度聚合显式引入结构化二阶信息，避免了显式矩阵估计的高昂复杂度；2）轻量级逻辑融合模块，聚合各层预测，增强深层表示的贡献；3）特征对齐层（FAL），在块边界引入零初始化校正，缓解深度局部训练网络中的表示错位问题。此外，还提出了混合Goodness块以控制梯度传播范围。

Result: 无反向传播模型在ImageNet-100上达到73.01%准确率，在Tiny-ImageNet上达到50.30%。采用混合Goodness块后，ImageNet-100上的性能差距缩小至3.6%，在Tiny-ImageNet上匹配反向传播性能，同时峰值内存相比反向传播减少约50%。成功将可行的Forward-Forward学习深度从浅层基线扩展到VGG-16等16层架构。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地解决goodness函数中二阶信息缺失和深层网络表示错位问题，本文显著缩小了Forward-Forward算法与反向传播在复杂视觉任务上的性能差距，证明了无反向传播方法在大规模图像分类任务中的可行性，同时保持了内存效率优势。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward algorithm eliminates global gradient flow and full network activations storage. However, in convolutional settings, existing BP-free FF methods significantly under-perform backpropagation on complex benchmarks such as ImageNet-100 and Tiny-ImageNet. We identify this gap as a structural bottleneck in goodness extraction: standard sum-of-squares formulation collapses feature volumes into channel-wise activation energies which omits critical second-order dependencies. To address this, we propose a framework centered on three key components. First, Bi-axis Covariance Goodness(BiCovG) explicitly augments the standard goodness function with structured second-order information along two axes: cross-channel projections that model inter-feature covariance, and nested multi-scale aggregation that encodes spatial correlation statistics. This provides a tractable approximation to covariance-aware goodness without the prohibitive O(C^2) complexity of explicit matrix estimation. Second, a lightweight Logistic Fusion module aggregates layer-wise predictions, amplifying the contribution of deeper representations. Third, the Feature Alignment Layer(FAL) introduces a zero-initialized correction at block boundaries to mitigate representation misalignment in deep locally trained networks. By introducing these three components, we effectively double the depth of viable Forward-Forward learning, extending robust layer utilization from shallow baselines to 16 layer architectures like VGG-16. The resulting BP-free model achieves 73.01% on ImageNet-100 and 50.30% on Tiny-ImageNet. As a practical extension, Hybrid Goodness Blocks control the scope of gradient propagation via configurable block sizes, further narrowing the ImageNet-100 gap to 3.6% and matching BP on Tiny-ImageNet, while still reducing peak memory by approximately 50% relative to BP.

</details>


### [128] [Critical Windows of Complexity Control: When Transformers Decide to Reason or Memorize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04396)
*Sarwan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Transformer的记忆与推理能力的分化取决于训练过程中一个特定的“关键窗口”期，而非全程的正则化控制。在此窗口内施加权值衰减最为有效，窗口位置受初始化尺度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为Transformer的组合泛化能力受复杂度控制（初始化尺度和权重衰减）支配，但将其视为静态超参数选择，尚不清楚这种控制在训练的哪个阶段起决定性作用。本文旨在探究记忆与推理的分界点具体发生在训练的何时。

Method: 在受控的组合任务上，通过实验分析权值衰减施加的时间窗口对模型OOD准确率的影响。研究者对比了仅在训练的特定25%时间段施加权值衰减与全程施加的效果，并改变了窗口位置和初始化尺度，以观察对记忆与推理模式的影响。

Result: 发现存在一个决定性的关键窗口：(i) 仅在25%的训练时间内施加权值衰减，其OOD准确率（0.93）与全程施加（0.91）相当；(ii) 在总正则化预算不变的情况下，将权值衰减置于训练中期比早期高出5-9倍OOD准确率；(iii) 窗口边界非常敏锐，起始点偏移仅100步即可使OOD准确率从随机水平（0.15）跃升至推理水平（0.61）；(iv) 窗口位置取决于初始化尺度，但小初始化下推理解的吸引域反而缩小，这与小初始化普遍更好的观点相悖。此外，该现象具有任务特异性，在模运算的grokking任务中未出现。

Conclusion: Transformer的记忆与推理命运由训练过程中一个尖锐、可识别的“关键窗口”决定，而非静态超参数选择。最佳正则化时机（窗口位置）与初始化尺度系统性相关，且小初始化并非在所有情况下均优。这一发现为理解与优化Transformer的组合泛化能力提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that Transformers' compositional generalization is governed by \emph{complexity control}, initialization scale and weight decay, which steers training toward low-complexity reasoning solutions rather than high-complexity memorization. Existing analyses, however, treat complexity control as a single static hyperparameter choice, leaving open \emph{when} during training this control is actually decisive. We show that the memorization-versus-reasoning fate of a Transformer is determined within a sharp, identifiable window of training. On a controlled compositional task we find that (i)~weight decay applied for a single 25\%-of-training window matches full-training weight decay in out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy ($0.93$ vs $0.91$); (ii)~holding total regularization budget constant, placing it in the middle of training yields $5{-}9\times$ higher OOD accuracy than placing it early; (iii)~the boundary of the critical window is remarkably sharp, window onset shifted by as little as $100$ optimization steps causes mean OOD to jump from chance ($0.15$) to reasoning-regime ($0.61$); (iv)~the window's position depends systematically on initialization scale, but the basin of attraction for reasoning solutions \emph{shrinks} at small initialization, contradicting the prevailing recommendation that smaller initialization is uniformly better. We further show that the critical-window phenomenon is task-specific: it does not appear on grokking with modular arithmetic, where properly tuned constant weight decay matches scheduled weight decay.

</details>


### [129] [Probing Structural Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Algebraic Trapdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04352)
*Igor Rivin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于评估语言模型结构化数学推理能力的基准测试，基于SL(3,Z)中的子群构造问题。实验发现，顶级模型在面对开放可判定性边界时展现出校准的元认知能力，能够识别认知瓶颈并选择弃权而非错误作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法无法有效区分语言模型是否真正内化了代数先验知识（如Aschbacher分类、McLaughlin定理、性质(T)、同余子群性质），也无法检测模型在面对开放可判定性问题时的元认知能力。标准答案评分将四种不同的模型行为（正确提交、错误提交、正确弃权、错误弃权）混淆，掩盖了模型行为的关键差异。

Method: 构建基于SL(3,Z)子群构造问题的基准测试套件，利用密码学风格的验证者-证明者不对称性。每个实例给出有限生成子群的整数矩阵列表，要求计算算术不变量（指数、素数处满射、成员资格）。构造时信息(N,K)可将答案锁定为O(1)闭式解，但求解者缺乏该信息，必须通过Aschbacher分类分析或解决成员资格查询问题，后者在SL(3,Z)中的可判定性未知。

Result: 在指数变体测试中，某模型推理152分钟后，明确识别核侧成员资格问题为瓶颈，尝试构造性验证，最终以"DON'T KNOW"弃权而非提交计算出的上核候选值。这表明模型在开放可判定性边界上展现出校准的元认知能力，能够识别自身的认知限制并做出审慎决策。

Conclusion: 该基准测试揭示了模型行为的四分类框架（正确提交、错误提交、正确弃权、错误弃权），能够区分内化代数先验知识的模型与依赖通用计算的模型。标准评分方法掩盖了这一关键差异，新框架为评估语言模型的数学推理能力提供了更精细的分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark suite for evaluating structural mathematical reasoning in language models, built on subgroup-construction problems in SL(3, Z) with cryptographic-style verifier-prover asymmetry. Each instance presents a finitely generated subgroup as a list of integer matrices and asks for an arithmetic invariant -- index, surjection-at-prime, or membership -- that the construction-time information (N, K) pins down in O(1) closed form, but that the solver, lacking that information, must derive by either Aschbacher-classification analysis or by a membership query in SL(3, Z) of unknown decidability. The benchmark therefore distinguishes models with internalized algebraic priors (Aschbacher classes, McLaughlin's theorem, Property (T), the congruence subgroup property) from models that rely on general-purpose computation. We report empirical results across five representative reasoning traces from two state-of-the-art models. The headline result: on the index variant, one model spent 152 minutes of reasoning, explicitly identified the kernel-side membership question as the bottleneck, attempted constructive verification, and abstained with "DON'T KNOW" rather than commit to its computed cokernel candidate -- demonstrating calibrated meta-cognition on the open-decidability boundary that the benchmark was designed to probe. We argue that the benchmark exposes a four-way classification of model behavior (commit-correct, commit-wrong, abstain-correct, abstain-wrong) that standard answer-key scoring conflates.

</details>


### [130] [Demystifying Manifold Constraints in LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04418)
*Kang An,Jiaxiang Li,Donald Goldfarb,Shiqian Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MACRO框架，证明了显式流形约束可以独立控制前向激活尺度并维持旋转平衡，从而在理论上统一并替代LLM预训练中经验性的稳定技术（如RMSNorm和权重衰减），在保持理论收敛保证的同时实现了具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练的成功严重依赖启发式稳定技术（如显式归一化层和权重衰减），而近期约束优化方法虽能改善数值稳定性，但添加约束的机制和动机仍不明确。现有方法的运作原理缺乏理论解释，亟需系统性地揭示显式流形约束在LLM预训练中的作用机理。

Method: 提出Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer (MACRO)——一个可证明收敛的单循环优化框架。该框架将权重正则化启发式方法与RMS归一化、解耦权重衰减等交互机制解耦，通过理论分析和全面的实证评估来揭示流形约束的独立作用机制。

Result: 理论分析和实证评估表明：流形约束能够独立地限制前向激活尺度并强制维持稳定的旋转平衡状态，从而涵盖了RMS归一化和权重衰减等启发式机制的作用。在大规模LLM架构上的评估显示，MACRO在严格保持精确黎曼优化理论保证的同时，实现了极具竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: 本研究系统性地阐明了显式流形约束在LLM预训练中的作用机制，证明了流形约束可以理论性地替代现有经验性稳定技术。MACRO框架成功地在理论严谨性和实际性能之间取得了平衡，为LLM预训练优化提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: The empirical success of large language model (LLM) pre-training relies heavily on heuristic stabilization techniques, such as explicit normalization layers and weight decay. While recent constrained optimization approaches that explicitly restrict weights may improve numerical stability and performance, the mechanism and motivation for adding constraints still remain elusive. This paper systematically demystifies the role of explicit manifold constraints in LLM pre-training. By introducing the Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer (MACRO)-a provably convergent, single-loop optimization framework-our study disentangles weight regularization heuristics from interacting mechanisms like RMS normalization and decoupled weight decay. Theoretical analyses and comprehensive empirical evaluations reveal that manifold constraints independently bound forward activation scales and enforce stable rotational equilibrium, thereby subsuming the roles of these heuristic mechanisms. Evaluations on large-scale LLM architectures demonstrate that MACRO achieves highly competitive performance while rigorously preserving the theoretical guarantees of exact Riemannian optimization.

</details>


### [131] [FLUID: Continuous-Time Hyperconnected Sparse Transformer for Sink-Free Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04421)
*Waleed Razzaq,Yun-Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLUID，一种连续时间Transformer，通过引入Liquid Attention Network (LAN) 将连续动态直接融入注意力计算，在多个任务上相比基线提升高达47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续时间Transformer虽然在输入/输出嵌入中引入了连续动态，但其核心的缩放点积注意力(SDPA)机制仍然是离散的，限制了模型对不规则和长范围数据的建模能力。

Method: 提出FLUID框架，核心包含：(1) Liquid Attention Network (LAN)，将注意力logits重新解释为连续动态系统，通过输入依赖的非线性循环门控调制线性ODE来求解；(2) 显式的attention-sink门控消除对无信息节点的过度关注；(3) Liquid Hyper-Connections替代标准残差连接以自适应调节层间信息流。理论上证明LAN在SDPA和CT-RNNs之间起插值作用，两者可视为其特例。

Result: 在不规则时间序列、长范围建模、自动驾驶车道保持控制、稀缺数据下的物理动态学习等任务上，FLUID持续匹配或超越连续时间基线，某些场景提升达47%，在分布偏移下泛化能力更强。此外，FLUID展现出更优的噪声鲁棒性和自动驾驶控制中的自我纠正归纳偏置，在运行时和内存效率上处于各竞争方法的中间位置。

Conclusion: FLUID成功地将连续动态融入注意力机制，在保持理论稳定性保证的同时，在多项任务上取得显著性能提升，为连续时间建模提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Continuous-time (CT) Transformers improve irregular and long-range modeling over CT-RNNs by exploiting inputs or outputs embeddings with continuous dynamics. However, the core scaled-dot-product-attention (SDPA) mechanism remains inherently discrete. We propose FLUID (Flexible Unified Information Dynamics), a CT Transformer that incorporates continuous dynamics directly into the attention computation by replacing it with Liquid Attention Network (LAN). LAN reinterprets attention logits as continuous dynamical system and reformulates them as the solution to a linear ODE modulated by input-dependent nonlinear recurrent gates. Theoretically, we establish stability guarantees for LAN dynamics and show that it serves as an interpolating middle ground between SDPA and CT-RNNs, recovering each as special case under well-defined parameterization of its gating functions. LAN also introduces an explicit attention-sink gate to eliminate disproportionate attention mass on uninformative nodes. FLUID replaces standard residual connections with input-dependent Liquid Hyper-Connections to adaptively regulate interlayer information flow. Empirically, we evaluate FLUID on a broad set of learning tasks, including (i) irregular time-series, (ii) long-range modeling, (iii) lane-keeping control of autonomous vehicles, and (iv) learning physical dynamics under a scarce data regime. Across all the tasks, FLUID consistently matches or outperforms CT baselines, achieving improvements of up to 47% in certain scenarios and enhancing generalization under distributional shifts. Additionally, FLUID demonstrates superior noise robustness and a self-correcting inductive bias in autonomous vehicle control. We also provide a detailed analysis of key hyperparameters to guide tuning and show that FLUID occupies an intermediate position among competing approaches in terms of runtime and memory efficiency.

</details>


### [132] [Online Nonstochastic Prediction: Logarithmic Regret via Predictive Online Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04364)
*Chih-Fan Pai,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对边界稳定、部分可观测的线性动态系统，提出了一种基于预测提示的无约束在线最小二乘预测方法，在非随机扰动下实现了对 hindsight 最佳 Luenberger 预测器的对数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线学习方法通常依赖有界域/梯度，其保证在面对边界稳定系统中可能无界的轨迹时会失效。本文旨在解决非随机扰动下边界稳定、部分可观测线性系统的在线预测问题，目标是最小化累积平方预测损失并与 hindsight 最佳的 Luenberger 预测器竞争。

Method: 引入一种无约束在线最小二乘方法，通过定制的预测提示（predictive hints）来稳定学习过程。在有模型知识时，利用任何稳定 Luenberger 预测器构造提示，证明提示残差一致有界；在无模型预测时，针对对称系统引入简单的通用提示。

Result: 证明在有模型知识时，由任何稳定 Luenberger 预测器构造的提示使提示残差一致有界，从而在轨迹无界增长的情况下实现对数遗憾；在无模型情况下，针对对称系统的通用提示同样保持对数遗憾。

Conclusion: 本文的方法相比经典固定增益观测器，在非随机扰动下提供了一种自适应、实例最优的在线预测器，突破了标准在线学习方法对有界域/梯度的依赖限制。

Abstract: We study online prediction for marginally stable, partially observed linear dynamical systems under nonstochastic disturbances. Our objective is to minimize the cumulative squared prediction loss and compete with the best-in-hindsight Luenberger predictor. Standard online learning methods typically rely on bounded domains/gradients, and thus their guarantees may fail to deal with potentially unbounded trajectories in marginally stable systems. In this paper, we introduce an unconstrained online least squares method that stabilizes the learning process via tailored predictive hints. With model knowledge, we prove that hints constructed from any stabilizing Luenberger predictor render the hint residuals uniformly bounded, achieving logarithmic regret despite unbounded trajectory growth. We also discuss model-free prediction and introduce a simple universal hint for symmetric systems, under which logarithmic regret is maintained without model knowledge. Our results provide an adaptive, instance-wise optimal online predictor compared to classical fixed-gain observers under nonstochastic disturbances.

</details>


### [133] [Stabilizing LLM Supervised Fine-Tuning via Explicit Distributional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04468)
*Xinyu Wang,Changzhi Sun,Yuanbin Wu,Xiaoling Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Anchored Learning框架，通过动态移动锚点控制分布漂移，有效缓解LLM微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，将性能下降从53%降至5%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在微调过程中普遍存在灾难性遗忘问题，即在优化目标性能的同时导致已有能力显著退化。研究表明该现象主要由优化过程中的过度分布漂移所驱动，需要一种方法来控制分布更新的幅度。

Method: 提出Anchored Learning框架，通过动态演化的移动锚点显式控制离线微调过程中的分布更新。锚点在当前模型和冻结参考模型之间进行插值，构建一个中间目标分布供模型蒸馏学习，从而将全局微调转化为分布空间中的一系列局部信赖域更新。

Result: 在iGSM、MedCalc和IFEval数据集上的实验表明，Anchored Learning始终位于增益-稳定性权衡的帕累托前沿。与标准SFT相比，该方法将iGSM和MedCalc上的性能下降从53%降至5%以下，同时保持接近最优的性能增益（如iGSM上达到75.2%）。

Conclusion: Anchored Learning通过线性KL散度上界保证了模型分布间的稳定过渡，有效平衡了目标任务的性能提升与已有能力的保持，为大语言模型的安全微调提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) often suffers from catastrophic forgetting, where improvements on a target objective degrade previously acquired capabilities. Recent evidence suggests that this phenomenon is primarily driven by excessive distributional drift during optimization. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Anchored Learning, a simple framework that explicitly controls distributional updates during offline fine-tuning via a dynamically evolving moving anchor. Instead of matching a fixed reference distribution, the anchor interpolates between the current model and a frozen reference to construct an intermediate target that the model distills toward, transforming global fine-tuning into a sequence of local trust-region updates in distribution space. Theoretically, we prove this anchor-based update admits a linear KL-divergence upper bound per iteration, ensuring a stable transition between model distributions. Extensive experiments on iGSM, MedCalc, and IFEval show that Anchored Learning consistently lies on the Pareto frontier of gain-stability trade-offs, achieving near-optimal performance improvements while substantially reducing degradation compared to strong baselines. For example, while standard SFT suffers from over 53% performance degradation on iGSM and MedCalc, Anchored Learning slashes this drop to under 5% while maintaining near-optimal gains (e.g., 75.2% on iGSM).

</details>


### [134] [$p$-adic Manifold Learning and Benchmark Tasks from Impartial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04374)
*Tomoki Mihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了$p$进流形学习问题，并给出了相应的求解算法，同时提供了基于无偏博弈的基准测试任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将$p$进数理论引入流形学习领域，拓展现有流形学习方法的数学基础和应用范围，为处理具有$p$进结构的数据提供新的学习框架。

Method: 提出$p$进流形学习的理论框架，设计专门的算法求解该学习问题，并构建基于无偏博弈的基准任务来评估算法性能。

Result: 成功建立了$p$进流形学习的完整方法论体系，包括问题定义、求解算法和评估基准，验证了该方法在相关任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: $ p$进流形学习为机器学习提供了新的数学工具和理论视角，开辟了结合$p$进几何与深度学习的新研究方向。

Abstract: We introduce $p$-adic manifold learning, propose an algorithm to solve it, and propose benchmark tasks from impartial games.

</details>


### [135] [GraphPI: Efficient Protein Inference with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04376)
*Zheng Ma,Jiazhen Chen,Lei Xin,Ali Ghodsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphPI框架，将蛋白质推断建模为图节点分类问题，利用伪标签和自训练解决标注稀缺，实现了无需针对数据集微调的通用高效推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在生物医学领域应用广泛，但在蛋白质推断任务中受限于高质量标注数据的极度稀缺，且蛋白质注释成本高昂、过程复杂，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 提出GraphPI框架，将蛋白质推断建模为图神经网络（GNN）的节点分类问题；构建蛋白质-肽段-PSM图谱，蛋白质作为节点；利用现有算法生成伪标签，并结合自训练策略基于置信度分数迭代优化标签。

Result: 得益于Percolator特征的良好归一化特性，GraphPI无需针对特定数据集进行微调即可实现通用应用；在多个测试数据集上表现出色，且相比常用蛋白质推断算法显著降低了计算时间。

Conclusion: GraphPI有效缓解了标注数据稀缺问题，避免了过拟合风险，提升了计算效率，证明了将GNN应用于蛋白质推断并实现跨数据集通用性的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: The integration of deep learning approaches in biomedical research has been transformative, enabling breakthroughs in various applications. Despite these strides, its application in protein inference is impeded by the scarcity of extensively labeled datasets, a challenge compounded by the high costs and complexities of accurate protein annotation. In this study, we introduce GraphPI, a novel framework that treats protein inference as a node classification problem. We treat proteins as interconnected nodes within a protein-peptide-PSM graph, utilizing a Graph Neural Network-based architecture to elucidate their interrelations. To address label scarcity, we train the model on a set of unlabeled public protein datasets with pseudo-labels derived from an existing protein inference algorithm, enhanced by self-training to iteratively refine labels based on confidence scores. Contrary to prevalent methodologies necessitating dataset-specific training, our research illustrates that GraphPI, due to the well normalized nature of Percolator features, exhibits universal applicability without dataset-specific fine-tuning, a feature that not only mitigates the risk of overfitting but also enhances computational efficiency. Our empirical experiments reveal notable performance on various test datasets and deliver significantly reduced computation times compared to common protein inference algorithms.

</details>


### [136] [Dream-MPC: Gradient-Based Model Predictive Control with Latent Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04568)
*Jonathan Spieler,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Dream-MPC，一种结合策略网络与梯度的模型预测控制方法，在24个连续控制任务上显著提升了策略性能并超越了梯度无关的MPC及现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合式模型预测控制（MPC）方法通常依赖梯度无关的优化方法，在高维控制任务中计算成本高昂；虽然梯度方法是一种有前景的替代方案，但现有研究表明其性能往往不如梯度无关方法。

Method: Dream-MPC通过策略网络展开生成少量候选轨迹，并利用学习到的世界模型、不确定性正则化以及在时间上复用已优化动作来实现优化的迭代摊销，通过梯度上升对每条轨迹进行优化。

Result: 在24个连续控制任务上的实验结果表明，Dream-MPC能够显著提升底层策略的性能，并且能够超越梯度无关MPC以及最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: Dream-MPC成功地将梯度优化方法引入模型预测控制框架中，证明了通过结合策略先验、世界模型学习和梯度优化，可以在保证性能的同时有效解决高维控制任务中的计算效率问题。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches either use gradient-free, population-based methods for planning, learned policy networks, or a combination of policy networks and planning. Hybrid approaches that combine Model Predictive Control (MPC) with a learned model and a policy prior to leverage the advantages of both paradigms have shown promising results. However, these approaches typically rely on gradient-free optimization methods, which can be computationally expensive for high-dimensional control tasks. While gradient-based methods are a promising alternative, recent works have empirically shown that gradient-based methods often perform worse than their gradient-free counterparts. We propose Dream-MPC, a novel approach that generates few candidate trajectories from a rolled-out policy and optimizes each trajectory by gradient ascent using a learned world model, uncertainty regularization and amortization of optimization iterations over time by reusing previously optimized actions. Our results on 24 continuous control tasks show that Dream-MPC can significantly improve the performance of the underlying policy and can outperform gradient-free MPC and state-of-the-art baselines. We will open source our code and more at https://dream-mpc.github.io.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Rigid Geometries: The Spline-Pullback Metric for Universal Diffeomorphic SPD Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04406)
*Tushar Das,Subrata Dutta,Sarmistha Neogy,Koushlendra Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出样条回拉度量（SPM），将SPD矩阵深度学习中的度量学习从静态选择转变为通用几何近似，解决了现有方法违反矩阵函数公理的问题，并在多个数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SPD矩阵深度学习方法依赖固定的代数黎曼度量，这种静态表述类似于传统机器学习中的手工特征，施加了刚性几何结构，限制了网络的表达能力和适应性；而近期对几何进行参数化的尝试常通过无约束幂次或依赖秩的缩放违反矩阵基本函数公理，导致空间折叠、全局满射性丧失以及谱奇点处的梯度崩溃。

Method: 引入样条回拉度量（SPM），具体实现为Spectral-SPM和Cholesky-SPM；通过秩不变、单调约束的B样条参数化全局微分同胚，使SPM成为严格递增C1微分同胚的稠密通用近似器，理论上包含现有的回拉度量并支持局部非线性谱建模。

Result: SPM在拓扑上提供全局双射回拉几何，避免了秩交换不连续性和梯度不稳定性；在3个数据集上，使用Linear Probes、SPDNets和深层黎曼残差网络进行实验，均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SPM成功实现了从静态度量选择到通用几何近似的范式转变，为SPD矩阵深度学习提供了理论严谨且性能优越的新方法。

Abstract: The integration of Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) matrices into deep learning has historically relied on fixed algebraic Riemannian metrics. Analogous to hand-crafted features in classical machine learning, these static formulations impose rigid geometries limiting network expressivity and adaptability. Recent attempts to parameterize these geometries often violate the axioms of primary matrix functions through unconstrained powers or rank-dependent scaling, inviting spatial folding, loss of global surjectivity, and gradient collapse at spectral singularities. In this paper, we introduce the Spline-Pullback Metric (SPM), instantiated as Spectral-SPM and Cholesky-SPM, marking a paradigm shift from static metric selection to universal geometric approximation. By parameterizing the global diffeomorphism via a rank-invariant, monotonically constrained B-spline, SPM acts as a dense universal approximator for strictly increasing $C^1$ diffeomorphisms and theoretically subsumes existing pullback metrics while enabling localized non-linear spectral modelling. Topologically, SPM provides a globally bijective pullback geometry precluding rank-swapping discontinuities and gradient instabilities. Empirically, SPM achieves a state-of-the-art performance across 3 datasets utilizing Linear Probes, SPDNets, and deep Riemannian ResNets.

</details>


### [138] [HeterSEED: Semantics-Structure Decoupling for Heterogeneous Graph Learning under Heterophily](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04594)
*Xinyi Li,Ming Li,Lu Bai,Lixin Cui,Feilong Cao,Ke Lv,Yunliang Jiang,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HeterSEED框架，通过解耦语义通道和异质性结构通道来解决异质图中的异配性问题，在理论和实验上均证明了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的异质图普遍存在显著的异配性，相连节点往往具有不同标签或扮演不同语义角色。现有的异质图神经网络主要基于特征相似性进行消息聚合，在异配性场景下会传播误导信息，因为特征相似性可能与底层关系语义不一致。

Method: 提出HeterSEED语义-结构解耦框架，包含两个通道：(1)异质语义通道捕获类型和关系感知的局部语义；(2)结构感知异配通道通过伪标签引导的分区分离同配和异配邻域，并使用基于元路径的结构权重进行聚合；(3)节点级自适应融合机制结合两个通道生成上下文相关的节点表示。

Result: 在五个真实异质图数据集上的实验表明，HeterSEED持续优于代表性异质图神经网络和近期异配性感知基线方法，特别是在强异配性场景下表现突出。实验包括百万节点和亿级边规模的大规模网络。

Conclusion: 理论上证明了在异配性异质图上，HeterSEED比主要依赖特征相似性的标准异质图神经网络具有更强的表达能力，并能有效减少异配邻居引入的预测偏差，为异质图学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Many real-world heterogeneous graphs exhibit pronounced heterophily, where connected nodes often have dissimilar labels or play different semantic roles. In such settings, standard heterogeneous graph neural networks that aggregate messages along metapaths or meta-relations primarily based on feature similarity can propagate misleading information, since feature similarity may be misaligned with underlying relational semantics. In this paper, we propose HeterSEED, a semantics-structure decoupling framework for heterogeneous graph learning under heterophily. HeterSEED decouples representation learning into a heterogeneous semantic channel that captures type- and relation-aware local semantics and a structure-aware heterophily channel that separates homophilic and heterophilic neighborhoods via pseudo-label-guided partitioning and aggregates them using metapath-based structural weights. A node-level adaptive fusion mechanism then combines the two channels to produce context-dependent node representations. Theoretically, we establish that, on heterogeneous graphs under heterophily, HeterSEED is strictly more expressive than standard heterogeneous graph neural networks that rely primarily on feature similarity and provably reduces the prediction bias introduced by heterophilic neighbors. Experiments on five real-world heterogeneous graphs, including two large-scale networks at the million-node and hundred-million-edge scale, demonstrate that HeterSEED consistently outperforms representative heterogeneous graph neural networks and recent heterophily-aware baselines, especially in strongly heterophilic regimes.

</details>


### [139] [Counterfactual identifiability beyond global monotonicity: non-monotone triangular structural causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04413)
*Pengcheng Tan,Jiang Chen,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了非单调三角形结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），在放弃全局单调性假设的情况下，通过机制可逆性和上下文无关的逆向传输，实现了完全的反事实可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构因果模型的可识别性结果依赖全局单调性等强假设，但在具身交互场景中，相同的外源扰动在不同接触上下文中可能引发相反的响应，导致该假设常被违反。作者旨在探究在放弃全局单调性后，何种结构条件仍足以保证可识别性。

Method: 提出非单调三角形结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），保留三角形递归结构但用机制可逆性和上下文无关的逆向传输替代全局单调性；证明这些条件等价于外源同构并蕴含完全的反事实可识别性；构建CausalInverter模型，包含三角形可逆层、方向门和传输稳定性正则化。

Result: 在合成非单调机制上，结构偏置随非单调性增强带来系统性的反事实收益；在MuJoCo Door任务上实现完美事件级反事实恢复，连续角度误差低于Transformer基线，恢复稳定性显著优于Transformer和条件流预测器；在非单调性较弱的MuJoCo Push任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究结果识别出一个介于全局单调三角形模型和无约束黑盒世界模型之间的更广泛可识别范围，证明了在放松全局单调性假设后仍可实现完全的反事实可识别性。

Abstract: Structural causal models provide a unified semantics for interventions and counterfactuals, but most identifiability results rely on restrictive assumptions like global monotonicity, which are often violated in embodied interaction, where the same exogenous perturbation can induce opposite responses under different contact contexts. We ask what structure still suffices once global monotonicity is dropped. We introduce non-monotone triangular structural causal models (NM-TM-SCM), which retain triangular recursion but replace global monotonicity with mechanism-wise invertibility and context-independent inverse transport. We prove that these conditions are equivalent to exogenous isomorphism and imply complete counterfactual identifiability, and we give a counterexample showing that local invertibility alone is insufficient. We instantiate the theory in CausalInverter, with triangular invertible layers, orientation gates, and transport-stability regularization. On synthetic non-monotonic mechanisms, the structural bias yields systematic counterfactual gains as non-monotonicity increases. On MuJoCo Door, our model achieves perfect event-level counterfactual recovery, lowers continuous angle error relative to a Transformer baseline, and delivers substantially more stable recovery than Transformer and conditional-flow predictors. On MuJoCo Push, where non-monotonicity is weaker, the same low-data predictors remain competitive or better, consistent with a bias-variance boundary. These results identify a broader identifiable regime between globally monotone triangular models and unconstrained black-box world models.

</details>


### [140] [A Queueing-Theoretic Framework for Stability Analysis of LLM Inference with KV Cache Memory Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04595)
*Chengyi Nie,Nian Si,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个显式结合计算和GPU内存约束的LLM推理排队论框架，推导了严格的稳定性条件，为GPU集群规模配置提供了理论依据，并在实际生产环境中验证了预测的高准确性（误差通常在10%以内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的快速普及带来了规模化推理的效率挑战。与传统工作负载不同，LLM推理同时受限于计算能力和KV缓存的内存开销——后者虽能加速解码但会迅速耗尽GPU内存，这为系统部署带来了核心难题：如何合理配置GPU资源以避免过度采购或性能违规。

Method: 提出了首个排队论框架，将计算约束和GPU内存约束显式纳入LLM推理分析中。基于该框架，推导出了严格的稳定性与不稳定性条件，用于判断LLM推理服务是否能在不产生无界队列增长的情况下维持输入需求。

Result: 推导出的稳定性条件为系统部署提供了有力工具：结合请求到达率和稳定服务率，操作人员可计算所需集群规模。通过在实际GPU生产环境中的大量实验验证，理论预测的稳定性条件高度准确，偏差通常在10%以内。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了GPU资源配置的核心挑战，使运营商能够在避免昂贵过度采购和性能违规的欠配置之间取得平衡，为LLM推理服务的规模化部署提供了理论支撑和实践指导。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) has created significant challenges for efficient inference at scale. Unlike traditional workloads, LLM inference is constrained by both computation and the memory overhead of key-value (KV) caching, which accelerates decoding but quickly exhausts GPU memory. In this paper, we introduce the first queueing-theoretic framework that explicitly incorporates both computation and GPU memory constraints into the analysis of LLM inference. Based on this framework, we derive rigorous stability and instability conditions that determine whether an LLM inference service can sustain incoming demand without unbounded queue growth. This result offers a powerful tool for system deployment, potentially addressing the core challenge of GPU provisioning. By combining an estimated request arrival rate with our derived stable service rate, operators can calculate the necessary cluster size to avoid both costly over-purchasing and performance-violating under-provisioning. We further validate our theoretical predictions through extensive experiments in real GPU production environments. Our results show that the predicted stability conditions are highly accurate, with deviations typically within 10%.

</details>


### [141] [Library learning with e-graphs on jazz harmony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04622)
*Zeng Ren,Maddy Bowers,Xinyi Guan,Martin Rohrmeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于库学习的计算模型，用于模拟人类对爵士乐和声模式的内化学习过程。该模型通过在由原始和声关系组成的程序空间中搜索，发现语料库的简洁生成解释，并结合e-graph上的演绎解析技术来高效导航程序与库的联合空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够获得高度结构化的音乐模式直觉理解，但这种内化过程通常需要多次反思和重新聆听才能完全实现。为了捕捉这种内化过程，研究者希望构建一个计算模型来模拟人类学习爵士乐和声模式的方式。

Method: 研究者提出了一个基于库学习的计算模型。给定一个和声进行语料库，模型首先为每首乐曲枚举可能的程序，然后在由原始和声关系组成的程序空间中搜索，联合学习一个和声模式库和重构后的程序。为了高效地导航程序和库的巨大联合空间，模型集成了演绎解析与e-graph上的库学习技术。

Result: 研究通过评估程序和库的直观性，以及与人类编写的和声推导的相似性，探索了该模型在捕捉人类音乐模式学习方面的能力。结果表明该模型能够有效地模拟人类对爵士乐和声模式的内化学习过程。

Conclusion: 该模型成功地捕捉了人类音乐模式学习的某些方面，为理解人类如何内化复杂的音乐结构提供了一个计算框架，并展示了库学习在音乐认知建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Humans can acquire a highly structured intuitive understanding of musical patterns, yet these patterns often require multiple iterations of reflection and re-listening to internalize fully. To capture such an internalization process, we present a computational model for the learning of jazz harmonic patterns based on library learning. Given a corpus of harmonic progressions, our model searches over a space of programs composed of primitive harmonic relations in order to discover concise generative explanations of the corpus. The model first enumerates possible programs for each piece, and then jointly learns a library of harmonic patterns and refactored programs. To efficiently navigate the vast joint space of programs and libraries, we integrate deductive parsing with library learning on e-graphs. We explore how well our model captures aspects of human musical pattern learning by evaluating the intuitiveness of both programs and libraries, as well as similarities to human-written harmonic derivations.

</details>


### [142] [Exact Dual Geometry of SOC-ICNN Value Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04722)
*Kang Liu,Jianchen Hu,Wei Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究二阶锥输入凸神经网络(SOC-ICNNs)的对偶几何性质，证明其一阶和局部二阶几何信息(支撑斜率、次微分、方向导数、局部Hessian)可直接从最优对偶变量中精确恢复，实现了白盒推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICNNs通常采用两阶段方法(先训练凸网络，后在输入上最小化)，缺乏对网络几何性质的深入理解。SOC-ICNNs可精确表示为二阶锥规划的价值函数，但其对偶视角的几何性质尚未被充分利用。

Method: 从对偶视角出发，建立SOC-ICNNs的一阶和局部二阶几何性质与最优对偶变量之间的显式联系。推导支撑斜率、次微分、方向导数和局部Hessian的解析公式，实现基于对偶乘子的几何信息直接读取机制。

Result: 证明了SOC-ICNNs的一阶和二阶几何信息可从最优对偶变量中精确恢复；通过数值实验验证了乘子读取的准确性、局部Hessian公式以及结构退化输入处的集值行为；提供了完整的白盒推理流程教程。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了SOC-ICNNs的对偶几何理论框架，为白盒推理提供了几何原语，突破了传统黑盒自动微分的局限。该方法使得基于凸分析的显式推理成为可能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Input Convex Neural Networks (ICNNs) are commonly used in a two-stage manner: one first trains a convex network and then minimizes it over its input in a downstream inference problem. Recent second-order-cone ICNNs (SOC-ICNNs) enrich ReLU-based ICNNs with quadratic and conic modules and admit an exact representation as value functions of second-order cone programs (SOCPs). This value-function structure enables an explicit convex-analytic treatment of SOC-ICNN inference. In this paper, we study the exact first-order and local second-order geometry of SOC-ICNNs from the dual viewpoint. We show that supporting slopes, subdifferentials, directional derivatives, and local Hessians can be recovered directly from optimal dual variables. These results provide the geometric primitives for white-box SOC-ICNN inference, going beyond black-box automatic differentiation. Numerical experiments validate the exact multiplier readout, the local Hessian formula, and the set-valued behavior at structurally degenerate inputs. We also provide a step-by-step tutorial showing how the readout mechanism instantiates a complete white-box inference loop. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SOC-ICNN-Theory-BEFC/.

</details>


### [143] [Discovering Sparse Counterfactual Factors via Latent Adjustment for Survey-based Community Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04460)
*Fatima Ashraf,Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Yufang Zhou,Yan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交通调查数据的稀疏反事实社区干预框架，通过潜在表示和最优传输方法，生成政策可行且可解释的干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通调查广泛应用于理解出行偏好和采用障碍，但现有的调查分析多为描述性或预测性，缺乏能够生成稀疏、政策可行的干预策略的方法。研究旨在解决如何通过可控的调查变量调整，将目标受访者群体转变为理想的参考群体这一关键问题。

Method: 将任务建模为政策可行的分布对齐问题：首先采用固定基非负潜在表示保持干预前后的可比性；利用Shapley引导的归因识别目标相关的潜在因子；通过最小化熵正则化最优传输差异学习可行的群体级调整，并引入加权ℓ2,1惩罚促进共享政策杠杆的稀疏性。

Result: 在真实交通调查数据集上的实验表明，该框架能够产生紧凑且可解释的政策可行干预策略，提供明确的调整幅度，改善人口层面的转化率，并保持干预的稀疏性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功填补了调查分析与政策干预之间的鸿沟，为交通领域提供了一种从调查数据生成可操作、可解释干预策略的有效工具，代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Transportation surveys are widely used to understand travel preferences and adoption barriers, yet most survey-based analyses remain descriptive or predictive and rarely provide sparse, policy-feasible intervention strategies.
