We study sparse counterfactual community intervention from survey responses, where the goal is to shift a target respondent group toward a desired reference group through controllable survey-variable adjustments. We formulate this task as a policy-feasible distributional alignment problem using a fixed-basis nonnegative latent representation that preserves pre/post comparability and provides a stable map from latent factors to original variables. To make latent movement actionable, target-relevant latent factors are identified through Shapley-guided attribution and transferred to controllable variables as intervention priorities. Feasible group-level adjustments are then learned by minimizing an entropy-regularized optimal-transport discrepancy between the post-intervention target distribution and the reference distribution, together with a weighted $\ell_{2,1}$ penalty that promotes shared policy-lever sparsity. Experiments on real-world transportation survey datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact and interpretable policy-feasible interventions with explicit adjustment magnitudes, improves population-level conversion, and preserves intervention sparsity. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/latent-group-alignment.git

</details>


### [144] [Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement for Incentivizing Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04747)
*Leon Witt,Togrul Abbasli,Kentaroh Toyoda,Wojciech Samek,Lucy Klinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需真实标签、公共测试集或分布知识的联邦学习贡献奖励机制，在类别报告和诚实多数假设下严格 truthful，解决了标签翻转攻击漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习贡献评估方法依赖真实标签、公共测试集或数据分布知识，且易受标签翻转攻击（如 Correlated Agreement 的漏洞），在去中心化/区块链场景中受限。

Method: 提出 Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement（KFCA）奖励机制：在类别报告设定和诚实多数假设下，无需任何先验知识，实现严格 truthful（激励诚实报告）的客户端贡献评估。

Result: 在联邦大语言模型适配器调优和真实 PCB 检测任务上验证，KFCA 实现高效的实时奖励计算，适用于去中心化和区块链激励设计。

Conclusion: KFCA 为联邦学习提供了一种理论上有保证、实践中高效的无知识依赖贡献评估方案，解决了标签翻转攻击问题，适合去中心化激励机制。

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement (KFCA) to reward client contributions in federated learning (FL) without relying on ground truth, a public test set, or distribution knowledge. Under categorical reports and an honest majority, KFCA is strictly truthful, addressing the label-flipping vulnerability of Correlated Agreement (CA). We evaluate KFCA on federated LLM adapter tuning and a real-world PCB inspection task, showing efficient real-time reward computation suitable for decentralized and blockchain-based incentive designs.

</details>


### [145] [Cognitive Twins: Investigating Personalized Thinking Model Building and Its Performance Enhancement with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04761)
*Wu-Yuin Hwang,Nur Alif Ilyasa,Muhammad Irfan Luthfi,Yuniar Indrihapsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了个性化思维模型（PTM），一种用于AI辅助教育的分层且可解释的学习者表征方法。该模型基于Marzano教育目标分类法构建认知孪生，在40名参与者的评估中取得了约75%的F1分数和良好的用户评价，验证了其可接受的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在AI辅助教育中实现对学习者思维模型的有效克隆并构建认知孪生，需要一个具有层级性且可解释的学习者表征方法，以系统性地组织和理解来自学习者的行为证据与思维特征。

Method: PTM基于Marzano教育目标新分类法，构建了一个包含行为实例、行为模式、认知常规、元认知倾向和自我系统价值观的五层结构。技术上采用结合大语言模型推理（Gemini 2.5 Pro）、句子嵌入、降维和共识聚类的流水线来处理学习者日志证据。

Result: 通过三种方法对40名参与者进行评估：1）自动评估显示经人工干预优化后F1分数为75.48%；2）用户Likert量表评分均值达4.30（5分制）；3）语义一致性验证显示主题连贯性从行为层的0.436提升至核心价值观层的0.626，证实了模型在跨层级的语义抽象能力。

Conclusion: PTM能够生成具有可接受保真度的输出，用户普遍认为其反映了自身思维，且模型展示了符合预期的跨层级语义抽象模式，证明了其作为学习者认知孪生方法的有效性和应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents the Personalized Thinking Model (PTM), a hierarchical and interpretable learner representation designed for AI supported education. PTM organizes evidence from learner journals into a five-layer structure covering behavioral instances, behavioral patterns, cognitive routines, metacognitive tendencies, and self-system values. PTM is grounded in Marzano's New Taxonomy of Educational Objectives and tries to clone learner's thinking model and build cognitive twin. It was constructed using a pipeline that combines large language model inference (Gemini 2.5 Pro), sentence embeddings, dimensionality reduction, and consensus clustering. This paper evaluates PTM fidelity through three methods applied to 40 participants in a seven-week study. First, automatic evaluation using atomic information point matching yielded an overall F1 score of 74.57% before human-in-the-loop (HITL) refinement and 75.48% after refinement. Second, user evaluation using a Likert scale produced mean ratings of 4.26 and 4.30 on a five-point scale for pre and post-HITL conditions respectively. Third, semantic alignment verification showed that topic coherence increased from 0.436 at the behavioral layer to 0.626 at the core value layer, while lexical overlap with journal vocabulary decreased from 0.114 to 0.007 across those same layers. These results suggest that the PTM produces outputs with acceptable fidelity, was generally perceived by users as reflecting their thinking, and showed a pattern consistent with semantic abstraction across layers.

</details>


### [146] [CRAFT: Counterfactual-to-Interactive Reinforcement Fine-Tuning for Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04470)
*Keyu Chen,Nanfei Ye,Yida Wang,Wenchao Sun,Danqi Zhao,Hao Cheng,Sifa Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT框架，通过结合反事实优势代理和交互关键事件的残差修正，解决了自动驾驶策略闭环微调中监督稀疏与偏差的问题，在Bench2Drive上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开环模仿学习在自动驾驶策略架构中取得进展，但闭环部署易受策略诱导的分布偏移影响。现有的后训练范式存在根本性权衡：闭环强化学习微调能提供执行动作的真实反馈，但受限于信息性事件的稀疏性；而反事实微调虽能提供候选未来的密集监督，却继承了来自不完美未来估计的偏差。

Method: 引入反事实到交互强化微调（CRAFT）框架，将闭环后训练建模为代理-残差优化。该方法使用组归一化的反事实优势作为真实闭环优势的密集代理，并通过交互关键事件的接地残差修正将代理与闭环世界对齐。采用非对称KL自蒸馏正则化在线策略向EMA教师靠拢以稳定适应过程。理论上将真实闭环策略梯度分解为同访问状态分布下的代理项和残差项。

Result: 在Bench2Drive基准测试中，CRAFT在分层规划、视觉-语言-动作和词汇评分三种架构上均取得最强的闭环性能提升。消融实验、缩放行为、稳定性分析和迁移结果进一步验证了密集反事实代理与接地残差修正的互补作用。

Conclusion: CRAFT成功解决了闭环策略微调中监督信号稀疏性与偏差之间的矛盾，通过代理-残差分解降低了残差方差并缓解了代理偏差，为自动驾驶策略的闭环后训练提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Open-loop imitation learning has advanced modern autonomous driving policy architectures, but closed-loop deployment remains vulnerable to policy-induced distribution shift. Existing post-training paradigms exhibit fundamental trade-offs: closed-loop RL fine-tuning provides grounded feedback from executed actions but is constrained by the sparsity of informative events, whereas counterfactual fine-tuning provides dense supervision over candidate futures but inherits bias from imperfect future estimates. We introduce Counterfactual-to-Interactive Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (CRAFT), an on-policy framework that formulates closed-loop post-training as proxy-residual optimization. CRAFT uses group-normalized counterfactual advantages as a dense proxy for real closed-loop advantages and aligns this proxy with the closed-loop world through grounded residual correction from interaction-critical events. To stabilize adaptation, CRAFT regularizes the online policy toward an EMA teacher via asymmetric KL self-distillation. Theoretically, CRAFT decomposes the real closed-loop policy gradient into proxy and residual terms under the same visited-state distribution, reducing residual variance with an aligned proxy while mitigating proxy bias through grounded residual approximation. Empirically, CRAFT achieves the strongest closed-loop gains on Bench2Drive across hierarchical planning, vision-language-action, and vocabulary-scoring architectures. Ablations, scaling behavior, stability analyses, and transfer results further validate the complementary roles of dense counterfactual proxy and grounded residual correction. Project page: https://currychen77.github.io/CRAFT.

</details>


### [147] [Quantile-Free Uncertainty Quantification in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04847)
*Soyoung park,Hwanjun Song,Sungsu Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QpiGNN框架，一种无需分位数输入和后处理校准的图神经网络不确定性量化方法，在19个基准测试中实现了平均22%更高的覆盖率和50%更窄的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在高风险领域的不确定性量化至关重要但极具挑战性。现有方法通常依赖强假设（如可交换性），这些假设在实践中很少满足，且可靠的不确定性量化通常需要昂贵的重采样或后处理校准。

Method: 提出Quantile-free Prediction Interval GNN (QpiGNN)框架，基于分位数回归但无需分位数输入。采用双头架构解耦预测和不确定性，通过仅标签监督的分位数自由联合损失函数进行训练，直接优化覆盖率和区间宽度。

Result: 在19个合成和真实世界基准测试中，QpiGNN相比基线方法平均覆盖率提高22%，区间宽度缩小50%。在温和假设下具有渐近覆盖率和近最优宽度的理论保证，同时对噪声和结构变化表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: QpiGNN提供了一种高效、鲁棒的图神经网络不确定性量化解决方案，避免了传统方法的强假设和高计算成本，在理论和实验上均证明了其有效性，为高风险领域的图学习应用提供了可靠的不确定性估计方法。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) in graph neural networks (GNNs) is crucial in high-stakes domains but remains a significant challenge. In graph settings, message passing often relies on strong assumptions such as exchangeability, which are rarely satisfied in practice. Moreover, achieving reliable UQ typically requires costly resampling or post-hoc calibration. To address these issues, we introduce Quantile-free Prediction Interval GNN (QpiGNN), a framework that builds on quantile regression (QR) to enable GNN-based UQ by directly optimizing coverage and interval width without requiring quantile inputs or post-processing. QpiGNN employs a dual-head architecture that decouples prediction and uncertainty, and is trained with label-only supervision through a quantile-free joint loss. This design allows efficient training and yields robust prediction intervals, with theoretical guarantees of asymptotic coverage and near-optimal width under mild assumptions. Experiments on 19 synthetic and real-world benchmarks show QpiGNN achieves average 22\% higher coverage and 50\% narrower intervals than baselines, while ensuring efficiency and robustness to noise and structural shifts.

</details>


### [148] [Automated Formal Proofs of Combinatorial Identities via Wilf-Zeilberger Guidance and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04472)
*Beibei Xiong,Hangyu Lv,Junqi Liu,Yisen Wang,Shaoshi Chen,Jianlin Wang,Zhengfeng Yang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了WZ-LLM神经符号框架，将Wilf-Zeilberger方法转化为Lean 4可执行证明草图，在组合恒等式证明任务上达到34%成功率，显著优于DeepSeek-V3等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的证明器在自动证明组合恒等式时面临重大挑战：长视界证明规划困难且无约束搜索会导致状态空间爆炸。现有的符号方法如WZ方法虽能机械化证明组合恒等式，但缺乏灵活性。因此需要结合符号方法的结构化优势与LLM的推理能力。

Method: 提出了WZ-LLM神经符号框架：1）将WZ证明计划转化为Lean 4中的可执行证明草图；2）使用基于LLM的证明器处理生成的机器可检查子目标；3）通过Lean内核验证的自举循环和专家验证迭代训练专用的WZ-Prover，随后进行基于DAPO的精炼。

Result: 在LCI-Test（100个经典组合恒等式）上达到34%的证明成功率，优于DeepSeek-V3和Goedel-Prover-V2等强基线；在CombiBench和PutnamBench-Comb上也取得一致提升。框架展现出双重优势：对超出WZ范围的恒等式有更好的直接证明能力，以及当WZ草图引导专用证明器时实现更高的端到端成功率。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法有效结合了符号方法的结构化证明规划和LLM的灵活推理能力，为组合恒等式的自动证明提供了可行路径，证明了领域特定知识与神经方法结合的价值。

Abstract: Automating formal proofs of combinatorial identities is challenging for LLM-based provers, as long-horizon proof planning is required and unconstrained search quickly explodes. Symbolic methods such as the Wilf-Zeilberger (WZ) method can achieve a mechanized proof of combinatorial identities by constructing special auxiliary functions and demonstrating that they satisfy specific recurrence relations. We propose WZ-LLM, a neuro-symbolic framework that turns WZ proof plans into executable proof sketches in Lean 4 and uses an LLM-based prover to discharge the resulting machine-checkable subgoals. We also train a dedicated WZ-Prover via a Lean-kernel-verified bootstrapping loop with expert-verified iteration, followed by DAPO-based refinement. Experiments show that WZ-LLM achieves a 34% proof success rate on LCI-Test (100 classic combinatorial identities), outperforming strong baselines such as DeepSeek-V3 and Goedel-Prover-V2, and delivering consistent gains on CombiBench and PutnamBench-Comb. These results indicate that our framework provides two complementary strengths: improved direct proving for identities beyond the scope of WZ, and substantially higher end-to-end success when WZ sketches guide a specialized prover.

</details>


### [149] [A Harmonic Mean Formulation of Average Reward Reinforcement Learning in SMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04880)
*Erel Shtossel,Alicia Vidler,Uri Shaham,Gal A. Kaminka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对半马尔可夫决策过程（SMDP）中平均奖励强化学习存在的问题，提出了修正调和平均算子，解决了非平稳奖励和持续时间分布下现有算法计算奖励率不正确的问题，并证明了其理论性质和实证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SMDP平均奖励强化学习算法通过优化奖励与持续时间的比率来逼近目标，但在奖励和持续时间非平稳（无限视界）的情况下，这种方法是不正确的。这激发了对能够正确处理非平稳分布的新方法的需求。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的修正调和平均算子（modified harmonic mean operator），该算子能够在奖励和持续时间非平稳的条件下正确计算奖励率。基于此算子，推导出了无模型学习算法，该算法适用于SMDP，且对随时间变化的非平稳奖励和持续时间分布具有鲁棒性。

Result: 理论上证明了修正调和平均算子的性质；实证表明，与现有算法相比，基于该算子的算法在处理非平稳奖励和持续时间分布时具有更好的效能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的修正调和平均算子为无限视界连续任务中的SMDP提供了一种理论上正确且实用的解决方案，解决了非平稳性带来的挑战，提升了算法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent research has revived and amplified interest in algorithms for undiscounted average reward reinforcement learning in infinite-horizon, non-episodic (continuing) tasks. Semi-Markov decision processes (SMDPs) are of particular interest. In SMDPs, discrete actions stochastically generate both rewards and durations, and the objective is to optimize the average reward rate. Existing algorithms approach this by optimizing the ratio of rewards to durations. However, when rewards and durations are non-stationary (in the infinite horizon), this can be incorrect. This paper presents a novel modified harmonic mean operator that correctly computes reward rates even under such conditions. This yields model-free learning algorithms that can work with SMDPs, while maintaining robustness to non-stationary reward and duration distributions over time. We prove theoretical properties of the modified harmonic mean operator, and empirically demonstrate its efficacy in comparison to existing algorithms.

</details>


### [150] [Geometry-Aware Neural Optimizer for Shape Optimization and Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04474)
*Guoze Sun,Tianya Miao,Haoyang Huang,Huaguan Chen,Han Wan,Rui Zhang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GANO框架，通过端到端可微分的方式统一几何表示、场预测和优化，实现了PDE系统中形状优化和反演的自动化与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDE系统的形状优化流程需要昂贵的正向模拟和专家干预；神经代理虽能加速但无法形成闭环；现有可微分方法受限于参数化约束或潜在空间优化不稳定，缺乏可解释性和部件级控制。

Method: 提出Geometry-Aware Neural Optimizer (GANO)，采用自解码器编码形状，通过去噪机制稳定潜在空间更新，使用几何注入代理提供可靠的梯度路径。支持通过零空间投影实现部件级控制，并采用无重网格投影加速几何处理。

Result: 在2D Helmholtz、2D翼型和3D车辆三个基准测试上达到最优精度；翼型升阻比提升达+55.9%，车辆阻力降低约7%。证明了去噪机制诱导隐式雅可比正则化，降低解码器敏感度，实现可控变形。

Conclusion: GANO成功实现了端到端可微分的形状优化框架，在保持稳定性和可控性的同时，显著提升了优化性能，为PDE系统的几何优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Geometry is central to PDE-governed systems, motivating shape optimization and inversion. Classical pipelines conduct costly forward simulation with geometry processing, requiring substantial expert effort. Neural surrogates accelerate forward analysis but do not close the loop because gradients from objectives to geometry are often unavailable. Existing differentiable methods either rely on restrictive parameterizations or unstable latent optimization driven by scalar objectives, limiting interpretability and part-wise control. To address these challenges, we propose Geometry-Aware Neural Optimizer (GANO), an end-to-end differentiable framework that unifies geometry representation, field-level prediction, and automated optimization/inversion in a single latent-space loop. GANO encodes shapes with an auto-decoder and stabilizes latent updates via a denoising mechanism, and a geometry-injected surrogate provides a reliable gradient pathway for geometry updates. Moreover, GANO supports part-wise control through null-space projection and uses remeshing-free projection to accelerate geometry processing. We further prove that denoising induces an implicit Jacobian regularization that reduces decoder sensitivity, yielding controlled deformations. Experiments on three benchmarks spanning 2D Helmholtz, 2D airfoil, and 3D vehicles show state-of-the-art accuracy and stable, controllable updates, achieving up to +55.9% lift-to-drag improvement for airfoils and ~7% drag reduction for vehicles.

</details>


### [151] [Delta-Based Neural Architecture Search: LLM Fine-Tuning via Code Diffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04903)
*Santosh Premi Adhikari,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Delta-Code Generation方法，利用微调LLM生成紧凑的统一差异（deltas）来改进基线架构，而非从头生成完整模型。在六个数据集上评估三个7B级LLM，有效率和准确率均大幅超越全生成基线，代码量减少75-85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM神经架构生成方法从零生成完整模型实现，计算成本高且代码冗长。作者旨在解决这一效率问题，提出一种更高效、简洁的架构生成替代方案。

Method: 提出Delta-Code Generation流水线：通过LoRA在LEMUR数据集精选架构上迭代微调LLM，生成统一差异（deltas）改进基线架构；采用MinHash-Jaccard新颖性过滤确保结构多样性。评估三个7B级LLM（DeepSeek-Coder-7B、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、Mistral-7B），使用22周期协议（每LLM 1,100个候选）。

Result: 三个LLM均大幅超越全生成基线（50.6%有效率，42.3%平均首轮准确率）：DeepSeek-Coder达75.3%有效率/65.8%平均准确率；Qwen2.5-Coder为72.1%/64.6%；Mistral为66.6%/66.1%。CIFAR-10最佳首轮准确率达85.5%（Mistral）、85.2%（DeepSeek）、80.6%（Qwen），远超全生成的63.98%和Gu等人方法的71.5%。输出长度30-50行 vs 全生成200+行（减少75-85%）。50轮实验证实1轮代理保持排名（Mistral Spearman ρ=0.926）。

Conclusion: Delta生成是一种token高效、多领域、LLM无关的全模型合成替代方案，为LLM驱动的神经架构搜索提供了更实用的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong potential for neural architecture generation, yet existing approaches produce complete model implementations from scratch -- computationally expensive and yielding verbose code. We propose Delta-Code Generation, where fine-tuned LLMs generate compact unified diffs (deltas) to refine baseline architectures rather than synthesizing entire models. Our pipeline iteratively fine-tunes the LLM via LoRA on curated architectures from the LEMUR dataset, with MinHash-Jaccard novelty filtering for structural diversity. We evaluate three 7B-class LLMs -- DeepSeek-Coder-7B, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B, and Mistral-7B -- across six datasets (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, MNIST, SVHN, ImageNette, CelebA) using a 22-cycle protocol (1,100 candidates per LLM). All three substantially surpass the full-generation baseline (50.6% valid rate, 42.3% mean first-epoch accuracy): DeepSeek-Coder reaches 75.3% valid rate and 65.8% mean accuracy; Qwen2.5-Coder 72.1%/64.6%; Mistral 66.6%/66.1%. On CIFAR-10, best first-epoch accuracies reach 85.5% (Mistral), 85.2% (DeepSeek), 80.6% (Qwen) -- well above 63.98% full generation and 71.5% for the concurrent approach of Gu et al. Output lengths are 30-50 lines versus 200+ for full generation (75-85% reduction). A 50-epoch study confirms the 1-epoch proxy preserves rankings (Mistral: Spearman $ρ$ = 0.926). Delta-based generation is a token-efficient, multi-domain, LLM-agnostic alternative to full-model synthesis for LLM-driven NAS.

</details>


### [152] [Data-dependent Exploration for Online Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04477)
*Zhen-Yu Zhang,Yuting Tang,Jiandong Zhang,Lanjihong Ma,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DEPO方法，通过利用历史数据构建不确定性奖励来解决在线RLHF中的探索问题，在理论和实验上均证明了其能提高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）面临探索的核心挑战：现有基于策略期望的探索策略难以从有限的历史偏好数据中可靠估计，导致策略过早忽视可能包含高价值行为的未探索区域，影响样本效率。

Method: 提出偏好优化的数据依赖探索方法（DEPO），利用历史数据为高不确定性区域构建额外的不确定性奖励，从而鼓励模型探索潜在的高价值数据。该方法简单且可扩展。

Result: 理论上为算法提供了数据依赖的遗憾界，证明其能适应学习任务的难度，实践中比最坏情况界更紧；实验上，该方法在各项基准测试中持续优于强基线，展示了更高的样本效率。

Conclusion: DEPO通过数据依赖的不确定性奖励有效解决了在线RLHF中的探索问题，理论保证与实践表现均验证了其优越性，为LLM的对齐训练提供了一种高效的新范式。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a promising paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) by continuously collecting new preference feedback during training. A foundational challenge in this setting is exploration, which requires algorithms that enable the LLMs to generate informative comparisons that improve sample-efficiency in online RLHF. Existing exploration strategies often derive bonuses via on-policy expectations, which are difficult to estimate reliably from the limited historical preference data available during training; as a result, the policy can prematurely down-weight under-explored regions that may contain high-value behaviors. In this paper, we propose data-dependent exploration for preference optimization (DEPO), a simple and scalable method that leverages historical data to construct an extra uncertainty bonus for high-uncertainty regions, encouraging exploration toward potentially high-value data. Theoretically, we provide a data-dependent regret bound for the proposed algorithm, showing that it adapts to the hardness of the learning task itself and can be tighter than worst-case bounds in practice. Empirically, the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines across benchmarks, demonstrating improved sample efficiency.

</details>


### [153] [Towards General Preference Alignment: Diffusion Models at Nash Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04494)
*Jiaming Hu,Jiamu Bai,Haoyu Wang,Debarghya Mukherjee,Ioannis Ch. Paschalidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Diff.-NPO方法，从博弈论角度优化扩散模型对齐，通过自博弈实现自我提升，在文本生成图像任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于偏好的扩散模型对齐方法（如DPO）依赖于奖励诱导的偏好信号，并假设人类偏好可以用Bradley-Terry模型充分建模，但这可能无法捕捉人类偏好的完整复杂性。

Method: 从博弈论角度重新构建扩散模型对齐问题，提出Diffusion Nash Preference Optimization (Diff.-NPO)框架，鼓励当前策略与自身对抗博弈，通过自我博弈实现自我改进和更好的对齐。

Result: 在文本生成图像任务上通过多种指标验证了Diff.-NPO的有效性，该方法一致性地优于现有的基于偏好的扩散模型对齐方法。

Conclusion: Diff.-NPO提供了一种直观的通用偏好框架用于扩散模型对齐，成功避免了传统方法对BT模型的依赖，通过博弈论方法实现了更好的对齐性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has been popular for aligning text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models with human preferences. As a mainstream branch of RLHF, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a computationally efficient alternative that avoids explicit reward modeling and has been widely adopted in diffusion alignment. However, existing preference-based methods for diffusion alignment still rely on reward-induced preference signals and typically assume that human preferences can be adequately modeled by the Bradley--Terry (BT) model, which may fail to capture the full complexity of human preferences. In this paper, we formulate diffusion alignment from a game-theoretic perspective. We propose Diffusion Nash Preference Optimization (Diff.-NPO), an intuitive general preference framework for diffusion alignment. Diff.-NPO encourages the current policy to play against itself to achieve self improvement and lead to a better alignment. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of Diff.-NPO on the text-to-image generation task via various metrics. Diff.-NPO consistently outperforms existing preference-based diffusion alignment methods.

</details>


### [154] [When Does Gene Regulatory Network Inference Break? A Controlled Diagnostic Study of Causal and Correlational Methods on Single-Cell Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04930)
*Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana,Ruben Sanchez-Corcuera,Unai Zulaika,Aritz Bilbao-Jayo,Aitor Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了受控诊断框架来隔离七种生物病理因素，通过6120次实验揭示了因果方法在基因调控网络推断中失败的根本原因：特定病理（特别是dropout和潜在混杂因子）选择性抵消了因果方法的优势，而现有基准测试因多重病理交织而掩盖了这一真相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管因果方法在理论上具有优势，但在单细胞RNA-seq数据的基因调控网络推断中，因果方法在现实基准测试中始终无法超越或匹敌基于相关性的基线方法，这一持续存在的谜题使人们对因果方法在此任务中的价值产生怀疑。现有基准测试在真实或半真实数据上进行评估，多种病理因素同时出现，混淆了失败模式，掩盖了不同推断方法在特定条件下表现优劣的具体情况。

Method: 作者引入了一个受控诊断框架，隔离了七种生物动机的病理因素：dropout、潜在混杂因子、细胞类型混合、反馈回路、网络密度、样本量和伪时间漂移。该框架测量了跨越三种推断范式的六种代表性方法随着每种病理强度增加而性能下降的情况。此外，作者还引入了误差类型分解方法，并进行了三种最具影响力病理因素的交互扫描分析。

Result: 在6120次受控实验中，因果方法在干净且结构有利的条件下确实占据优势，但特定病理因素（尤其是dropout和潜在混杂因子）选择性抵消了这些优势。具有相似总体准确率的方法会犯性质不同的错误。三种最主要病理因素的联合效应呈现亚相加性，同时暴露了单因素分析无法发现的密度条件交叉现象。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于不同方法在基因调控网络推断中何时及为何成功或失败的细致理解，为方法开发提供了可操作的见解，为实践者提供了实用指导。因果方法并非无效，而是其优势被特定病理因素所掩盖，这解释了现有基准测试结果的矛盾性。

Abstract: Despite theoretical advantages, causal methods for Gene Regulatory Network (GRN) inference from single-cell RNA-seq data consistently fail to match or outperform correlation-based baselines in many realistic benchmarks, a persistent puzzle which casts doubt on the value of causality for this task. We argue that existing benchmarks are insufficiently controlled to answer this question because they evaluate on real or semi-real data where multiple pathologies co-occur, confounding failure modes, and obscuring the specific conditions under which different inference methods excel or fail. To address this gap, we introduce a controlled diagnostic framework that isolates seven biologically motivated pathologies (dropout, latent confounders, cell-type mixing, feedback loops, network density, sample size, and pseudotime drift) and measure how six representative methods spanning three inference paradigms degrade as each pathology intensifies. Across 6,120 controlled experiments, we find that causal methods genuinely dominate in clean and structurally favorable regimes, but specific pathologies (notably dropout and latent confounders) selectively neutralize their advantages. We further introduce an error-type decomposition that reveals methods with similar aggregate accuracy commit qualitatively different errors. To probe whether single-pathology effects persist when multiple stressors co-occur, we perform an interaction sweep over the three most impactful pathologies and find that their joint effects are sub-additive, while also exposing density-conditional cross-overs invisible to single-dial analysis. Our findings offer a nuanced understanding of when and why different methods succeed or fail for GRN inference, providing actionable insights for method development and practical guidance for practitioners.

</details>


### [155] [Quadrature-TreeSHAP: Depth-Independent TreeSHAP and Shapley Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04497)
*Ron Wettenstein,Rory Mitchell,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Quadrature-TreeSHAP方法，通过高斯-勒让德求积重构路径依赖TreeSHAP，实现数值稳定、支持任意阶Shapley交互值，且对树深度不敏感。在XGBoost基准测试中，CPU上比TreeSHAP快1.06-10.59倍，GPU上比GPUTreeSHAP快1.84-6.95倍，高阶交互值计算提速高达1200倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有树集成模型的Shapley值解释方法存在权衡：深度依赖的运行时间、数值稳定性问题以及对高阶交互支持不足。Path-Dependent SHAP作为最广泛使用的变体，仍无法同时解决这些挑战。

Method: 基于加权Banzhaf交互多项式，将Banzhaf交互值表示为特征参与概率p下的期望。通过对p在[0,1]区间积分恢复Shapley值和任意阶交互值，使用高斯-勒让德求积进行数值积分。实践中仅需8个固定求积点即可达到机器精度，该固定点形式消除了深度依赖性并支持高效SIMD执行。实现了CPU和GPU版本并集成到XGBoost中。

Result: 在12个XGBoost基准测试上：CPU上Shapley值计算比TreeSHAP快1.06-10.59倍；GPU上比GPUTreeSHAP快1.84-6.95倍；CPU上成对交互值计算快3.80-58.11倍；高阶交互值相比TreeSHAP-IQ提速高达1200倍。8点求积实现比TreeSHAP更高的数值稳定性。

Conclusion: Quadrature-TreeSHAP成功解决了现有方法的权衡问题，同时实现了数值稳定性、任意阶交互支持和对树深度不敏感性，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了计算效率，为树集成模型的解释性提供了更优工具。

Abstract: Shapley values are a standard tool for explaining predictions of tree ensembles, with Path-Dependent SHAP being the most widely used variant. Despite substantial progress, existing methods still exhibit trade-offs between depth-dependent runtime, numerical stability, and support for higher-order interactions. To address these challenges, we introduce Quadrature-TreeSHAP, a quadrature-based reformulation of Path-Dependent TreeSHAP that is numerically stable, naturally extends to any-order Shapley interaction values and is practically insensitive to tree depth. Our implementation supports both CPU and GPU and is integrated into XGBoost.
  Our method is based on a weighted-Banzhaf interaction polynomial, which expresses Banzhaf interaction values as expectations under a feature participation probability $p$. Shapley values and any-order interaction values are then recovered by integrating these polynomials over $p$ from 0 to 1. We evaluate these integrals using Gauss-Legendre quadrature, and show that, in practice, only 8 fixed quadrature points are sufficient to reach machine precision. In fact, Quadrature-TreeSHAP with 8 fixed points achieves greater numerical stability than TreeSHAP. This fixed-point formulation removes depth dependence from the inner computation and enables efficient SIMD execution.
  We confirm these advantages empirically. On 12 XGBoost benchmarks, Quadrature-TreeSHAP computes Shapley values 1.06x-10.59x faster than TreeSHAP on CPU and 1.84x-6.95x faster than GPUTreeSHAP on GPU. Shapley pairwise interactions are 3.80x-58.11x faster on CPU, with higher-order interactions achieving speedups of up to 1200x compared to TreeSHAP-IQ.

</details>


### [156] [EP-GRPO: Entropy-Progress Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization with Implicit Process Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04960)
*Song Yu,Li Li,Wenwen Zhao,Zhisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EP-GRPO框架，通过熵门控调制、隐式过程信号和累积熵映射三种机制，解决了GRPO在强化学习中的信用分配失败问题，在数学推理基准测试上实现了更优的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO存在三种信用分配失败：统一token粒度忽略异质信息价值、统一极性惩罚正确步骤并奖励错误步骤、零方差坍缩消除结果驱动梯度。作者系统量化了这些失败，发现token信息高度不均匀、步骤级极性不匹配广泛存在以及大量训练浪费。

Method: 提出熵-进度对齐GRPO (EP-GRPO)框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 熵门控调制优先处理高熵决策枢纽；(2) 基于锚定结果优势的策略发散隐式过程信号，提供方向性token级反馈且无需外部奖励模型；(3) 累积熵映射实现进度对齐的优势归一化，在零奖励方差下自然维持梯度流。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试上的大量实验表明，EP-GRPO相比GRPO及其变体实现了更优的准确性和效率。

Conclusion: EP-GRPO通过挖掘模型内在信息流实现密集自监督指导，有效解决了GRPO的信用分配失败问题，为LLM推理提供了更高效的强化学习方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has advanced LLM reasoning. However, GRPO suffers from three credit assignment failures: uniform token-level granularity that ignores heterogeneous informational value, uniform polarity that penalizes correct steps and rewards incorrect ones, and zero-variance collapse that erases outcome-driven gradients. We systematically quantify these failures, revealing highly non-uniform token informativeness, widespread step-level polarity misalignment, and substantial training waste.
  To address these limitations, we propose Entropy-Progress Aligned GRPO (EP-GRPO), a framework that mines the model's intrinsic information flow for dense, self-supervised guidance. EP-GRPO integrates entropy-gated modulation to prioritize high entropy decision pivots, implicit process signals from policy divergence anchored to outcome advantages for directional token-level feedback without external reward models, and cumulative entropy mapping that enables progress-aligned advantage normalization, naturally maintaining gradient flow under zero reward variance.
  Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EP-GRPO achieves superior accuracy and efficiency compared to GRPO and its variants. The code will be available.

</details>


### [157] [Gradient Scaling Effects in Adaptive Spectral PINNs for Stiff Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04502)
*Isabela M. Yepes,Pavlos Protopapas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了PINNs中初始条件(IC)嵌入方式对刚性动力学系统训练的影响，发现IC门控函数的选择会诱导梯度缩放，从而显著影响优化条件。在中等刚度下指数门控表现较好，而在高刚度下线性门控更优，证明了IC嵌入并非中性设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在训练刚性及振荡动力学系统时面临优化条件差的问题。虽然已有研究关注谱参数化等表示层面的改进，但自适应谱PINNs中初始条件(IC)嵌入的优化影响尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 使用非线性刚性弹簧摆ODE作为受控基准测试，比较指数和线性IC门控函数与固定及自适应傅里叶谱主干的组合效果。通过Wilcoxon符号秩检验配合Holm校正进行统计验证。

Result: 在中等刚度(k=20)下，指数门控通常产生较低误差但跨随机种子表现异质；在高刚度(k=60)下，线性门控更优，且在更大k值时观察到额外的反转。该趋势在相对L²误差和最大逐点误差上均成立。

Conclusion: IC嵌入在PINNs中不是中性的设计选择：诱导的梯度缩放显著影响刚性区域的优化条件，且基线模型和自适应谱模型呈现不同的敏感性模式。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) often struggle to train reliably on stiff and oscillatory dynamical systems due to poor optimization conditioning. While prior work has emphasized representational remedies such as spectral parameterizations, the optimization implications of initial-condition (IC) embeddings in adaptive spectral PINNs have not been well characterized. In this work, we show that the choice of IC gating function induces explicit time-dependent gradient scaling, which interacts with spectral representations during training. Using a nonlinear stiff spring-pendulum ODE as a controlled benchmark, we compare exponential and linear IC gates in combination with fixed and adaptive Fourier spectral trunks. We observe stiffness-dependent changes in relative dominance for adaptive PINNs: at moderate stiffness ($k=20$), exponential gating often yields lower error but exhibits heterogeneous behavior across random seeds, whereas at higher stiffness ($k=60$), linear gating becomes preferable, with additional reversals observed at larger $k$. These trends hold for both relative $L^2$ error and maximum pointwise error and are confirmed by paired Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. Overall, our results demonstrate that IC embeddings are not a neutral design choice in PINNs: the induced gradient scaling materially shapes optimization conditioning in stiff regimes, with distinct sensitivity patterns in baseline and adaptive spectral models.

</details>


### [158] [Reliable Modeling of Distribution Shifts via Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04965)
*Philip Naumann,Jacob Kauffmann,Klaus-Robert Müller,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport (ReshapeOT)，通过利用观测样本位移信息将地面度量重塑为马氏距离，从而生成更符合真实位移几何的最优传输路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优传输方法直接在输入空间比较分布，若地面度量设计不当，优化器可能违背数据变化的真实几何结构。因此需要一种能够利用观测位移知识来重塑地面度量的方法，以确保传输路径符合实际变化模式。

Method: ReshapeOT通过计算位移的二阶矩估计马氏距离，用该距离替代欧氏度量作为新的地面度量。该方法计算轻量，可无缝集成到任何基于代价矩阵的OT求解器中，并支持核化以获得更大灵活性。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据上的实验表明，ReshapeOT在传输可靠性方面取得了显著提升。作者还在两个实际应用场景中验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ReshapeOT提供了一种有效的方式，通过整合观测位移信息来重塑地面度量，从而获得更可靠的最优传输方案，为建模分布迁移问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) is a central framework for modeling distribution shifts. Because OT compares distributions directly in input space, a well-designed ground metric between observations is essential to ensure that the optimizer does not violate the true geometry of change. We propose Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport (ReshapeOT), a method that reshapes the ground metric by integrating observed sample displacements as an additional source of knowledge. Technically, ReshapeOT replaces the Euclidean metric with a Mahalanobis distance estimated from displacement second moments. This effectively carves expressways through the input space, inviting transport solutions that better align with observed displacements. Our method is computationally lightweight, integrates seamlessly into any OT solver that operates on a cost matrix, and can be kernelized for further flexibility. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that ReshapeOT achieves substantial gains in transport reliability. We further demonstrate our method's usefulness in two practical use cases.

</details>


### [159] [FL-Sailer: Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning for Scalable Single-Cell Epigenetic Data Analysis via Adaptive Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04519)
*Guangyi Zhang,Yi Dai,Yiyun He,Junhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FL-Sailer，首个针对单细胞ATAC-seq数据的联邦学习框架，通过自适应特征采样和解耦技术信号的创新方法，解决了超高维度、极端稀疏性和跨机构异质性三大挑战，实现了隐私保护下的多机构协作分析，性能甚至超越中心化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞ATAC-seq技术能够高分辨率绘制染色质可及性图谱，但隐私法规和数据规模限制阻碍了多机构数据共享。虽然联邦学习提供了隐私保护方案，但在scATAC-seq分析中面临三大根本障碍：超高维度、极端稀疏性和严重的跨机构异质性，亟需针对性的解决方案。

Method: FL-Sailer框架整合了两项关键创新：（1）自适应杠杆分数采样，在减少80%维度的同时选择具有生物学可解释性的特征；（2）不变性VAE架构，通过互信息最小化解耦生物学信号与技术混杂因素。框架提供了收敛性保证，证明能以有界误差收敛到原始高维问题的近似解。

Result: 在合成和真实表观基因组数据集上的大量实验表明：FL-Sailer不仅实现了此前不可行的多机构协作，还通过自适应采样作为隐式正则化器抑制技术噪声，性能超越了中心化方法。这证明了联邦学习在处理领域特定挑战后，可以成为协作表观基因组研究的优越范式。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了针对领域特定挑战定制的联邦学习可以成为协作表观基因组研究的优越范式。FL-Sailer框架成功克服了scATAC-seq数据的关键技术障碍，为隐私保护下的多机构科研协作提供了有效解决方案，推动了联邦学习在生物信息学领域的应用发展。

Abstract: Single-cell ATAC-seq (scATAC-seq) enables high-resolution mapping of chromatin accessibility, yet privacy regulations and data size constraints hinder multi-institutional sharing. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative, but faces three fundamental barriers in scATAC-seq analysis: ultra-high dimensionality, extreme sparsity, and severe cross-institutional heterogeneity. We propose FL-Sailer, the first FL framework designed for scATAC-seq data. FL-Sailer integrates two key innovations: (i) adaptive leverage score sampling, which selects biologically interpretable features while reducing dimensionality by 80%, and (ii) an invariant VAE architecture, which disentangles biological signals from technical confounders via mutual information minimization. We provide a convergence guarantee, showing that FL-Sailer converges to an approximate solution of the original high-dimensional problem with bounded error. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real epigenomic datasets demonstrate that FL-Sailer not only enables previously infeasible multi-institutional collaborations but also surpasses centralized methods by leveraging adaptive sampling as an implicit regularizer to suppress technical noise. Our work establishes that federated learning, when tailored to domain-specific challenges, can become a superior paradigm for collaborative epigenomic research.

</details>


### [160] [Skill Neologisms: Towards Skill-based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04970)
*Antonin Berthon,Nicolas Astorga,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“技能新词”（skill neologisms），即通过优化加入词表的软token来扩展LLM新技能，无需更新权重即可实现持续学习与零样本组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展LLM新技能的方法存在局限：微调及其参数高效变体面临灾难性遗忘风险，而基于上下文的方法表达能力有限且受上下文窗口约束。因此亟需一种可扩展、选择性扩展模型能力的新方法。

Method: 引入“技能新词”（skill neologisms）——将软token集成到模型词表中，针对特定技能进行优化以提升能力，无需更新模型权重。首先观察到现成预训练LLM中已存在与程序性知识相关的token，进而学习技能新词并验证其可组合性。

Result: 实验表明，技能新词能有效提升模型在特定技能上的表现，且可与分布外技能组合使用；独立训练的技能新词能够实现零样本组合，表明该方法具备良好的组合性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 技能新词为基于技能的持续学习提供了一条可扩展的可行路径，能够在不更新权重的情况下选择性扩展模型能力，并支持不同技能之间的灵活组合。

Abstract: Modern LLMs show mastery over an ever-growing range of skills, as well as the ability to compose them flexibly. However, extending model capabilities to new skills in a scalable manner is an open-problem: fine-tuning and parameter-efficient variants risk catastrophic forgetting, while context-based approaches have limited expressiveness and are constrained by the model's effective context. We explore skill neologisms--i.e., soft tokens integrated in the model's vocabulary and optimized to improve capabilities over a specific skill--as a way to selectively extend model capabilities to new skills without weight updates. We first observe that off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs already demonstrate tokens associated with procedural knowledge. We then show that skill neologisms can be learned to improve model capabilities on specific skills while being composable with out-of-distribution skills, and that independently trained skill neologisms can be composed zero-shot. These results suggest that skill neologisms may provide a scalable path towards skill-based continual learning.

</details>


### [161] [Why Geometric Continuity Emerges in Deep Neural Networks: Residual Connections and Rotational Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04971)
*Kyungwon Jeong,Won-Gi Paeng,Honggyo Suh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度网络权重矩阵几何连续性的成因：残差连接通过梯度对齐维持结构，对称性破缺的非线性层防止坐标漂移。激活层与归一化分工明确，Transformer中连续性呈现投影特异性分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中相邻层权重矩阵的主奇异向量方向相似（几何连续性）这一现象已被广泛观察，但其产生原因尚不明确。作者旨在通过实验探究该性质的起源与机制。

Method: 在简单MLP和小型Transformer上进行受控实验，解耦分析残差连接、非线性激活和归一化的作用。通过对比旋转保持但非线性的激活函数，分离对称性破缺的作用。

Result: 发现两个关键机制：(1)残差连接创造跨层梯度一致性，对齐各层权重更新；(2)对称性破缺的非线性约束所有层到共享坐标系，防止旋转漂移。激活层集中连续性于主奇异方向，归一化将其分布到多方向。Transformer中Q、K、Gate、Up形成输入空间连续性，O、Down形成输出空间连续性，V因无非线性相邻层而连续性低。

Conclusion: 权重连续性的核心驱动因素是对称性破缺而非非线性本身。残差连接与对称性破缺的非线性协同作用，共同维持深层网络的几何连续性，这一发现在MLP和Transformer中均得到验证。

Abstract: Weight matrices in deep networks exhibit geometric continuity -- principal singular vectors of adjacent layers point in similar directions. While this property has been widely observed, its origin remains unexplained. Through experiments on toy MLPs and small transformers, we identify two mechanisms: residual connections create cross-layer gradient coherence that aligns weight updates across layers, and symmetry-breaking nonlinearities constrain all layers to a shared coordinate frame, preventing the rotation drift that would otherwise destabilize weight structure. Crucially, a nonlinear but rotation-preserving activation fails to retain continuity, isolating symmetry breaking -- not nonlinearity itself -- as the active ingredient. Activation and normalization play distinct roles: activation concentrates continuity in the leading singular direction, while normalization distributes it across multiple directions. In transformers, continuity is projection-specific: Q, K, Gate, and Up (which read from the residual stream) develop input-space ($\mathbf{v}_1$) continuity; O and Down (which write to it) develop output-space ($\mathbf{u}_1$) continuity; V alone, lacking an adjacent nonlinearity, develops only low continuity.

</details>


### [162] [From Video-to-PDE: Data-Driven Discovery of Nonlinear Dye Plume Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04535)
*Cesar Acosta-Minoli,Sayantan Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从视频数据直接推断连续介质偏微分方程（PDE）的完整流程，成功从墨水羽流的灰度视频中识别出一个精简、预测性强且结构可解释的输运模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从视频推断连续介质模型面临两大核心障碍：记录场是未经校准的图像强度而非物理状态量，以及对噪声帧进行数值微分会导致计算不稳定。

Method: 提出了一套视频到PDE的流程：首先将灰度视频转化为归一化标量场，通过强度加权质心分离整体漂移与内在扩散；采用弱形式稀疏回归在紧凑的梯度库中识别有效输运定律；利用物理信息神经网络精炼系数并通过前向推演重校准；最后使用按时间顺序的分块Bootstrap量化不确定性。

Result: 筛选出的简化模型为 $u_t+\mathbf v(t)\!\cdot\!\nabla u = 9.005\,|\nabla u|^{2}+0.666\,Δu$，该模型在留出帧上优于标准的对流-扩散基线，拉普拉斯系数保持正值，并可通过Cole-Hopf变换简化为线性对流-扩散方程。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当将模型发现、校准和不确定性分析视为独立阶段时，未经校准的视觉数据可以产生紧凑、具有预测能力和结构可解释性的连续介质模型。

Abstract: Inferring continuum models directly from video is hampered by two facts: the recorded field is uncalibrated image intensity rather than a physical state, and direct numerical differentiation of noisy frames is unstable. We develop a video-to-PDE pipeline that converts grayscale recordings of an ink plume into a normalised scalar field $u(x,y,t)$, isolates a bulk drift $\mathbf{v}(t)$ from intrinsic spreading via the intensity-weighted centroid, and identifies an effective transport law by weak-form sparse regression. Conditioning, threshold-sweep and random-centre diagnostics show that overcomplete libraries are strongly collinear; the search is therefore restricted to compact gradient-based libraries. Coefficients are refined by an inverse physics-informed network and recalibrated against forward rollouts, with a chronological block bootstrap quantifying uncertainty. The selected reduced model $u_t+\mathbf v(t)\!\cdot\!\nabla u = 9.005\,|\nabla u|^{2}+0.666\,Δu$ outperforms advection--diffusion baselines on held-out frames, retains a positive Laplacian coefficient, and admits a Cole--Hopf reduction to a linear advection--diffusion equation. The framework demonstrates that uncalibrated visual data can yield compact, predictive and structurally interpretable continuum models when discovery, calibration and uncertainty are treated as distinct stages.

</details>


### [163] [Federated Learning for Early Prediction of EV Charging Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04993)
*Vasilis Perifanis,Foteini Nikolaidou,Nikolaos Pavlidis,Panagiotis Thomakos,Andreas Sendros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对电动汽车充电需求的早期预测问题，利用充电开始阶段的信息预测总充电量。通过构建ACN数据集并采用联邦学习框架，在保护数据隐私的前提下实现了接近集中式模型的预测性能，为EV网络运营商提供了可操作的实时决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的电动汽车充电需求预测对电网稳定性、基础设施规划和实时充电优化至关重要。现有的预测方法往往无法在充电会话进行过程中提供及时的需求估计，而网络运营商需要在充电早期阶段就获得可靠的需求预测，以便在会话仍在进行时做出可操作的决策。这一需求推动了开发能够在充电开始几分钟内预测总充电量的早期预测方法。

Method: 研究团队从Adaptive Charging Network (ACN)构建了会话级别数据集，将会话元数据与早期时间窗口的充电测量数据相结合，提取了捕捉用户意图、时间模式和初始充电行为的表格特征。研究聚焦于单个运营站点Caltech，通过站点级客户端分区建模站内异构性，并在联邦学习(FL)环境下评估多种模型家族，实现了数据保留在站点内的隐私保护训练。

Result: 联邦学习模型能够接近集中式预测性能，同时保持数据在站点内部，实现了跨分布式充电基础设施的隐私增强训练。研究证明仅需充电会话早期阶段的少量数据即可获得可靠的需求估计，联邦学习为EV充电网络提供了可扩展且具有隐私意识的分析路径。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了在充电会话早期阶段（仅使用插入时信息和最初几分钟的充电数据）即可获得可靠的充电需求估计。联邦学习方法为实现跨分布式充电基础设施的可扩展且隐私保护的预测分析提供了实用的解决方案，为EV网络运营商的实时决策提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of electric vehicle (EV) charging demand is critical for grid stability, infrastructure planning, and real-time charging optimization. In this work, we study the problem of early prediction of charging demand, where the total energy of a session is estimated using only information available at plug-in time and during the first minutes of charging. This enables actionable decisions while the session is still in progress, which is of direct importance for EV network operators. We construct a session-level dataset from the Adaptive Charging Network (ACN), combining session metadata with early-window charging measurements, and derive tabular features capturing user intent, temporal patterns, and initial charging behavior. We focus on a single operational depot, Caltech, and model intra-depot heterogeneity through station-level client partitions while evaluating multiple model families in a federated learning (FL) setting. Our results show that federated models can approach centralized predictive performance while keeping data in-depot, enabling privacy-enhanced training across distributed charging infrastructures. Overall, we demonstrate that reliable demand estimates can be obtained early in the session with minimal data, and that FL provides a practical pathway toward scalable and privacy-aware analytics for EV charging networks. Code is available at https://github.com/Indigma-Innovations/federated-learning-ev-charging-demand.

</details>


### [164] [Power Distribution Bridges Sampling, Self-Reward RL, and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04542)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了幂次分布是连接采样、自奖励KL正则化强化学习和自蒸馏的桥梁，并提出了幂次自蒸馏方法，在降低推理成本的同时达到或超越幂次采样的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究质疑强化学习是否是大语言模型推理能力的主要原因，而蒸馏和推理时采样（如幂次采样）已成为提升模型性能的有效方法。然而，强化学习、蒸馏和采样之间的关系尚不明确，本文旨在揭示它们之间的内在联系。

Method: 本文聚焦于幂次采样目标分布——幂次分布，从两个角度进行分析：(1)采样视角：研究低成本局部近似能否重现序列级幂次分布；(2)强化学习视角：证明幂次分布是KL正则化强化学习在使用模型序列级对数概率作为奖励时的闭式优化器。基于此提出幂次自蒸馏方法，将幂次采样成本分摊到教师样本的有监督训练中。

Result: 理论分析表明：(1)局部近似无法在无后缀信息时重现序列级幂次分布；(2)幂次分布可统一采样、自奖励RL和自蒸馏；(3)幂次自蒸馏可实现自奖励锐化，下游真实奖励的提升取决于幂次分布下真实奖励与自奖励的协方差。推理任务实验证实：幂次采样提升自奖励，真实奖励增益取决于与自奖励的对齐程度，幂次自蒸馏以更低推理成本达到或超越幂次采样性能。

Conclusion: 幂次分布是统一采样、自奖励强化学习和自蒸馏的关键桥梁；幂次自蒸馏作为一种离线蒸馏替代方案，通过在训练阶段分摊采样成本，实现了与幂次采样相当甚至更优的性能，同时大幅降低了推理时计算成本。

Abstract: Recent analyses question whether reinforcement learning (RL) is responsible for strong reasoning in large language models (LLMs). At the same time, distillation and inference-time sampling, including power sampling, have emerged as effective ways to improve LLM performance. However, the relationship among RL, distillation, and sampling remains unclear. In this study, we focus on the power distribution, the target distribution of power sampling, and show that the power distribution bridges sampling, self-reward KL-regularized RL, and self-distillation. From the sampling perspective, we show that inexpensive local approximations cannot reproduce sequence-level power without information about possible suffixes. From the RL perspective, the power distribution is the closed-form optimizer of KL-regularized RL when the model's sequence-level log-probabilities are used as the reward. This identification leads to power self-distillation, an offline distillation surrogate that shares the same target distribution and amortizes the cost of power sampling into supervised training on teacher samples. We further show that power self-distillation can achieve self-reward sharpening, while improvement in a downstream true reward is governed by the covariance between true reward and self-reward under the power distribution. Experiments on reasoning tasks support our analysis: power sampling raises self-reward, true-reward gains depend on alignment with self-reward, and power self-distillation can match or exceed the performance of power sampling at much lower inference cost.

</details>


### [165] [Preference-Based Self-Distillation: Beyond KL Matching via Reward Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05040)
*Xin Yu,Liuchen Liao,Yiwen Zhang,Yingchen Yu,Lingzhou Xue,Qinzhen Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出偏好自蒸馏方法（PBSD），通过奖励正则化视角优化策略学习过程，在数学推理与工具使用任务上取得优于基线的稳定表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏方法将学习过程简化为对情境增强教师模型的KL匹配，存在训练不稳定、推理性能退化以及缺乏真实外部教师的探索多样性等问题。

Method: 提出偏好自蒸馏（PBSD），从奖励正则化视角重新构建目标，推导出解析最优解为奖励重加权教师分布；在保持同策略采样的同时，优化教师与学生样本间的偏好差距，并给出理论分析。

Result: 在数学推理与工具使用基准上，多规模模型实验表明PBSD在可比基线中平均表现最强，训练稳定性优于先前自蒸馏方法且保持token效率。

Conclusion: PBSD为同策略自蒸馏提供了更稳定、有效的替代方案，理论证明其可优于外部教师学习，为强化学习训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an efficient alternative to reinforcement learning, offering dense token-level training signals. However, its reliance on a stronger external teacher has driven recent work on on-policy self-distillation, where the same model serves as both teacher and student under different prompt contexts. Yet, existing self-distillation methods largely reduce learning to KL matching toward the context-augmented teacher model. This approach often suffers from training instability and can degrade reasoning performance over time. Moreover, self-distillation from the same model with prompt augmentation lacks the exploratory diversity provided by a genuine external teacher. To address these limitations, we move beyond fixed-teacher KL matching and propose \textbf{P}reference-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{D}istillation (\textbf{PBSD}), which revisits on-policy self-distillation through a reward-regularized perspective. Instead of directly matching the teacher distribution, we derive a reward-regularized objective whose analytic optimum is a reward-reweighted teacher distribution, yielding a target policy provably superior to the original teacher under this objective. Practically, PBSD optimizes preference gaps between teacher and student samples while maintaining on-policy student sampling. We support this framework with a statistical analysis of the induced preference-learning problem, formally establishing when on policy self-distillation is preferable to learning from an external teacher in our setting. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks across multiple model scales demonstrate that PBSD consistently achieves the strongest average performance among comparable baselines, showing improved training stability over prior self-distillation baselines while preserving token efficiency.

</details>


### [166] [Event-Based Early Warning of Vineyard Disease Risk from Environmental Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04548)
*Ivica Dimitrovski,Ivan Kitanovski,Danco Davcev,Slobodan Kalajdziski,Kosta Mitreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件的方法来预警葡萄园病害风险，将传统的每日病害存在分类任务重构为预测未来3-7天窗口内进入病害风险期的转变，从而鼓励模型捕捉环境前兆而非单纯依赖时间持续性，实验证明该方法能支持实用的短期预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将病害预测表述为每日是否存在病害的分类问题，这种方式容易导致模型倾向于依赖时间持续性进行预测，难以提供具有可操作性的短期预警支持。为了实现及时干预和更可持续的作物保护，需要一种能够准确预警病害风险的新方法。

Method: 1. 将任务重构为基于事件的预测：不再预测每日病害状态，而是预测未来3-7天窗口内是否进入病害风险期。
2. 定义事件：引入最小无病害间隔阈值，减少二值标签因短期中断产生的碎片化问题，新事件仅在足够长的无病害间隔后定义。
3. 构建输入特征：利用多年农业气象数据，构建捕捉湿度动态、降雨累积、温度变异性以及季节性结构的输入表示，其中季节性通过循环时间编码实现。
4. 模型对比：评估了经典机器学习（XGBoost）和深度学习方法（LSTM、TCN），使用标准分类指标和面向事件的预警协议进行评估。

Result: 1. 基于事件的表述方式支持实用的短期预警（3-7天窗口）。
2. 不同模型在事件召回率、提前预警时间和误报率之间存在明显的权衡。
3. 该方法鼓励模型捕捉与即将到来的风险期相关的环境前兆，而非单纯复现时间持续性模式。

Conclusion: 研究强调了环境时间序列学习中问题表述的重要性，证明了基于事件的预测方法在葡萄园病害预警系统中的价值，为实现及时干预和可持续作物保护提供了有效支持。

Abstract: Accurate early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental observations is essential for timely intervention and more sustainable crop protection. However, many existing studies formulate disease prediction as daily presence classification, which can favor persistence-driven predictions and provide only limited support for actionable short-horizon warning. In this paper, we present an event-based approach for early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental time series and evaluate it through a vineyard case study. Rather than predicting daily disease status, the task is reformulated to predict transitions into annotated disease-risk periods within a future window of 3-7 days. To reduce fragmentation caused by short interruptions in the binary labels, new events are defined only after a minimum disease-free gap. This formulation encourages models to capture environmental precursors associated with upcoming risk periods instead of merely reproducing temporal persistence. Using multi-year agro-meteorological data, we construct input representations that capture humidity dynamics, rainfall accumulation, temperature variability, and seasonal structure through cyclic temporal encoding. We evaluate representative methods from classical machine learning and deep learning, including XGBoost, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCNs), using both standard classification metrics and an event-oriented early warning protocol. The results show that the event-based formulation supports practical short-horizon warning, while the compared models exhibit distinct trade-offs between event recall, lead time, and false-alert behavior. Overall, the study underscores the importance of problem formulation in environmental time-series learning and demonstrates the value of event-based prediction for vineyard disease warning systems.

</details>


### [167] [Counter-Dyna: Data-Efficient RL-Based HVAC Control using Counterfactual Building Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04555)
*Jan Marco Ruiz de Vargas,Fabian Raisch,Zoltan Nagy,Pierre Pinson,Christoph Goebel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Counter-Dyna方法，通过利用状态空间不变性创建反事实代理模型(CSM)，将HVAC控制中MBRL算法所需的环境交互数据从6-12个月缩短至5周，同时实现5.3%-17.0%的节能效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MBRL方法在HVAC控制中仍需数月交互数据，主要因为代理模型试图预测整个状态空间（包括不受控制影响的天气和电价）或完全忽略这些变量，导致数据效率低下。

Method: 提出Counter-Dyna方法，通过利用状态空间不变性创建数据高效的反事实代理模型(CSM)，将其集成到Dyna框架中；使用BOPTEST框架和PPO算法进行大规模仿真研究。

Result: 相比现有方法需要6-12个月环境交互，本方法仅需5周；在假设部署场景中实现5.3%至17.0%的成本节约潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提高了数据效率，是推动RL算法在HVAC控制中实际部署的重要一步，使现实应用更具可行性。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) offers a promising approach for data-efficient energy management in buildings, combining the strengths of predictive modeling and reinforcement learning. While previous MBRL methods applied to HVAC control have reduced training data requirements, they still require several months of interaction with the building to learn a satisfactory control policy. A key reason is that existing surrogate models attempt to predict the entire state-space, including weather and electricity prices that are unaffected by control actions, or completely ignore these variables. Addressing these issues, we propose Counter-Dyna, a method that enhances the data-efficiency of Dyna, an MBRL method. We create data-efficient counterfactual surrogate models (CSM) by leveraging invariances in the state-space. Using a CSM in Dyna speeds up RL training measured in environment interaction data compared to previous results. In comparison with previous state-of-the-art that used 6-12 months of environment interactions, our method needs only 5 weeks. We evaluate our method in a large simulation study using the literature standard BOPTEST framework and proximal policy algorithm (PPO) as the RL algorithm. Our results show cost-saving potentials of 5.3% to 17.0% in a hypothetical deployment scenario. Our work is a significant step towards making real-world deployment of RL algorithms in HVAC control practically viable.

</details>


### [168] [Adaptive Learning Strategies for AoA-Based Outdoor Localization: A Comprehensive Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05055)
*Bac Trinh-Nguyen,Sara Berri,Sin G. Teo,Tram Truong-Huu,Arsenia Chorti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应AoA定位框架，包含离线和在线两种学习策略，分别适用于大规模和小规模训练数据集，在真实mMIMO-OFDM CSI数据集上验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G/6G网络中的定位对智能交通、智慧工厂等应用至关重要。现有深度学习方法虽然提高了定位精度，但训练过程因部署场景和数据集收集工作的不同而存在显著差异。针对这一问题，并结合AoA定位的鲁棒性特点，需要一种能够适应不同数据规模的定位框架。

Method: 提出了一种自适应的AoA定位框架，包含两种学习策略：1) 针对大规模数据集的离线学习策略，采用分层框架先区分LoS/NLoS区域再进行精细定位，结合批量重训练和超参数优化机制；2) 针对小规模数据集的在线学习策略，使用增量式树模型和集成模型处理流数据，并引入在线少样本学习模型快速初始化新类别。

Result: 在真实的mMIMO-OFDM室外CSI数据集上进行了评估。离线学习方法在大规模数据集上实现了高性能定位；在线学习方法能够在网络运行过程中增量式地实现高精度鲁棒定位，显著减轻了对大规模数据集收集活动的需求。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了不同数据规模下的定位模型训练问题，证明了在线学习方法可以在有限数据条件下实现高精度定位，为5G/6G网络定位应用提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Localization in 5G and 6G networks is essential for important use cases such as intelligent transportation, smart factories, and smart cities. Although deep learning has enabled improving localization accuracy, depending on the deployment scenario and the effort required for dataset collection campaigns on a given infrastructure, the training process for localization models can vary significantly. Furthermore, with respect to feature selection, recent works have demonstrated the robustness of angle-of-arrival (AoA) based localization. In view of these two points, we propose an adaptive framework for AoA-based localization that consists of two alternative learning strategies, each suited either for large or small training datasets. The proposed framework is evaluated on a real, massive multiple input multiple output (mMIMO) orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) outdoor channel state information (CSI) dataset. First, we investigate offline learning when large training datasets are available; we propose a hierarchical framework that first distinguishes between line of sight (LoS) and non line of sight (NLoS) regions and then moves to more fine grained localization in the respective region. This approach provides high-performance localization through accumulated batch retraining and an integrated hyperparameter optimization mechanism. Second, when only a small training dataset is available, an online learning framework is proposed, using incremental tree-based and ensemble-based models for handling streaming data and continuously updating mode, as well as an online few-shot learning model for rapidly initializing new classes from a limited labeled support set. These results showcase that highly accurate robust localization can be achieved incrementally during network operation by exploiting online learning, alleviating the need for large dataset collection campaigns.

</details>


### [169] [Continual Knowledge Updating in LLM Systems: Learning Through Multi-Timescale Memory Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05097)
*Andreas Pattichis,Constantine Dovrolis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Memini系统，将生物记忆的多时间尺度耦合动力学机制引入外部记忆管理，通过有向图结构和快慢变量耦合实现自适应的记忆巩固与遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM外部记忆系统多采用显式管理，缺乏自适应能力。受生物记忆机制启发，作者希望构建一种能够自动适应、强化有效信息并遗忘过时内容的动态记忆系统。

Method: 提出Memini系统，将知识组织为有向图，每条边采用Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型，设置快慢两个耦合内部变量。快变量实现对新关联的即时响应，慢变量负责长期巩固。

Result: 从快慢变量的单一耦合机制中自然涌现出三种特性：情景敏感性（快速适应新信息）、渐进式巩固（重复验证的信息被强化）和选择性遗忘（无用信息逐渐衰减），实现了记忆的自主重组。

Conclusion: 该工作将外部记忆重新定义为一种通过自身动力学进行重组的学习基质，而非被动存储，为LLM的记忆管理提供了更接近生物机制的解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs are trained once, then deployed into a world that never stops changing. External memory compensates for this, but most systems manage it explicitly rather than letting it adapt on its own. Biological memory works differently: coupled multi-timescale dynamics make new associations immediately usable, strengthen what repetition confirms, and let the rest fade. We argue that external memory should follow a similar principle. In Memini, this view takes the form of an associative memory that organizes knowledge as a directed graph. Each edge carries two coupled internal variables, one fast and one slow, following the Benna-Fusi model of synaptic consolidation. From this coupling, episodic sensitivity, gradual consolidation, and selective forgetting emerge as facets of a single mechanism, reframing external memory as a learning substrate that reorganizes through its own dynamics.

</details>


### [170] [On the Wasserstein Gradient Flow Interpretation of Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05118)
*Arthur Gretton,Li Kevin Wenliang,Alexandre Galashov,James Thornton,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）视角分析了Deng等人提出的生成式建模漂移（GMD）框架，揭示了其算法与特定WGF不动点之间的对应关系，并将该思想扩展至MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别器函数等多种度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解GMD框架的理论基础，将其置于Wasserstein梯度流的数学框架下进行分析，以揭示该方法的本质特性及其与现有方法的关系。

Method: 通过WGF视角分析GMD框架，研究其与KL散度、Sinkhorn散度等不同度量的WGF不动点之间的对应关系，并探索向MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别器函数的扩展可能性。

Result: 证明了三个主要结论：（1）Deng等人提出的一种算法对应于带Parzen平滑的KL散度WGF的极限点；（2）实际实现的算法对应于类似Sinkhorn散度WGF不动点的过程，但缺乏后者的一些理想性质；（3）该思想可扩展至MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别器函数的WGF极限点。

Conclusion: GMD框架本质上是针对特定WGF不动点的直接求解方法，这一视角为理解和改进生成模型提供了新的理论基础，并可推广至多种概率度量空间。

Abstract: Recently, Deng et al. (2026) proposed Generative Modeling via Drifting (GMD), a novel framework for generative tasks. This note presents an analysis of GMD through the lens of Wasserstein Gradient Flows (WGF), i.e., the path of steepest descent for a functional in the space of probability measures, equipped with the geometry of optimal transport. Unlike previous WGF-based contributions, GMD can be thought of as directly targeting a fixed point of a specific WGF flow. We demonstrate three main results: first, that one algorithm proposed by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to finding the limiting point of a WGF on the KL divergence, with Parzen smoothing on the densities. Second, that the algorithm actually implemented by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to a different procedure, which bears some resemblance to the fixed point of a WGF on the Sinkhorn divergence, but lacks certain desirable properties of the latter. Third, the same same idea can be extended to the limiting point of other WGFs, including the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), the sliced Wasserstein distance, and GAN critic functions.

</details>


### [171] [Adaptive Policy Selection and Fine-Tuning under Interaction Budgets for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05123)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Xiaoan Xu,Shangtong Zhang,Miroslav Pajic,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线到在线强化学习(O2O-RL)中策略选择和微调问题，提出了一种自适应方法，在有限在线交互预算下，基于上置信界(UCB)方法动态选择和微调策略，有效解决了传统方法依赖单一策略部署不可靠或穷举微调超预算的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统O2O-RL流程存在两个关键问题：(1)离策略评估(OPE)不可靠导致部署风险，而在线评估(OE)虽准确但消耗本可用于微调的交互预算；(2)无法预判预训练策略在部署后微调中是否能提升性能，尤其在非平稳环境中，导致单策略部署方案不切实际，而穷举微调所有候选策略又会违反交互预算约束。

Method: 首先使用不同离线RL算法和超参数训练一组候选策略并获得OPE初始性能估计；然后基于上置信界(UCB)方法预测策略性能，在在线交互预算约束下自适应地选择和微调策略，以高效利用有限的在线交互资源。

Result: 在多个基准测试上证明，该方法相比现有O2O-RL基线方法取得了性能提升，能够在有限在线交互预算下更有效地进行策略选择和微调。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应策略选择和微调方法成功解决了O2O-RL中策略评估与预算约束之间的矛盾，通过UCB方法实现了在线交互资源的高效利用，为实际场景中不可预测的策略微调效果提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In offline-to-online reinforcement learning (O2O-RL), policies are first safely trained offline using previously collected datasets and then further fine-tuned for tasks via limited online interactions. In a typical O2O-RL pipeline, candidate policies trained with offline RL are evaluated via either off-policy evaluation (OPE) or online evaluation (OE). The policy with the highest estimated value is then deployed and continually fine-tuned. However, this setup has two main issues. First, OPE can be unreliable, making it risky to deploy a policy based solely on those estimates, whereas OE may identify a viable policy with substantial online interaction, which could have been used for fine-tuning. Second--and more importantly--it is also often not possible to determine a priori whether a pretrained policy will improve with post-deployment fine-tuning, especially in non-stationary environments. As a result, procedures committing to a single deployed policy are impractical in many real-world settings. Moreover, a naive remedy that exhaustively fine-tunes all candidates would violate interaction budget constraints and is likewise infeasible. In this paper, we propose a novel adaptive approach for policy selection and fine-tuning under online interaction budgets in O2O-RL. Following the standard pipeline, we first train a set of candidate policies with different offline RL algorithms and hyperparameters; we then perform OPE to obtain initial performance estimates. We next adaptively select and fine-tune the policies based on their predicted performance via an upper-confidence-bound approach thereby making efficient use of online interactions. We demonstrate that our approach improves upon O2O-RL baselines with various benchmarks.

</details>


### [172] [FAAST: Forward-Only Associative Learning via Closed-Form Fast Weights for Test-Time Supervised Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04651)
*Guangsheng Bao,Hongbo Zhang,Han Cui,Yanbin Zhao,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAAST方法，一种前向传播的关联自适应方案，在单次前向传播中将标注样本解析为快速权重，实现了常数时间推理，在匹配反向传播性能的同时减少90%以上的适应时间，并节省高达95%的内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练模型自适应方法存在权衡困境：反向传播训练成本高昂，而基于记忆或上下文学习的方法在推理时开销巨大。因此亟需一种既能高效训练又能高效推理的自适应方案。

Method: 提出FAAST（Forward-only Associative Adaptation），一种纯前向传播的关联自适应方法。该方法通过解析方式将标注样本在单次前向传播中编译为快速权重（fast weights），消除了对记忆或上下文的依赖，从而实现常数时间推理并将任务适应与预训练表示解耦。

Result: 在图像分类和语言建模基准测试中，FAAST在性能上匹配或超越基于反向传播的自适应方法，同时将适应时间减少超过90%；与基于记忆/上下文的自适应方法相比保持竞争力，并节省高达95%的内存使用。

Conclusion: FAAST是一种高效、可扩展的有监督任务自适应解决方案，特别适用于资源受限的模型场景，有效解决了传统方法在训练成本与推理开销之间的权衡难题。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained models typically involves a trade-off between the high training costs of backpropagation and the heavy inference overhead of memory-based or in-context learning. We propose FAAST, a forward-only associative adaptation method that analytically compiles labeled examples into fast weights in a single pass. By eliminating memory or context dependence, FAAST achieves constant-time inference and decouples task adaptation from pretrained representation. Across image classification and language modeling benchmarks, FAAST matches or exceeds backprop-based adaptation while reducing adaptation time by over 90\% and is competitive to memory/context-based adaptation while saving memory usage by up to 95\%. These results demonstrate FAAST as a highly efficient, scalable solution for supervised task adaptation, particularly for resource-constrained models. We release the code and models at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/faast.

</details>


### [173] [Joint Treatment Effect Estimation from Incomplete Healthcare Data: Temporal Causal Normalizing Flows with LLM-driven Evolutionary MNAR Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05125)
*Olivia Jullian Parra,Sara Zoccheddu,David Catalan Cerezo,Tom Forzy,Franziska Ulrich,William Sutcliffe,Jakob Martin Burgstaller,Oliver Senn,Patrick Owen,Nicola Serra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段的因果推断流程，用于从不完整的纵向电子健康记录（EHR）中估计处理效应。第一阶段采用DAG约束的标准化流模型CausalFlow-T进行精确反事实推断；第二阶段引入大语言模型（LLM）驱动的进化插补器处理缺失数据，在真实世界的2型糖尿病患者数据上成功估计出与随机试验证据一致的处理效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验（RCT）在某些情况下不可行，而现有的处理效应估计方法通常分别处理因果估计、缺失性和时间结构，这限制了它们在电子健康记录（EHR）中的稳健性，尤其是在存在时变混杂和高达50%-80%的非随机缺失（MNAR）生物标志物的情况下。因此需要一种能够同时处理这些挑战的统一方法。

Method: 提出了一个两阶段管道：1）CausalFlow-T模型，采用有向无环图（DAG）约束的标准化流和LSTM编码的患者历史，进行精确可逆的反事实推断，避免变分推断的近似误差并通过显式因果结构分离混杂；2）LLM驱动的进化插补器，提出可执行的插补算子而非单个条目，在三个LLM后端上进行评估以处理MNAR缺失问题。

Result: 在四个合成和一个半合成基准上的消融实验表明，DAG约束和精确推断解决了不同的失败模式且互不可替代。在30%-80%的MNAR缺失率下，LLM插补器在生物标志物和因果指标上取得了最佳综合排名，在点精度和时间外推方面领先，同时保持了平均处理效应（ATE）恢复能力。在瑞士初级护理EHR的2型糖尿病患者数据上，该流程估计GLP-1受体激动剂相比SGLT-2抑制剂的按方案体重减轻差异为-0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96]，与随机试验证据一致。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了所提出的两阶段流程能够有效处理真实世界EHR数据中的时变混杂和高比例MNAR缺失问题，成功实现了与随机试验证据一致的处理效应估计，为从现实不完整的EHR数据中进行因果推断提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Target trial emulation (TTE) enables causal questions to be studied with observational data when randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are infeasible. Yet treatment-effect methods often address causal estimation, missingness, and temporal structure separately, limiting their robustness in electronic health records (EHRs), where time-varying confounding and missing-not-at-random (MNAR) biomarkers can reach 50%--80%. We propose a two-stage pipeline for treatment effect estimation from incomplete longitudinal EHRs. First, CausalFlow-T, a directed acyclic graph (DAG)-constrained normalizing flow with long short-term memory (LSTM)-encoded patient history, performs exact invertible counterfactual inference, avoiding approximation errors from variational inference and separating confounding through explicit causal structure. Ablations on four synthetic and one semi-synthetic benchmark with known counterfactuals show that DAG constraints and exact inference address distinct failure modes: neither compensates for the other. Second, because CausalFlow-T requires completed inputs, we introduce an LLM-driven evolutionary imputer that proposes executable imputation operators rather than individual entries, and evaluate it with three large language model (LLM) backends, including two open-source models. Across 30%--80% MNAR missingness, this imputer achieves the best pooled rank over biomarker and causal metrics, leading in point-wise accuracy and temporal extrapolation while preserving average treatment effect (ATE) recovery as statistical baselines degrade. On Swiss primary-care EHRs from adults with type 2 diabetes initiating a GLP-1 receptor agonist or SGLT-2 inhibitor, the pipeline estimates a per-protocol weight-loss difference of -0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96] favoring GLP-1 receptor agonists, consistent with randomized evidence and obtained from realistically incomplete real-world EHRs.

</details>


### [174] [Threshold-Guided Optimization for Visual Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04653)
*Jinbin Bai,Yu Lei,Qingyu Shi,Aosong Feng,Yi Xin,Zhuoran Zhao,Fei Shen,Kaidong Yu,Jason Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种阈值引导的对齐框架，通过数据驱动的全局阈值替代不可达的基准，将标量反馈下的视觉生成模型对齐转化为二元决策任务，无需成对比较数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成模型的人类反馈对齐方法主要依赖成对偏好优化，这种方法依赖标注的成对数据，在仅有独立标量评分的场景下可扩展性受限。

Method: 作者重新审视KL正则化对齐目标，揭示最优策略隐含地将每个样本的奖励与实例特定的基准进行比较，而该基准通常不可计算。为此，提出了一个阈值引导的对齐框架，利用经验分数统计量估计数据驱动的全局阈值替代该基准，将对齐问题转化为无配对数据上的二元决策任务，并引入置信度加权项以强调分数偏离阈值较大的样本，提高样本效率。

Result: 在扩散模型和掩码生成模型范式下，覆盖三个测试集和五种奖励模型的实验表明，该方法在偏好对齐效果上始终优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该阈值引导框架为无需成对比较的视觉生成模型对齐提供了一种简单且原则性的替代方案。

Abstract: Aligning large visual generative models with human feedback is often performed through pairwise preference optimization. While such approaches are conceptually simple, they fundamentally rely on annotated pairs, limiting scalability in settings where feedback is collected as independent scalar ratings. In this work, we revisit the KL-regularized alignment objective and show that the optimal policy implicitly compares each sample's reward to an instance-specific baseline that is generally intractable. We propose a threshold-guided alignment framework that replaces this oracle baseline with a data-driven global threshold estimated from empirical score statistics. This formulation turns alignment into a binary decision task on unpaired data, enabling effective optimization directly from scalar feedback. We also incorporate a confidence weighting term to emphasize samples whose scores deviate strongly from the threshold, improving sample efficiency. Experiments across both diffusion and masked generative paradigms, spanning three test sets and five reward models, show that our method consistently improves preference alignment over previous methods. These results position our threshold-guided framework as a simple yet principled alternative for aligning visual generative models without paired comparisons.

</details>


### [175] [Superposition Is Not Necessary: A Mechanistic Interpretability Analysis of Transformer Representations for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05151)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现Transformer在时序预测中不依赖强叠加表示，解释了为何简单线性模型仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构在时序预测中广泛应用，但其强大的表示机制是否真正发挥作用尚不明确；简单线性模型（如DLinear）持续保持竞争力，但缺乏机制性解释。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）探究PatchTST的内部表示，在FFN的post-GELU激活上训练字典大小从0.5x到4.0x原生维度的SAE，并进行针对性因果干预。

Result: 单层窄维度Transformer即可匹配深层配置性能；字典扩展对下游性能影响微乎其微（平均0.214%），大部分过完备字典保持不活跃；对主导潜在特征的因果干预产生最小预测扰动；未发现FFN表示依赖强叠加的经验证据，表示保持稀疏且稳定。

Conclusion: 叠加对标准时序预测基准的竞争性能并非必要，这些基准可能不需要驱动Transformer在语言建模中成功的丰富组合表示，这解释了简单线性模型持续竞争力的原因。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have been widely adopted for time series forecasting, yet whether the representational mechanisms that make them powerful in NLP actually engage on time series data remains unexplored. The persistent competitiveness of simple linear models such as DLinear has fueled ongoing debate, but no mechanistic explanation for this phenomenon has been offered. We address this gap by applying sparse autoencoders (SAEs), a tool from mechanistic interpretability, to probe the internal representations of PatchTST. We first establish that a single-layer, narrow-dimensional transformer matches the forecasting performance of deeper configurations across commonly used benchmarks. We then train SAEs on the post-GELU intermediate FFN activations with dictionary sizes ranging from 0.5x to 4.0x the native dimensionality. Expanding the dictionary yields negligible downstream performance change (average 0.214%), with large portions of overcomplete dictionaries remaining inactive. Targeted causal interventions on dominant latent features produce minimal forecast perturbation. Across all evaluated settings, we observe no empirical evidence that the analyzed FFN representations rely on strong superposition. Instead, the representations remain sparse, stable under aggressive dictionary expansion, and largely insensitive to latent interventions. These results demonstrate that superposition is not necessary for competitive performance on standard forecasting benchmarks, suggesting they may not demand the rich compositional representations that drive transformer success in language modeling, and helping explain the persistent competitiveness of simple linear models

</details>


### [176] [Evidence-based anomaly detection in clinical domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04664)
*Milos Hauskrecht,Michal Valko,Branislav Kveton,Shyam Visweswaran,Gregory Cooper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于概率模型的异常检测方法，用于识别心脏术后患者管理决策中的异常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测方法在识别有趣或令人关注的事件方面非常有用。本文旨在开发能够评估特定患者管理决策并识别与同类患者相比高度异常决策的新方法。

Method: 开发了新的概率异常检测方法，使用从过去患者病例数据库中学习的贝叶斯网络等概率模型来推导统计量。这些方法能够评估针对特定患者的管理决策，并识别相对于具有相同或相似病症的患者而言高度异常的决策。

Result: 将这些方法应用于识别心脏术后患者管理决策中的异常决策问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的概率异常检测方法能够有效识别患者管理决策中的异常行为，为医疗决策质量评估提供了新的技术手段。

Abstract: Anomaly detection methods can be very useful in identifying interesting or concerning events. In this work, we develop and examine new probabilistic anomaly detection methods that let us evaluate management decisions for a specific patient and identify those decisions that are highly unusual with respect to patients with the same or similar condition. The statistics used in this detection are derived from probabilistic models such as Bayesian networks that are learned from a database of past patient cases. We apply our methods to the problem of identifying unusual patient-management decisions in post-surgical cardiac patients.

</details>


### [177] [Feature importance analysis for patient management decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04666)
*Michal Valko,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了临床数据特征对医生决策（开化验单和开处方药）的影响，发现仅需少量特征即可较好地预测医生的决策行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解临床数据的哪些特征和属性对医生决定是否开具化验单或处方药影响最大。

Method: 基于4486名心脏术后患者的电子健康记录数据，对335种化验单决策和407种药物决策进行提取与统计分析。

Result: 在许多情况下，仅需从全部特征中选取一个小子集，即可较好地预测医生的化验单和药物决策。

Conclusion: 医生的化验单与处方决策通常高度依赖于少量关键临床特征，这有助于理解临床决策模式并优化决策支持系统。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to understand what characteristics and features of clinical data influence physician's decision about ordering laboratory tests or prescribing medications the most. We conduct our analysis on data and decisions extracted from electronic health records of 4486 post-surgical cardiac patients. The summary statistics for 335 different lab order decisions and 407 medication decisions are reported. We show that in many cases, physician's lab-order and medication decisions can be well predicted from a small subset of all features.

</details>


### [178] [ITBoost: Information-Theoretic Trust for Robust Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04671)
*Ye Su,Longlong Zhao,Diego Garcia-Gil,Jipeng Guo,Gangchun Zhang,Jinxin Chen,Jinsong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ITBoost算法，通过信息论方法分析样本残差轨迹的复杂性来解决梯度提升在标签噪声下的性能下降问题，在噪声环境中表现出色且保持干净数据上的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升方法在表格数据学习中表现优异，但在训练标签存在噪声时性能显著下降。这是因为传统提升算法通过大梯度来强调困难样本，却无法区分这些错误是来自真正有价值的困难样本还是不可靠的噪声标签。

Method: 提出信息论信任提升（ITBoost）算法。核心创新在于不再依赖瞬时误差评估样本可靠性，而是追踪每个样本残差在迭代过程中的演化轨迹。利用最小描述长度原则度量残差轨迹的复杂性：残差模式波动不规则的样本被视为可信度较低，在学习过程中被降低权重。

Result: 在理论上推导出了ITBoost在标签噪声下更紧的泛化界。在多个表格数据基准测试上的实验结果表明：ITBoost在噪声环境中比领先的提升算法和深度表格模型具有更强的鲁棒性，同时在干净数据上保持最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: 通过信息论方法重新审视提升算法中的样本可靠性评估机制，ITBoost成功解决了标签噪声问题，实现了噪声鲁棒性与干净数据性能的良好平衡，为表格数据学习提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Gradient boosting remains a strong and widely used method for tabular data learning, but its performance often degrades when training labels are noisy. This behavior is largely related to the way boosting algorithms emphasize samples with large gradients, without explicitly accounting for whether such errors originate from informative hard cases or from unreliable labels. We address this issue by reconsidering how sample reliability is evaluated during boosting. Instead of relying on instantaneous error, we examine the evolution of each sample's residuals across iterations. Based on this insight, we propose Information-Theoretic Trust Boosting (ITBoost), which uses the Minimum Description Length principle to measure the complexity of residual trajectories. Samples whose residual patterns fluctuate in an irregular manner are treated as less trustworthy and are down-weighted during learning. Theoretically, we derive a tighter generalization bound for ITBoost under label noise. Empirical results on various tabular benchmarks indicate that ITBoost provides improved robustness in noisy environments over leading boosting and deep tabular models, while retaining best average performance on clean data.

</details>


### [179] [HEXST: Hexagonal Shifted-Window Transformer for Spatial Transcriptomics Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04682)
*Keunho Byeon,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEXST，一种基于六边形几何对齐的Transformer模型，用于从常规H&E染色组织切片预测空间基因表达，在七个数据集上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间转录组计算方法大多忽略广泛使用的点阵平台所采用的六边形采样几何特性，且逐点回归目标常导致预测的基因表达过于平滑，掩盖了基因特异性的空间异质性。

Method: HEXST直接在六边形点坐标上操作，设计了适配的移位窗口注意力机制和六边形旋转位置编码，以实现高效的局部到全局上下文建模；同时，引入对比敏感的差分目标，并结合预训练单细胞基础模型的转录组先验知识，以增强基因层面的空间对比度。

Result: 在七个空间转录组数据集上的实验表明，HEXST始终优于现有最先进模型，能够提供准确、鲁棒的空间基因表达预测，同时有效保留了基因对比度和空间异质性。

Conclusion: HEXST通过几何对齐的Transformer架构与对比学习目标，有效解决了现有方法在几何建模和空间异质性保留方面的不足，有望推动空间基因表达预测技术的大规模应用。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics offers spatially resolved gene expression profiling within tissue sections, but its cost and limited throughput hinder large-scale deployment. To extend this capability to routine practice, recent computational methods aim to infer spatial gene expression directly from ubiquitous hematoxylin and eosin-stained histology slides. However, most existing models assume Cartesian or geometry-agnostic locality, despite the hexagonal sampling of widely used spot-array platforms, and point-wise regression objectives often yield over-smoothed gene expression profiles, obscuring gene-specific spatial heterogeneity. To address these, we propose HEXST, a geometry-aligned Transformer for spatial gene expression prediction from histology. HEXST operates directly on hexagonal spot coordinates to enable efficient local-to-global contextual modeling via tailored shifted-window attention mechanism and hexagonal rotary positional encoding. To enhance gene-wise spatial contrast, HEXST complements point-wise regression with a contrast-sensitive differential objective and transcriptomic priors from a pretrained single-cell foundation model during training. Across seven spatial transcriptomics datasets, HEXST consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, providing accurate and robust spatial gene expression predictions while preserving gene-wise contrast and spatial heterogeneity.

</details>
