### [180] [Learning Time-Inhomogeneous Markov Dynamics in Financial Time Series via Neural Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04690)
*Jan Rovirosa,Jesse Schmolze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将神经网络约束为参数化引擎以生成显式时变马尔可夫转移矩阵的框架，在保持完全结构可解释性的同时解决了高维噪声环境下经验估计的数据稀疏问题，成功应用于金融时间序列的复杂状态转换捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典马尔可夫转移算子虽具有明确的理论基础和可解释性，但在高分辨率、高噪声环境（如金融时间序列）中面临严重的数据稀疏问题，导致经验估计失效；需要平衡深度学习的表示能力与经典模型的数学透明性。

Method: 构建一个框架，将神经网络严格作为参数化引擎来生成显式的时变马尔可夫转移矩阵，通过约束神经网络输出形式化的随机算子来保持完整的结构可解释性；将Chapman-Kolmogorov方程重新定位为局部诊断工具，用于识别一阶记忆假设失效的时间窗口。

Result: 状态条件模型实现平均行异质性\barρ = 0.0073，而无状态消融模型完全坍缩为零；算子行熵与实现方差的相关系数r = -0.62（p ≈ 10^{-251}），揭示高波动状态会使转移动力学同质化而非多样化；成功捕捉复杂的状态转换特征。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了神经网络可以被约束以实现严格的经典算子分析，从而使其适用于复杂真实世界时间序列，为非平稳随机系统动力学建模提供了新的解决思路，平衡了表示能力与数学透明性。

Abstract: Modeling the dynamics of non-stationary stochastic systems requires balancing the representational power of deep learning with the mathematical transparency of classical models. While classical Markov transition operators provide explicit, theoretically grounded rules for system evolution, their empirical estimation collapses due to severe data sparsity when applied to high-resolution, high-noise environments. We explore this statistical barrier using financial time series as a canonical, real-world testbed. To overcome the degeneracy of empirical counting, we introduce a framework that utilizes neural networks strictly as parameterization engines to generate explicit, time-varying Markov transition matrices. By constraining the neural network to output its predictions as a formal stochastic operator, we maintain complete structural interpretability. We demonstrate that these learned operators successfully capture complex regime shifts: the state-conditioned model achieves mean row heterogeneity $\barρ = 0.0073$ while the state-free ablation collapses to exactly zero, and operator row entropy correlates with realized variance at $r = -0.62$ ($p \approx 10^{-251}$), revealing that high-volatility regimes homogenize transition dynamics rather than diversify them. Furthermore, rather than enforcing the Chapman-Kolmogorov equations as a rigid structural requirement, we repurpose them as a localized diagnostic tool to pinpoint specific temporal windows where first-order memory assumptions break down. Ultimately, this framework demonstrates how neural networks can be constrained to make rigorous, classical operator analysis viable for complex real-world time series.

</details>


### [181] [Differentiable Chemistry in PINNs for Solving Parameterized and Stiff Reaction Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04708)
*Miloš Babić,Franz M. Rohrhofer,Stefan Posch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将可微化学求解器集成到改进的物理信息神经网络中，成功解决了传统PINN难以处理的刚性化学反应系统问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着神经ODE和连续时间机器学习的发展，可微求解器使物理、优化和仿真能够成为深度学习系统中的可训练组件。然而，标准的物理信息神经网络在处理本质刚性的参数化反应系统时存在局限性，需要新的方法来拓展其在科学计算中的应用范围。

Method: 提出一个集成可微化学求解器的改进物理信息神经网络框架。该框架包含三个关键组件：（1）可微化学求解器；（2）用于参数化解的网络架构；（3）针对刚性反应的残差加权策略。

Result: 在与氢燃烧相关的一系列微分方程上对框架进行了评估，包括初值/边值问题、逆参数识别和参数化偏微分方程。结果表明该方法能够将物理信息神经网络扩展到以前无法处理的刚性化学系统。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功克服了标准物理信息神经网络在处理刚性化学反应系统时的局限性，为科学计算领域深度学习框架的发展开辟了新路径，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: From neural ODEs to continuous-time machine learning, differentiable solvers allow physics, optimization, and simulation to become trainable components within deep learning systems. This has opened the path to a new generation of deep learning frameworks for scientific computing, with many promising applications still emerging. In this paper, we integrate a differentiable chemistry solver into a modified physics-informed neural network to solve parameterized reaction systems that are inherently stiff. The proposed framework introduces several key components required to overcome limitations of standard physics-informed neural networks. These include a differentiable chemistry solver, a network architecture for parameterized solutions, and residual weighting tailored to stiff reactions. We evaluate the framework on a set of differential equations related to hydrogen combustion, which include initial/boundary value problems, inverse parameter identification, and a parameterized partial differential equation. Our results highlight the ability of the proposed approach to extend physics-informed neural networks to stiff chemical systems that were previously inaccessible.

</details>


### [182] [ELVIS: Ensemble-Calibrated Latent Imagination for Long-Horizon Visual MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04709)
*Yurui Du,Pinhao Song,Yutong Hu,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELVIS是一种基于潜在模型的预测控制方法，通过高斯混合MPPI和多假设规划解决长视野规划中的多模态问题，并利用不确定性感知的lambda-return稳定深度想象，在DMControl任务上达到SOTA并成功迁移至真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的视觉强化学习在长视野规划中面临两大核心挑战：1) 长程推演中的分支未来导致动作价值分布多模态化，传统单峰规划器会产生模式平均问题；2) 视觉遮挡放大的累积模型误差使深度想象变得脆弱。

Method: ELVIS在Dreamer风格的循环状态空间模型(RSSM)中进行规划，提出两项核心创新：1) 高斯混合MPPI(GMM-MPPI)替代标准单峰MPPI，在长视野上维护多个相干假设，避免分支推演下的模式平均；2) 共享的不确定性感知lambda-return，通过潜在评论家集成定义UCB分数来调节时变lambda，在规划中自适应权衡自举与前瞻以限制累积误差。该回报同时用于训练演员-评论家先验和评估候选轨迹，实现RL目标与规划器优化的对齐。

Result: 在十四个DeepMind Control Suite视觉任务上，ELVIS相比TD-MPC2和DreamerV3建立了最先进性能。在具有严重遮挡的真实世界喷沙任务中实现零样本迁移，改善了表面质量指标，展示了超越模拟的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ELVIS成功将长视野规划在视觉控制中实用化，通过多假设规划避免模式崩溃，通过不确定性感知机制稳定深度想象，实现了模拟环境到真实世界的高效迁移，为模型强化学习的实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: A central challenge of visual control with model-based reinforcement learning (RL) is reliable long-horizon planning: long rollouts with learned latent dynamics exhibit branching futures and multi-modal action-value distributions. In addition, compounding model errors amplified by visual occlusions make deep imagination brittle. We present ELVIS, a latent model predictive controller (MPC) designed to make long-horizon planning practical. ELVIS plans in a Dreamer-style recurrent state space model (RSSM) and replaces standard unimodal model predictive path integral (MPPI) with a Gaussian-mixture MPPI that maintains multiple coherent hypotheses over long horizons, avoiding mode averaging under branching rollouts. In parallel, ELVIS stabilizes deep imagination with a shared uncertainty-aware lambda-return: an ensemble of latent critics defines an upper-confidence-bound (UCB) score that gates a time-varying lambda, adaptively trading off bootstrapping versus look-ahead to limit compounding error during planning. The same return is used both to train an actor-critic prior from imagined rollouts and to score candidate trajectories inside GMM-MPPI, aligning RL objectives with the planner's long-horizon optimization. On fourteen DeepMind Control Suite visual tasks, ELVIS establishes state-of-the-art performance compared with TD-MPC2 and DreamerV3. Finally, ELVIS transfers zero-shot to a real-world sand-spraying task with severe occlusions, improving surface-quality metrics and demonstrating robustness beyond simulation.

</details>


### [183] [SPHERE: Mitigating the Loss of Spectral Plasticity in Mixture-of-Experts for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04712)
*Lirui Luo,Guoxi Zhang,Hongming Xu,Cong Fang,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPHERE方法，通过Parseval惩罚解决持续强化学习中MoE策略的可塑性丢失问题，在MetaWorld和HumanoidBench上分别提升133%和50%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续强化学习设置中，混合专家(MoE)网络虽然能够学习多样化技能，但随着训练进行会出现可塑性丢失问题，即从新经验中学习新技能的能力随时间减弱，导致性能退化。

Method: 基于神经切线核(NTK)理论，将MoE策略中的可塑性丢失形式化为谱可塑性丢失，推导出可用单个专家特征矩阵表示的谱可塑性可处理代理指标，并据此引入SPHERE——一种针对MoE策略的实用Parseval惩罚方法，以缓解谱可塑性的丢失。

Result: 在MetaWorld和HumanoidBench基准测试中，SPHERE相比未正则化的MoE基线，在持续强化学习下平均成功率分别提升133%和50%，同时在训练过程中保持更高的谱可塑性。

Conclusion: SPHERE方法有效解决了持续强化学习中MoE策略的可塑性丢失问题，通过维持谱可塑性显著提升了MoE网络在持续学习场景下的性能表现。

Abstract: In deep reinforcement learning (DRL), an agent is trained from a stream of experience. In a continual learning setting, such agents can suffer from plasticity loss: their ability to learn new skills from new experiences diminishes over training. Recently, Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) networks have been reported to enable scaling laws and facilitate the learning of diverse skills. However, in continual reinforcement learning settings, their performance can degenerate as learning proceeds, indicating a loss of plasticity. To address this, building on Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) theory, we formalize the plasticity loss in MoE policies as a loss of spectral plasticity. We then derive a tractable proxy for spectral plasticity, one expressible in terms of individual expert feature matrices. Leveraging this proxy, we introduce SPHERE, a practical Parseval penalty tailored for MoE-based policies that alleviates the loss of spectral plasticity. On MetaWorld and HumanoidBench, SPHERE improves average success under continual RL by 133% and 50% over an unregularized MoE baseline, while maintaining higher spectral plasticity throughout training.

</details>


### [184] [Ensuring Reliability in Programming Knowledge Tracing: A Re-evaluation of Attention-augmented Models and Experimental Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04727)
*Jaewook Kim,Hyeoncheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了编程知识追踪（PKT）模型，发现以往报告的性能提升很大程度上受模型配置和序列构建方式（如忽略时间戳顺序）影响。在统一且严格的实验设置下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT之间的性能差距显著缩小，增加架构复杂度并不一定能带来更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管混合了注意力机制和RNN的PKT模型报告了优异的实证性能，但其可靠性对实现细节和实验设计选择非常敏感。作者旨在揭示并修正导致性能评估偏差的因素（如注意力维度设置、序列排序违反时序因果关系等），以建立可靠且可比较的评估标准。

Method: 作者提出了严格的评估协议：通过网格搜索在一个指定折上选择超参数并固定应用于所有交叉验证折；纠正了注意力维度设置问题；确保学生尝试序列遵循ServerTimestamp以维护时序因果性；并系统分析了作业特征和最大序列长度的影响。在CodeWorkout数据集上使用该协议重新评估了代表性的PKT模型。

Result: 在受控且一致的实验设置下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT之间的性能差距显著减小。增加架构复杂度并未始终转化为优越的性能，表明以往部分性能增益源于不恰当的实验设计而非模型本身的创新。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了对PKT模型进行严谨实验设计的重要性，并为该领域的可靠评估提供了实用的指导方案，建议后续研究关注一致的序列构建和超参数调优策略以确保结果的可比性。

Abstract: Programming Knowledge Tracing (PKT) has recently advanced through hybrid approaches that integrate attention-based feature modeling for code representation with RNN-based sequential prediction. While these models report strong empirical performance, their reliability can be sensitive to subtle implementation and experimental design choices. This study revisits representative PKT models and shows that reported gains can be substantially influenced by model configuration and sequence construction practices. We identify issues in attention dimension settings that affect performance estimates, and demonstrate that improper ordering of student attempts, such as ignoring ServerTimestamp, can violate temporal causality and lead to overly optimistic results. To ensure consistent evaluation, hyperparameters are selected via grid search guided by a single designated fold and then fixed uniformly across all folds during cross-validation. We further analyze the role of assignment-wise characteristics and systematically explore the impact of maximum sequence length. Using this protocol, we re-evaluate PKT models on the CodeWorkout dataset. Our results show that, under controlled and consistent settings, the performance gap between attention-enhanced models and standard DKT is significantly reduced, and increased architectural complexity does not consistently translate into superior performance. Beyond individual model comparisons, this work provides practical guidance for reliable and comparable evaluation in programming knowledge tracing.

</details>


### [185] [Using Common Random Numbers for Simulation-based Planning with Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04732)
*Sandarbh Yadav,Frederic J Maliakkal,Harshad Khadilkar,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在基于模拟的规划中使用公共随机数来降低相对效用方差的简单方法，并在养老金发放和Ludo游戏中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模拟的规划（如Rollout算法）是随机环境中决策的主流技术，其核心是通过采样模型生成轨迹并估计动作效用。然而，采样过程固有的方差会影响动作选择的准确性，因此需要探索减少方差的方法以提高规划质量。

Method: 本文研究了在模拟过程中使用公共随机数（Common Random Numbers）的技术。研究者推导出了一个简单的方案，当模拟调用超过一定深度的rollout策略时，可以证明该方法能够降低相对效用估计的方差。这是一种方差缩减技术（Variance Reduction Technique），旨在通过控制随机性来获得更稳定的效用对比。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 在合成任务上，该方案确实提高了任务性能；(2) 在养老金发放任务的单步前瞻规划中取得了实际应用效果；(3) 在Ludo游戏中部署著名的UCT算法时也验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在基于模拟的规划中使用公共随机数是一种有效的方差缩减策略。该创新不仅具有理论保证（可证明的方差减少），而且在实际应用（如财务规划和游戏AI）中具有广泛的意义，能够显著提升决策质量。

Abstract: Simulation-based planning with rollouts is a widely-deployed technique for decision making in stochastic environments. The primary instrument of simulation-based planning is a sampling model, which is repeatedly called to generate trajectories and estimate the utilities of available actions. Among the actions thus explored, one with the maximum estimated utility is then executed. In this paper, we examine the effect of using common random numbers in the simulation process. We obtain a simple recipe for (provably) reducing variance in relative utility when simulations invoke a rollout policy beyond some depth. Experiments on synthetic tasks confirm that our scheme improves task performance. The broader significance of our innovation is apparent from two practical applications: (1) single-step lookahead planning in a pension-disbursement task, and (2) a deployment of the well-known UCT algorithm for the game of Ludo.

</details>


### [186] [OSAQ: Outlier Self-Absorption for Accurate Low-bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04738)
*Zhikai Li,Zhen Dong,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSAQ方法，利用Hessian矩阵的低秩一致性识别稳定零空间，通过加性权重变换抑制权重离群值，实现无推理开销的低比特LLM量化，2-bit下困惑度降低超40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数规模庞大导致推理资源消耗高、延迟大；权重量化虽可缓解内存瓶颈，但权重中固有的系统性离群值严重影响量化性能，现有方法效果不佳。

Method: 提出Outlier Self-Absorption Quantization (OSAQ)。观察到Hessian矩阵跨输入呈现低秩一致性，某些方向曲率趋零，据此识别稳定的Hessian零空间。在该零空间内线性组合向量构建加性权重变换以抑制离群值，且不影响任务损失。该变换可离线吸收至权重，无需层间变换和迭代训练，通过闭式解高效求解。

Result: OSAQ有效抑制权重离群值并提升低比特量化性能。在2-bit量化场景下，与GPTQ结合时，困惑度相比vanilla GPTQ降低超过40%。

Conclusion: OSAQ通过利用Hessian零空间实现无推理开销的离群值抑制，为大语言模型低比特权重量化提供了一种高效、有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities. However, their massive parameter scale leads to significant resource consumption and latency during inference. Post-training weight-only quantization offers a promising solution by reducing model size and accelerating token generation through alleviating the memory-bound issue. Nevertheless, the presence of inherent systematic outliers in weights continues to be a major obstacle. While existing methods, such as scaling and rotation, attempt to address this issue, the performance remains unsatisfactory. In this paper, we propose Outlier Self-Absorption Quantization (OSAQ), which performs additive weight suppression guided by the second-order low-rank property for low-bit weight-only quantization of LLMs. Specifically, we observe that the Hessian exhibits low-rank consistency across different inputs, with certain directions consistently showing vanishing curvature. Leveraging this property, we identify a stable null space of the Hessian and then construct an additive weight transformation by linearly combining the vectors within this null space, thereby suppressing weight outliers without affecting the task loss. This additive transformation can be absorbed into the weights offline, requiring no inter-layer transformations and introducing no inference overhead. Moreover, the construction is efficiently achieved by a closed-form solution, without resource-intensive training or iterative procedures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OSAQ effectively suppresses outliers and enhances low-bit quantization performance. For instance, in 2-bit quantization, OSAQ, when integrated with GPTQ, achieves over 40% lower perplexity compared to vanilla GPTQ.

</details>


### [187] [MixINN: Accelerating Plant Breeding by Combining Mixed Models and Deep Learning for Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04744)
*Aike Potze,Fred van Eeuwijk,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MixINN方法，结合混合模型与深度神经网络预测基因型-环境互作，成功提高了气候变化下玉米高产基因型的预测精度，有助于培育气候适应性作物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化改变了作物生长环境，导致作物基因型的相对表现发生偏移，这对依赖基因型排名与选择的植物育种过程构成挑战。准确预测未来环境下的产量相对变化对保障粮食安全至关重要，但目前这仍是植物育种领域的开放性难题，且在AI社区中探索较少。

Method: 提出MixINN方法：首先利用混合模型分离出高质量的基因型-环境互作标签，随后利用深度神经网络预测新作物品种在未来环境条件下的互作效应。研究在美国大陆的玉米多环境试验数据上进行了评估。

Result: MixINN在预测基因型排名方面优于当前植物育种方法。在识别前20%最高产的玉米基因型时，平均产量提升了5.8%；在针对特定种植环境时，产量提升进一步提高至7.2%。

Conclusion: MixINN在真实育种项目中展现了具有竞争力的结果，证明了AI研究在加速气候适应性作物开发和改善气候变化下未来粮食安全方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Plant breeding underpins global food security through incremental, accumulating improvements in crop yield, quality and sustainability, achieved via repeated cycles of crop ranking, selection and crossing. Climate change disrupts this process by altering local growing conditions, thereby shifting the relative performance of crop genotypes. Predicting these relative changes in yield is critical for food security. Yet, this problem remains an open challenge in plant breeding, and relatively unexplored within the AI community. We propose MixINN, an approach that first isolates high-quality genotype-environment interaction labels using mixed models, and then predicts these interactions for new crop varieties in future environmental conditions with a deep neural network. We evaluate our method on a corn multi-environment trial across the continental United States and show improved prediction of genotype ranking over current plant breeding methods. MixINN demonstrated superior performance in identifying the 20% most productive corn genotypes, leading to a 5.8% higher average yield, which further improved to 7.2% when targeting specific growing environments. These are competitive results for real-world breeding programs, demonstrating the potential of AI research in accelerating the development of climate-adapted crops, and improving future food security under climate change.

</details>


### [188] [Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC for Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04793)
*Matan Pagi,Zohar Sorek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双线性Mamba-Koopman神经MPC，通过引入控制相关的隐状态耦合，解决了传统方法中条件独立性约束导致的适应性不足问题，在时变条件下显著提升了预测精度和闭环控制鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Koopman的神经MPC模型通过强制系统算子独立于当前控制输入来保持凸性，但这种条件独立性约束限制了系统在单个MPC时域内对动态变化的适应能力，特别是在时变条件和延迟规划执行场景下表现不佳。

Method: 提出双线性Mamba-Koopman神经MPC方法，在隐状态动力学中引入控制相关的耦合，使有效算子能够适应当前输入。该方法通过低秩结构仅增加不到1%的参数，是标准线性条件独立性公式的严格推广，并支持精确的模型雅可比矩阵，可在标准信赖域假设下实现具有单调下降和KKT收敛保证的高效序列凸规划（SCP）。

Result: 在CartPole和RSCP基准测试中，该方法在时不变和时变场景下均匹配或提升了预测精度，特别是在存在控制-状态耦合结构的区域有严格增益。在RSCP TV任务中，迭代SCP显著改善了时域内适应性并大幅稳定训练；在延迟重规划实验中，双线性模型在陈旧规划执行下退化更缓慢，在CartPole TV上保持一致优势，在RSCP TV上具有显著更大的鲁棒性边界。

Conclusion: 控制相关的隐状态动力学为变化条件下的鲁棒MPC提供了一种简单有效的机制，证明了在保持计算效率和理论收敛性的同时，通过最小化扩展实现更强的适应性和鲁棒性是可行的。

Abstract: Koopman-based neural MPC models generate time-varying dynamics from historical data, but preserve convexity by enforcing that the system operator is independent of the current control input. This conditional independence constraint limits adaptation to changing dynamics within a single MPC horizon, particularly under time-varying conditions and under stale-plan execution.
  We propose Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC, a minimal extension that introduces control-dependent coupling in the latent dynamics, allowing the effective operator to adapt to the current input. The resulting model is a strict generalization of the standard linear, conditional-independence formulation, adds less than 1% parameters through a low-rank structure, and admits exact model Jacobians that enable efficient Sequential Convex Programming (SCP) with monotone-descent and KKT convergence results under standard trust-region assumptions.
  Across CartPole and RSCP benchmarks in time-invariant and time-varying regimes, the proposed model matches or improves forecasting accuracy on every cell when training noise is averaged out, with strict gains where control-state coupling is structurally present. Its main closed-loop gains appear in the RSCP TV task, where iterative SCP improves adaptation within the horizon and substantially stabilizes training; in CartPole TV, the gains are modest but consistent. In delayed re-planning experiments on the time-varying variants, the bilinear model degrades more gracefully under stale-plan execution, maintaining a consistent advantage on CartPole TV and a substantially larger robustness margin on RSCP TV. These results show that control-dependent latent dynamics provide a simple and effective mechanism for robust MPC under varying conditions.

</details>


### [189] [A Biased Nonnegative Block Term Tensor Decomposition Model for Dynamic QoS Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04813)
*Wenjing Liu,Yujia Lei,Qu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BNBT动态QoS预测框架，通过块项张量分解、线性偏置项和高效更新算法，显著提升了服务质量预测精度，优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于CP分解或Tucker分解的QoS预测方法受限于其固有结构特性，无法准确捕捉用户-服务交互中复杂且动态的依赖关系，限制了预测性能。

Method: 提出基于偏置非负块项张量分解模型（BNBT）的动态QoS预测框架，具体包括：1) 采用块项张量分解增强潜在特征学习的表示能力；2) 引入线性偏置项进一步提升预测精度；3) 设计面向张量的单元素依赖非负乘法更新算法（SLF-NMUT）进行高效参数估计。

Result: 在真实世界QoS数据集上的大量实验表明，BNBT框架在预测精度上持续优于多种最先进的QoS预测方法。

Conclusion: BNBT框架通过改进张量分解结构和优化算法，有效解决了现有方法在捕捉复杂动态依赖方面的不足，为QoS预测提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid development of cloud computing and Web services, Quality of Service (QoS) has become a key criterion for service selection and recommendation. Tensor latent feature analysis provides an effective way to model multidimensional QoS data, and most existing QoS prediction methods are mainly based on Canonical Polyadic (CP) decomposition or Tucker decomposition. However, constrained by their inherent structural properties, these methods cannot accurately capture the complex and dynamic dependencies in user-service interactions, which limits their prediction performance. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dynamic QoS prediction framework based on the Biased Nonnegative Block Term Tensor Decomposition Model, termed BNBT. Specifically, the proposed framework is developed from three aspects: (1) block term tensor decomposition is employed to enhance the representation capability of latent feature learning; (2) linear bias terms are incorporated to further improve prediction accuracy; and (3) a tensor-oriented single-element-dependent nonnegative multiplicative update algorithm, called SLF-NMUT, is designed for efficient parameter estimation. Extensive experiments on real-world QoS datasets demonstrate that the proposed BNBT framework consistently outperforms several state-of-the-art QoS prediction methods in terms of prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [190] [Unsat Core Prediction through Polarity-Aware Representation Learning over Clause-Literal Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04819)
*Zhenchao Sun,Shuai Ma,Ping Lu,Chongyang Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子句-文字超图的极性感知表示学习框架，通过超图建模和高阶交互捕获，以及极性分解机制和一致性正则化，显著提升了SAT问题的求解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAT问题图神经网络方法主要将SAT公式建模为二部图或有向无环图，难以有效捕捉文字与子句之间的高阶交互作用。此外，这些方法在建模SAT问题内在的极性相关属性（如变量的正负文字之间的互补关系）方面存在局限性，限制了模型的表达能力和求解性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于子句-文字超图的极性感知表示学习框架。首先，将SAT公式建模为增强子句关联图的子句-文字超图，以捕获高阶结构交互。其次，引入极性感知分解机制，将变量表示分解为极性不变和极性等变组件，显式建模正负文字之间的关系，并在超图结构上传播结果文字表示。此外，还引入极性反转一致性正则化，在训练过程中强化极性一致的表示。

Result: 在多个SAT数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在unsat-core预测等SAT相关任务上取得了优异性能，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入超图建模、极性感知分解机制和一致性正则化，本文方法有效解决了现有方法在捕获高阶交互和极性相关属性方面的不足，为SAT问题的图神经网络求解提供了新的思路，具有重要的理论意义和应用价值。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have been widely used in Boolean satisfiability (SAT) tasks to learn structural information from SAT formulas. The goal of these studies is to solve SAT instances or to enhance SAT solvers, including tasks such as unsat-core prediction. However, most existing approaches model a SAT formula as a bipartite graph or a directed acyclic graph, which are less expressive in capturing higher-order interactions among literals and clauses. Moreover, these approaches are limited in modeling intrinsic polarity-related properties of SAT, such as the complementary relationship between the positive and negative literals of a variable. To address these limitations, we propose a polarity-aware representation learning framework over clause-literal hypergraphs. We model SAT formulas as clause-literal hypergraphs augmented with a clause incidence graph to capture higher-order structural interactions. We then introduce a polarity-aware decomposed mechanism that separates variable representations into polarity invariant and equivariant components, explicitly modeling the relationship between positive and negative literals, with the resulting literal representations propagated along the hypergraph structure. We further incorporate a polarity-inversion consistency regularization to reinforce polarity-consistent representations during training. Experimental results on multiple SAT datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [191] [Improving FMQA via Initial Training Data Design Considering Marginal Bit Coverage in One-Hot Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04825)
*Taiga Hayashi,Yuya Seki,Kotaro Terada,Yosuke Mukasa,Shuta Kikuchi,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对FMQA算法在处理独热编码变量时存在的初始采样覆盖不足问题，提出通过拉丁超立方采样（LHS）和Sobol序列构建初始训练数据，以确保完全边际位覆盖，从而提升优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将FMQA应用于通过独热编码表示的整数或离散化连续变量时，传统的均匀随机初始采样会导致许多二元变量从未被激活（取值为1）。这导致FM中对应的参数无法从观测响应中获得直接的梯度更新，进而影响模型性能和优化效果。

Method: 设计了具有完全边际位覆盖的初始训练数据，确保独热编码后的每一个二元变量在初始数据中至少被激活一次。具体采用了两种空间填充采样方法：拉丁超立方采样（LHS）和Sobol序列，分别提出了LHS-FMQA和Sobol'-FMQA两种改进算法。

Result: 在包含17个和32个设计变量的人力飞机机翼形状优化基准测试中，两种提出的方法均获得了比基准FMQA数值更高的平均最终巡航速度。在32变量的问题上，这种性能优势更为显著。

Conclusion: 通过确保初始训练数据的完全边际位覆盖，可以有效解决FMQA在处理独热编码变量时的参数更新缺失问题，显著提升高维黑盒优化问题的求解质量。

Abstract: Factorization machine with quadratic-optimization annealing (FMQA) is a black-box optimization method that combines a factorization machine (FM) surrogate with QUBO-based search by an Ising machine. When FMQA is applied to integer or discretized continuous variables via one-hot encoding, uniform random initial sampling can leave many binary variables never active in the initial training data, and the corresponding FM parameters receive no direct gradient updates from the observed responses. We address this by designing the initial training data to achieve complete marginal bit coverage, namely, ensuring that every binary variable obtained by one-hot encoding takes the value one at least once. We use two space-filling sampling methods, Latin hypercube sampling (LHS) and the Sobol' sequence, yielding LHS-FMQA and Sobol'-FMQA. On the human-powered aircraft wing-shape optimization benchmark with 17 and 32 design variables, both proposed methods achieved numerically higher mean final cruising speeds than the baseline FMQA, with the advantage more pronounced on the 32-variable problem.

</details>


### [192] [Trustworthy Federated Label Distribution Learning under Annotation Quality Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04827)
*Junxiang Wu,Zhiqiang Kou,Hongwei Zeng,Wenke Huang,Biao Liu,Hanlin Gu,Yuheng Jia,Di Jiang,Yang Liu,Xin Geng,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦标签分布学习（Fed-LDL）中因数据隔离导致的异质标注质量问题，提出了一个质量感知框架FedQual，通过质量自适应客户端训练和可靠性感知服务器聚合机制解决了信任困境，理论证明优于统一校准，并在构建的四个新基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签分布学习（LDL）的成功依赖于高保真度的标签分布，但在隐私敏感应用场景下，联邦学习中的数据隔离导致各客户端的标注质量异质，使得本地更新可靠性不均，传统的基于样本量的聚合方法（如FedAvg）失效，形成了信任困境。

Method: 提出FedQual框架，包含两个耦合机制：(i) 基于全局语义锚点的质量自适应客户端训练，用于校准低质量客户端同时保留高质量客户端的自主性；(ii) 可靠性感知的服务器聚合，根据有效可靠信息而非原始样本量对客户端贡献进行重加权。同时构建了四个带有受控标注质量差异的Fed-LDL基准数据集。

Result: 理论证明在异质监督质量下，客户端特定校准严格优于任何统一校准方法；在构建的四个新基准（FER-LDL、FI-LDL、PIPAL-LDL、KADID-LDL）上进行了大量实验，验证了FedQual的有效性。

Conclusion: FedQual成功解决了联邦标签分布学习中的异质标注质量问题，通过质量感知的训练和聚合机制实现了更可靠的联邦学习，为隐私敏感场景下的LDL应用提供了新思路和基准。

Abstract: Label Distribution Learning (LDL) models supervision as an instance-wise probability distribution, enabling fine-grained learning under inherent ambiguity, but its success relies on high-fidelity label distributions that are costly to obtain and thus often noisy. Motivated by privacy-sensitive applications, we study Federated Label Distribution Learning (Fed-LDL), where data isolation further induces heterogeneous annotation quality across clients, making local updates unevenly reliable and breaking sample-size-based aggregation (e.g., FedAvg). To address this trust dilemma, we propose FedQual, a quality-aware Fed-LDL framework with two coupled mechanisms: (i) quality-adaptive client training guided by a global semantic anchor that calibrates low-quality clients while preserving high-quality autonomy, and (ii) reliability-aware server aggregation that reweights client contributions by effective reliable information rather than raw sample size. To enable rigorous evaluation, we construct four new Fed-LDL benchmarks (FER-LDL, FI-LDL, PIPAL-LDL, and KADID-LDL) with controlled annotation quality disparity. We further provide a theoretical guarantee showing that under heterogeneous supervision quality, client-specific calibration is strictly better than any uniform calibration. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of FedQual.

</details>


### [193] [Concurrence of Symmetry Breaking and Nonlocality Phase Transitions in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04830)
*Yifan F. Zhang,Fangjun Hu,Guangkuo Liu,Mert Okyay,Xun Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在实践中统一了扩散模型相变的两种理论视角，发现现代扩散Transformer中非局域性与对称破缺的临界时间近乎同时发生，为条件引导和全局去噪的时机提供了具体诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成动力学中存在临界时间窗口的相变现象，存在两种互补的理论解释：对称破缺观点（轨迹分叉到不同语义极小值）和非局域性观点（局部去噪失效）。本文旨在研究现代扩散Transformer中这两种相变概念是否同时发生。

Method: 通过评估生成轨迹的动力学过程和生成结果，分析现代扩散Transformer模型在生成过程中非局域性临界时间与对称破缺临界时间的出现时机及其相互关系。

Result: 实验观察到非局域性临界时间与对称破缺临界时间在现代扩散Transformer中近乎同时发生，首次在实践中统一了扩散模型相变的两种理论概念。

Conclusion: 该研究为扩散模型何时及为何依赖条件引导和全局去噪提供了具体诊断工具，能够实现模型效率的原则性评估，并指导架构设计和采样方案优化以避免不必要的计算开销。

Abstract: Diffusion models undergo a phase transition in a critical time window during generation dynamics, with two complementary diagnoses of criticality. The symmetry breaking picture views the critical window as when trajectories bifurcate into different semantic minima of the energy landscape, whereas the nonlocality picture views the critical window as when local denoising fails. We study whether two notions of such phase transitions are concurrent in modern diffusion transformers. By evaluating the dynamics and outcomes of the generation trajectory, we observe a near-simultaneous occurrence of the non-locality and symmetry breaking critical times. Our work is the first to unify the two notions of phase transitions in practice: it provides a concrete diagnostic for when and why diffusion models rely on conditioning and global denoising, enabling principled evaluation of model efficiency and guiding the design of architectures and sampling schemes that avoid unnecessary computation.

</details>


### [194] [Replay-Based Continual Learning for Physics-Informed Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04832)
*Yizheng Wang,Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk,Yinghua Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经算子在分布外（OOD）数据上性能显著下降的问题，将持续学习引入基于物理信息的神经算子（以Transolver架构为例），提出了一种基于重放的简单有效策略，成功缓解了灾难性遗忘并提升了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子在分布内（ID）问题上表现优异，但在面对分布外（OOD）数据时性能会显著下降。为了解决这一局限性，需要提升模型在处理新数据时的适应能力，同时避免对旧知识的遗忘。

Method: 提出一种基于重放的持续学习策略，该方法完全基于物理信息，仅依赖输入场和物理约束进行无标签训练。当新OOD数据出现时，通过基于蒸馏的约束引入少量历史数据以保留先前知识并缓解灾难性遗忘；同时引入迁移学习LoRA以实现对新数据的快速适应。

Result: 在三个代表性物理问题（流体力学Darcy流问题、生物力学二维超弹性脑肿瘤问题、固体力学三维线性弹性三重周期最小表面问题）上进行了系统验证。结果表明，该方法有效缓解了对已学数据的灾难性遗忘，同时保持了对新数据的快速适应能力。与传统的联合训练策略相比，显著提高了训练效率并降低了额外的内存使用和计算成本。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将持续学习与物理信息神经算子相结合，为解决神经算子在OOD数据上的性能退化问题提供了一种高效、低成本的解决方案，在保持对旧任务记忆的同时实现了对新任务的快速适应。

Abstract: Neural operators generally demonstrate strong predictive performance on in-distribution (ID) problems. However, a critical limitation of existing methods is their significant performance degradation when encountering out-of-distribution (OOD) data. To address this issue, this work introduces continual learning into physics-informed neural operators, with particular emphasis on neural operators built upon the Transolver architecture, and proposes a simple yet effective replay-based continual learning strategy. The proposed method is fully physics-informed and does not require labeled data, relying solely on input fields together with physical constraints for training. When new OOD data become available, a small number of past data are incorporated through a distillation-based constraint to preserve previously acquired knowledge and alleviate catastrophic forgetting. Meanwhile, a transfer learning LoRA is employed to enable rapid adaptation to the new data. The proposed framework is systematically validated on three representative physical problems, including the Darcy flow problem in fluid mechanics, a two-dimensional hyperelastic brain tumor problem in biomechanics, and a three-dimensional linear elastic Triply Periodic Minimal Surfaces problem in solid mechanics. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting on previously learned data while maintaining fast adaptability to new data. Compared with conventional joint training strategies, the proposed method significantly improves training efficiency while reducing additional memory usage and computational cost.

</details>


### [195] [Bridging Input Feature Spaces Towards Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04834)
*Moshe Eliasof,Krishna Sri Ipsit Mantri,Beatrice Bevilacqua,Bruno Ribeiro,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ALL-IN方法，通过将节点特征投影到共享随机空间并基于协方差统计构建表示，解决了图学习中不同数据集输入特征不一致导致的迁移困难问题，实现了跨数据集的免重训迁移学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与视觉和语言领域不同，图学习缺乏统一的输入空间。不同图数据集的输入特征在语义、值范围和维度上均存在差异，这种不对齐阻碍了图模型在不同数据集间的泛化，限制了其作为基础模型的应用潜力。

Method: 提出ALL-IN方法：将节点特征投影到共享的随机空间，通过基于协方差的统计量构建节点表示，从而消除对原始特征空间的依赖。证明了所计算的节点协方差算子和节点表示对输入特征的置换具有分布不变性，且期望算子对输入特征的一般正交变换也具有不变性。

Result: ALL-IN在多样的节点级和图级任务上表现优异，能够在未见过的、具有新输入特征的数据集上直接运行，无需修改模型架构或重新训练，验证了其跨数据集迁移能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果为构建输入无关、可迁移的图模型指明了有前景的方向，证明了通过特征投影和协方差统计可以实现跨异构特征空间的模型迁移。

Abstract: Unlike vision and language domains, graph learning lacks a shared input space, as input features differ across graph datasets not only in semantics, but also in value ranges and dimensionality. This misalignment prevents graph models from generalizing across datasets, limiting their use as foundation models. In this work, we propose ALL-IN, a simple and theoretically grounded method that enables transferability across datasets with different input features. Our approach projects node features into a shared random space and constructs representations via covariance-based statistics, thus eliminating dependence on the original feature space. We show that the computed node-covariance operators and the resulting node representations are invariant in distribution to permutations of the input features. We further demonstrate that the expected operator exhibits invariance to general orthogonal transformations of the input features. Empirically, ALL-IN achieves strong performance across diverse node- and graph-level tasks on unseen datasets with new input features, without requiring architecture changes or retraining. These results point to a promising direction for input-agnostic, transferable graph models.

</details>


### [196] [Hybrid Iterative Neural Low-Regularity Integrator for Nonlinear Dispersive Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04853)
*Zhangyong Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HIN-LRI混合框架，通过神经算子修正经典数值求解器的结构化截断误差，实现了非线性色散PDE的高精度、稳定且高效的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的数值求解器在处理非线性色散偏微分方程时，受限于低正则性积分器的结构化截断误差，难以在保持稳定性的同时实现高精度。纯数据驱动方法则常面临泛化性和稳定性问题。因此，研究动机在于结合两者的优势，设计一种既能保持数值方法稳定性又能通过学习提高精度的混合方法。

Method: 该方法构建了一个混合框架：1) 使用基础低正则性积分器作为底层求解器，提供一致的一阶近似；2) 引入轻量级神经网络，在低维潜在流形上学习解析方法无法闭合的残差缺陷；3) 对神经修正项施加显式时间步长缩放，确保其Lipschitz贡献保持O(τ)量级，从而保证稳定性；4) 采用solver-in-the-loop端到端训练策略，展开完整迭代并使用Bourgain型范数惩罚轨迹误差。

Result: 理论分析表明，在给定假设下，全局误差满足$C(\varepsilon_{net}+δ)\,τ^γ\ln(1/τ)$，其中$\varepsilon_{net}$衡量网络逼近质量，$δ$为训练不足量。在三个色散基准测试上的实验表明，HIN-LRI在粗糙数据上相比解析积分器、分裂方法和神经PDE代理均提升了精度，同时具有稳定的空间细化能力、有效的分布外迁移能力和适度的在线开销。

Conclusion: HIN-LRI成功地将数值求解器的物理约束与神经网络的学习能力相结合，在保证稳定性（步长一致有界）和低计算开销的前提下，显著提升了非线性色散PDE的求解精度，为科学计算领域提供了一种有效的混合建模范式。

Abstract: We propose HIN-LRI, a hybrid framework that augments a classical numerical solver with a neural operator trained to correct the solver's structured truncation error. A base low-regularity integrator provides a consistent first-order approximation to nonlinear dispersive PDEs, while a lightweight neural network, operating on a low-dimensional latent manifold, learns the residual defect that analytical methods cannot close. An explicit time-step scaling on the neural correction ensures that its Lipschitz contribution remains $\mathcal{O}(τ)$, yielding a Gronwall stability factor bounded uniformly in the step size and independent of the spatial resolution. The network is trained end-to-end through a solver-in-the-loop objective that unrolls the full iteration and penalises trajectory error in a Bourgain-type norm, aligning learning with multi-step solver dynamics rather than isolated one-step targets. Under stated assumptions, the global error satisfies $C(\varepsilon_{net}+δ)\,τ^γ\ln(1/τ)$, where $\varepsilon_{net}$ measures the network approximation quality and $δ$ the training shortfall. Experiments on three dispersive benchmarks with rough data show that HIN-LRI improves accuracy over analytical integrators, splitting methods, and neural PDE surrogates, with stable spatial refinement, effective out-of-distribution transfer, and modest online overhead.

</details>


### [197] [Uncertainty-Aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04874)
*Huatian Zhang,Zhendong Mao,Lei Zhang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UE-DPO方法，通过引入令牌级的认知不确定性来引导模型探索自身的认知缺陷，解决了现有DPO方法中自参照偏差的问题，从而实现了更细粒度的视觉对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的直接偏好优化（DPO）方法在多模态大语言模型中利用模型自评估的视觉敏感度信号来分配训练重点，这导致了自参照偏差：模型会强化已学好的视觉线索，而忽略难以感知但关键的细节，限制了更深层次的对齐。

Method: 提出不确定性感知探索性直接偏好优化（UE-DPO）。首先量化模型在给定图像上进行令牌预测失败时的不确定性；然后基于不确定性感知的探索强度，对偏好样本中视觉缺陷明显的令牌施加更多学习压力，同时减轻对非偏好样本中有益知识的过度惩罚。

Result: 提供了该方法的理论证明，并通过大量实验验证了UE-DPO的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UE-DPO通过显式量化和利用令牌级认知不确定性，成功缓解了自参照偏差，使模型能够主动发现并纠正认知缺陷，实现了更精准的多模态对齐。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has proven to be an effective solution for mitigating hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by learning from preference pairs. One of its key challenges lies in how to transfer the sequence-level preference into fine-grained supervision on visual fidelity. To safeguard vision-related tokens that are prone to hallucination, existing methods typically allocate training emphasis according to the model's self-assessed visual sensitivity signals. However, such sensitivity, estimated by a model still under training, introduces self-referential bias: reinforcing already well-learned visual cues while neglecting hard-to-perceive but critical details, thereby limiting deeper alignment. In this work, we propose an Uncertainty-aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization (UE-DPO) method for MLLMs, which enables the model to uncover its cognitive deficiencies and actively explore for self-correction, guided by token-level epistemic uncertainty. Specifically, we first quantify the uncertainty from the model's failure to ground token predictions in the given image. Then, based on an uncertainty-aware exploration intensity, we encourage more learning pressure on visually deficient tokens in preferred samples, and alleviate the over-penalization of beneficial knowledge in dispreferred samples. Further, we provide a theoretical justification for our method, and extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [198] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双轴诊断框架（容量φ+方向G），揭示注意力路由失败的两种形状模式，并通过理论证明和实验验证其在幻觉检测中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的幻觉问题源于注意力机制的异常路由模式（过度集中或过度分散），现有谱方法仅分析对称成分而忽略了方向性信息，无法准确诊断信息流动方向。

Method: 1) 证明转置不变谱诊断具有结构性的方向盲性，提出非对称系数G作为方向控制参数；2) 给出典型因果架构的二分Cheeger景观闭式解；3) 构建双轴诊断体系：容量指标φ（Cheeger常数）和方向指标G（非对称系数）。

Result: 均匀因果注意力的φ≥1/5（n无关），窗口注意力为O(w/n)；在长度控制评估中，传输特征的LC-AUROC达0.62-0.84（8B参数模型），HaluEval和MedHallu基准呈现相反极性，验证了瓶颈主导与扩散主导模式的极性预测。

Conclusion: 注意力失败模式具有可诊断的形状特征（非仅数值差异），双轴诊断框架能够有效检测幻觉并预测不同基准的极性反转，为大语言模型的可靠性评估提供了理论可解释的量化工具。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate in predictable ways: attention routing fails by over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or by spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport capacity; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally orientation-blind (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a quantitative converse establishing the asymmetry coefficient $G$ as the unique control parameter for direction.
  Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$ with worst cut at $t^\ast/n \approx 0.32$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (LC-AUROC from 0.62 to 0.84) on tested models up to 8B parameters, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.

</details>


### [199] [Regime-Conditioned Evaluation in Multi-Context Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04895)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现迁移贝叶斯优化方法的性能排名会随预算比率反转；提出可移植机制得分PRS预测最优策略，并设计RegimePlanner算法实现动态切换，在HPO任务上全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移贝叶斯优化对比研究通常估计隐藏机制变量上的平均处理效应，而实践者需要针对特定先验质量、预算比率和指标的条件效应。对40篇论文的审计发现98%的研究从未将B/|A|作为受控变量轴，导致排名不稳定。

Method: 提出可移植机制得分PRS=(B/|A|)(1-ρ)来刻画策略转换点，其中ρ为先验秩相关系数，可从引导上下文中估计。设计RegimePlanner算法在线估计ρ并动态切换采集策略。建立分层模型分析79个实验条件，并提出无免费排行榜命题解释无条件排名的不稳定性。

Result: 在GDSC2基准上，仅改变预算即可反转排名(B=50时Greedy优于UCB 0.050 Hit@1，B=100时UCB优于Greedy 0.035 Hit@1)。PRS得分β=0.50(p=1.1e-9)，成功预测5个已发表的排名反转案例。RegimePlanner在16个HPO-B搜索空间B=100时全胜，在GDSC2上超越匹配的{Greedy,UCB}上下文预言机18%。预注册预测准确率67.5%，在EMA先验族内超过90%。

Conclusion: 无条件排名因条件平均处理效应跨机制变号而不稳定，报告的结论依赖于基准混合。建议在宣称采集策略优势时同时报告B/|A|、ρ、K和指标等关键参数，以提供更有参考价值的条件效应评估。

Abstract: Published transfer-BO comparisons often estimate an average treatment effect of acquisition choice over hidden regime variables, while practitioners need the conditional effect for their specific prior quality, budget ratio, and metric. An audit of 40 transfer-BO papers from NeurIPS, ICML, ICLR, AISTATS, UAI, TMLR, JMLR, and AutoML-Conf (2022-2025) finds that 98% never vary B/|A| as a controlled axis. On the same GDSC2 benchmark, changing only the budget reverses the ranking: at B=50, Greedy outperforms UCB by 0.050 Hit@1, while at B=100, UCB outperforms Greedy by 0.035. We capture this transition with the Portable Regime Score PRS=(B/|A|)(1-rho), where rho is the prior rank correlation and can be estimated from pilot contexts before the main comparison. Across 79 conditions spanning chemistry, drug-response biology, and HPO, a hierarchical model gives beta=0.50 (p=1.1e-9), and 19% of conditions fall in an equivalence zone where |advantage|<0.01 Hit@1. In five published reversal cases, PRS predicts the winner from pre-comparison observables. A No-Free-Leaderboard proposition explains why unconditional rankings are unstable: when CATE changes sign across regimes, the reported ATE becomes a function of benchmark mixture. RegimePlanner, which estimates rho online and switches acquisition accordingly, wins all 16 HPO-B search spaces at B=100 and exceeds the matched {Greedy,UCB} per-context oracle on GDSC2 by 18%. Pre-registered predictions achieve 27/40=67.5% overall accuracy and above 90% within EMA prior families. The practical protocol is simple: report B/|A|, rho, K, and metric alongside any claimed acquisition advantage.

</details>


### [200] [A geometric relation of the error introduced by sampling a language model's output distribution to its internal state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04899)
*Albert F. Modenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现GPT类语言模型对生成点敏感，该敏感性源于token嵌入的几何结构；由此导出的几何曲率具有语义含义，能反映模型在象棋推理任务中的世界模型结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于GPT类语言模型在生成过程中对单个token变化表现出敏感性，尤其是在预测概率分布分散于多个token的生成点。作者希望理解这种敏感性的本质来源，并探究token空间的几何结构是否与模型的内部表示及语义理解相关联。

Method: 将模型敏感性视为几何性质，推导出一个仅依赖于token嵌入几何结构的$\mathfrak{so}(n)$值1-形式。通过计算该几何对象的曲率，分析其与语义信息的关联。在象棋推理任务上，使用现成的指令微调模型进行实验验证。

Result: 发现尽管该曲率源自纯几何结构，却具有明确的语义含义：在象棋推理任务中，曲率与模型的内部世界模型相耦合，变换按棋盘区域聚类并遵循棋子重要性规则。这表明token空间的几何结构直接反映了模型内部表示问题的方式。

Conclusion: token嵌入空间的几何结构与模型的语义表示之间存在内在联系，纯几何导出的曲率能够揭示模型的内部世界模型结构，为理解语言模型的表示学习机制提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: GPT-style language models are sensitive to single-token changes at generation points where the predicted probability distribution is spread across multiple tokens. Viewing this sensitivity as a geometric property, we derive an $\mathfrak{so}(n)$-valued 1-form that depends only on the geometry of the token embeddings. Despite this purely geometric origin, we show that its curvature is semantically meaningful: On chess reasoning tasks, the curvature couples to the world model of an off-the-shelf instruction-tuned model, with transformations clustering by board region and respecting piece importance. Our findings suggest that token space geometry directly reflects how models internally represent problems.

</details>


### [201] [Cross-Model Consistency of Feature Importance in Electrospinning: Separating Robust from Model-Dependent Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04905)
*Mehrab Mahdian,Ferenc Ender,Tamas Pardy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比21种机器学习模型发现，单一模型的特征重要性分析可能不可靠。虽然多个模型预测精度相当，但特征排名差异显著，其中溶液浓度最稳健，而流速和电压排名波动大，强调了跨模型验证的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静电纺丝研究中，大多数仅依赖单一机器学习模型来分析工艺参数的重要性，隐含假设所得特征重要性是稳健且可复现的。然而，这一假设缺乏验证，可能导致对参数影响力的误判。

Method: 基于96组聚乙烯醇（PVA）静电纺丝实验数据，训练并对比了涵盖线性、树模型、核方法、神经网络和实例学习等5大类共21种机器学习模型。采用SHAP值作为统一的解释框架计算特征重要性，并利用基于排序的统计分析量化模型间的一致性。

Result: 预测性能与解释可靠性是两个独立的属性。尽管多个模型取得了相当的预测精度，但在特征重要性排序上存在显著差异。溶液浓度被一致识别为最具影响力的参数（变异性=0），而流速和施加电压的排名变异性极高（>0.9），表明其结果强烈依赖于所选模型。

Conclusion: 单一机器学习模型得出的特征重要性可能不可靠，特别是在小样本实验数据集中。研究强调，在机器学习辅助的静电纺丝研究中，必须进行跨模型验证以确保解释的可信度。

Abstract: Electrospinning is a highly sensitive fabrication process in which small variations in operating parameters can significantly influence fiber morphology and material performance. Machine learning (ML) methods are increasingly employed to model these process-structure relationships and to identify the relative importance of processing variables. However, most existing studies rely on a single ML model, implicitly assuming that the resulting feature importance is robust and reproducible. In this study, the consistency of feature importance across multiple ML model families was systematically evaluated using a curated dataset of 96 polyvinyl alcohol (PVA) electrospinning experiments. Twenty-one ML models representing linear, tree-based, kernel-based, neural network, and instance-based approaches were trained and compared. To provide a unified interpretability framework, SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) values were used to calculate feature importance consistently across all models. A rank-based statistical analysis was then performed to quantify inter-model agreement and assess the robustness of parameter rankings. The results demonstrate that predictive performance and interpretive reliability are fundamentally distinct properties. Although several models achieved comparable predictive accuracy, substantial differences were observed in their feature importance rankings. Solution concentration emerged as the most robust and consistently influential parameter (variability = 0), whereas flow rate and applied voltage exhibited high ranking variability (variability > 0.9), indicating strong model dependence. These findings suggest that feature importance derived from a single ML model may be unreliable, particularly for small experimental datasets, and highlight the importance of cross-model validation for achieving trustworthy interpretation in ML-assisted electrospinning research.

</details>


### [202] [Breaking the Quality-Privacy Tradeoff in Tabular Data Generation via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04911)
*Xinyan Han,Yan Lu,Xiaoyu Lin,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yuanrui Wang,Xuanyue Li,Wenchao Zou,Xingxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffICL方法，通过将表格数据生成建模为上下文学习问题，利用在大量数据集上预训练的结构先验，有效解决了小数据场景下生成质量与隐私保护之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成模型在小数据场景下存在明显的质量-隐私权衡问题：提高数据质量通常会导致对训练样本记忆增加，从而削弱隐私保护。这种权衡产生的原因是小训练集使得数据集特定的生成模型难以区分可泛化的结构与样本特定的模式。

Method: 提出DiffICL方法，将表格数据生成形式化为上下文学习问题。该方法不是从头拟合每个数据集，而是利用从大量数据集合中学习的预训练结构先验，使其能够从有限的上下文中推断数据分布，而不是记忆单个样本。

Result: 在14个真实世界数据集上的评估结果显示，DiffICL同时提高了数据质量和隐私保护，生成的合成数据能够提供有效的数据增强效果。实验表明通过更好的训练范式可以改善质量-隐私权衡。

Conclusion: 质量-隐私权衡可以通过更好的训练范式得到改善，预训练的结构先验能够帮助模型从小数据集中学习可泛化的结构而非记忆个体样本，为表格数据合成提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Tabular data synthesis aims to generate high-quality data while preserving privacy. However, we find that existing tabular generative models exhibit a clear tradeoff in the small-data regime: improving data quality typically comes at the cost of increased memorization of training samples, thereby weakening privacy protection. This tradeoff arises because small training sets make it difficult for dataset-specific generative models to distinguish generalizable structure from sample-specific patterns. To address this, we propose DiffICL, which formulates tabular data generation as an in-context learning problem. Instead of fitting each dataset from scratch,DiffICL leverages pretrained structural priors learned from a large collection of datasets, enabling it to infer data distributions from limited context rather than memorizing individual samples. We evaluate DiffICL on 14 real-world datasets. Results show that DiffICL improves both data quality and privacy, and generate synthetic data that provides effective data augmentation. Our findings suggest that the quality-privacy tradeoff can be improved through better training paradigms.

</details>


### [203] [Koopman Identification of Nonlinear Systems via Reservoir Liftings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04917)
*Weibin Gu,Chen Yang,Lu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RC-Koopman框架，利用储备池作为有限维Koopman字典，解决了传统Koopman方法中的字典选择、时序记忆编码和数值病态问题，实现了非线性动态系统的高效线性表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于Koopman算子理论的学习非线性动态系统可处理线性表示方法，常受到字典选择、时序记忆编码和数值病态问题的阻碍。现有的方法如EDMD和Hankel提升方法难以在重建精度和动态稳定性之间取得良好平衡，亟需一种能有效解决这些问题的方法。

Method: 受储备池计算范式启发，提出RC-Koopman框架。该方法将储备池解释为具有状态、有限维的Koopman字典，其时序深度通过谱半径显式控制。利用回声状态特性(ESP)保证提升的Koopman逼近的良好适定性和数值条件性。提出基于相关性的谱半径选择算法，使储备池记忆与系统主导时间尺度对齐。分析了储备池的有限记忆如何决定哪些Koopman特征函数可从提升特征中观测到。

Result: 在合成基准测试上的评估表明，与扩展动态模态分解(EDMD)和基于Hankel的提升方法相比，RC-Koopman在底层非线性动态的重建精度和动态稳定性之间实现了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: RC-Koopman框架成功地将储备池计算与Koopman算子理论相结合，通过谱半径控制时序记忆、利用ESP保证数值稳定性，并提供理论分析揭示有限记忆对特征函数可观测性的影响。该方法为非线性动态系统的线性表示提供了一种有效且理论有据的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning tractable linear representations of nonlinear dynamical systems via Koopman operator theory is often hindered by dictionary selection, temporal memory encoding, and numerical ill-conditioning. Inspired by Reservoir Computing (RC) paradigm, this paper introduces the RC-Koopman framework, which interprets reservoir as a stateful, finite-dimensional Koopman dictionary whose temporal depth is explicitly controlled by its spectral radius. We show that the Echo State Property (ESP) guarantees well-posedness and favorable numerical conditioning of the lifted Koopman approximation. A correlation-based spectral radius selection algorithm aligns reservoir memory with dominant system timescales. Analysis reveals how the finite memory of the reservoir determines which Koopman eigenfunctions remain observable from the lifted features. Evaluation on synthetic benchmarks demonstrates that RC-Koopman achieves a favorable balance between reconstruction accuracy of the underlying nonlinear dynamics and dynamical stability, compared to Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) and Hankel-based lifting approaches. Code available at: https://github.com/NEAR-the-future/RC-Koopman.git

</details>


### [204] [Reinforcement Learning for Compositional Generalization with Outcome-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04920)
*Xiyan Fu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用结果级强化学习替代监督微调来解决组合泛化问题，通过重塑输出分布显著提升了模型对未见组合的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合泛化（正确解释已知基元的全新组合）是一个重大挑战。现有方法多依赖监督微调，这种token级训练范式鼓励模型模仿目标输出，但无法捕获全局组合结构，导致对未见组合的泛化能力不足。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）方法，基于最终输出的反馈进行优化。在框架内探索了两种奖励机制：简单的二元结果奖励和提供额外组合反馈的复合奖励。

Result: 在多个组合基准测试上，强化学习方法相比监督微调显著提升了组合泛化性能。分析发现监督模型倾向于过拟合训练中的高频组合，而强化学习通过重塑输出分布改善了泛化，尤其是对更复杂的组合类型效果更佳。

Conclusion: 结果级强化学习是提升组合泛化的有效方法，能够克服监督微调的局限性，通过输出分布重塑提高模型对未见组合的泛化能力。

Abstract: Compositional generalization refers to correctly interpret novel combinations of known primitives, which remains a major challenge. Existing approaches often rely on supervised fine-tuning, which encourages models to imitate target outputs. This token-level training paradigm fails to capture the global compositional structure required for generalizing to unseen combinations. In this work, we investigate whether compositional generalization can instead be improved through outcome-level reinforcement learning. We adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization to optimize models based on feedback on their final outputs. Within this framework, we explore both a simple binary outcome reward and a composite reward that provides additional composition feedback. Experiments on multiple compositional benchmarks show that reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization compared to supervised fine-tuning. Further analysis reveals that supervised models tend to overfit frequent training compositions, whereas reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization by reshaping the output distribution, particularly for more complex composition types.

</details>


### [205] [Training-Time Batch Normalization Reshapes Local Partition Geometry in Piecewise-Affine Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04946)
*Xuan Qi,Yi Wei,Fanqi Yu,Furao shen,Vittorio Murino,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从函数层面揭示了训练时批归一化（BN）的几何机制：BN通过以批次中心为参考定义开关超平面，从而增加ReLU及分段仿射网络的局部分区细化，提供了一种基于几何的批次条件重中心化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批归一化（BN）是现代深度网络的核心组件，但现有研究主要关注其优化收益，对其在训练过程中对所实现函数的具体影响理解不足。本文旨在从函数层面和几何视角深入理解训练时BN的作用机制。

Method: 本文研究连续分段仿射（CPA）网络中训练时BN的行为。方法包括：1）利用开关超平面和诱导仿射区域划分的几何性质进行分析；2）证明BN为每个神经元定义了一个通过批次中心的参考超平面，且断点开关超平面是其平行偏移；3）提出基于精确仿射区域计数的局部区域密度泛函，并给出开关超平面与局部$\ell_\infty$窗口相交的精确判据。

Result: 在明确的充分条件下，本文证明BN能够增加ReLU及更广义分段仿射网络中的期望局部分区细化。此外，该机制在父仿射区域内（上游表示映射为仿射嵌入时）能够通过网络深度在局部进行传递。这为训练时BN提供了一种基于几何的“批次条件重中心化”解释。

Conclusion: 本文通过几何分析揭示了训练时BN在函数层面的具体作用，将其刻画为一种数据附近的批次条件重中心化机制，加深了对BN在深度网络训练中行为的理论理解。

Abstract: Batch normalization (BN) is central to modern deep networks, but its effect on the realized function during training remains less understood than its optimization benefits. We study training-time BN in continuous piecewise-affine (CPA) networks through the geometry of switching hyperplanes and the induced affine-region partition. Conditioned on a mini-batch, we show that BN defines for each neuron a reference hyperplane through the batch centroid, and that breakpoint-switching hyperplanes are parallel translates whose offsets are expressed in batch-standardized coordinates and are independent of the raw bias. This yields an exact criterion for when a switching hyperplane intersects a local $\ell_\infty$ window and motivates a local region-density functional based on exact affine-region counts. Under explicit sufficient conditions, we show that BN increases expected local partition refinement in ReLU and more general piecewise-affine networks, and that this mechanism transfers locally through depth inside parent affine regions where the upstream representation map is an affine embedding. These results provide a function-level geometric account of training-time BN as a batch-conditional recentering mechanism near the data.

</details>


### [206] [Adaptive Inverted-Index Routing for Granular Mixtures-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04952)
*Klaus-Rudolf Kladny,Maximilian Mordig,Bernhard Schölkopf,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AIR-MoE，一种基于向量量化的自适应倒排索引路由架构，通过两阶段流程（粗筛选+精细打分）解决了细粒度专家混合模型中路由成本过高的问题，且无需修改模型架构即可作为标准路由器的直接替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，使用许多小专家（细粒度）的MoE模型性能更优，但这会导致路由成本显著增加甚至主导计算开销。现有方法难以在保持高性能的同时有效降低细粒度场景下的路由成本，因此需要一种高效且不限制专家参数结构的路由机制。

Method: 提出AIR-MoE，受倒排索引启发，利用向量量化（VQ）实现两阶段路由：第一阶段将Token分配给VQ码字以构建候选专家集（粗筛选）；第二阶段仅对该候选集计算精确路由分数（精细打分）。该过程近似真实的Top-k路由，避免了全专家打分，且对专家参数无结构约束，可作为标准路由器的直接替代。

Result: 提供了AIR-MoE所达到的质量召回率的下界分析；实验证明，在细粒度MoE设置中，AIR-MoE相比现有路由方法实现了性能提升，有效解决了路由成本问题。

Conclusion: AIR-MoE成功解决了细粒度MoE模型中的高路由成本问题，在无需修改模型架构或损失函数的前提下，实现了性能提升，为构建可扩展的高效Transformer架构提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models enable scalable transformer architectures by activating only a subset of experts per token. Recent evidence suggests that performance improves with increasingly granular experts, i.e., many small experts instead of a few large ones. However, this regime substantially increases routing cost, which can dominate computation. We introduce adaptive inverted-index routing for MoE (AIR-MoE), an inverted-index-inspired routing architecture based on vector quantization (VQ). In a first stage, AIR-MoE performs coarse shortlisting by assigning tokens to VQ codewords to construct a candidate set of experts. In a second stage, fine scoring computes exact routing scores restricted to this shortlist. This two-stage procedure approximates true top-k routing while avoiding full expert scoring and, in contrast to prior work, imposing no structural constraints on expert parameters. AIR-MoE serves as a drop-in replacement for standard routers and requires no modifications to the model architecture or loss function. We further provide a lower bound on the mass recall achieved by AIR-MoE that yields insights into its inner workings. Empirically, we demonstrate that AIR-MoE achieves improved performance compared to existing routing approaches in granular MoE settings.

</details>


### [207] [Order-based Rehearsal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04955)
*Yu-Xuan Tao,Tian-Zuo Wang,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对避免不良未来（AUF）问题，提出了首个基于序结构的重演学习方法。该方法无需依赖完整图结构，仅通过序结构即可识别决策影响，在AUF决策任务中表现优于现有基于图的方法，甚至匹敌拥有真实图的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的避免不良未来（AUF）重演学习方法依赖于底层图结构，但从观测数据中学习图结构具有挑战性且会产生显著的估计误差。作者发现序结构对于AUF决策可能是足够的，因为序结构虽然信息量少于图结构，但足以从观测数据中识别决策的影响，表明学习完整图结构并非总是必要。

Method: 提出了首个基于序结构的重演学习方法。在序学习方面，开发了信息论方法，对结构函数形式和噪声分布类型无限制。在AUF决策方面，构建了基于序的采样器来近似决策影响，结合最大化决策后成功概率的代理目标，将AUF任务转化为可微分优化问题。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的序学习方法优于现有方法；AUF方法不仅超越了依赖学习图或学习序的方法，而且还匹配甚至超越了给定真实图的基线模型。

Conclusion: 序结构足以支持AUF决策，无需学习完整的因果图。所提出的基于序的方法为避免不良未来问题提供了更高效且准确的解决方案，突破了传统依赖图结构方法的局限性。

Abstract: When a machine learning (ML) model forecasts an undesired event, one often seeks a decision to avoid it, known as the avoiding undesired future (AUF) problem. Many rehearsal learning methods have been proposed for AUF, but they rely on an underlying graph structure; learning such a graph from observational data is challenging and can incur substantial estimation error. In this work, we demonstrate that the order structure can be sufficient for AUF decision-making, and propose the first order-based rehearsal learning method. Although an order is less informative than a graph, it can be sufficient to identify the influence of decisions from observational data, suggesting that learning the entire graph is not always necessary. To learn the order, we develop an information-theoretic method that imposes no restrictions on the form of structural functions or the type of noise distributions. For AUF decision-making, we construct an order-based sampler to approximate the influence of decisions and, combined with a surrogate objective for maximizing the post-decision success probability, reduce the AUF task to a differentiable optimization problem. Experiments show that our order learning method outperforms existing methods, and that our AUF approach not only surpasses methods relying on learned graphs or learned orders, but also matches or even exceeds oracle baselines that are given the true graph.

</details>


### [208] [KernelBench-X: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04956)
*Han Wang,Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Haoxu Wang,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了KernelBench-X基准测试，通过15个类别176个任务的评估发现：任务结构是决定正确性的关键因素，迭代优化提升了正确性但降低了性能，正确性不等于效率，且量化任务完全失败，揭示了当前LLM生成Triton内核的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的Triton内核生成引起了广泛关注，但一个基本的实证问题仍未得到解答：这种能力在哪里会出现故障，以及为什么？本文旨在通过系统性评估回答这一问题。

Method: 提出KernelBench-X基准，包含15个类别176个任务，进行类别感知的正确性和硬件效率评估。系统性地比较了五种代表性方法，分析了任务结构、迭代优化等对正确性和性能的影响。

Result: 三个主要发现：1) 任务结构比方法设计更能决定正确性（类别解释的方差为9.4% vs 方法的3.3%），72%的融合任务失败，数学任务则一致解决；2) 迭代优化提高正确性但降低性能，编译率从52.3%升至68.8%，平均加速比从1.58×降至1.44×；3) 正确性不意味着效率，46.6%的正确内核比PyTorch基线慢，跨硬件加速方差达21.4×；量化任务完全失败（0/30成功）。

Conclusion: 未来进展取决于处理全局协调、显式建模数值精度以及将硬件效率纳入生成过程。量化任务的失败揭示了系统性地对数值计算契约的误解，而非表面语法错误。

Abstract: LLM-based Triton kernel generation has attracted significant interest, yet a fundamental empirical question remains unanswered: where does this capability break down, and why? We present KernelBench-X, a benchmark designed to answer this question through category-aware evaluation of correctness and hardware efficiency across 176 tasks in 15 categories. Our systematic comparison of five representative methods yields three main findings. First, task structure determines correctness more than method design. Category explains nearly three times more variance in semantic correctness than method (9.4% vs 3.3% explained deviance), and 72% of Fusion tasks fail across all five methods while Math tasks are solved consistently. Second, iterative refinement improves correctness, but not performance. Across GEAK iterations, compile rate rises from 52.3% to 68.8% while average speedup declines from $1.58\times$ to $1.44\times$; newly rescued kernels consistently underperform persistently correct ones ($1.16\times$ vs $1.58\times$ speedup in round~0$\to$1). Third, correctness does not imply efficiency. 46.6% of correct kernels are slower than the PyTorch eager baseline, and cross-hardware speedup variance reaches $21.4\times$. Besides, quantization remains completely unsolved (0/30 successes) despite non-trivial compilation rates, revealing systematic misunderstanding of numerical computation contracts rather than surface-level syntax errors. These findings suggest that future progress depends on handling global coordination, explicitly modeling numerical precision, and incorporating hardware efficiency into generation. The code is available at https://github.com/BonnieW05/KernelBenchX

</details>


### [209] [Delving into Non-Exchangeability for Conformal Prediction in Graph-Structured Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04957)
*Ruichao Guo,Xingyao Han,Luo Wenshui,Zhe Liu,Chen Gong,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图结构多变量时间序列预测中因节点耦合违反可交换性假设的问题，提出谱图条件可交换性(SGCE)概念和基于图小波变换的谱共形预测方法(SCALE)，通过分离高低频分量在谱域实现有效的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构多变量时间序列的预测不确定性量化研究不足。共形预测(CP)虽能提供可靠的覆盖保证，但其依赖的可交换性假设在图结构时间序列中因内在的跨节点耦合而被破坏，导致直接应用CP不可靠。

Method: 受谱图理论启发，提出谱图条件可交换性(SGCE)概念，将可交换的高频分量以低频分量为条件，在保留全局趋势的同时实现谱域的有效CP。基于此提出SCALE方法：利用图小波变换分解低/高频分量，通过低频嵌入上的自适应门控对高频残差进行共形化处理。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上的实验表明，SCALE不仅实现了有效的覆盖率，还在覆盖率-效率权衡方面持续优于最先进的CP方法。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了图结构时间序列中共形预测的可交换性违反问题，为图结构多变量时间序列预测提供了可靠的不确定性量化解决方案，具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: Point forecasting for graph-structured multivariate time series is a fundamental problem, but rigorous uncertainty quantification for such predictions is still underexplored. Conformal prediction (CP) offers uncertainty estimation with a solid coverage guarantee under the exchangeability assumption, which requires the joint data distribution to be unchanged under permutation. However, in graph-structured time series, inherent cross-node coupling can violate the exchangeability condition, making direct application of CP unreliable. Inspired by the spectral graph theory, such coupling resides in global trends and can be characterized by the low-frequency components, while high-frequency components are nearly exchangeable. Therefore, we propose a novel concept named Spectral Graph Conditional Exchangeability (SGCE), which conditions exchangeable high-frequency components on low-frequency ones to preserve global trends and enable effective CP in the spectral domain. Based on SGCE, we further propose Spectral Conformal prediction via wAveLEt transform (SCALE). SCALE uses graph wavelets to decompose low/high-frequency components and conformalizes high-frequency residuals via adaptive gating over a low-frequency embedding. Experimental results on real-world traffic datasets show that SCALE not only achieves valid coverage but also consistently improves the coverage-efficiency trade-off over the state-of-the-art CP methods.

</details>


### [210] [Conceptors for Semantic Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04980)
*Ilias Triantafyllopoulos,Young-Min Cho,Ren Tao,Miranda Muqing Miao,Sunny Rai,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku,Neville Ryant,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用概念器（conceptors）替代单一方向向量来引导LLM行为。概念器保留概念的多维子空间，几何分析证明其严格优于单向量基线，并提供无需参数的层选择诊断（相关性高达0.96），同时支持布尔代数组合操作，在多个维度上匹配或超越现有方法且产生更少退化输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于激活的引导方法将每个概念简化为单一方向向量，但其几何结构未被充分研究。单一方向可能无法完整表示概念的复杂性，且缺乏对不同层选择的理论指导，组合能力也受限。作者希望开发一种更几何化原则性的方法来捕获概念的完整多维子空间。

Method: 作者引入概念器（conceptors）：从概念两极的激活池估计的软投影矩阵，保留概念完整的多维子空间。方法包括：(1)几何分析证明双子空间严格包含单向量基线；(2)利用概念器配额作为无参数层选择诊断；(3)建立概念器的闭式布尔代数（AND、OR、NOT）实现组合操作；(4)系统性的五轴设计空间评估。

Result: 在三个指令微调模型和三个语义维度上：(1)概念器配额预测概念可分性的Pearson相关性高达r=0.96；(2)在概念子空间多维的层上，概念器匹配或超越加性基线；(3)产生显著更少的退化输出；(4)组合性评估显示在主题相关子概念上表现良好。

Conclusion: 概念器引导是一种几何原则性、可组合且实践上更安全的替代方法，能够从有限对比对中进行引导，克服了单一方向引导的局限性，为LLM推理时行为控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Activation-based steering provides control of LLM behavior at inference time, but the dominant paradigm reduces each concept to a single direction whose geometry is left largely unexamined. Rather than selecting a single steering direction, we use conceptors: soft projection matrices estimated from activations pooled across both poles of a bipolar concept, which preserve the concept's full multidimensional subspace. A geometric analysis shows the bipolar subspace strictly subsumes the single-vector baseline. We further show that the conceptor quota provides a parameter-free layer-selection diagnostic, predicting concept separability with Pearson correlations up to r=0.96 across three instruction-tuned models and three semantic dimensions. Beyond selection, conceptors admit a closed-form Boolean algebra (AND, OR, NOT): we evaluate conceptor compositionality on thematically related sub-concepts. Across a systematic five-axis design-space evaluation, conceptors match or outperform additive baselines at layers where concept subspaces are multi-dimensional while producing substantially fewer degenerate outputs. Conceptor steering is a geometrically principled, compositional, and practically safer alternative to single-direction steering from a limited number of contrastive pairs.

</details>


### [211] [Self-Induced Outcome Potential: Turn-Level Credit Assignment for Agents without Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04984)
*Senkang Hu,Yong Dai,Xudong Han,Zhengru Fang,Yuzhi Zhao,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SIOP方法，通过将最终答案的语义聚类作为潜在未来结果状态，实现了无监督的回合级信用分配，显著提升了长视野LLM智能体在搜索增强推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野LLM智能体依赖中间信息收集回合，但训练反馈通常仅在最终答案时观察到；过程级奖励需要高质量人工标注，现有回合级塑造方法需要答案监督或任务特定验证器，而无标签强化学习方法无法为中间回合分配信用。

Method: 提出Self-Induced Outcome Potential (SIOP)，将最终答案的语义聚类视为潜在未来结果状态进行基于潜力的回合级信用分配。对每个查询采样多次展开，将最终答案聚类为语义结果模式，构建可靠性感知的目标分布，然后使用可处理的聚类级近似来奖励增加可靠未来状态后验支持的回合。

Result: 在七个搜索增强智能推理基准测试上，SIOP相比无验证器的结果级基线提升了平均性能，同时接近有黄金监督的结果基线表现。

Conclusion: SIOP成功将信息潜力塑造从黄金答案监督推广到无任务特定黄金验证器的设置中，避免了标准GRPO使用的广播展开级优势，为长视野智能体训练提供了一种有效的无监督回合级信用分配方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents depend on intermediate information-gathering turns, yet training feedback is usually observed only at the final answer, because process-level rewards require high-quality human annotation. Existing turn-level shaping methods reward turns that increase the likelihood of a gold answer, but they require answer supervision or stable task-specific verifiers. Conversely, label-free RL methods extract self-signals from output distributions, but mainly at the answer or trajectory level and therefore cannot assign credit to intermediate turns. We propose Self-Induced Outcome Potential (SIOP), which treats semantic clusters of final answers as latent future outcome states for potential-based turn-level credit assignment. For each query, SIOP samples multiple rollouts, clusters final answers into semantic outcome modes, and builds a reliability-aware target distribution over these states. It then rewards turns for increasing posterior support for reliable future states using a tractable cluster-level approximation. The objective generalizes information-potential shaping from gold-answer supervision to settings without task-specific gold verifiers while avoiding the broadcasted rollout-level advantages used by standard GRPO. We formalize the framework, characterize its supervised gold-answer limit, and show that SIOP improves average performance over verifier-free outcome-level baselines on seven search-augmented agentic reasoning benchmarks while approaching a gold-supervised outcome baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/dl-m9/SIOP.git.

</details>


### [212] [Adaptivity Under Realizability Constraints: Comparing In-Context and Agentic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04995)
*Anastasis Kratsios,A. Martina Neuman,Philipp Petersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了固定查询的上下文学习与自适应查询的智能体学习在任务族均匀逼近上的表现。研究发现，自适应在两种设定下均不会损害逼近性能，但表示约束（ReLU网络实现）会深刻影响自适应优势的显现方式，导致四种不同的逼近场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解和比较两种学习范式（上下文学习与智能体学习）在任务族逼近问题上的性能差异，特别是探究自适应查询相比固定查询是否具有优势，以及网络实现约束（ReLU网络）如何影响这种优势。

Method: 作者考虑两种设定：无限制设定（查询和逼近可以是任意函数）和可实现设定（要求这些操作由ReLU神经网络实现）。通过构造四个明确的任务族，分别展示四种逼近场景：（a）自适应无优势；（b）无限制设定下的优势在ReLU实现下保持；（c）优势仅在实现约束下出现；（d）优势在实现约束下消失。

Result: 研究表明，在两种设定下，自适应查询都不会阻碍逼近性能。然而，从无限制设定过渡到可实现设定时，自适应优势会发生变化。作者成功识别并证明了四种不同的逼近场景，展示了表示约束与自适应效应之间存在深刻的相互作用。

Conclusion: 自适应查询在逼近性能上不会劣于固定查询，但表示约束（如ReLU网络实现）会显著影响自适应优势的表现形式。这一发现揭示了表示能力约束与查询适应性之间的复杂交互关系，为理解学习系统的设计提供了重要洞察。

Abstract: We compare in-context learning with fixed queries and agentic learning with adaptive queries for uniform approximation of task families. We consider two settings: an unrestricted regime, where querying and approximation are arbitrary functions, and a realizable regime, where we require these operations to be implemented by ReLU neural networks. In both settings, adaptivity never hinders approximation performance. However, this advantage can change when one passes from the unrestricted regime to the realizable regime. We identify four distinct approximation scenarios, each witnessed by an explicit task family: (a) no advantage of adaptivity; (b) an advantage in the unrestricted regime that persists under ReLU realizability; (c) an advantage that arises only under realizability; and (d) an advantage that disappears under realizability. This demonstrates that representational constraints interact profoundly with the effect of adaptivity.

</details>


### [213] [DualTCN: A Physics-Constrained Temporal Convolutional Network for 2 Time-Domain Marine CSEM Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04997)
*Khaled Ahmed,Ghada Omar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualTCN是首个用于海洋可控源电磁(MCSEM)瞬态数据反演的深度学习框架，相比传统方法在预测精度上提升显著(R²=0.898)，计算速度快21,000倍，并通过课程式数据增强实现了高噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的海洋可控源电磁数据反演方法依赖地下离散化，计算成本高昂且收敛速度慢。本文旨在开发一种高效的深度学习框架，直接回归地球模型参数并重建电导率-深度剖面，以突破传统局部优化方法(如Levenberg-Marquardt和L-BFGS-B)在计算效率和预测精度上的局限。

Method: 提出DualTCN框架，核心架构包括：(1)时序卷积网络(TCN)编码器配合晚期时间分支；(2)辅助海底深度预测头；(3)可微分软阶跃解码器用于重建电导率-深度剖面。模型直接回归四个地球模型参数(σ₁, σ₂, d₁, d₂)，共379K参数。采用课程式振幅数据增强策略提升噪声鲁棒性，并通过MC Dropout实现不确定性量化。

Result: 模型在A100 GPU上达到3.5ms/样本的反演速度，R²最高达0.898(σ₂)。相比基线模型损失降低25.3%，在±2%随机振幅误差下R²保持0.858(无增强时仅0.363)。与传统方法相比，R²均值从0.129-0.439提升至0.877，计算成本降低21,000倍。对三层模型扩展中基底电导率预测R²约0.88，但薄层分辨率仍受限(R²≈0.23)。不确定性量化显示σ₁校准良好(PICP90=0.944)，但短偏移距下d₂存在覆盖率不足(PICP90=0.572)。

Conclusion: DualTCN成功实现了海洋CSEM瞬态数据的高效、精准反演，证明了深度学习方法在地球物理反演中的优势。框架具备良好的泛化能力和噪声鲁棒性，不确定性量化可通过温度缩放或分裂保形预测进一步改进。该工作为海洋电磁数据解释提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: DualTCN is the first deep-learning framework for inverting time-domain marine controlled-source electromagnetic (MCSEM) transient data. Moving away from traditional subsurface discretization, the framework regresses four earth-model parameters -- $σ_1$, $σ_2$, $d_1$, $d_2$ -- and reconstructs conductivity-depth profiles using a differentiable soft-step decoder. The optimized architecture (379K parameters) features a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) encoder paired with a late-time branch and an auxiliary seafloor-depth head. This design achieves a 25.3\% loss reduction over baseline models, with high predictive accuracy ($R^2 = 0.898$ for $σ_2$) and an inversion speed of 3.5~ms per sample on an A100 GPU.
  The framework demonstrates high robustness to noise through curriculum-based amplitude augmentation, maintaining a mean $\bar{R}^2$ of 0.858 at $\pm2\%$ random amplitude error, compared to $0.363$ without augmentation. DualTCN generalizes effectively to three-layer extensions (seawater/resistive layer/basement), accurately resolving basement conductivity ($R^2 \approx 0.88$), though thin-layer resolution remains a physical limitation ($R^2 \approx 0.23$).
  In comparative benchmarks, DualTCN significantly outperforms traditional local optimization methods like Levenberg-Marquardt and L-BFGS-B, yielding a mean $\bar{R}^2 = 0.877$ versus 0.129-0.439 for multi-start baselines, while operating at up to 21,000$\times$ lower computational cost. Finally, the framework incorporates uncertainty quantification via Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout. While well-calibrated for $σ_1$ (PICP90 = 0.944), inherent signal limitations at short offsets (200m) lead to under-coverage for $d_2$ (PICP90 = 0.572), which can be mitigated through post-hoc temperature scaling or split conformal prediction.

</details>


### [214] [Learned Neighbor Trust for Collaborative Deployment in Model-Agnostic Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05009)
*Michael Lanier,Luise Ge,Sastry Kompella,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LNTrust方法，通过学习邻居信任函数，在无需服务器、仅交换查询和软预测的条件下，解决分布式蒸馏中推理时协作缺失的问题，显著提升异构设备部署准确率并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去中心化蒸馏方法主要关注训练时的协调，但在推理时每个节点独立运行，未充分利用更有能力的邻居节点。对于物联网（IoT）等场景，设备具有异构性、数据稀缺且分布偏斜，节点的邻居可能远超其自身能力，因此需要一种机制在推理时有效组合邻居的预测能力。

Method: 提出Learned Neighbor Trust (LNTrust)方法：1) 采用无服务器、模型无关的协议，节点间仅交换查询和软预测；2) 每个节点基于本地验证证据学习一个紧凑的邻居信任函数；3) 该信任函数在训练时控制辅助蒸馏过程，在推理时定义部署集成模型，实现训练时学到的协作直接转移到部署阶段。

Result: 实验表明，LNTrust在各种数据集和网络拓扑结构上，相比最强的仅输出基线方法，部署准确率有大幅提升，同时通信开销显著低于以往方法。

Conclusion: LNTrust成功解决了分布式蒸馏中推理时协作的缺失问题，通过学习信任函数实现训练与部署的有效衔接，在异构、数据稀缺场景下表现优异，为IoT等分布式学习场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Many decentralized distillation methods are designed around training-time coordination, yet deploy each node in isolation even when more capable neighbors remain available at inference time. This is an incomplete objective for settings such as IoT, where devices are heterogeneous, data is scarce and skewed, and a node's strongest neighbors may far exceed its own local capacity. We study how nodes should train so that their predictions compose well at deployment, and how each node should learn whom to trust. Under a server-free, model-agnostic protocol where nodes exchange only queries and soft predictions, we propose Learned Neighbor Trust (LNTrust) wherein each node learns a compact trust function over its neighborhood from local validation evidence. This trust function gates auxiliary distillation during training and defines a deployment ensemble at inference, so that collaboration learned during training transfers directly to deployment. Across datasets and topologies, LNTrust improves deployed accuracy over the strongest output-only baseline by large margins while using significantly less communication than previous methods.

</details>


### [215] [CuBridge: An LLM-Based Framework for Understanding and Reconstructing High-Performance Attention Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05023)
*Xing Ma,Yangjie Zhou,Wu Sun,Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yun Lin,Shixuan Sun,Minyi Guo,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CuBridge，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，通过“提升-迁移-降低”的工作流程改编专家编写的注意力算子CUDA内核，解决了现有方法在灵活性与性能之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习系统需要高效的注意力机制CUDA实现，但支持多样化且不断演进的注意力变体具有挑战性。现有框架和编译器牺牲了性能以换取灵活性，而专家手动编写的内核虽然高效但难以适配。近期基于LLM的内核生成研究在处理复杂算子（如注意力机制）时存在正确性不稳定和性能差距大的问题。

Method: 提出了CuBridge框架，采用结构化的“提升-迁移-降低”（lift-transfer-lower）工作流。该方法首先将专家编写的CUDA注意力算子内核“提升”为可执行的中间表示（IR），显式化执行编排并抽象底层CUDA语法；随后根据用户提供的PyTorch规范生成并验证目标IR程序；最后通过参考引导的“降低”步骤重构出优化的CUDA代码。

Result: 在多种注意力变体和GPU平台上进行评估，CuBridge能够持续生成正确的内核，并且在性能上显著优于通用框架、基于编译器的方法以及先前的基于LLM的方法。

Conclusion: CuBridge成功结合了专家内核的高效性与LLM的灵活性，证明了通过结构化的中间表示工作流，可以稳定且高效地生成针对不同注意力变体的正确CUDA内核，为解决高性能算子生成的难题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Efficient CUDA implementations of attention mechanisms are critical to modern deep learning systems, yet supporting diverse and evolving attention variants remains challenging. Existing frameworks and compilers trade performance for flexibility, while expert-written kernels achieve high efficiency but are difficult to adapt. Recent work explores large language models (LLMs) for GPU kernel generation, but prior studies report unstable correctness and significant performance gaps for complex operators such as attention.
  We present CuBridge, an LLM-based framework that adapts expert-written attention kernels through a structured lift-transfer-lower workflow. CuBridge starts from expert-written CUDA attention kernels and lifts them into an executable intermediate representation that makes execution orchestration explicit while abstracting low-level CUDA syntax. Given a user-provided PyTorch specification, CuBridge generates and verifies a target IR program, then reconstructs optimized CUDA code via reference-guided lowering. Across diverse attention variants and GPU platforms, CuBridge consistently produces correct kernels and substantially outperforms general frameworks, compiler-based approaches, and prior LLM-based methods.

</details>


### [216] [The Predictive-Causal Gap: An Impossibility Theorem and Large-Scale Neural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05029)
*Kejun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预测表征学习存在结构性失效：模型倾向于编码环境而非目标系统，导致因果保真度低下，需显式的系统-环境边界约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预测性表征学习中是否存在系统性失效模式，以及预测目标与因果表征之间的潜在冲突。
