Method: 在2695种神经网络配置上进行线性高斯动力学预测实验；引入“因果保真度”指标量化编码器对系统自由度的敏感度；提供理论证明；在非线性Duffing-GRU任务中验证，并测试“操作性约束”方法。

Result: 最优编码器普遍追踪环境而非系统：平均因果保真度仅0.49，高维（N=100）时近乎因果盲（~10^-8）但预测误差更低；理论证明这是预测目标的结构属性；非线性实验中55%任务出现环境主导表征，引发分布外泛化性能下降。

Conclusion: 预测-因果差距是学习过程的结构性限制，对自监督学习、世界模型及规模化范式具有重要意义；需显式引入系统-环境边界才能恢复因果保真度。

Abstract: We report a systematic failure mode in predictive representation learning. Across 2695 neural network configurations trained to predict linear-Gaussian dynamics, the optimal encoder tracks the environment rather than the system it is meant to model. The mean causal fidelity -- the fraction of encoder sensitivity allocated to system degrees of freedom -- is 0.49, and only 2.5% of configurations exceed 0.70. The failure intensifies with dimension: at N=100, the optimal encoder becomes causally blind (fidelity ~10^{-8}) while achieving 92% lower prediction error than the causal representation. We prove this is not an optimization artifact but a structural property of the predictive objective: when environment modes are slower or less noisy than system modes, every minimizer of the population risk encodes the former. The set of dynamics exhibiting this predictive-causal gap is open and of positive measure in parameter space. In a nonlinear Duffing-GRU sweep, unconstrained predictors learn environment-dominant representations in 55% of tasks (95% CI 41--68%) versus 24% under operational grounding (p=2.3e-3); the median out-of-distribution MSE inflation under environment shift is 1.82x versus 1.00x. Operational grounding -- restricting the loss to system observables -- partially suppresses the gap, but causal fidelity is never recovered without an explicit system-environment boundary. The results identify the predictive-causal gap as a structural limit of learning, with implications for self-supervised representation learning, world models, and the scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [217] [Full-chip CMP modelling based on Fully Convolutional Network leveraging White Light Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05062)
*Jules Exbrayat,Renan Bouis,Elie Sezestre,Viorel Balan,Arnaud Cornelis,Damien Hebras,Catherine Euvrard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的两阶段深度学习方法，结合白光干涉仪（WLI）和原子力显微镜（AFM）两种表面分析技术的数据，实现了纳米级精度的全芯片CMP后纳米形貌预测，有效解决了传统DSH模型校准耗时和硬件资源消耗大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成电路产业中，缩短上市时间至关重要。化学机械抛光（CMP）是IC制造的关键工艺，但受布局相关效应（LDE）影响显著。现有的CMP建模主要依赖密度台阶高度（DSH）方法，该方法在校准过程中耗时且需要大量硬件资源才能进行精细化预测，难以满足快速验证布局可制造性的需求。

Method: 提出一种结合白光干涉仪（WLI）和原子力显微镜（AFM）两种表面分析技术优势的深度学习建模方法。该方法基于卷积神经网络（CNN），采用两阶段流水线架构。模型首先分别在两种技术数据上进行独立训练，最终构建详细的CMP全芯片模型。

Result: 成功开发出能够预测全芯片CMP后纳米形貌的深度学习模型，该模型实现了纳米级的预测精度。通过结合WLI和AFM的技术优势，模型能够有效预测全芯片CMP后的表面形貌特征。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于深度学习的CMP建模方法有效克服了传统DSH模型的局限性，为IC设计团队提供了一种准确且高效的表面不平整度预测工具，使设计团队能够在制造前修正表面不平整问题，从而降低成本并加速设计阶段，具有重要的工业应用价值。

Abstract: As time-to-market is crucial in the Integrated Circuit (IC) industry, speeding up layout manufacturability verifi-cation is essential. Chemical-Mechanical Polishing (CMP) plays a vital role in IC fabrication but is significantly influenced by Layout-Dependent Effects (LDE). An accurate and efficient CMP model enables design teams to correct surface unevenness before fabrication, reducing costs and accelerating the design phase. However, existing models often rely on Density Step Height (DSH) modeling, which is time-consuming for calibration and requires substantial hardware resources for fine-grained predictions. In this paper, we propose combining the advantages of two surface analysis techniques, White Light Interfer-ometry (WLI) and Atomic Force Microscopy (AFM), to train a deep learning model. This model aims to predict full-chip post-CMP nanotopography with nanometer-scale accuracy. Our deep learning model is based on a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) and follows a two-step pipeline. The model is trained on each technique separately, resulting in a detailed full-chip CMP model.

</details>


### [218] [Provable imitation learning for control of instability in partially-observed Vlasov--Poisson equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05081)
*Xiaofan Xia,Qin Li,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用模仿学习将基于全相空间信息的专家控制策略蒸馏为仅依赖稀疏宏观观测的实际控制器，从理论上证明了其稳定性保障，并通过数值实验验证了该方法能够有效实现Vlasov-Poisson等离子体动力学的稳定化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在核聚变等离子体控制问题中，理想控制器通常依赖全相空间状态信息，但实际实验中的反馈控制往往只能获取稀疏的宏观诊断数据，这种信息差距促使研究者寻找能够弥合这一差距的方法。

Method: 采用模仿学习方法，将基于完全观测信息的专家策略提炼为仅基于宏观测量信息运行的控制器，并分析学习策略的稳定性保证以及最小行为克隆损失与初始分布复杂度之间的关系。

Result: 证明了学习策略的稳定性，其中误差下限取决于观测约束下可达到的最小行为克隆损失；该损失可以用熵的概念表征，量化初始分布的复杂性；数值实验表明学习策略能够仅使用宏观观测稳定系统，且时间跨度显著长于非自适应基线控制器。

Conclusion: 理论上证明了从宏观观测量学习等离子体动力学稳定反馈策略的可行性，展示了学习方法对低复杂度结构的自适应性，为核聚变等离子体控制提供了新的理论框架和实践方法。

Abstract: We consider the stabilization of Vlasov--Poisson plasma dynamics, a central control problem in nuclear fusion. Our focus is the gap between what an ideal controller would use and what experiments can actually observe: while optimal policy may rely on the full phase-space state, practical feedback is typically limited to sparse macroscopic diagnostics. We therefore study imitation learning methods that distill a fully observed expert policy into controllers operating only on macroscopic measurements. We show the stability guarantees of the learned policy, where the error floor depends on the minimal behavior cloning loss achievable under the observation constraints. We further characterize this minimal loss in terms of a notion of entropy that quantifies the complexity of the initial distribution. Our results demonstrates the theoretical feasibility of learning stabilizing feedback policies for kinetic plasma dynamics from macroscopic observations, and exhibits the adaptivity of the learning approach to low-complexity structures. Through extensive numerical experiments, we validate our theory and show that the learned policies can stabilize the system using only macroscopic observations, within a significantly longer time horizon than non-adaptive baseline controllers.

</details>


### [219] [Order Matters: Improving Domain Adaptation by Reordering Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05084)
*Andrea Napoli,Paul White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ORDERED方法，通过优化训练数据的采样顺序来减少随机域差异估计的方差，从而提升无监督域适应（UDA）在目标域上的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督域适应（UDA）旨在解决模型部署中的域偏移问题，但在随机设置下，域差异估计存在高方差，这削弱了该方法的理论优势。

Method: 提出了一种名为ORDERED的无偏随机方差缩减技术。该方法针对相关性对齐和最大均值差异两种域差异损失，将其随机估计误差建模为数据采样顺序的函数，并提出了一种实用的优化算法来寻找最优采样顺序，以减少估计误差。

Result: 仿真实验表明，与相关方法相比，ORDERED能有效降低方差；在两个域偏移图像分类基准测试中，目标域的分类准确率得到了提升。

Conclusion: 通过优化数据采样顺序来减少域差异估计的方差是一种有效的策略，能够显著改善无监督域适应方法的实际性能。

Abstract: Domain shift remains a key challenge in deploying machine learning models to the real world. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) aims to address this by minimising domain discrepancy during training, but the discrepancy estimates suffer from high variance in stochastic settings, which can stifle the theoretical benefits of the method. This paper proposes Optimal Reordering of Data for Error-Reduced Estimation of Discrepancy (ORDERED), a novel unbiased stochastic variance reduction technique which reduces the discrepancy estimation error by optimising the order in which the training data are sampled. We consider two specific domain discrepancy losses (correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy), formulate their stochastic estimation error as a function of the data sampling order, and propose a practical optimisation algorithm. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on two domain shift image classification benchmarks show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [220] [Gated Multimodal Learning for Interpretable Property Energy Performance Prediction and Retrofit Scenario Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05088)
*Yunfei Bai,Aaron Tesfa Tsion,Raul Rosales,Barbara Shollock,Wei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种门控多模态模型，融合EPC表格数据、评估员文本和GIS空间特征来预测建筑能效评分，在伦敦威斯敏斯特案例中取得了良好的预测精度，为城市规模建筑节能改造筛选和净零排放转型提供了可扩展的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅建筑是城市温室气体排放的重要来源（占英国约20%，欧盟25%），现有能源绩效证书（EPCs）依赖现场检查，难以实现及时的城市规模评估，因此需要一种可扩展的方法来评估建筑能效并支持脱碳改造规划。

Method: 开发了一种门控多模态模型，整合三种数据源：EPC表格变量、评估员撰写的自由文本、以及GIS衍生的空间特征（建筑足迹几何形状、高度、面积和朝向）。模型采用样本级门控机制学习特定属性的模态权重，并使用辅助波段分类头稳定训练过程。

Result: 在伦敦威斯敏斯特案例研究中，模型预测SAP能效评分和环境影响（EI）评分的MAE分别为4.03和4.76分，R²值分别为0.757和0.748，平均MAE为4.39。消融实验显示完整多模态融合在评分预测和波段分类上均优于单模态和双模态基线。可解释性分析揭示了各模态的贡献和关键特征。

Conclusion: 该框架为建筑改造筛选、干预优先级排序和净零排放住房转型提供了可扩展的房产级证据，成功应用于墙体保温、屋顶保温和窗户玻璃升级等改造情景分析，能够预测SAP、EI、年度能源成本和等效CO2排放的改善情况。

Abstract: Achieving resilient and sustainable cities requires scalable approaches to decarbonising residential buildings, which account for about 20% of UK greenhouse gas emissions and 25% of energy-related emissions in the European Union. Energy Performance Certificates (EPCs) support regulation and retrofit planning, but their reliance on on-site inspections limits timely city-scale assessment. This study introduces a gated multimodal model to predict Standard Assessment Procedure (SAP) energy efficiency and Environmental Impact (EI) scores by integrating EPC tabular variables, assessor-written free text, and Geographic Information System (GIS)-derived spatial features describing footprint geometry, height, area, and orientation. Sample-wise gating learns property-specific modality weights, while an auxiliary band classification head stabilises training. In a Westminster, London case study, the model predicts SAP and EI scores with MAEs of 4.03 and 4.76 points and R2 values of 0.757 and 0.748, respectively, achieving a mean MAE of 4.39. Ablation results show that full multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal and bimodal baselines for both score prediction and band-level classification. Interpretability analyses provide decision-relevant evidence: gating weights indicate strong reliance on assessor text; SHAP highlights main fuel, built form, and construction age band; text occlusion prioritises roof and wall fields; and spatial attribution is dominated by height and footprint area, with sensitivity to footprint shape. The validated framework is further applied to retrofit scenarios for wall insulation, roof insulation, and window glazing upgrades, indicating projected improvements in SAP, EI, annual energy cost, and equivalent CO2 emissions. Overall, the framework provides scalable property-level evidence for retrofit screening, intervention prioritisation, and net-zero housing transitions.

</details>


### [221] [Unified Framework of Distributional Regret in Multi-Armed Bandits and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05102)
*Harin Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架研究随机多臂老虎机和情景强化学习的后悔值分布，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári关于分布性后悔界的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注期望后悔值，缺乏对后悔值分布特征的系统性理解。本文旨在建立分布性后悔界的理论框架，以刻画不同置信水平下的概率保证，揭示期望性能、尾部风险与实例依赖行为之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出了一个简单的UCBVI风格算法，探索奖励设为min{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/√N}，其中N为访问计数，(c_{1,k},c_{2,k})为用户可调参数。针对任意参数序列，推导了通用的间隙无关和间隙依赖的分布性后悔界。

Result: 获得了极小化和实例依赖情形下期望后悔与分布性后悔之间的最优权衡；对于A臂、视界T的多臂老虎机，得到了O(√AT log(1/δ))阶的分布性后悔界，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári (2020)的猜想。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了分布性后悔的理论框架，提出的算法在期望性能与尾部风险之间实现了有原则的权衡，参数选择可控制这一权衡，为理解和设计鲁棒的强化学习算法提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the distribution of regret in stochastic multi-armed bandits and episodic reinforcement learning through a unified framework. We formalize a distributional regret bound as a probabilistic guarantee that holds uniformly over all confidence levels $δ\in (0,1]$, thereby characterizing the regret distribution across the full range of $δ$. We present a simple UCBVI-style algorithm with exploration bonus $\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$, where $N$ denotes the visit count and $(c_{1,k},c_{2,k})$ are user-specified parameters. For arbitrary parameter sequences, we derive general gap-independent and gap-dependent distributional regret bounds, yielding a principled characterization of how the parameters control the trade-off between expected performance, tail risk, and instance-dependent behavior. In particular, our bounds achieve optimal trade-offs between expected and distributional regret in both minimax and instance-dependent regimes. As a special case, for multi-armed bandits with $A$ arms and horizon $T$, we obtain a distributional regret bound of order $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/δ))$, confirming the conjecture of Lattimore & Szepesvári (2020, Section 17.1) for the first time.

</details>


### [222] [Rollout Pass-Rate Control: Steering Binary-Reward RL Toward Its Most Informative Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05112)
*Tianshu Zhu,Wenyu Zhang,Xiaoying Zuo,Lun Tian,Haotian Zhao,Yucheng Zeng,Jingnan Gu,Daxiang Dong,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prefix Sampling (PS)方法，通过重放轨迹前缀将pass rate控制在信息量最大的50%水平，在SWE-bench上实现2.01x和1.55x加速且性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench风格的智能体强化学习依赖昂贵的有状态轨迹，但在采样得到的rollout组中，倾斜的通过率导致大量计算浪费，二元奖励提供的对比信号较弱。

Method: 将效率问题建模为通过率控制问题，论证50%通过率是最优操作点。提出Prefix Sampling (PS)，通过重放轨迹前缀引导倾斜组向该状态靠拢：成功前缀为失败组提供起点，失败前缀为成功组提供障碍。在有状态环境中重构前缀状态，排除重放token的损失，仅优化当前策略生成的延续部分。

Result: 在SWE-bench风格的智能体RL上，Qwen3-14B端到端墙钟加速2.01倍，Qwen3-32B加速1.55倍，同时保持或提升最终验证性能。14B模型在SWE-bench Verified上的峰值从基线0.273提升至0.295。AIME 2025数学推理实验验证了相同的通过率控制模式。

Conclusion: 50%通过率作为信息量最大的操作点具有理论支撑，Prefix Sampling有效解决了有状态智能体强化学习中的计算效率问题，在加速训练的同时保持或提升性能。

Abstract: SWE-bench-style agentic reinforcement learning relies on expensive stateful trajectories, yet substantial compute is wasted on sampled rollout groups with skewed pass rates, where binary rewards provide a weak contrastive signal. We frame this inefficiency as a pass-rate control problem and show that a 50% pass rate is the most informative operating point: it maximizes reward entropy, the probability of surviving group filtering, RLOO advantage energy under GRPO, and success--failure contrastive structure. Guided by this principle, we propose Prefix Sampling (PS), which replays trajectory prefixes to steer skewed groups toward this regime: successful prefixes serve as head starts for mostly failing groups, while failing prefixes serve as handicaps for mostly passing groups. In stateful agent environments, prefix states are reconstructed through replay while replayed tokens are excluded from the loss, restricting optimization to continuations generated by the current policy. On SWE-bench-style agentic RL, PS delivers end-to-end wall-clock speedups of 2.01x on Qwen3-14B and 1.55x on Qwen3-32B while preserving or improving final verified performance. For 14B, the SWE-bench Verified peak rises from the baseline peak of 0.273 to 0.295 under PS. Additional mathematical reasoning experiments on AIME 2025 show the same pass-rate control pattern and decompose the gains into replay, bidirectional coverage, and adaptive control.

</details>


### [223] [How Long Does Infinite Width Last? Signal Propagation in Long-Range Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05113)
*Mariia Seleznova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了有限宽度下线性循环模型的精确信号传播公式，发现当循环深度$t$超过$\sqrt{n}$时，无限宽度理论失效，且有限宽度效应在循环模型中比前馈模型积累更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信号传播理论主要依赖于无限宽度极限近似，但在现代循环序列模型处理长序列（即大循环深度$t$）时，该近似在有限宽度下的准确性尚不清楚。研究者需要理解当深度和宽度联合增长时，无限宽度理论何时失效。

Method: 在复高斯初始化条件下，推导了线性循环中隐藏状态信号能量的精确有限宽度公式。基于这些公式，分析了深度$t$与宽度$n$联合增长的标度关系。

Result: 识别出三种联合深度-宽度标度区域：（i）亚临界区域$t=o(\sqrt{n})$，无限宽度近似有效；（ii）临界区域$t\sim c\sqrt{n}$，出现对无限宽度预测的非平凡偏差；（iii）超临界区域$t\gg \sqrt{n}$，有限宽度效应占主导。循环深度标度$\sqrt{n}$是无限宽度理论失效的临界点。

Conclusion: 有限宽度效应在循环模型中比前馈模型随深度积累更快，导致定性不同的信号传播行为。该结果揭示了标准初始化方案（如Glorot）在长程线性循环中何时变得不稳定，为现代循环序列模型的初始化和训练提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study signal propagation in linear recurrent models at finite width. While existing signal propagation theory relies predominantly on the infinite-width limit, it remains unclear for how long that approximation remains accurate when recurrent depth $t$ grows jointly with width $n$. This question is especially relevant for modern recurrent sequence models, whose natural operating regime involves long input sequences, i.e., large $t$. We derive exact finite-width formulas for the hidden state signal energies in linear recurrences under complex Gaussian initialization. Using these formulas, we identify the joint depth-width scaling regimes that govern signal propagation: (i) a subcritical regime $t=o(\sqrt n)$, in which the infinite-width approximation remains valid; (ii) a critical regime $t\sim c\sqrt n$, in which non-negligible deviations from infinite-width predictions appear and a nontrivial joint scaling limit emerges; and (iii) a supercritical regime $t\gg \sqrt n$, in which finite-width effects dominate. Thus, our results pinpoint the precise recurrent depth scale at which infinite-width theory breaks down in long-range linear recurrences. In turn, this shows when standard initialization schemes, such as Glorot, become unstable. More broadly, our results demonstrate that finite-width effects accumulate more rapidly with depth in recurrent models than in feedforward ones, leading to qualitatively different signal propagation behavior.

</details>


### [224] [Manifold Steering Reveals the Shared Geometry of Neural Network Representation and Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05115)
*Daniel Wurgaft,Can Rager,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Sheridan Feucht,Usha Bhalla,Tal Haklay,Eric Bigelow,Raphael Sarfati,Thomas McGrath,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Noah Goodman,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了神经表征的几何结构对模型行为具有因果影响。通过引入“流形调控”方法，证明沿着表征流形进行干预能产生自然行为，而传统的线性调控则会失败，从而将模型调控的核心问题从“寻找正确方向”转变为“寻找正确几何结构”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表征蕴含丰富的几何结构，但目前尚不清楚这种结构是否在因果层面上塑造了模型行为。现有干预方法通常假设激活空间具有欧几里得几何性质，忽略了其内在流形结构，可能导致非自然的输出。因此，研究旨在探究表征几何与行为之间的因果联系，并寻求基于几何结构的更优干预策略。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于流形的干预方法。首先，分别拟合激活流形$M_h$（表征）和行为流形$M_y$（输出概率分布）。随后，对比两种干预方式：“流形调控”（沿$M_h$移动）与“线性调控”（假设欧几里得几何）。此外，还进行了逆向验证：优化激活空间中的干预路径以拟合$M_y$，观察其是否能恢复$M_h$的曲率。实验涵盖语言模型（推理任务、上下文学习）和视频世界模型（物理动力学任务）。

Result: 实验发现，流形调控产生的行为轨迹紧随行为流形$M_y$，输出自然；而线性调控因穿过流形外区域而产生非自然输出。逆向实验亦表明，优化路径以符合$M_y$能够恢复激活流形$M_h$的曲率。这种表征几何与行为几何之间的双向关系在多种任务和模态中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 神经表征中的几何结构并非无关紧要的副产品，而是实现原理性内部控制的关键对象。这一发现重新定义了模型调控的核心挑战：重点不在于寻找正确的干预方向，而在于识别并遵循正确的几何结构。

Abstract: Neural representations carry rich geometric structure; but does that structure causally shape behavior? To address this question, we intervene along paths through activation space defined by different geometries, and measure the behavioral trajectories they induce. In particular, we test whether interventions that respect the geometry of activation space will yield behaviors close to those the model exhibits naturally. Concretely, we first fit an activation manifold $M_h$ to representations and a behavior manifold $M_y$ to output probability distributions. We then test the link $M_h \leftrightarrow M_y$ via interventions: we find that steering along $M_h$, which we term manifold steering, yields behavioral trajectories that follow $M_y$, while linear steering -- which assumes a Euclidean geometry -- cuts through off-manifold regions and hence produces unnatural outputs. Moreover, optimizing interventions in activation space to produce paths along $M_y$ recovers activation trajectories that trace the curvature of $M_h$. We demonstrate this bidirectional relationship between the geometry of representation and behavior across tasks and modalities. In language models, we use reasoning tasks with cyclic and sequential geometries as well as in-context learning tasks with more complex graph geometries. In a video world model, we use a task with geometry corresponding to physical dynamics. Overall, our work shows that geometry in neural representation is not merely incidental, but is in fact the proper object for enabling principled control via intervention on internals. This recasts the core problem of steering from finding the right direction to finding the right geometry.

</details>


### [225] [On the Hardness of Junking LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05116)
*Marco Rando,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大语言模型中“自然后门”的发现难度，发现尽管比标准越狱攻击更难，但通过简单的贪婪随机搜索仍能高效找到这些可触发有害输出的无意义token序列，证实了自然后门的存在性和易恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，无需对抗性提示，仅通过优化的token序列即可诱导大语言模型产生有害行为，这暗示了“自然后门”的存在。然而，该领域尚未被深入探索，发现此类自然后门的难度也未被评估。因此，本文旨在形式化并评估这一“垃圾化问题”的求解难度。

Method: 作者将“垃圾化问题”形式化为寻找能够最大化生成有害响应目标前缀概率的token序列，并提出了一种简单的贪婪随机搜索方法，用于评估此类序列是否容易被发现。

Result: 实验结果表明，该问题比标准越狱攻击更难，验证了语义信息在提示设计中的重要性。同时，简单的搜索策略足以高成功率地解决问题，表明自然后门不仅存在且易于恢复。困惑度分析显示，发现的token序列位于模型分布的低概率区域，支持其隐式产生于训练过程的假设。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了大语言模型中自然后门的存在性和可恢复性，强调了仅依赖随机搜索即可发现触发不安全输出的token序列，为理解模型潜在风险提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to be vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, which typically rely on carefully designed prompts containing explicit semantic structure. These attacks generally operate by fixing an adversarial instruction and optimizing small adversarial components (e.g., suffixes or prefixes). In this setting, prompt structure is fundamental for performance, and recent results show that even simple random search can achieve strong performance when combined with sophisticated prompt design. Recently, it has been observed that harmful behaviors can be elicited even without the adversarial prompt, relying solely on optimized token sequences. This suggests the existence of natural backdoors, i.e., token sequences naturally emerged during LLMs training that trigger unsafe outputs without any meaningful instruction. However, despite these observations, this setting remains largely unexplored, and in particular the hardness of finding natural backdoors has not been assessed yet. In this work, we provide a first proof-of-concept study investigating the hardness of this task, which we refer to as the junking problem. We formalize it as the problem of finding token sequences that maximize the probability of generating a target prefix of harmful responses, propose a greedy random-search method to assess is such sequences can be discovered easily. Our results show that this problem is harder than standard jailbreak attacks, confirming the importance of semantic information in prompt design. At the same time, we find that our simple strategy is sufficient to solve it with a high success rate, suggesting that natural backdoors are present and easily recoverable. Finally, through perplexity analysis, we observe that the discovered token sequences lie in low-probability regions of the model distribution, supporting the hypothesis that they emerged implicitly from the training process.

</details>


### [226] [Physiologically Grounded Driver Behavior Classification: SHAP-Driven Elite Feature Selection and Hybrid Gradient Boosting for Multimodal Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05120)
*Sahar Askari,Mohammad Mahdi Mirza Ali Mohammadi,Fatemeh Ensafdoust,Amin Golnari,Saeid Sanei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可解释且可扩展的多模态生理信号驾驶行为解码框架，通过融合EEG、EMG和GSR信号，使用SHAP特征选择和梯度提升集成模型，在测试集上实现了80.91%的准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，显著优于单模态基线和传统机器学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码驾驶行为对于理解驾驶员状态和提高道路安全至关重要。然而，现有的研究多集中于单一模态的生理信号分析，缺乏对多模态信号融合的系统研究，且面临高维度数据和模型可解释性的挑战。因此，本研究旨在开发一个可解释且可扩展的多模态生理信号分析框架，以提升驾驶行为解码的性能和可信度。

Method: 研究构建了一个包含同步脑电(EEG)、肌电(EMG)和皮电反应(GSR)的大规模多模态生理驾驶行为数据集。首先对数据进行严格的预处理，随后提取时域、频域及衍生生理指标等特征。为解决高维问题，采用基于SHAP的精英特征选择方法，保留前250个特征以降低计算开销。使用Optuna进行贝叶斯优化，对XGBoost和LightGBM模型进行超参数调优，最后构建加权软投票集成模型以融合两者的优势。

Result: 提出的集成模型在测试集上达到了80.91%的准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，显著优于单模态基线和传统机器学习模型。消融实验显示，相比最佳单模态(EEG)，多模态融合带来了8%的性能提升，验证了多模态融合的必要性。SHAP分析进一步验证了模型的生理合理性，揭示EEG贡献了大部分预测权重，而GSR和EMG特征为高唤醒和运动密集型操作提供了关键的判别信号。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的可解释多模态生理信号解码框架有效提升了驾驶行为识别的性能，证明了多模态融合相对于单模态分析的显著优势。SHAP分析增强了模型的可解释性，揭示了不同生理信号在驾驶行为预测中的差异化贡献，为驾驶行为分析和驾驶员状态监测提供了新的研究思路和技术支持。

Abstract: An interpretable and scalable framework for decoding driving behaviors from multimodal physiological signals is proposed in this study. We utilize multimodal physiological driving behavior large-scale dataset comprising synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG), electromyography (EMG), and galvanic skin response (GSR) signals. Our approach involves rigorous preprocessing followed by a domain-specific feature extraction pipeline targeting time-domain, frequency-domain, and derived physiological indices. To address high dimensionality, we employ SHAP-based elite feature selection, retaining the top 250 features to reduce computational overhead while preserving predictive power. Hyperparameter optimization for extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) and light gradient boosting machine (LightGBM) models is conducted using Bayesian optimization via Optuna. Finally, a weighted soft-voting ensemble is constructed to leverage the complementary strengths of both gradient boosting frameworks. The results demonstrate that the proposed ensemble achieves a test accuracy of 80.91% and a macro-F1 score of 0.79, significantly outperforming single-modality baselines and traditional machine learning models. Ablation studies confirm an 8% performance gain over the best single modality (EEG), validating the necessity of multimodal fusion. SHAP analysis further validates the physiological plausibility of the model, revealing that the EEG contributes the majority of predictive weight, GSR and EMG features provide critical discriminatory signals for high-arousal and motor-intensive maneuvers.

</details>


### [227] [Conditional outlier detection for clinical alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05124)
*Milos Hauskrecht,Michal Valko,Shyam Visweswaran,Iyad Batal,Gilles Clermont,Gregory Cooper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于电子健康档案（EHR）的数据驱动方法，通过检测异常的患者管理行为来预警潜在医疗错误，并在心脏术后患者数据中验证了其可行性与较低的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于“相对于过往病例而言异常的患者管理行为可能暗示潜在医疗错误”这一假设，作者旨在开发一种自动化检测机制，以便在临床遇到此类异常情况时及时发出警示，从而提高患者安全性。

Method: 利用EHR系统中存储的历史病例数据，开发数据驱动的异常检测算法来识别不寻常的患者管理行为；使用4,486名心脏术后患者的EHR数据进行实证评估，并以专家小组的意见作为判断异常行为是否值得报警的金标准。

Result: 评估结果表明，基于异常的警报系统能够实现合理的低误报率；同时，异常程度越强，其对应的警报率越高，验证了假设的合理性。

Conclusion: 利用历史病例数据进行异常检测是一种有前景的辅助临床决策工具，能够在保持较低误报率的前提下，有效识别潜在的异常管理行为并触发警示。

Abstract: We develop and evaluate a data-driven approach for detecting unusual (anomalous) patient-management actions using past patient cases stored in an electronic health record (EHR) system. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to past patients may be due to a potential error and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. We evaluate this hypothesis using data obtained from the electronic health records of 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients. We base the evaluation on the opinions of a panel of experts. The results support that anomaly-based alerting can have reasonably low false alert rates and that stronger anomalies are correlated with higher alert rates.

</details>


### [228] [Transformed Latent Variable Multi-Output Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05133)
*Xiaoyu Jiang,Xinxing Shi,Sokratia Georgaka,Magnus Rattray,Mauricio A Álvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出T-LVMOGP框架，通过Lipschitz正则化神经网络构建多输出深度核，解决了MOGP在高维输出空间的可扩展性问题，在气候建模和空间转录组学等大规模任务上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多输出高斯过程(MOGPs)在处理高维输出空间数据时面临可扩展性瓶颈。现有方法为保持计算可行性，通常采用低秩或可分离和核等限制性假设，牺牲了模型表达能力。如何在扩展到大规模输出的同时保持捕获输出间依赖关系的能力，是该领域亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出Transformed Latent Variable MOGP (T-LVMOGP)框架：1）利用Lipschitz正则化神经网络将输入和输出特定隐变量映射到嵌入空间，构建灵活的多输出深度核；2）结合随机变分推理技术，实现对高维输出设置的有效扩展。该方法避免了对核函数的过度简化假设。

Result: 在多样化基准测试中，T-LVMOGP在预测精度和计算效率上均超越基线方法。具体包括：在超过10,000个输出的气候建模任务，以及零膨胀空间转录组学数据上表现出色，验证了该方法在大规模输出场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: T-LVMOGP成功突破了MOGPs在高维输出空间的可扩展性限制，在保持对输出间依赖关系建模能力的同时实现了计算可行性，为大规模多输出建模提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-Output Gaussian Processes (MOGPs) provide a principled probabilistic framework for modelling correlated outputs but face scalability bottlenecks when applied to datasets with high-dimensional output spaces. To maintain tractability, existing methods typically resort to restrictive assumptions, such as employing low-rank or sum-of-separable kernels, which can limit expressiveness. We propose the Transformed Latent Variable MOGP (T-LVMOGP), a novel framework that scales MOGPs to a massive number of outputs while preserving the capacity to capture meaningful inter-output dependencies. T-LVMOGP constructs a flexible multi-output deep kernel by mapping inputs and output-specific latent variables into an embedding space using a Lipschitz-regularised neural network. Combined with stochastic variational inference, our model effectively scales to high-dimensional output settings. Across diverse benchmarks, including climate modelling with over 10,000 outputs and zero-inflated spatial transcriptomics data, T-LVMOGP outperforms baselines in both predictive accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [229] [Low-Cost Black-Box Detection of LLM Hallucinations via Dynamical System Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05134)
*Dan Wilson,Mohamed Akrout*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Koopman算子理论的低成本幻觉检测方法，通过将LLM响应投影到高维流形并分析其动力学特性，在单次推理中即可实现最先进的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型经常生成看似合理但不符合事实的内容（即幻觉现象）。现有的检测方法通常依赖计算成本高昂的基于采样的一致性检查或外部知识检索，存在资源开销大的问题。

Method: 将LLM视为黑盒动力系统，通过嵌入模型将LLM响应投影到高维流形中，将生成的向量序列表征为模型潜在状态空间动力学的可观实现。利用Koopman算子理论拟合事实和幻觉两种状态下的转移算子，并基于各自的预测误差定义差分残差分数。同时引入偏好感知校准机制，根据少量演示样本优化分类阈值。

Result: 在三个数据基准上的广泛测试表明，该方法以较低的资源开销实现了最先进的幻觉检测性能。该方法能够在单次推理中完成检测，避免了额外的采样或外部基础支持。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一种低成本的幻觉检测方法，通过动力系统视角和Koopman算子理论实现了高效准确的幻觉识别，为LLM可靠性评估提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently generate plausible but non-factual content, a phenomenon known as hallucination. While existing detection methods typically rely on computationally expensive sampling-based consistency checks or external knowledge retrieval, we propose a new method that treats the LLM as a black-box dynamical system. By projecting LLM responses into a high-dimensional manifold via an embedding model, we characterize the resulting vector sequences as observable realizations of the model's latent state-space dynamics. Leveraging Koopman operator theory, we fit the transition operators for both factual and hallucinated regimes and define a differential residual score based on their respective prediction errors. To accommodate varying user requirements and domain-specific sensitivities, we introduce a preference-aware calibration mechanism that optimizes the classification threshold based on a small set of demonstrations. This approach enables low-cost hallucination detection in a single-sample pass, avoiding the need for secondary sampling or external grounding. Extensive testing across three data benchmarks demonstrates that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with reduced resource overhead.

</details>


### [230] [Human-AI Co-Mentorship in Project-Based Learning: A Case Study in Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05144)
*Freyaa Chawla,Ahan Chawla,Rishi Singh,Joe Germino,Grigorii Khvatskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一个由研究生指导、高中生和本科低年级学生参与的AI研究项目，展示了AI工具如何帮助基础薄弱的学生快速聚焦高阶问题求解，并成功完成ETF价格预测等实际任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的教学模式通常要求学生先在课堂中掌握必要的理论知识，随后才能进行实际应用。本研究旨在探索一种新的教学模式：在AI工具的辅助下，让学生先进行工作流程设计，再利用AI工具执行具体步骤，从而让基础有限的高中生也能参与并完成有意义的研究项目。

Method: 在研究生导师的指导下，高中生和本科低年级学生在AI工具的辅助下开展研究。项目强调工作流程设计：学生首先确定解决问题的步骤序列，然后借助AI工具（如代码生成工具）执行各步骤。团队通过每日站会进行调试和解答概念性问题，并让学生根据个人兴趣深入计算机科学或金融领域。

Result: 尽管参与者在AI和金融领域背景有限，但他们表现出了对技术市场分析和ETF价格预测的强烈热情。学生通过与AI工具的迭代交互成功开发了必要的代码，并协作取得了显著进展。每位学生都能在自己感兴趣的领域（计算机科学或金融）深入钻研，最终构建出具有现实应用价值的模型。

Conclusion: 该项目是一次重要的教学实践，证明了AI工具可以有效用于指导高中生进行研究。这种模式允许学生聚焦于自身特定兴趣，并通过每日站会专注于问题定义和概念理解。即使技术资质有限，学生也能借助AI工具构建具有实际意义的模型，为未来AI辅助教育提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: This paper reflects on a AI research project carried out by a team of high-school and early-undergraduate students under the mentorship of graduate researchers and ably assisted by AI tools. We share our experience in not only on the learning experience for the high school students, but also on how AI tools accelerated the process that enabled the high school students to focus on higher order problem formulation and solution. Although the participants entered the project with limited background in both AI and finance, they showed strong enthusiasm for technical market analysis and ETF price prediction. Traditional learning settings would first teach the necessary methods in a classroom setting and only later let students apply them. In contrast, our project emphasized workflow design: students identified the sequence of steps needed to address the problem and then used AI-driven tools to execute each step.
  We note that the high school students developed the necessary code through iterating with the AI tools, and we used our daily stand-ups to debug and answer conceptual questions. Each of the student was able to dig deeper into their area of interest whether computer science or finance, while collaboratively making a significant advance over the summer of 2025. This project was an important pedagogical exercise on how AI tools can be used for mentoring high school students, allowing them to focus on their specific interests and using the daily stand-ups to focus on problem definition and conceptual understanding. Despite their limited technical qualifications, the students were able to leverage AI tools to build meaningful models with real-world application.

</details>


### [231] [Understanding In-Context Learning for Nonlinear Regression with Transformers: Attention as Featurizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05176)
*Alexander Hsu,Zhaiming Shen,Wenjing Liao,Rongjie Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了显式的Transformer网络实现非线性特征，建立了非线性上下文回归的理论分析框架，并提供了有限样本泛化误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文学习理论主要局限于线性模型，对于非线性回归场景的理论理解仍然不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究非线性回归设置下的上下文学习。

Method: 通过注意力机制中的交互机制，显式构建Transformer网络以实现非线性特征（如多项式基或样条基），这些特征能够覆盖广泛的函数类别。基于此构造，建立了端到端上下文非线性回归的分析框架。

Result: 提出了基于上下文长度和训练集大小的有限样本泛化误差界，并在合成回归任务上进行了数值验证，证实了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了上下文学习的理论理解至非线性回归场景，通过显式构造Transformer网络实现了非线性特征学习，为理解Transformer在非线性任务中的能力提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Pre-trained transformers are able to learn from examples provided as part of the prompt without any weight updates, a remarkable ability known as in-context learning (ICL). Despite its demonstrated efficacy across various domains, the theoretical understanding of ICL is still developing. Whereas most existing theory has focused on linear models, we study ICL in the nonlinear regression setting. Through the interaction mechanism in attention, we explicitly construct transformer networks to realize nonlinear features, such as polynomial or spline bases, which span a wide class of functions. Based on this construction, we establish a framework to analyze end-to-end in-context nonlinear regression with the constructed features. Our theory provides finite-sample generalization error bounds in terms of context length and training set size. We numerically validate the theory on synthetic regression tasks.

</details>


### [232] [Estimating the expected output of wide random MLPs more efficiently than sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05179)
*Wilson Wu,Victor Lecomte,Michael Winer,George Robinson,Jacob Hilton,Paul Christiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需采样的方法来估计MLP在初始化时对高斯输入的期望输出，在宽网络上比蒙特卡洛采样更高效，且在估计稀有事件概率方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中估计期望损失的常规方法是采样并计算经验平均，但采样不一定是最优的。本文旨在探索是否存在比采样更高效的方法来估计期望输出。

Method: 不通过网络运行样本，而是利用累积量和埃尔米特展开等工具，在每一层产生激活分布的近似表示，从而估计MLP在初始化时对高斯输入的期望输出。

Result: 理论和实证表明，对于足够宽的网络，该估计器在达到目标均方误差时所需的FLOPs远少于蒙特卡洛采样；在估计稀有事件概率方面表现尤为突出；该方法还可用于模型训练。

Conclusion: 这些发现为构建能够大幅降低灾难性尾部风险概率的模型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: By far the most common way to estimate an expected loss in machine learning is to draw samples, compute the loss on each one, and take the empirical average. However, sampling is not necessarily optimal. Given an MLP at initialization, we show how to estimate its expected output over Gaussian inputs without running samples through the network at all. Instead, we produce approximate representations of the distributions of activations at each layer, leveraging tools such as cumulants and Hermite expansions. We show both theoretically and empirically that for sufficiently wide networks, our estimator achieves a target mean squared error using substantially fewer FLOPs than Monte Carlo sampling. We find moreover that our methods perform particularly well at estimating the probabilities of rare events, and additionally demonstrate how they can be used for model training. Together, these findings suggest a path to producing models with a greatly reduced probability of catastrophic tail risks.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Constraint-Aware Execution Planning for Hybrid Space-Ground Compute Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04052)
*Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了约束感知执行(CAE)系统，通过四阶段规划解决低轨卫星数据生成与下行链路带宽不匹配问题，在2秒内生成可行执行方案并已部署为生产API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星每轨道产生的数据量比其下行能力高约两个数量级，迫使操作者为每个工作负载决策计算位置、中间数据传输时机及如何在噪声信道上保证传输可靠性，亟需自动化规划系统解决此挑战。

Method: CAE采用四阶段流程：(1)通过SGP4传播、日食检测和地面站过境预测构建轨道环境；(2)使用成本模型比较星上资源消耗与传输开销进行计算放置决策；(3)插入传输环节，包含自适应前向纠错和安全开销建模；(4)在功率、热控、计算和通信约束下采用贪心首次适应策略调度至轨道窗口。

Result: 在五种代表性工作负载模式和不同轨道体制卫星上的评估表明：CAE在2秒内生成可行规划，有效利用星上数据压缩最小化传输量，并根据信道条件自适应调整前向纠错和多轮分配策略。

Conclusion: CAE成功实现了卫星工作负载的自动化规划，已在生产环境中部署为API服务，可为任何编目卫星利用实时两行轨道根数数据计算执行方案，为星地协同计算提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites increasingly carry compute hardware capable of on-board processing, yet each satellite generates roughly two orders of magnitude more data than it can downlink per orbit. This mismatch forces operators to decide, for every workload, which computation runs on-board and which runs on the ground, how intermediate data crosses the space-ground boundary through narrow contact windows, and how to maintain delivery guarantees over noisy channels. We present Constraint-Aware Execution (CAE), a planning system that takes a satellite identifier, a workload expressed as a directed acyclic graph of processing steps, and a set of orbital and resource constraints, and produces a deterministic, physically grounded execution plan. CAE operates in four phases: (1) orbital environment construction via SGP4 propagation with eclipse detection and ground station pass prediction, (2) compute placement using a cost model that compares on-board resource consumption against transfer overhead, (3) transfer insertion with adaptive forward error correction and security overhead modeling, and (4) greedy first-fit scheduling into orbital windows under power, thermal, compute, and communication constraints. We evaluate CAE against five representative workload patterns across satellites in distinct orbital regimes and demonstrate that the system produces feasible plans in under two seconds, correctly exploits onboard data reduction to minimize transfer volume, and adapts FEC and multi-pass allocation to varying channel conditions. CAE is deployed as a production API computing plans for any cataloged satellite using live two-line element data.

</details>


### [234] [Microbenchmark-Driven Analytical Performance Modeling Across Modern GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04178)
*Aaron Jarmusch,Sunita Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对NVIDIA Blackwell (B200) 和 AMD CDNA3 (MI300A) 设计并开发了高精度的分析性能模型，有效弥合了理论峰值与实际性能之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU架构（如复杂的内存层次结构、矩阵单元和多种精度格式）快速演进，理论峰值性能与实际可达成性能之间的差距日益扩大，传统的Roofline模型误差较大，迫切需要更精准的性能建模方法。

Method: 基于系统的微基准测试特征分析，构建了针对不同架构特点的分析性能模型：针对Blackwell架构捕获了张量内存（TMEM）、异步批量复制（TMA）和第五代张量核心特性；针对CDNA3架构捕获了Infinity Cache层次结构、VGPR约束和占用率特性。模型进一步适配至H200 (Hopper) 和 MI250X (CDNA2) 架构。

Result: 模型验证取得了高精度结果：在B200上（21个内核）平均绝对误差（MAE）为1.31%，在MI300A上（27个内核）MAE仅为0.09%；相比之下，朴素Roofline基线在同一组内核上的误差超过95%。使用Rodinia 3.1和SPEChpc 2021 Tiny进行了进一步验证。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了跨架构的高精度GPU性能模型，显著优于传统方法，且模型具有良好的可扩展性和通用性，无需重大重构即可应用于架构更新（如H200和MI250X）。所有模型和基准测试将作为开源发布。

Abstract: Rapidly evolving GPU architectures featuring complex memory hierarchies, matrix units, and varied precision formats continue to widen the gap between theoretical peaks and achievable performance. We design and develop analytical performance models for NVIDIA Blackwell (B200) and AMD CDNA3 (MI300A) grounded in systematic microbenchmark characterization. For Blackwell, the model captures Tensor Memory (TMEM), asynchronous bulk copy (TMA), and 5th-generation tensor cores; for CDNA3, the model captures Infinity Cache hierarchy, VGPR constraints, and occupancy. Validation yields 1.31% MAE on B200 (21 kernels) and 0.09% on MI300A (27 kernels), while naive roofline baselines exceed 95% error on the same kernels. We further validate the models using Rodinia~3.1 and SPEChpc 2021 Tiny.The models are updated with HBM bandwidth, capacity, and cache parameters and applied to H200 (Hopper) and MI250X (CDNA2), indicating no major restructuring of the models are needed. All models and benchmarks will be released as open-source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [235] [phys-MCP: A Control Plane for Heterogeneous Physical Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04256)
*Stefan Fischer,Maliheh Hariri,Sebastian Otte*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了phys-MCP，一种面向异构物理神经网络(PNN)的基板感知编排架构，通过统一控制平面将分子、化学、生物、光子、忆阻器和机械等不同物理基板暴露为可发现、可调用的资源，实现了跨异构物理AI资源的描述符可移植集成、遥感知故障恢复以及对湿件API路径的成功执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理神经网络(PNNs)将计算直接嵌入材料动力学中，适用于极端边缘计算场景。然而，由于每种基板（分子、化学、生物、光子、忆阻器、机械等）暴露不同的接口、时序行为、可观察性限制和生命周期需求，PNN难以集成到边缘-云端软件栈中。现有的系统集成缺乏针对异构PNN的统一控制平面。

Method: 提出phys-MCP基板感知编排架构：1) 定义能力模型、生命周期语义、遥测接口和数字孪生绑定；2) 保留基板特定属性（延迟、可重置性、可塑性、I/O模态）；3) 通过包含三个代表性后端类、HTTP支持执行路径和集成Cortical Labs适配器的原型进行实例化；4) 暴露湿件面向的API路径。

Result: 实验验证表明：1) 跨异构后端的描述符可移植集成；2) 相比简单基线改善了运行时感知匹配；3) 在代表性故障下实现遥感知恢复；4) 成功执行API支持的湿件路径；5) 本地控制路径开销小。原型级证据证明基板感知控制可跨越异构物理AI资源、孪生支持后端和湿件面向API路径。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过统一的基板感知控制平面架构，可以有效解决异构物理神经网络集成到边缘-云软件栈的挑战，为极端边缘计算场景下物理AI资源的编排管理提供了可行的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: Physical neural networks (PNNs) embed computation directly in material dynamics, including molecular, chemical, biological, photonic, memristive, and mechanical substrates. They are attractive for edge computing, especially at the extreme edge, where computation can be placed at the interface to sensing, actuation, or the physical process itself. However, PNNs are difficult to integrate into edge-cloud software stacks because each substrate exposes distinct interfaces, timing behavior, observability limits, and lifecycle requirements. This paper argues that the missing systems component is a common control plane for heterogeneous PNNs. We present phys-MCP, a substrate-aware orchestration architecture that exposes physical neural substrates as discoverable and invocable resources for edge, fog, and cloud workflows, while preserving their possible placement at the extreme edge. phys-MCP defines a capability model, lifecycle semantics, telemetry interfaces, and digital-twin bindings that retain substrate-specific properties such as latency, resetability, plasticity, and I/O modality. We instantiate the architecture through a prototype with three representative backend classes, an HTTP-backed execution path, and an integrated Cortical Labs adapter exposing a wetware-facing API path through the same control model. The evaluation combines controlled experiments on representative backends with end-to-end validation of the Cortical Labs path. Results show descriptor-portable integration across heterogeneous backends, improved runtime-aware matching over simpler baselines, telemetry-aware recovery under representative faults, successful execution against the API-backed wetware path, and small local control-path overhead. Overall, results provide prototype-level evidence that substrate-aware control can span heterogeneous physical AI resources, twin-backed backends, and a wetware-facing API path.

</details>


### [236] [ClusterLess: Deadline-Aware Serverless Workflow Orchestration on Federated Edge Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04310)
*Reza Farahani,Mario Colosi,Ilir Murturi,Stefan Nastic,Massimo Villari,Schahram Dustdar,Radu Prodan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ClusterLess，一种面向联邦多边缘K8s集群的截止期感知无服务器工作流编排方法，在64个异构边缘节点的测试平台上将工作流完成时间降低高达40%，截止期满足率从低于50%提升至超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边缘计算、无服务器执行和基于Kubernetes的容器编排融合方案在单一边缘集群内有效，但无法推广到联邦多边缘环境，而该环境中多个工作流在严格的端到端（E2E）截止期约束下并发执行，缺乏有效的跨集群编排机制。

Method: 提出ClusterLess方法，管理工作流执行的E2E生命周期，包括依赖分析、执行模式选择和资源感知放置。该方法将结构化的集群内编排与领导者选择、超级主控驱动的集群间协调层相集成，确定联邦边缘集群间每个工作流函数的执行位置和方式。基于OpenFaaS作为无服务器执行底座、Argo进行工作流管理进行实现。

Result: 在包含6个边缘集群、64个异构边缘节点的现实测试平台上，使用并发无服务器工作流，跨越18种工作负载配置（不同输入大小和截止期类别）进行实验。与四种基线方法相比，ClusterLess将工作流完成时间降低高达40%，将截止期满足率从低于50%提升至超过90%，并将截止期违约限制在个位数秒内。

Conclusion: ClusterLess有效解决了联邦多边缘环境下无服务器工作流的编排问题，显著提升了工作流执行效率和截止期满足率，为联邦边缘计算场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The recent convergence of edge computing, serverless execution, and Kubernetes (K8s) based container orchestration has enabled the processing of application workflows close to data sources. While effective within a single edge cluster, existing schemes do not generalize to federated multi edge environments, where multiple workflows execute concurrently under strict end to end (E2E) deadline constraints. This paper introduces ClusterLess, a deadline aware serverless workflow orchestration method for federated multi edge K8s clusters. ClusterLess manages the E2E lifecycle of workflow execution, including dependency analysis, execution mode selection, and resource aware placement. To this end, it integrates structured intra cluster orchestration with a leader selected, super master driven intercluster coordination layer, determining where and how each workflow function should be executed across the federated edge clusters. We implement ClusterLess using OpenFaaS as the serverless execution substrate and Argo for workflow management, and deploy it on a realistic testbed of six edge clusters comprising 64 heterogeneous edge nodes. Experimental results with concurrent serverless workflows, spanning 18 workload configurations across different input sizes and deadline classes, show that ClusterLess reduces workflow completion time by up to 40 %, increases deadline satisfaction from below 50 % to over 90 %, and confines deadline violations to single digit seconds compared to four baseline methods.

</details>


### [237] [Orchestrating Serverless Applications in the Edge Cloud Space Continuum: What Breaks and What is Next?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04316)
*Hadi Tabatabaee Malazi,Reza Farahani,Nitinder Mohan,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出传统无服务器编排在LEO边缘云场景下存在十个失效假设，提出了一种新的架构以应对动态图上的时空执行、约束感知的函数放置与扩展、以及去中心化延迟状态下的正确性保障等核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算在边缘云环境中已取得成功，促使研究者尝试将其扩展到包含低地球轨道（LEO）星座的“云-边-端”连续体中。然而，LEO系统具有时变接触图、间歇性链路可用性以及严格的资源（能量、内存、通信、成本）约束，导致现有的无服务器编排假设不再适用，因此亟需新的编排机制。

Method: 首先识别并归纳了现有无服务器编排在LEO环境下的十个失效假设，将其组织为三大核心挑战：动态图上的时空执行、约束感知的函数放置与扩展、以及去中心化和延迟状态下的正确性与进展保障。基于这些挑战，提出了一种面向连续体的鲁棒高效无服务器执行架构，并通过洪水响应用例进行演示。

Result: 文章系统性地揭示了现有技术在LEO动态环境中的局限性，并提出了相应的架构解决方案，通过典型用例验证了该架构在应对时变接触图、间歇链路及多重约束下的有效性。

Conclusion: 为在包含LEO星座的边缘云连续体中实现稳健高效的无服务器计算，必须重新审视并解决传统编排中的核心假设问题，本文提出的架构为此提供了理论基础与实践参考。

Abstract: Serverless computing has matured into an effective execution model for edge cloud environments, enabling function level decomposition, demand driven scaling, and workflow execution across stable, well provisioned infrastructure. This success motivates extending it to the edge cloud space continuum, where Low Earth Orbit (LEO) constellations are increasingly explored as distributed compute substrates. However, existing serverless orchestration is not directly applicable in this setting, where LEO systems impose time varying contact graphs, intermittent link availability, and strict feasibility constraints on energy, memory, communication, and operational cost. This article identifies ten broken assumptions in existing serverless orchestration and organizes them into three core challenges: spatiotemporal execution over dynamic graphs, constraint aware function placement and scaling, and correctness and progress under decentralized and delayed state. It then proposes an architecture that enables robust and efficient serverless execution across the continuum, grounded in these challenges and demonstrated through a representative flood response use case.

</details>


### [238] [Coral: Cost-Efficient Multi-LLM Serving over Heterogeneous Cloud GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04357)
*Yixuan Mei,Zikun Li,Zixuan Chen,Shiqi Pan,Mengdi Wu,Xupeng Miao,Zhihao Jia,K. V. Rashmi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Coral，一个异构感知的多LLM服务系统，通过联合优化资源分配和服务策略，在20种GPU配置下相比最佳基线降低服务成本高达2.79倍，提升有效吞吐量高达2.39倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM使用日益分散，没有单一模型占主导地位；同时云提供商提供大量中低端和旧一代GPU，这些GPU可用性更好且性价比与高端硬件相当。如何高效利用这些异构资源并发服务多个LLM成为关键挑战。

Method: 提出Coral系统，核心思想是跨所有模型联合优化资源分配和每个模型副本的服务策略；采用无损两阶段分解方法，在保持联合最优性的同时将在线求解时间从数小时缩短至数十秒，以适应不断变化的吞吐量需求和资源可用性。

Result: 在6个模型和20种GPU配置上的评估表明：Coral相比最佳基线降低服务成本高达2.79倍；在资源稀缺情况下提供高达2.39倍的更高有效吞吐量。

Conclusion: Coral成功实现了在异构GPU资源上高效并发服务多个LLM，显著降低了服务成本并提升了资源稀缺场景下的系统性能，为云环境下的多LLM部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The usage of large language models (LLMs) has grown increasingly fragmented, with no single model dominating. Meanwhile, cloud providers offer a wide range of mid-tier and older-generation GPUs that enjoy better availability and deliver comparable performance per dollar to top-tier hardware. To efficiently harness these heterogeneous resources for serving multiple LLMs concurrently, we introduce Coral, an adaptive heterogeneity-aware multi-LLM serving system. The key idea behind Coral is to jointly optimize resource allocation and the serving strategy of each model replica across all models. To keep pace with shifting throughput demand and resource availability, Coral applies a lossless two-stage decomposition that preserves joint optimality while cutting online solve time from hours to tens of seconds. Our evaluation across 6 models and 20 GPU configurations shows that Coral reduces serving cost by up to 2.79$\times$ over the best baseline, and delivers up to 2.39$\times$ higher goodput under scarce resource availability.

</details>


### [239] [One Pool, Two Caches: Adaptive HBM Partitioning for Accelerating Generative Recommender Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04450)
*Wenjun Yu,Shuguang Han,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HELM通过联合管理GPU HBM中的嵌入缓存与KV缓存分配及请求路由，解决了生成式推荐系统中的内存竞争问题，在32节点A100集群上实现了24-38%的P99延迟降低和高达99.6%的SLO满足率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器(GR)推理中，嵌入热缓存(EMB)与KV缓存竞争有限的GPU HBM资源。现有系统孤立优化二者，忽视了最优EMB-KV分配比例在不同工作负载下可变化达0.35，导致20-30%的延迟优化潜力未被挖掘。而在线重分配需要在关键路径上引入H2D填充流量，会导致P99 SLO违规。

Method: HELM包含两个核心组件：(1)自适应内存分配——基于PPO的三层控制器（冻结基础策略、在线残差适配器、突发感知恢复控制器），实现32μs决策延迟，且与离线最优比例偏差仅0.024-0.029；(2)EMB-KV感知调度——联合考虑KV驻留性、嵌入局部性和节点负载进行请求路由，避免异构分配下的路由低效问题。

Result: 在32节点A100集群上的三个生产级数据集评估显示：相比最优静态策略，HELM将P99延迟降低24-38%；在Steady、Trend和Burst工作负载下实现93.5-99.6%的SLO满足率，显著优于最先进基线方法，且不牺牲吞吐量。

Conclusion: HELM证明了在生成式推荐系统中联合优化HBM分配与请求路由的有效性，通过运行时自适应调整内存分配比例并智能调度请求，成功解决了EMB与KV缓存竞争问题，实现了显著的性能提升，为工业级部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative Recommender (GR) inference places embedding hot caches (EMB) and KV caches in direct competition for limited GPU HBM: allocating more memory to one improves its efficiency but degrades the other. Existing systems optimize them in isolation, overlooking that the optimal EMB-KV allocation ratio can shift by up to 0.35 across workload regimes, leaving 20-30\% latency improvement unrealized. While online reallocation is required to close this gap, naive approaches introduce H2D refill traffic on the critical path, causing P99 SLO violations.
  To address this, we present HELM, which jointly manages HBM allocation and request routing at runtime through two key components: (1) Adaptive Memory Allocation, a three-layer PPO-based controller (frozen base policy, online residual adapter, and burst-aware recovery controller) that achieves $32\,\mathrm{μs}$ decision latency while staying within 0.024-0.029 of the offline-optimal ratio; and (2) EMB-KV-Aware Scheduling, which routes requests by jointly considering KV residency, embedding locality, and node load to avoid routing inefficiencies under heterogeneous allocations. Evaluations on three production-scale datasets over a 32-node A100 cluster show that HELM reduces P99 latency by 24-38\% over the best static policy and achieves 93.5-99.6\% SLO satisfaction across Steady, Trend, and Burst workloads, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines without sacrificing throughput.

</details>


### [240] [CCL-D: A High-Precision Diagnostic System for Slow and Hang Anomalies in Large-Scale Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04478)
*Yida Gu,Fakang Wang,Jianhao Fu,Zhenhang Sun,Qianyu Zhang,Hairui Zhao,Xingchen Liu,Yang Tian,Wenjing Huang,Zedong Liu,Yifan Chen,Jinwu Yang,Yueyuan Zhou,Qian Zhao,Haoxu Li,Tao Wang,Feng Yu,Zhan Wang,Guangming Tan,Dingwen Tao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了CCL-D，一个针对大规模分布式训练中集合通信库（CCL）慢速/挂起异常的高精度诊断系统，能在6分钟内精准定位故障GPU节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着训练规模扩大，硬件、软件和环境因素间的复杂交互导致集合通信库（CCL）频繁出现异常（主要表现为通信慢速或挂起）。传统诊断方法准确性低且效率低下，通常需要数小时甚至数天才能完成根因分析。

Method: CCL-D集成了节点级实时探针和智能决策分析器。探针利用轻量级分布式追踪框架测量跨层异常指标以监控通信流量；分析器执行自动化异常检测与根因定位，精准识别故障GPU节点。

Result: 在4000 GPU集群上部署一年，CCL-D实现了对已知慢速/挂起异常的近乎完全覆盖，并在6分钟内定位受影响的节点，显著优于现有解决方案。

Conclusion: CCL-D系统有效解决了大规模分布式训练中CCL通信异常诊断难、耗时长的问题，大幅提升了异常检测的准确性与定位效率，具有很高的实用价值。

Abstract: As training scales grow, collective communication libraries (CCL) increasingly face anomalies arising from complex interactions among hardware, software, and environmental factors. These anomalies typically manifest as slow/hang communication, the most frequent and time-consuming category to diagnose. However, traditional diagnostic methods remain inaccurate and inefficient, frequently requiring hours or even days for root cause analysis. To address this, we propose CCL-D, a high-precision diagnostic system designed to detect and locate slow/hang anomalies in large-scale distributed training. CCL-D integrates a rank-level real-time probe with an intelligent decision analyzer. The probe measures cross-layer anomaly metrics using a lightweight distributed tracing framework to monitor communication traffic. The analyzer performs automated anomaly detection and root-cause location, precisely identifying the faulty GPU rank. Deployed on a 4,000-GPU cluster over one year, CCL-D achieved near-complete coverage of known slow/hang anomalies and pinpointed affected ranks within 6 minutes-substantially outperforming existing solutions.

</details>


### [241] [Delay-Aware Large-Small Model Collaboration over LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04565)
*Mingyu Guo,Wen Wu,Ying Wang,Songge Zhang,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向低地球轨道(LEO)卫星网络的延迟感知大-小模型协作方案，通过联合优化卸载决策和路由策略，利用多智能体强化学习与在线二分搜索算法，实现了服务延迟降低高达31.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络面临计算资源受限与通信负载不均衡的挑战。传统方案难以同时平衡卫星间的计算负载与星间链路的通信负载，导致服务延迟较高，因此需要一种能够协同大模型与小模型优势的新型协作机制。

Method: 提出大-小模型协作架构：资源受限的遥感卫星负责数据采集与小模型本地处理，计算卫星提供大模型处理服务。将联合优化问题建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(DEC-POMDP)，开发了结合离线策略训练与在线二分搜索的多智能体强化学习(MARL)算法，离线训练确定路由策略，在线二分搜索迭代调整卸载决策。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与基准方案相比，该方案能够将服务延迟降低高达31.85%，有效实现了计算负载与通信负载的均衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的延迟感知大-小模型协作方案能够有效解决LEO卫星网络中的负载均衡问题，显著降低服务延迟，为卫星边缘智能提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a delay-aware largesmall model collaboration scheme for low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks, which can balance the computational load among satellites and the communication load across inter-satellite links. Specifically, computational resource constrained remote sensing satellites are responsible for data collection and local processing using small models, while collaborating with computing satellites that provide large model processing. To minimize the service delay, we formulate a joint optimization problem for offloading decision and routing strategy design, which is transformed into a decentralized partially observable Markov decision process. To solve the problem, we develop a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL)-based algorithm with offline policy training and online bisection search. The offline trained policy determines routing strategies, while online bisection search iteratively adjusts the offloading decisions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme can reduce the service delay by up to 31.85% compared with the benchmarks.

</details>


### [242] [Communication Offloading on SmartNIC DPUs: A Quantitative Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04842)
*Jacob Wahlgren,Andong Hu,Roger Pearce,Maya Gokhale,Ivy Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文设计了一个名为Buddy的通信卸载引擎，探索了SmartNIC DPU支持“即发即弃”异步通信模型的可行性，发现访存通信比是性能预测的关键指标，在主机主导的应用中最高实现了1.55倍加速，同时揭示了DPU因缺乏直接缓存访问支持导致DRAM流量激增的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SmartNIC数据处理器（DPU）通过将任务卸载到网络接口附近的可编程核心，为节省高端CPU资源提供了一种有前景的解决方案。本文旨在探索SmartNIC DPU在支持“即发即弃”异步通信模型（特别是其核心消息路由服务）方面的可行性。

Method: 设计了一个名为Buddy的通信卸载引擎，该引擎将通信任务与应用进程解耦。Buddy可以灵活运行在SmartNIC DPU（如Nvidia BlueField-3 DPU）和通用x86 CPU上。研究人员在五个应用上进行了评估，分析内存与通信比率对卸载性能的影响。

Result: 在五个应用的评估中发现，内存与通信比率是卸载性能的关键预测指标。对于主机主导的工作负载（如Quicksilver和稀疏矩阵转置），通信卸载到DPU实现了最高1.55倍的加速。此外，研究还发现由于DPU上缺乏直接缓存访问支持，DRAM流量增加了625倍。

Conclusion: Buddy系统验证了在SmartNIC DPU上实现通信卸载的可行性，并识别出内存与通信比率作为关键性能预测指标。研究结果揭示了未来SmartNIC设计中对直接缓存访问支持的关键需求，以解决DRAM流量激增的问题。

Abstract: SmartNIC Data Processing Units (DPUs) offer a promising solution for saving high-end CPU resources by offloading tasks to programmable cores near the network interface. In this work, we explore the feasibility of SmartNIC DPUs in supporting an asynchronous communication model called "fire-and-forget", particularly its core message routing service. We design a communication offloading engine called Buddy that decouples communication tasks from the application process. Buddy runs flexibly on SmartNIC DPUs such as the Nvidia BlueField-3 DPU and generic x86 CPUs. Our evaluation results in five applications identify the memory-to-communication ratio as a key predictor of the offloading performance. Host-dominated workloads, such as Quicksilver and Sparse Matrix Transpose, achieved up to 1.55x speedup with communication offloaded to the DPU. We further identify a 625x increase in DRAM traffic due to the absence of Direct Cache Access support on the DPU, highlighting a critical need in future SmartNIC designs.

</details>


### [243] [Piper: Efficient Large-Scale MoE Training via Resource Modeling and Pipelined Hybrid Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05049)
*Sajal Dash,Feiyi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对MoE模型在HPC平台上训练的挑战，提出了Piper框架，通过资源建模和优化的流水线并行调度，实现了比现有框架高2-3.5倍的MFU，并提出了一种新的all-to-all算法，带宽比厂商实现高1.2-9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型越来越多地采用混合专家（MoE）架构以在降低成本的同时实现大模型性能。然而，在HPC平台上训练MoE模型面临三大挑战：大内存占用、异构网络间频繁的大规模通信以及严重的负载不平衡。为了表征和解决这些问题，需要深入分析MoE配置在各种并行化方案下的资源需求。

Method: 1. 开发数学模型量化MoE配置在各种并行化方案下的内存、计算和通信需求，并通过微基准测试、代码插桩和硬件分析进行验证。2. 提出Piper框架，利用资源建模为目标HPC平台识别高效的MoE模型训练策略，应用具有优化调度方案的流水线并行。3. 提出一种新颖的all-to-all算法以提升通信效率。

Result: 1. 分析识别出关键性能瓶颈：专家并行导致的大规模all-to-all延迟、计算与通信重叠不足、不平衡的瘦GEMM导致的GPU利用率低、以及缺乏平台感知的混合并行策略。2. Piper框架实现了比最先进框架（如X-MoE）高2-3.5倍的MFU（模型FLOPs利用率）。3. 新颖的all-to-all算法相比厂商实现实现了1.2-9倍的带宽提升。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的资源建模和优化的并行策略，Piper框架有效解决了MoE模型在HPC平台上的训练挑战，显著提升了训练效率和资源利用率，为大规模MoE模型的高效训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Frontier models increasingly adopt Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures to achieve large-model performance at reduced cost. However, training MoE models on HPC platforms is hindered by large memory footprints, frequent large-scale communication across heterogeneous networks, and severe workload imbalance. To characterize these challenges, we develop a mathematical model that quantifies memory, compute, and communication requirements for MoE configurations under various parallelization schemes, verified through micro-benchmarking, code instrumentation, and hardware profiling. Our analysis identifies performance bottlenecks: all-to-all latency at scale from expert parallelism, insufficient compute-communication overlap, low GPU utilization from imbalanced skinny GEMMs, and the absence of platform-aware hybrid parallelization strategies. To address these, we introduce Piper, a framework that leverages resource modeling to identify efficient training strategies for MoE models on target HPC platforms, applying pipeline parallelism with optimized schedules. Piper achieves 2-3.5X higher MFU than state-of-the-art frameworks such as X-MoE, and a novel all-to-all algorithm delivers 1.2-9X bandwidth over vendor implementation.

</details>


### [244] [Toward a Risk Assessment Framework for Institutional DeFi: A Nine-Dimension Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05145)
*Eva Oberholzer,Valeriy Zamaraiev*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个九维度DeFi风险评估框架，在现有六维度分类基础上新增了可组合性风险、理解债务和时间风险动态三个维度，通过对8000多个协议的结构分析验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi协议现已中介超过1000亿美元的价值，但缺乏机构级严格的风险评估框架。现有方法侧重于特定协议参数优化或概念分类，未提供可解释、感知可组合性且结构独立的评估方法。

Method: 提出九维度风险评估框架，扩展Moody's Analytics和Gauntlet的六维度分类，新增可组合性风险、理解债务和时间风险动态三个维度，引入透明度置信修正因子。基于覆盖8000多个DeFi协议的本体驱动协议智能基础设施进行协议依赖的结构分析。

Result: 对2024-2026年12起重大DeFi事件（约25亿美元直接损失）进行回溯分析，其中5起事件需要至少一个新维度才能完整表征根本原因，包括数据集中系统性影响最大的两起事件。

Conclusion: 新增的风险维度对于完整刻画高影响DeFi事件的根本原因至关重要，所提框架能够有效支持机构级别的DeFi风险评估需求。

Abstract: Decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols now intermediate over USD 100 billion in value, including regulated stablecoins and tokenized assets deployed as collateral, yet no widely adopted framework operationalizes risk assessment at the rigor institutional adoption demands. Existing approaches emphasize protocol-specific parameter optimization or conceptual taxonomies without providing explainable, composability-aware, and structurally independent assessment methodologies.
  We propose a nine-dimension DeFi risk assessment framework extending the six-dimension taxonomy introduced by Moody's Analytics and Gauntlet with three novel dimensions: composability risk, comprehension debt, and temporal risk dynamics. We additionally introduce a transparency confidence modifier separating assessment reliability from risk severity.
  The framework is grounded in structural analysis of protocol dependencies conducted through an ontology-based protocol intelligence infrastructure covering more than 8,000 DeFi protocols. We retrospectively analyze 12 major DeFi-related incidents from 2024-2026 representing approximately USD 2.5 billion in direct losses. Five of the 12 incidents require at least one novel dimension for complete root-cause characterization, including the two highest-systemic-impact events in the dataset.

</details>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [ipc_shared_ptr: A Publish/Subscribe-Aware Smart Pointer for Cross-Process Object Lifetime Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04226)
*Takahiro Ishikawa-Aso,Atsushi Yano,Koichi Imai,Takuya Azumi,Shinpei Kato*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出ipc_shared_ptr智能指针，通过利用发布/订阅结构特性优化跨进程消息生命周期管理，在单写入者架构下实现了比现有方案更优的延迟性能，证明了实现简洁性优于复杂所有者驱动回收策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ROS 2等发布/订阅中间件中实现真正的零拷贝IPC需要订阅者引用发布者拥有的共享内存中的消息对象，这带来了跨进程对象生命周期管理的挑战：既要防止对象在被引用时被回收，又要确保最终回收，还需正确处理崩溃恢复和Transient Local QoS保持需求。

Method: 提出ipc_shared_ptr发布/订阅感知智能指针，利用发布/订阅结构特性改进Birrell引用列表机制，将全局元数据更新限制为每订阅者0<->1转换。对比两种架构：iceoryx2采用所有者驱动回收，Agnocast采用单写入者架构实现结构原子性，后者以集中化瓶颈为代价消除并发成员变更与回收决策的竞争复杂性。

Result: 在Autoware规模（最大开源ROS 2应用）的对比评估中，单写入者架构展现出足够可扩展性：在200个主题、每主题2个订阅者、100Hz条件下，Agnocast的端到端p99.9延迟比iceoryx2低2.9倍，验证了实现简洁性优于所有者驱动回收策略。

Conclusion: 单写入者架构在实现简洁性上优于所有者驱动回收，且在实际大规模应用场景下能够提供足够好的可扩展性和性能表现，证明了在发布/订阅系统中采用ipc_shared_ptr配合单写入者架构是实现真正零拷贝IPC的有效方案。

Abstract: True zero-copy Inter-Process Communication (IPC) in publish/subscribe (pub/sub) middleware such as Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) requires subscribers to reference message objects in publisher-owned shared memory. Objects must not be reclaimed while referenced, yet must eventually be reclaimed, with correct handling of crash recovery and Transient Local QoS retention requirements. We propose ipc_shared_ptr, a pub/sub-aware smart pointer for cross-process message lifetime management. ipc_shared_ptr exploits pub/sub structural properties to specialize Birrell's reference listing, limiting global metadata updates to per-subscriber 0<->1 transitions and achieving an order-of-magnitude reduction in global communication over general-purpose distributed reference counting. We analyze the key metadata management tradeoff: scalability versus implementation simplicity. Owner-driven reclaim offers greater scalability, but concurrent membership changes and reclamation decisions produce races that widen the correctness-verification state space. Single-writer achieves structural atomicity, eliminating this complexity at the cost of a centralized bottleneck. iceoryx2 (owner-driven reclaim) and Agnocast -- a true zero-copy ROS 2 IPC middleware sharing the publisher's heap with subscribers and adopting ipc_shared_ptr with single-writer -- embody each architecture. Comparative evaluation at the scale of Autoware -- the largest open-source ROS 2 application -- confirms that single-writer achieves sufficient scalability: at 200 topics, two subscribers per topic and 100 Hz, Agnocast's E2E p99.9 is 2.9x lower than iceoryx2's, justifying implementation simplicity over owner-driven reclaim.

</details>
